# Srī-vidagdha-mādhava (S&E)

## Cast of Characters

Kṛṣṇa: son of Nanda and Yaśodā

Balarāma: brother of Kṛṣṇa

Subala: priya-narma-sakhā of Kṛṣṇa

Rādhā: daughter of Vṛṣabhānu and Kirtidā

Viśākhā: daughter of Pāvana and Dakṣiṇā, who was the daughter of Jaṭilā’s sister.

Lalitā: married to Bhairava, a friend of Govardhana

Vṛndā: daughter of Candrabhānu and Phullarā, wife of Mahīpāla

Nāndīmukhī: daughter of Sāndīpani and Sumukhī, and brother of Madumaṅgala

Madhumaṅgala: son of Sāndīpanī and Sumukhī

Paurṇamāsī: mother of Sāndīpani, and grandmother of Madhumaṅgala and Nāndīmukhī

Mukharā: friend of Krsna’s material grandmother Pāṭalā, acting like his grandmother

Sāraṅgī: daughter of Jaṭilā’s sister and sister of Viśāla

Viśāla: brother of Sāraṅgaī and a friend of Kṛṣṇa

Jaṭilā: wife of Gola, mother-in-law of Rādhā

(Gola: brother of Kṛṣṇa’s maternal grandmother, Pāṭalā)

Abhimanyu: son of Gola and Jaṭilā, and apparent husband of Rādhā

Candrāvalī: Rādhā’s main competitor for Kṛṣṇa’s affection

Karālā: maternal grandmother of Candrāvalī

Govardhana: apparent husband of Candrāvalī, wrestler of Kaṁsa

Padmā: chief sakhī of Candrāvalī

Śaibyā: sakhī of Candrāvalī

## Act One: Sound of the Flute

*sudhānām cāndrīṇāṁ mādhurīmonmāda-damanī*  
*dadhānā rādhādi-praṇaya-ghana-sāraiḥ surabhitām \|*  
*samantāt santāpodgama-viṣama-saṁsāra-saraṇī*  
*praṇītāṁ te tṛṣṇāṁ haratu hari-līlā-śikhariṇī \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa reduce the miseries existing in the material world and nullify all unwanted desires. The pastimes of the Lord, like sugar candy, overpower the pride of even the nectar produced from the moon, and distribute the sweet fragrance of Rādhā’s love.**

*anarpita-cariṁ cirāt karuṇayāvatirṇaḥ kalau*  
*samparyitum unnatojjvala-rasāṁ sva-bhakti-śriyam \|*  
*hariḥ puraṭa-sundara-dyuti-kadamba-sandīpitaḥ*  
*sadā hṛdaya-kandare sphuratu vaḥ śaci-nandanaḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May the son of Śacī, shining more brilliant than a mass of gold, who has descended in Kali-yuga by his mercy to offer devotion to himself, who is full of** ***mādhurya-rasa*** **which had never been offered before, appear eternally in the cavity of your heart.\[2\]**

*sutradhāraḥ: alam ati-vistareṇa \| bho bhoḥ! samākarṇyatām \| adyāhaṁ svapnāntare samādiṣṭo’smi bhaktāvatāreṇa bhagavatā śri-śaṅkara-devena yathā, "aye tāṇḍava-kalā-paṇḍita! iha kila ballavī-cakra-ceto-vṛtti-makarī-vihāra-makarālayasya niravadya-veṇu-vādana-vidyā-svādhyāya-siddhīnāṁ prathamādhyāpakasya sugandhi-puṣpāvali-saundarya-tuṇḍilāyām aravinda-bāndhava-nandinī-tirāntaḥ-kānana-lekhāyām avalambita-matta-puṁs-kokila-līlasya paramānanda-vardhini govardhana-nitambe sambhṛta-navyāmbudāḍambarasya kiśora-śiro-maṇer nanda-nandanasya prema-bharākṛṣṭa-hṛdayo nānā-dig-deśataḥ sāmprataṁ rasika-sampradāyo vṛndāvana-vilokanotkaṇṭhayā keśi-tirthopakaṇṭhe samīyivān \|*

*sa ca dhanyaḥ \|*  
*kṛtaṁ gopī-vṛndair iha bhagavato mārgaṇam abhūd*  
*ihāsit kālindī-pulina-valaye rāsa-rabhasaḥ \|*  
*iti śrāvaṁ śrāvaṁ caritāmāsakṛd gokula-pater*  
*luṭhann udbāṣpo’yaṁ katham api dināni kṣapayati \|\|3\|\|*

*tad idānim etasya bhakta-vṛndasya mukunda-viśleṣoddīpanena bahir bhavantaḥ prāṇāḥ kam api tasyaiva keli-sudhā-kallolinīm ullāsayatā parirakṣaṇiyā bhavatā \| mat-kṛpaiva te sāmagrīṁ samagrayiṣyati" iti \| tenādiya jagad-guror asya nideśam evānuvartiṣye \|*

**Перевод:**

**Director: Why speak so many words? Gentlemen! Gentlemen! Please hear me! Last night Gopīsvara Śiva, a devotee of the Lord, instructed me in a dream and said: "O Rūpa Gosvāmī, O expert writer of plays! A fortunate group of devotees has gathered near Keśī-tīrtha with great desire to see Vṛndāvana. Their hearts are completely inundated with p*****rema f*****or Kṛṣṇa, who is the ocean in which all the** ***gopīs*** **play like** ***makarīs,*** **who is the first teacher of all those who have perfected flawless flute playing, who performs pastimes like an intoxicated cuckoo in the groves full of beauty of fragrant flowers on the bank of the Yamunā, and who is the crest jewel of all youthful boys, impulsive as a fresh cloud on the slope of Govardhana which increases the highest bliss.**

**Here the** ***gopīs*** **searched for Kṛṣṇa, and here, in this circle on the bank of the Yamunā, they experienced the joy of the** ***rāsa*** **dance. Hearing and hearing these pastimes of Gokula's master, the devotees, weeping and rolling on the ground, somehow pass their days.**

*pāripārśvikaḥ (praviśya): bhāva, bhavatā nibaddhasya vidagdha-mādhava-nāmno navīna-nāṭakasya prayogānusāreṇa gṛhīta-bhūmikāḥ kuśīlavā raṅga-praveśāya tatra-bhavantam anujñāpayanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Assistant Director (entering): O learned master! The actors, dressed according to the roles in your new drama, are requesting to enter the stage.**

*sutradhāraḥ: māriṣa! nirmitaḥ kim iti tan-nāṭaka-paripāṭībhir varṇikā-parigrahaḥ \| (kṣaṇaṁ vimṛśya) bhavatu,*  
*mamāsmin sandarbhe yad api kavitā nātilalitā*  
*mudaṁ dhāsyanty asyāṁ tad api hari-gandhād budha-gaṇāḥ \|*  
*apaḥ śālagrāmāplavana-garimodgāra-sarasāḥ*  
*sudhīḥ ko vā kaupīr api namita-murdhā na pibati \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Director: O my friend! Have you prepared all the costumes according to the scenes in the play? (After considering a moment) Let that be. Though my play is not attractive with poetic ornaments, the intelligent person will obtain joy since it has a trace of Kṛṣṇa in it. Who would not drink with bowed head the well water which has attained greatness because of bathing a** ***śalagrāma?***

*pāripārśvikaḥ bhāva, raṅga-lakṣmī-kauśala-stutibhir eva sabhyān abhyārthayāmahe \| yad amī vidyādibhir devān api tān upālabdhum utsahante kim uta naṭān asmān \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Assistant: O master! We request the audience to accept the skilful portrayal in this drama. Since the audience can criticize the** ***devatās*** **because of their superior knowledge, what can be said about their criticizing us?**

*sutradhāraḥ: māriṣa, kṛtam etayā vṛthopacāra-caryayā, yataḥ --*  
*aprekṣya klamam ātmano vidadhati prītyā pareṣāṁ priyaṁ*  
*lajjante dūritodyamād iva nija-stotrānubandhād api \|*  
*vidyā-vitta-kulādibhiś ca yad amī yānti kramān namratāṁ*  
*ramyā kāpi satām iyaṁ vijayate naisargikī prakriyā \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Director: O attendant! What is the use of applying such standards to these people! Not considering their own difficulties, they show affection for others with sympathy. Just as others are ashamed because of their sins, they are ashamed when others glorify them--since they grow more and more humble when possessing more and more education, wealth and good parentage. This delightful, natural quality of the cultured people remains victorious.**

*(samantād avalokya, sa-harṣam uccaiḥ) haṁho ballava-siṁha-priyāḥ \| bhagavad-dharmajña-goṣṭhī-gurūṇām api yuṣmākaṁ samakṣaṁ kim apy eṣa vivakṣamāṇas tāṇḍaviko nirapatrapāṇāṁ padavīm āroḍhum upakramate \| tad imāṁ kṣamadhvaṁ cāpalārabhaṭim \| (iti sa-prāṇamaṁ paśyan)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking all around, he exclaims in joy.) O devotees of the best cowherd! This actor wishes to say something to you, who are the gurus in the assembly of devotees. This actor has ascended the podium of the shameless. Please forgive his bold insolence. (Looking, he offers respects.)**

*abhivyaktā mattaḥ prakṛti-laghu-rūpād api budhā*  
*vidhātrī siddhārthān hari-guṇa-mayī vaḥ kṛtir iyam \|*  
*pulindenāpy agniḥ kim u samidham unmathya-janito*  
*hiraṇya-śreṇīnām apaharati nāntaḥ-kalūṣatām \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This work, full of Kṛṣṇa’s qualities, has arisen from me, whose nature is most fallen, but it will fulfill your desires. Does not even a fire produced by a Pulinda drive away the inner contamination of quantities of gold?**

*tad idānīm abhīṣṭa-devaṁ bhagavantam anusmṛtya nṛtya-mādhurīm ullāsayāmi \| (ity añjaliṁ baddhvā)*  
*prapanna-madhurodayaḥ sphurad-amanda-vṛndāṭavī-*  
*nikuñja-maya-maṇḍapa-prakara-madhya-baddha-sthitiḥ \|*  
*niraṅkuśa-kṛpāmbudhir vraja-vihāra-rajyan-manāḥ*  
*sanātana-tanuḥ sadā mayi tanotu tuṣṭiṁ prabhūḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Now remembering my Lord, I will manifest the sweetness of the drama. (He folds his hands.)**

**May my master Kṛṣṇa possessing eternal form, who manifests sweet mercy to the surrendered souls, who remains in a multitude of bowered pavilions, who is an ocean of uncontrollable mercy, whose mind shines in the pastimes of Vraja, always bestow satisfaction to me!**

*pāripārśvikaḥ: bhāva, paśya paśya!*  
*bhaktānām udagād anargala-dhiyāṁ vargo nisargojjvalaḥ*  
*śīlaiḥ pallavitaḥ sa ballava-vadhū-bandhoḥ prabandho’py asau \|*  
*lebhe catvaratāṁ ca tāṇdava-vidher vṛndāṭavī-garbha-bhūr*  
*manye mad-vidha-puṇya-maṇḍala-parīpāko’yam unmilati \|\|8\|\|*  
*tat tvarasva rasa-mādhuri-pariveṣaṇāya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Assistant: O master! Look, look! A naturally effulgent group of devotees, having unimpeded intelligence, has arrived. This composition concerning Kṛṣṇa is permeated with ornate statements. This area within Vṛndāvana has become the arena for enacting the drama. I think that the sum of my pious acts has now born fruit. Therefore without hesitating, distribute the sweetness of rasa.**

*sutradhāraḥ: māriṣa, nīrasāvalī-vaimukhyād viśaṅkamāno manthara ivāsmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Director: O attendant! I proceed somewhat slowly, since I am worried about the opposition from those who have no taste for this.**

*pāripārśvikaḥ: bhāva, kṛtam atra śaṅkayā \| yataḥ \|*  
*udāsatāṁ nāma rasānabhijñāḥ*  
*kṛtau tavāmī rasikāḥ sphuranti \|*  
*kramelakaiḥ kāmam upekṣite’pi*  
*pikāḥ sukhaṁ yānti paraṁ rasāle \|\|9\|\|*  
*tad ārabhyatāṁ sāmājika-cetaś-camatkārāya gāndharva-brahma-vidyā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Assistant: There is no need to worry, master, since people ignorant of rasa will ignore it and those who appreciate rasa will rejoice in your composition. Cuckoos rejoice only in the mango while camels ignore it. Let this play representing the highest spiritual art begin, in order to astonish the hearts of the assembly.**

*sutradhāraḥ: māriṣa, paśya paśya \|*  
*so’yaṁ vasanta-samayaḥ yasmin*  
*purṇaṁ tam iśvaram upoḍha-navānurāgam \|*  
*guḍha-grahā rucirayā saha rādhayāsau*  
*raṅgāya saṅgamayitā niśi paurṇamāsi \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Director: O attendant, look! This is the spring season in which the moon has become newly red in response. The full moon along with the nine planets produces a meeting with beautiful Viśākhā constellation in the night.**

*(nepathye): aye nartaka-sāmanta-sārvabhauma! kathaṁ bhavataḥ karṇa-purī-bhūtā bāḍhaṁ nigūḍheyaṁ sandarbha-mañjarī, yad ahaṁ rādhayā sārdham iśvaraṁ taṁ saṅgamayiṣyāmīti\|*

**Перевод:**

**(offstage) O director famous in all the worlds among all dramatists! How did you hear this secret--that I will arrange a meeting of the lord with Rādhā?**

*sutradhāraḥ (savismayaṁ nepathyābhimukham avalokya) aho, katham ita eva bhagavati paurṇamāsi \| paśya paśya \|*  
*vahanti kāṣāyāmbaram urasi sāndīpani-muneḥ*  
*savitrī sāvitrī-sama-rucir alaṁ pāṇḍura-kacā \|*  
*surarṣeḥ śiṣyeyaṁ parijanavatī nanda-bhavanād*  
*ito mandaṁ mandaṁ sphuṭam uṭaja-vīthīṁ praviśati \|\|11\|\|*  
*tad āvām apy agrataḥ karaṇīyaṁ varṇikāṅgīkāram ālocayāva \| (iti niṣkrāntau)*

**Перевод:**

**Director (looking off stage in astonishment): The great devotee Paurṇamāsī is speaking. Look! With red cloth covering her chest, the white-haired mother of Sāndīpanī, the disciple of Nārada, Paurṇamāsī, effulgent as Brahmā’s wife, leaves Nanda’s house and slowly enters the street lined with huts, accompanied by a friend.**

**Перевод:**

**Let the two of us see if everyone is properly dressed for the beginning of the play. (They exit). (end of the introduction)**

*(tataḥ praviśati sa-parijanā paurṇamāsi \|)*  
*paurṇamāsī ("aye nartaka-sāmanta" iti paṭhitvā): hanta vatse nāndīmukhi! kim api kamanīyaṁ gāyatā sphuṭam ānanditāsmi naṭendreṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Then Paurṇamāsī enters with her friend after saying “O director famous in all the worlds among all dramatists! How did you hear this secret--that I will arrange a meeting of the lord with Rādhā?”)**

**Paurṇamāsī: O dear Nāndīmukhi! The director has given me great bliss by singing something so pleasant.**

*nāndi: bhaavadi \| kiṁ jahatthaṁ edam?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhi: O Paurṇamāsī, is this true?**

*paurṇamāsī:*  
*sambhāvyate phalam alambita-mūla-puṣṭes*  
*tat tādṛśaṁ kva mama bhāgya-taror varoru \|*  
*yenānayoḥ subhagayor ucitā bhaveyaṁ*  
*śṛṅgāra-māṅgalikayor nava-sāṅgamāya \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Nāndīmukhī! How is such a result possible when the tree of my good fortune has been insufficiently nourished in its roots? But I would be delighted if the fortunate couple, blessed with conjugal love, had a fresh meeting by good fortune.**

*nāndi: bhaavadi, ja{i} visahāṇu-ṇandiṇī rāhiā tue kahṇeṇa saṅgamaṇijjā, tado saṅgamāṇu-ulabāsaṁ goulaṁ ujjhia sāntaṇu-bāsa-saṇṇe bhāṇu-titthe kiṁ ti eṣā saṅgobia rakkhidāsī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O great devotee! If Rādhikā is supposed to unite with Kṛṣṇa by your arrangement, why has she given up living in a place favorable for meeting and remain hidden in Bhānu-tīrtha, at a place called Santanu?**

*paurṇamāsī: vatse, nṛśaṁsataḥ kaṁsa-bhūpateḥ śaṅkayā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child! She did this out of fear of cruel king Kaṁṣa.**

*nāndi: bhaavadi, tahabi kahaṁ raṇṇā viṇṇādā rāhī?*

**Перевод:**

**How was Rādhā known to the king?**

*paurṇamāsī: rādhā-saundarya-vṛndam eva vijñāpane nidānam \| yataḥ –*  
*lokottarā guṇa-śriḥ*  
*prathayati parito nigūḍham api vastu \|*  
*pihitām api prayatnād*  
*vyanakti kasturikāṁ gandhaḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There was a cause of his knowing about her great beauty: a hidden object with extraordinary wealth of good qualities spreads itself everywhere. Though hidden with effort, the fragrance of musk reveals its whereabouts.**

*nāndi: bhaavadi, jasoā-dhattīe muhurāe appaṇo ṇattiṇī rāhiā goula-majjhe āṇia jaḍilā-puttassa ahimaṇṇuṇo hatthe ubbāhidā tti, tādisaṁ jebba asamañjasaṁ āpaḍidam \| jaṁ kahṇādo aṇṇena puriseṇa tādisīṇaṁ kara-pphaṁsaṇaṁ tado kadhaṁ tumaṁ tumaṁ ṇiccindā bia dīsasi?*

**Перевод:**

**O revered Paurṇamāsī! Rādhā was brought to Gokula by Mukharā, Yaśodā’s nurse and was married to Jaṭilā’s son Abhmanyu. That is completely improper since persons like her should not be touched by any man except Kṛṣṇa. Why do you seem unconcerned about this?**

*paurṇamāsī: tasyaiva hetoḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Paurṇamāsī: There is a reason for this.**

*nāndi: kahaṁ bia \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nandi: What is that?**

*paurṇamāsī (vihasya): tad vañcanādy-artham eva svayaṁ yoga-māyayā mithyaiva pratyāyitaṁ tad-vidhānām udvāhādikam \| nitya-preyasya eva khalu tāḥ kṛṣṇasya \|*

**Перевод:**

**This was an illusion created by** ***yoga-māyā*** **in order cheat him. He believed that the gopīs got married to other persons. Bur they belong to Kṛṣṇa, their eternal lover.**

*nāndi (sa-harṣam) tā bāḍhaṁ tumaṁ ṇiccindāsi saṁbuttā, jaṁ esā ajja goula-majjhe āṇidā*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore you are without worry, since Rādhā has been brought to Gokula.**

*paurṇamāsī: vatse, satyaṁ bravīṣi \| kaṁsataś cintā me śaithilyam ivopalabdhā, kintu duṣṭābhimanyutaḥ sphuṭaṁ anyā sāmpratam ajaniṣṭa \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child! You speak the truth. Fear of Kaṁsa has decreased but another great worry has arisen because of wicked Abhimanyu.**

*nāndi: kerisi sā?*

**Перевод:**

**What is that worry?**

*paurṇamāsī:*  
*ballavi-nava-latāsu raṅgiṇaṁ*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhṛṅgaṁ adhigatya matsarī \|*  
*rādhikā-puraṭa-padminīm ayaṁ*  
*netum icchati punar vanāntaram \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Understanding that a black bee is playing among the new creepers of gopīs, Abhimanyu has become jealous. He wants to take the golden lotus Rādhā to another forest.**

*nāndi: etthabi joa-māā jjebba samāhāṇaṁ karissadi \|*

**Перевод:**

***Yoga-māyā*** **will solve this problem.**

*paurṇamāsī: putri, ko jānāti svatantrāyās tasyāś caritam? yata īdṛśe’rthe sā taṭasthāyate\|*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter! Who knows the character of Yogamāyā who is independent? At this time case she is aloof.**

*nāndi: aṇṇo bā ettha kobi ubāotthi jeṇa eso paḍibaddho bhave \|*

**Перевод:**

**Is there some other way by which he can be stopped?**

*paurṇamāsī: vatse, tatra mayā pratibhuvā bhavantyā yukti-mādhuri-medureṇa vāg-argalena nisargād agambhīro’yaṁ viṣkambhito’sti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Acting as surety, I will stop superficial Abhimanyu by profuse words, endowed with intense sweetness**

*nāndi (saharṣam) bhaavadi, kaṁsassa go-maṇḍalajjhakkho goaḍḍhaṇo kahṇāṇusāriṇā candāalī-caritteṇa kudo na kuppai?*

**Перевод:**

**(Speaking with joy) Why was Govardhana, appointed by Kaṁsa to be in charge of the cows, not angry by the activities of Candrāvalī when she followed Kṛṣṇa?**

*paurṇamāsī: putri, rāja-kulopalabdhena gauraveṇa garvito’yaṁ vyaktam api tan na śraddadhāti*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter! Proud because of his great royal lineage, Govardhana did not believe this.**

*nāndi: kahaṁ kahṇena paḍhamaṁ se saṅgamo saṁbutto?*

**Перевод:**

**How did Candrāvalī first meet Kṛṣṇa?**

*paurṇamāsī: putri, saṅgame khalu gāḍhānurāgitaiva dūtī babhūva \| mad-udyamānāṁ kevalam ajaniṣṭa piṣṭa-peṣitā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter! As an arranger I had deep attachment for their meeting. My efforts only duplicated what already occurred.**

*nāndi: ajje, tuha kahaṁ erisī bhāa-visesa-bhāvidā gāḍhāṇurāidā uppaṇṇā, jaṁ appaṇo ahiṭṭha-de ahmi aṇuppaṇṇe kahṇe ujjaiṇīṁ ujjhia paḍhamaṁ ccea goulaṁ laddhāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected Paurṇamāsī! How did such intense attraction with special** ***bhāvas*** **arise in you-- so that you gave up Ujjain and first came to Gokula?**

*paurṇamāsī: putri, guru-pādānām upadeśa-prasādena \|*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter! This happened by the mercy of instructions from guru.**

*nāndi: ettha vasantiṁ tumaṁ mahā-bhāo saṁdibaṇī kiṁ kkhu jāṇādi?*

**Перевод:**

**Does the great Sāndīpani know you are living here?**

*paurṇamāsī: atha kim \| yatas tena madhumaṅgalābhidhaḥ sva-putro mamātra paricaryārthaṁ preṣitaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes. Because of this he sent Madhumaṇgala, his son, to serve me here.**

*nāndi: mahumaṅgalo tue suṭṭhu aṇuggahīdo jaṁ eso ṇanda-ṇaaṇa{i}ndīara-candassa sahaaradā mahūsabe ṇiutto \|*

**Перевод:**

**You have been very merciful to Madhumaṅgala for you have engaged him in the joyful service of Kṛṣṇa, the moon and blue lotus for Nanda’s eye.**

*paurṇamāsī: putri, mama sarvasva-rūpāyā rādhāyāḥ kṛṣṇe’nurāga-vistārāya tvaṁ ca niyujyase\|*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter! You should be engaged in making my Rādhā, the highest form, develop love for Kṛṣṇa.**

*nāndi (sānandam): bhaavadi, adibhūmiṁ gado se kahṇe anurāo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(in joy) O Paurṇamāsī! Her love of Kṛṣṇa is the highest of all.**

*paurṇamāsī: katham etal lakṣitam?*

**Перевод:**

**How is that perceived?**

*nāndi: jadā kahā-pasaṅge esā kahṇa tti ṇāmaṁ suṇādi, tadā romāñcidā kampi bhāaṁ vindai\|*

**Перевод:**

**When she hears his name mentioned during a conversation, her hairs stand on end.**

*paurṇamāsī: putri, yuktam idam \| tathā hi --*  
*tuṇḍe tāṇḍavini ratiṁ vitanute tuṇḍāvali-labdhaye*  
*karṇa-kroda-kaḍambini ghaṭayate karṇārbudebhyaḥ spṛhām \|*  
*cetaḥ-prāṅgaṇa-saṅgini vijayate sarvendriyānāṁ kṛtiṁ*  
*no jāne janitā kiyabdhir amṛtaiḥ kṛṣṇeti varṇa-dvayi \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter that is proper. I do not know how much bliss the two syllables Kṛṣṇa produce. When the syllables dance in the mouth, they make one desire to have many mouths. As soon as they enter the ears, they make one desire to have millions of ears. Entering the courtyard of the heart, the syllables defeat the activities of all the senses.**

*nāndi: ajje, dohiṁ lalidā-visāhāhiṁ sahīhiṁ saddhaṁ rāhā sūram ārāhehi \| candāali uṇa pa{u}mā-sebbā-pahudīhiṁ saddhaṁ caṇḍiam \| tā takkemi deadā-pasāa-ṇippādio imāṇaṁ īriso kahṇe anurāo \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā along with Lalitā and Viśākhā worships the sun. Candrāvalī along with Padmā and Śaivyā worships the moon. I suppose that they attained such love of Kṛṣṇa by the mercy of these** ***devatās.***

*paurṇamāsī:*  
*daivata-sevā kevalam*  
*iha vana-yātrānusāriṇī mudrā \|*  
*vraja-subhruvās tu kṛṣṇe*  
*sahajaḥ premā sa jāgarti \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though they went to the forest to worship the** ***devatās,*** **their natural love for Kṛṣṇa simply awakened.**

*nāndi: saccaṁ rāhīe sāhābiaṁ ccea pemmaṁ tatthabi sahīṇaṁ kosalaṁ uddīaṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**True. Rādhā’s natural love acted as a stimulus for that of her friends.**

*paurṇamāsī: putri, mad-girā sandiśyatām ālekhya-vicakṣaṇā viśākhā yatheyaṁ sva-sakhī-netrāravindayor ānandanāya nanda-sūnoḥ praticchandaṁ nirmāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter! Viśākhā, skilful at drawing, should follow my instructions and draw an image of Kṛṣṇa for the bliss of her friend’s lotus eyes.**

*nāndi: jaha āṇabedi bhaavadī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī! As you order I will do.**

*paurṇamāsī: mayāpi modaka-vṛnda-dānāpadeśād vṛndāṭavī-madhyam āsādya rādheti maṅgalākṣara-mādhuryeṇa mādhava-karṇayor dvandvam ānandiyām \|*

**Перевод:**

**And on the pretext of gifting some sweets, I will enter the forest and delight Kṛṣṇa’s ears with the sweet sound of Rādhā’s name.**

*nāndi: ajje, pekkha pekkha eso rāma-mahumaṅgala-siridāma-pahudīhiṁ sahaarehiṁ saddhaṁ goulādo ṇikkamia bundāaṇaṁ gacchanto kahṇo-siṇiddhehiṁ pidarehiṁ jaso-āṇandehiṁ lālijjai \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respectable Paurṇamāsī! Look! Look! Along with Balarāma, Madhumaṅgala,Śrīdāmā and others, Kṛṣṇa leaves Gokula and goes to Vṛndāvana. He is embraced by his affectionate parents Yaśodā and Nanda.**

*paurṇamāsī (vilokya saharṣam \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(She looks with joy.)**

*ayaṁ nayana-daṇḍita-pravara-puṇdarika-prabhaḥ*  
*prabhāti nava-jāguda-dyuti-viḍambi-pitāmbaraḥ \|*  
*araṇyaja-pariṣkriyā-damita-divya-veśādaro*  
*hariṇ-māṇi-manohara-dyutibhir ujjvalāṅgo hariḥ \|\|17\|\|*  
*tad ahaṁ modaka-sampādanāya gaccheyam \| tvaṁ viśākhāṁ yāhi \|*

**Перевод:**

**His eyes surpass the best lotuses. His yellow cloth surpasses the brilliance of fresh** ***kuṁkuma.*** **His forest ornaments surpass respect given to heavenly dress. His limbs shine with the attractive effulgence of emeralds.**

**I should go now to prepare the sweets. You should go to Viśākhā.**

**(She exits.)**

*viṣkambhakaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Interlude**

**(Kṛṣṇa as previously mentioned enters.)**

*śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ (purastād avalokya sānandam)*  
*śreṇī-bhūta-vapuḥ-śriyām abhimukhe go-maṇḍalīṇāṁ kramad*  
*āsāṁ sphāṭika-gaṇḍa-śaila-paṭalī-pāṇḍu-tviṣāṁ vyājataḥ \|*  
*śāṅke jñāta-guṇā purandara-purāc caskanda mandākinī*  
*vṛndāraṇya-vihāri-dhanya-yamunā-sevā-pramodārthinī \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking in front of him with joy) I surmise that the beauty of the pale effulgence of crystal-sided mountains is playing the role of a herd of cows. Or the Mandākinī River, in front of Indra’s city, desiring the pleasure of service to the Yamunā, fortunate to wander in Vṛndāvana, has sprung up here.**

*nanda: vatsa, sādhu varṇitam \| kintu goṣṭha-lakṣmīr api pṛṣṭhataḥ prekṣyatām iti \| (parāvṛtya)*  
*viśālair gośālair bahu-śikhara-śākhā-vitatibhiḥ*  
*parītaiḥ sambādhī-kṛta-savidham ambhodhi-gahanam \|*  
*samṛddhām ā-govardhana-kaṭakam ā-kāliya-hradaṁ*  
*śriyaṁ bibhrad-goṣṭhaṁ sphurati paritas tāvakam idam \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Nanda: O child, you have described this well. But please look at the wealth of cow pastures behind. (Turning, he speaks) Your pastures, nourishing ever-increasing beauty, covering the slopes of Govardhana and extending to Kāliya lake, and crowded closely, like a thick ocean, with groups of huge spires, shine in all directions.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ: sakhe madhumaṅgala! dūram anuyāto’smi tātena \| tad avilambam ambayā sārdhaṁ goṣṭhaṁ praviśyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Madhumaṅgala! I have come from afar with my father. Without delay he returns to the village with my mother.**

*yaśodā: jāda! kitti avaraṇṇebi goṭṭhaṁ ṇa sumarasi jaṁ paramādareṇa mae randhidāiṁ paccahaṁ sīalīhonti miṭṭhaṇṇāiṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O son! Why do you not remember the village in the afternoon? All the food I cook with devotion becomes cold.**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ: goulessari, suṇāhi \| (iti sankṛtena)*  
*gobhyaḥ śape kim api dūsaṇam asya nāsti*  
*(iti vāg-upakrame kṛṣṇaḥ sa-sneham enaṁ paśyati \|)*  
*tābhir yad eṣa rabhasād abhikṛṣyamāṇaḥ*  
*kuñjaṁ viśaty adhika-keli-kalotsukābhiḥ (iti vag-asamaptau)*

**Перевод:**

**Please listen, queen of Gokula! I swear by the cows that Kṛṣṇā is not at fault.**

**(Kṛṣṇa glances at him with affection.) He is strongly attracted by them, eager for play, and enters a bower with them…(He does not complete his speech.)**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (sāpatrapam ātma-gatam): vyaktam eṣa bāliśo ballavībhir iti vakṣyati \| tad enaṁ saṁjñāyā nivārayāmi \| (iti śiras tiro dhunayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Embarrassed, he speaks to himself) The fool will reveal my affair with the gopīs. I must prevent him using a signal. (He begins to shake his head.)**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ: bho baassa, kiṁti me nibāresi? jaṁ ṇiccidaṁ ajja ajjāyā aggado edaṁ biṇṇabissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend! Why do you stop me? For today I will announce this in front all the elders.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (sva-gatam): hanta hanta, lajjā-jāle jālma-dhiyāhaṁ patito’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Ah! Ah! I have fallen in an ocean of shame because of his inconsiderate action.**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ: pitāmbaras tvaritam amba suhṛd-ghaṭābhiḥ \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O mother! Kṛṣṇa hurries to the forest with his group of friends.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (sānandam ātma-gatam): katham anyad evāsya hṛd-gatam?*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully to himself). How could anything else be in his mind?**

*yaśodā: baccha mahumaṅgala! saccaṁ saccaṁ \| lalidā-pahudīo goba-bāliāo yaha idaṁ kahenti tā ḍimbhehiṁ hadahmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear Madhumaṅgala! That is true. That is true. The gopīs like Lalitā tell me this. I am miserable because of these boys.**

*nandaḥ: kuṭumbini! kaccid anurūpā nirūpitāsti gokule kācid bālikā yām udvāhayāmo vatsam\|*

**Перевод:**

**O wife! Can any suitable girl be found in Gokula to whom we can marry our son?**

*yaśodā: ajja, duddha-muhassa bacchassa ko kkhu dāṇīṁ ubbāhe osaro \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respectable husband! He still drinks milk. How can he get married now?**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ (apavārya): baassa, saccaṁ duddha-muho’si jaṁ duddha-luddhāiṁ goba-kisorī-sahassāiṁ tujjha muhaṁ pianti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend! This is true. You still have milk on your mouth. Thousands of gopīs, desiring milk, drink your mouth.**

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa smiles.)**

*nandaḥ: vatsa, paśya paśya—*  
*ahaha kamala-gandher atra saundarya-vṛnde*  
*vinihita-nayaneyaṁ tvan-mukhendor mukunda \|*  
*kuca-kalaśa-mukhābhyām ambara-knopam ambā*  
*tava muhur atiharṣād varṣati kṣira-dhārām \|\|21\|\|*  
*(iti śri-kṛṣṇam āliṅgya sānanadam)*  
*jita-candra-parāga-candrikā-*  
*naladendīvara-candana-śriyam \|*  
*parito mayi śaitya-mādhuriṁ*  
*vahati sparśa-mahotsavas tava \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O son look! Ah! With her eyes focused on the great beauty of the moon of your face, fragrant like a lotus, your mother continually showers streams of milk out of great joy from the mouths of her pot-like breasts, moistening her garments. (Nanda embraces Kṛṣṇa in bliss.) The complete bliss I feel on touching you brings me a cool sweetness that defeats the beauty of camphor, moonlight, the root of** ***vīraṇa*** **grass, the blue lotus and sandalwood.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ: tāta, bubhukṣākṛṣṭam api mat-pratīkṣayā svayaṁ tastambhe go-kadambakam \| tan nivartetāṁ tatra-bhavantau \|*

**Перевод:**

**O father! The herds of cows, though attracted by hunger, remain motionless, waiting for me. O respected parents, please return home.**

*nandaḥ: yathāha vatsaḥ \| (iti sa-snehaṁ kṛṣṇam avalokayan sa-bhāryo niṣkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**I will do as my son says.**

**Перевод:**

**(While looking at Kṛṣṇa with affection, he exists with his wife.)**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (puro’valokya)*  
*sugandhau mākanda-prakara-makarandasya madhure*  
*vinisyande vandī-kṛta-madhupa-vṛndaṁ muhur idam \|*  
*kṛtāndolaṁ mandonnatibhir anilaiś candana-girer*  
*mamānandaṁ vṛndā-vipinam atulaṁ tundilayati \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking in front) The incomparable Vṛndāvana forest agitated by increasing soft breezes from Malaya mountain, making bees hum as sweet, fragrant honey flowers from mango groves, increases my bliss.**

*rāmaḥ: śridāman, paśya paśya \|*  
*vṛndāvanaṁ divya-latā-parītaṁ*  
*latāś ca puṣpa-sphuritāgra-bhājaḥ \|*  
*puṣpāṇy api sphīta-madhu-vratāni*  
*madhu-vratāś ca śruti-hāri-gītāḥ \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Sridāmā! Look! Look! Vṛndāvana is full of delightful creepers. The creepers have flowers on their tips. The flowers are laden with bees. The bees sing songs superior to the Vedas.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ: sakhe madhumaṅgala, bhavad-vidhānām āsatti-śaṁsibhir vaṁśī-gītair ānandayāmi vṛndāṭavī-vāstavyān \| (ity adhare veṇuṁ vinyasyati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Madhumaṅgala! I will delight the inhabitants of the forest including persons like you with the continual song of my flute. (He places his flute on his lips.)**

*rāmaḥ (sāścaryam): hanta, paraspara-viparyasta-svabhāvānām api bhāvānāṁ dharma-viparyayaḥ paśyata \|*  
*jāta-stambhatayā payāṁsi saritāṁ kāṭhinyam āpedire*  
*grāvāṇo drava-bhāva-saṁvalanataḥ sākṣād amī mārdavam \|*  
*sthairyaṁ vepathunā jahur muhur agāj jāḍyād gatiṁ jaṅgamāṁ*  
*vaṁśīṁ cumbati hanta yāmuna-taṭī-krīḍā-kuṭumbe harau \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Astonished) Ah! Objects of contrary natures take on qualities opposite to their natures.**

**The waters of the rivers become hard because of becoming petrified. The rocks, melting, have become soft. Because of trembling, insentient mountains which are immovable by nature accept the active nature of moving creatures, when Kṛṣṇa touches his lips to the flute as he performs profuse pastimes on the bank of the Yamunā.**

*madhumaṅgala: hī hī accariaṁ accariaṁ –*  
*pauradara-galanda-cchīra-kalloliṇīhiṁ*  
*ṇaa-kusuma-ladāṇaṁ hanta soaṁ kuṇantī \|*  
*pibia mahura-baṁśī-ṇāda-pīūsa-pūraṁ*  
*phurai garua-saukkha-tthambhidā dheṇu-patti \|\|26\|\|*  
*(iti kṛṣṇaṁ hastena cālayan) bho pia-baassa, kīsa ṇibbharaṁ gabbāesi \| edāe ccea veṇu-jādīe esā ummādiā paidī \| ettha uṇa ṇimitta-mettaṁ kkhu tumaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**This is most astonishing! Watering the fresh flowers on the creepers with profuse showers of milk, the herds of cows, become stunned in bliss on heartily drinking the nectar of the flute’s sweet sound. (With his hand he makes Kṛṣṇa move forward.) Dear friend! Why are you proud? The nature of the flute is to create madness. You are only its instrument.**

*(ākāśe):*  
*rundhann ambu-bhṛtaś camatkṛti-paraṁ kurvan muhus tumburuṁ*  
*dhyānād antarayan sa-nandana-mukhān vismāpayan vedhasam \|*  
*autsukyāvalibhir baliṁ caṭulayan bhogindram āghūrṇayan*  
*bhindann aṇḍa-kaṭāha-bhītim abhito babhrāma vaṁśī-dhvaniḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Someone recites a verse from the sky.)**

**The sound of Kṛṣṇa’s flute wandered everywhere, piercing through the shell of the universe. It stopped the clouds, amazed the Gandharva Tumburu, broke the meditation of the yogīs headed by Sananda, astonished Brahmā, made Bali unsteady with longing, and made Ananta dizzy.**

*rāmaḥ (sa-harṣam ūrdhvam avalokya sva-gatam): kathaṁ meghāntarito’yaṁ surarṣiḥ padyam upavīṇayāmāsa \| (punar ākāśe kalakalaḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking up in joy he speaks to himself) Why does the sage among the** ***devatās*** **Nārada produce messages using his** ***vīṇa*** **while hiding in the clouds? (More sounds arise in the sky)**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ (ūrdhvam avalokya sa-bhayam): abbahmaṇṇaṁ abbahmaṇṇaṁ \| bho bho, palāahma palāahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking up in fear) Help! Help! I am going to flee.**

*śrīdāmā: bāula! kiṁti ṇiraggalaṁ palabasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Fool! Why do you babble so freely?**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ (ūrdhvam avalokya sa-bhayam) are mukkha goāliā, kiṁ ṇa pekkhasi? eso samārūḍha-haṁso ṇaggeṇa bhuaṅga-dhāriṇā keṇabi bedāleṇa saddhaṁ caummuho kobi jakkho rakkhaso vā āacchadi \| (punar vīkṣya sotkampam \|) hi māṇahe, ede a acchīhiṁ pūrida-sabbaṅgaṁ kaṁpi dāṇaaṁ agge-kadua abare asurā gaaṇaṁ ākkamandi \| tā saṁkemi hada-kaṁsassa kiṁkarā hubissandi \| (iti sa-trāsaṁ kṛṣṇa-kakṣāntare śiras tirayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking to the sky with fear) Ah! Does the foolish cowherd not see? A four-headed demon riding on a swan is approaching with a naked ghost holding a snake. (He looks again while trembling.) Oh! I think that some other demons are assaulting the sky, putting a Dānava with eyes spread all over his body in front. I think these are the servants of dead Kaṁsa. (He moves to the side, behind Kṛṣṇa, in fear.)**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (sva-gatam): katham ete veṇu-nāda-mādhurībhir ākṛṣṭāḥ payoda-vīthīm avagāhante diśām adhīśāḥ \| (iti punar veṇuṁ kvaṇayati):*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Why do the controllers of the direction, attracted by the sweet sound of the flute, hide behind the clouds? (He plays his flute again.)**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ (vilokya socchvāsam ātma-gatam) ede duṭṭha-dāṇaā baassassa veṇu-sadda-metteṇa bihmalā bhavia sajjhaseṇa mujjhanti \| tā jīido hmi \| (iti sāṭopaṁ parikramya prakāśam \|) re re duṭṭhā asurā, ciṭṭhada ciṭṭhada \| eso haṁ sābeṇa cābeṇa vā tuhmāṇaṁ muṇḍāiṁ khaṇḍemi \|(iti daṇḍam udyamya muhur ūrdhvaṁ kūrdati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking around while breathing heavily he speaks to himself.) Confused by the sound of my friend’s flute, these wicked demons have become bewildered with fright. Thus I am still alive. O wicked demons, stand still! I will break your heads with my bow. (Taking a stick, he repeatedly leaps in the air.)**

*rāmaḥ (vihasya): vayasya, maivaṁ bravīḥ \| etau hi bhagavantau hara-hiraṇyagarbhau \| savyataś cāmi purandarādayo vṛndārakāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) O friend do not speak in this manner. These two persons are Śiva and Brahmā. That is Indra on the left along with his followers.**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ (suṣṭhu samāśvasya) bho, jāṇanteṇa ccea mae edaṁ paḍihasidam \| tado tuhmemiṁ kkhu rakkhasa-buddhie bhiluehiṁ palāiduṁ pauttam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Now in a calm state) Oh! I knew that. I was only joking. In fright you were prepared to flee.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (smitvā): haṁho devānām-priya! nijām eva jālmatāṁ teṣu saṅkrāmayasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(smiling) Oh! Madhumaṅgala, dear to the** ***devatās*** **(animal)! You attribute your own foolishness to them.**

*rāmaḥ: paśyata paśyata \|*  
*aṣṭābhiḥ śruti-puṭakair*  
*nava-vaiṇava-kākaliṁ kalayan \|*  
*śata-dhṛtir api dhṛti-mukto*  
*marāla-pṛṣṭhe muhur luṭhati \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look! Listening to fresh flute music with his eight ears, Brahmā, having lost his composure, is rolling about on the back of his swan.**

*ākāśe (punar vīṇā-gītiḥ)*  
*udite hari-vaktreṇdau*  
*veṇu-nāda-sudhā-muci \|*  
*hanta rudra-samudreṇa*  
*sva-maryādā vilaṅghitā \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(In the sky** ***vīṇā*** **music is again heard.)**

**When the moon of Kṛṣṇa’s face rises, releasing the nectar of his flute notes, the ocean of Śiva flows beyond its shores.**

*rāmaḥ (sotkaṇṭhaṁ):*  
*muralī-kalā-parimalān ākarṇya ghūrṇat-tanor*  
*etasyākṣi-sahasrataḥ sura-pater aśruṇi sasrur bhuvi \|*  
*citraṁ vāri-dharān vināpi tarasā yair adya dhārā-mayair*  
*dūrāt paśyata deva-mātṛkam abhūd vṛndāṭavī-maṇḍalaḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(With longing) Hearing the sound of Kṛṣṇa’s flute, the thousand eyes on Indra’s shaking body are shedding tears, which are falling on the ground. Though there are no clouds in the sky, Vṛndāvana has been nourished by showers made of those tears.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (sva-gatam): purāṇānām amīṣāṁ purastād vihāre saṅkucanti me ceto-vṛttayaḥ \| tad-agre yāmi \| (iti tarūṇām antaram āsādya prakāśam \|) sakhe madhumaṅgala, paśya paśya mādhaviyāṁ vana-mādhurīm \|*  
*kvacid bhṛṅgī-gītaṁ kvacid anila-bhaṅgī-śiśiratā*  
*kvacid vallī-lāsyaṁ kvacid amala-mallī-parimalaḥ \|*  
*kvacid dhārā-śālī karaka-phala-pālī-rasa-bharo*  
*hṛṣīkāṇāṁ vṛndaṁ pramadayati vṛndāvanam idam \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I feel hesitant to play in front of these** ***devatās.*** **I will go ahead. (He goes among some trees.) O my friend Madhumaṅgala, look at the sweetness of the forest in spring! Vṛndāvana, with singing of the bees, the cool touch of the intermittent breeze, the dancing of creepers, the fragrance of pure jasmines, and the taste of abundant pomegranates flowing in streams, gives joy to all the senses.**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ: bho baassa, edāe duṭṭha-bhiṅgī-bhaaṅkarīe kiṁ me kodūhalaṁ tujjha bundāḍaie \| ahaṁ kkhu caubbihehiṁ aṇṇehiṁ sabbendiahāriṇīṁ goulessarīe rasabaiṁ jjebba daṭṭhuṇa rañjemi \|*

**Перевод:**

**How can I be happy in your forest when it is filled with frightful bees? I become happy on seeing tasty four types of foods, attractive to all the senses, cooked by Yaśodā.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ: vayasya, vandasva vṛndāṭavīm eva \| sphuṭam asyāḥ purāṇa-vallarībhir api tavābhīṣṭaṁ phalam ullāsayituṁ samarthyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, please offer respects to Vṛndāvana. It can satisfy your desires with its ancient creepers.**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ: bho pia-baassa! tumaṁ saccabādi tti sabba-loehiṁ bhaṇijjasi \| tā imassa tujjha vaaṇassa mae parikkhā kādabbā \| (ity añjaliṁ baddhvā) bho ballarīo, eso haṁ bandemi\| buhu khido me baasso \| tā dentu khaṇḍa-laḍḍuāiṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear friend! Everyone says you are truthful. Therefore I should test those words. (He folds his hands.) O creepers! I offer respects to you! My friend is hungry. Please give him some laḍdus.**

*(praviśya modaka-pūrṇa-pātra-hastā paurṇamāsi \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Paurṇamāsī enters with a plate of sweets in her hand.)**

*paurṇamāsī: candrānana kṛṣṇa! gṛhāṇa rasajñā-modakān abhūn modakāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O moon-faced Kṛṣṇa! Accept these tasty sweets. They should please you.**

*rāmaḥ (sa-smitam) vayasya, dṛṣṭā jarad-vallarī-vadānyatā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O friend, I have seen the generosity of this old creeper.**

*paurṇamāsī: saṅkarṣaṇa! jarad-ballavī-vadānyateti bhaṇyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Balarāma! You should say “I have sseen the generosity of the old cowherd woman.”**

*kṛṣṇaḥ: ārye, keyaṁ jarad-ballavī?*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī! What old cowherd woman do you mean?**

*paurṇamāsī: candramukha, mukharā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O moon-faced Kṛṣṇa! It is Mukharā.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ: tayā kim akāṇde khaṇḍa-laḍḍukāni samarpitāni?*

**Перевод:**

**Why did she send these laḍḍus unexpectedly?**

*paurṇamāsī: naptrī tāvad etayā abhimanyoḥ pāṇau pariṇāyitā \| tad-utsavābhirūpaḥ samudācāro’yam anusāsre \|*

**Перевод:**

**Her granddaughter will be married to Abhimanyu by Mukharā’s arrangement. As part of the festivities she has ordered this.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ: keyaṁ naptrī?*

**Перевод:**

**Who is the granddaugther?**

*paurṇamāsī: rādhikābhidhā kācid ānanda-kaumudī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Some blissful moonlight named Rādhikā.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (saromāñcaṁ sva-gatam) śrutaṁ nūnam ambayoḥ saṁvāde śaśvad asyāḥ sauṣṭhavam \| (iti kampamāno vrīḍāṁ nāṭayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(His hairs stand on end. He speaks to himself.) I have heard of her great beauty when Yaśodā and Rohiṇī speak. (Trembling, he becomes embarrassed.)**

*paurṇamāsī (sva-gatam): kṛṣṇaṁ vilakṣam avekṣya nūnaṁ rāmaḥ sa-vyājāmāsau savyataḥ prayāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Seeing how Kṛṣṇa reacts, Balarāma has moved to his left side to hide his reaction.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (punar ātma-gatam): vikriyāṁ saṅgopayituṁ prasaṅgāntaram aṅgīkuryām \| (prakāśam) ārye! adya madhu-vāsare tvayāpi kācin mahotsava-lakṣmīr alaṅkriyatām \| paśya jarad-vallī-śreṇir iyaṁ phullā pallavitā ca \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) To hide these reactions, I should change the topic. (Aloud) O Paurṇamāsī! Today, on this spring day, you should decorate yourself with a wealth of ornaments. See the old creepers blossoming and laden with buds.**

*paurṇamāsī (sa-smitam) nāgara! tavaiva mahotsavānām avasaro’yaṁ pravṛttaḥ \| yad atra puṣpāṇāṁ pallavānāṁ ca tṛṣṇayā ballavānāṁ vilāsinyaḥ sameṣyanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O clever boy! The opportunity for your festivals has come. The gopīs out of thirst for flowers and buds will come here.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (sa-smitaṁ tiryag avekṣya \|) ārye! tataḥ kim?*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling, he looks to the side.) O respected Paurṇamāsī, then what will happen?**

*paurṇamāsī (vihasya): vilāsin! sva-vāsanānusārād anyathā mā śaṅkiṣṭhāḥ \| param evam abhiprāyāsmi \| tatas tāsāṁ śūnyeṣu sadmasu sakhibhis te sukham apahartavyāni gavyāni \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) O playful boy! Do not have anxiety that your desires will not be fulfilled. This is what I mean: when they come here, your friends can steal the yogurt from their empty houses.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ: dhūrte! kiṁ parihasyate? paśya komala-mañjarīm avacinvatīnāṁ ballavīnāṁ maṇḍalena khaṇḍitāni me vṛndāṭavī-śākhi-viṭapāni \| tad etās te nivāraṇīyāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O deceiver! Why do you joke? The shrubs and branches in my forest were broken by the group of gopīs picking the tender buds. You should stop them from doing this.**

*paurṇamāsī: mohana! navya-stavakottaṁsinā bhavataiva samullāsito’yaṁ kusumeṣu rāgo ballavīnām \| tāḥ katham ito nivāryatām? \[51\]*

**Перевод:**

**O enchanting Kṛṣṇa! The gopīs’ desire for flowers has been stimulated by seeing you wear your crown of new flower clusters. How can I stop them?**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (smitvā): ayi balākāvalakṣa-keśi \| kathopakramād vakram eva panthānam adhirūḍhāsi, yad aparādhikāsv api ballavīṣu pakṣa-pātaṁ na muñcasi \|\[52\]*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O white-haired Paurṇamāsī! You walk a crooked path with such words since you do not give up favoring the offending gopis.**

*paurṇamāsī: sundara! samprati sa-rādhikāḥ khalu ballavyaḥ katham aparādhikāḥ santu \| tena te priyasya punnāgasyāpi sumana-steyaṁ haṭhena kariṣyanti \|\[53\]*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful Kṛṣṇa! How can these gopīs with Rādhā be offenders, since they will only steal some flowers from your favorite** ***punnāga*** **tree?**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (sva-gatam): hanta, kathaṁ maṇohāriṇī saiva daivāt punar āvartate rādhikā-vārtā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Ah! How has attractive news about Rādhā again come here by fate?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sva-gatam): kadhaṁ rāhī tti ṇāma metteṇa unmaṇāedi eso \| (prakāśam) bho baassa! mā kkhu imāe ubari ṇibbharaṁ sattiṇṇo hohi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) He becomes excited just by hearing Rādhā’s name. (Aloud) O friend! Do not have such greed for that.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-praṇaya-roṣam) dhig vācāla! kutrāhaṁ sa-tṛṣṇaḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**(With affectionate anger) O talkative fellow! For whom do I show thirst?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho, mā kuppaha \| sarasāe manoharālīe ubari tti bhaṇāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Oh! Do not become angry! I was speaking about the tasty sweets called “Manoharī.”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, bhrānto’si \| nemāni manoharākhyāni kintu mauktikākhyāni laḍḍukāni \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend you are mistaken. These are not called Manoharī but Mauktikā laḍdus.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(vihasya): pia-baassa, ṇa kkhu ahaṁ bhami-sīle rāhā-cakked vaṭṭāmi \| kudo bhamissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) Dear friend, I am not standing in the revolving zodiac with Viśākhā constellation. How could I make a mistake?**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sva-gatam): satyaṁ parihasyate baṭunā \| yad eṣa bhāvodvṛtta-ceto-vṛttitayā vailakṣya-bhāg abhilakṣyate tad adya purṇa-kāṁasmi \| (prakāśam) sundara, kṛtam atrotkaṇṭhayā \| sā viṣṇu-pada-vīthī-sañcāriṇī rādhā nṛ-loke kena labhyatām?*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) It is true. Madhumaṅgala is joking with him and he reveals embarrassment with revelation of his inner feelings. Today my desires are fulfilled. (Aloud) O beautiful Kṛṣṇa! What is the use of hankering? How can a human obtain Rādhā (viśākhā) constellation wandering on the path in the sky?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-smitaṁ viṣṇu-pada-vīthīm avekṣya rāmam anusarpan): ārya! vyatīteyaṁ madhyāhna-maryādā \| tataḥ kālindī-tire’vatīrya samāpāyantu bhavantaḥ paśūnāṁ pāniya-tṛṣṇām \| svadayantu ca svādūni laḍḍukāni \| mayā tu suhṛttamābhyāṁ śridāma-subalābhyāṁ saha muhūrtam agrato viśramitavyam \|*  
*(rāmaḥ sakhibhiḥ saha niṣkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling, looking at the sky, he follows Balarāma.) O brother! Noon has now passed. You should relieve your cows of thirst by going to the bank of the Yamunā. They should eat tasty laḍdus. I will relax for a few moments with my friends Śridāmā and Subala.**

**Перевод:**

**(Balarāma exits with his friends.)**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sva-gatam):* *mayāpi praticchandasya siddhim avadhārayituṁ gantavyam \| (iti kṛṣṇam abhinandya parikrāmati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) I should go and see if the picture is finished. (Saying farewell to Kṛṣṇa she exits.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(padāntare sthitvā): sakhe śridāman! kiṁ dṛṣṭa-pūrvā te jagad-apūrvā rādhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Taking a step he stops.) O friend Śridāmā! Have you ever seen the remarkable Rādhā?**

*(śrīdāmā sa-lajja-smitaṁ mukham avāñcayati)*

**Перевод:**

**(Śridāmā lowers his face in embarrassment.)**

***subalaḥ:*** *baassa, diṭṭha-pūbbetti kiṁ ettiaṁ bhaṇāsi? ṇa imassa bahiṇi kkhu esā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Why do you ask if he has ever seen her? She is his sister.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *tad ehi \| kṣaṇam atra kadamba-sambādhe rodhasi niviśya rādhānudhāvanād udvegi-ceto vaṁśī-vādana-vinodenāyataḥ kṣipāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Come. Going to the edge of this** ***kadamba*** **forest for a moment, I will absorb my heart, agitated from purusing Rādhā, in playing the flute.**

*(They exit.)*

**Перевод:**

**paurṇamāsī (parikramya puraḥ paśyanti paśyanti sānandam \|) katham ita eva vayasyayā vihasyamāṇa vikrīḍati me vatseyaṁ rādhikā \| (iti latāntare sthitvā):**

*balād akṣnor lakṣmīḥ kavalayati navyaṁ kuvalayaṁ*  
*mukhollāsaḥ phullaṁ kamala-vanam ullaṅghayati ca \|*  
*daśāṁ kaṣṭām aṣṭā-padamapi nayaty āṅgika-rucir*  
*vicitraṁ rādhāyāḥ kim api kila rūpaṁ vilasati \|\|32\|\|*  
*tad etayor nirmala-narma-goṣṭhi-pratibandhaṁ parihantī vīrun-niruddhenādhvanā viśākhāṁ yāmi \| (iti niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking forward while looking everywhere with joy.) Why is my dear Rādhā laughing and playing with a friend. (She stands behind a creeper.)**

**How the remarkable beauty of Rādhā shines! The beauty of her eyes forcibly devours the fresh blue lotus. The splendor of her face surpasses a forest of blossoming lotuses. The luster of her body defeats gold. Without interrupting their pure joking, I will go to Viśākhā on this path hidden by bushes.**

*(tataḥ praviśati lalitayānugamyamānā rādhikā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā enters with Lalitā.)**

***rādhikā:*** *halā lalide! kiṁ karedi ajjiyā?*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā! What is old Jaṭilā doing?**

***lalitā:*** *sahi, tuha sura-deassa puākide esā tamāla-tale vediāṁ ṇimmādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**She is making an altar under a** ***tamāla*** **tree for worshipping the sun god.**

***rādhikā*** *(puro’valokya) halā lalide, saṅkemi sā ccea esā bundāḍa{i} jāe māhurī tue puṇo puṇo mama vaṇṇiadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking in front) O Lalitā! I think this must be the Vṛndāvana forest whose sweetness you have repeatedly described.**

***lalitā:*** *halā, sā jjebba esā kahṇassa līlā-rukkha-bāḍiā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend! This is the banyan tree where Kṛṣṇa performs pastimes.**

***rādhikā*** *(sautsukyam ātma-gatam): aho mahurattaṁ doṇaṁ accharāṇam \| (prakāśam) sahi, kassa tti bhaṇāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself with longing) Ah! What sweetness in these two syllables. (Aloud) Friend! Whose name did you say?**

***lalitā*** *(sākuta-smitam): halā, bhaṇāmi kahṇassa tti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I said “Kṛṣṇa.”**

***rādhikā*** *(****punaḥ sva-gatam):*** *hanta jassa ṇāmābi bāmā-cittaṁ itthaṁ mohedi \| so kkhu kidiso vā ṇāmi tti \| (iti sāvahitthaṁ prakāśam) halā, imāiṁ niuñjobari puñjidāiṁ guñjā-phalāiṁ biiṇissaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Again to herself) Ah! Who is the person with this name that bewilders the noble heart? (Aloud, concealing her feelings) O friend, I will try to pick the piles of** ***guñja*** **berries on the top of that grove.**

***lalitā*** *(saparihāsam, saṁskṛtena):*  
*dehaṁ te bhuvanāntarāla-virala-cchāyā-vilāsāspadaṁ*  
*mā kautūhala-cāñcalākṣi latikā-jāle praveśaṁ krṭhāḥ \|*  
*navyām añjana-puñja-mañjula-ruciḥ kuñje-carī devatā*  
*kāntāṁ kāntibhir aṅkitām iha vane niḥśaṅkam ākarṣati \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(joking) O girl with restless eyes! Your body is a temple of splendor rare in the three worlds. Do not enter that tangle of tender creepers. A** ***devatā*** **whose beautiful complexion is like lamp-black moves in that forest. He fearlessly attracts a woman marked with beauty.**

***rādhikā*** *(kiñcid bhiteva parāvṛtya sa-narma-smitam): sahi lalide, tāe deadāe ṇumaṁ tumaṁ āaḍḍhidāsi jaṁ edaṁ jāṇāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(As if afraid, she conceals her joking smile.) O my friend Lalitā! You know that you are under the control of this** ***devatā.***

***lalitā*** *(vihasya): halā, kīsa maṁ esā āaḍḍhadu \| ṇa kkhu ahaṁ tumaṁ bia kāntīhiṁ aṅkidā\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend! Why should the** ***devatā*** **allure me? I have not been marked by lovers like you.**

*(nepathye vaṁśī-dhvaniḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(The sound of the flute is heard offstage)**

***rādhikā*** *(niśamya camatkāraṁ sva-gatam): ammahe, imassa mohaṇattaṇaṁ saddassa \| (iti vaivaśyaṁ nāṭayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Listening in astonishment, she speaks to herself.) How astonishing is the bewildering power of that sound. (She loses control.)**

***lalitā*** *(vilokya sva-gatam) huṁ, esā komalāṅgī kuraṅgī paḍhamaṁ jāle ṇipaḍidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at her, she speaks to herself) I understand. This young doe has fallen into a trap for the first time.**

***rādhikā*** *(prayatnena dhairyam ālambya sva-gatam): abi ṇāma ṇaṁ saddāmi-apuraṁ uggirantaṁ jaṇaṁ pekkhissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Bringing herself under control, she says to herself) I must behold the person who is the source of this sweet sound.**

***lalitā*** *(upasṛtya): halā rāhi, atthi mad-ubari tuha visaddha-buddhī \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Coming close) O Rādhā! You should trust me.**

***rādhikā:*** *halā, kīsa ebbaṁ bhaṇāsi? tumaṁ jebba tattha pamāṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, what are you saying? You are the authority for this.**

***lalitā:*** *kadhedu pia-sahi kiṁti akāṇḍe vivasā asi tumam?*

**Перевод:**

**Please tell me friend, have you become helpless by this sound?**

***rādhikā*** *(salajjam, saṁskṛtena):*  
*nādaḥ kadamba-viṭapāntarato visarpan*  
*ko nāma karṇa-padavīm aviśan na jāne \|*  
*hā hā kulīna-gṛhiṇī-gaṇa-garhaṇīyāṁ*  
*yenādya kām api daśāṁ sakhi lambhitāsmi \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Shamefully) My friend,what is this sound that has emerged from the interior the** ***kadamba*** **forest and hasenteredmy ears? I cannot understand it. Oh! It has forced me into a condition very abominable for a respectable woman.**

***lalitā:*** *halā, eso muralī-rao \|*

**Перевод:**

**That is the sound of his flute.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-vyatham, saṁskṛtena):*  
*ajaḍaḥ kampa-sampādī*  
*śāstrād anyo nikṛntanaḥ \|*  
*tāpano’nuṣṇatādhārī*  
*ko vāyaṁ muralī-ravaḥ \|\|35\|\|*  
*(ity udvegaṁ nāṭayantī) halā, ṇāhaṁ muralī-ṇāassa aṇahiṇṇā \| tā alaṁ vippalambheṇa \| phuḍaṁ eso keṇa bi mahā-ṇāareṇa kobi mohaṇa-manto paḍhīadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(in pain) What is the sound of this flute? Without being cold, it causes shivering. Though it is not a weapon, it cuts. Though it is not hot, it burns. (In confusion) O friend! I am not ignorant of this flute. There is too much deception here. It is clear that some great cheater is reciting mantras which cause illusion.**

*(praviśya citra-paṭa-hastā viśākhā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Viśākhā enters with a picture in her hand.)**

***viśākhā*** *(rādhām avadhārayantī sva-gatam): dāṇiṁ aṇṇādisi esā lakkhīadi \| tā ṇūṇaṁ kaṇḥassa vaṁsīāe ḍaṁsidā \| hodu, pucchissam \| (ity upasṛtya prakāśam, saṁskṛtena \|)*  
*kṣoṇīṁ paṅkilayanti paṅkaja-rucor akṣṇoḥ payo-bindavaḥ*  
*śvāsās tāṇḍavayanti pāṇḍu-vadane dūrād urojāṁśukam \|*  
*mūrtiṁ danturayanti santatam amī romāñca-puñjāś ca te*  
*manye mādhava-mādhurī śravaṇayor abhyāsam abhyāyayau \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing Rādhā she speaks to herself.) She seems to be someone else. She must have been bitten by Kṛṣṇa’s flute. Let that be. I will ask. (Approaching, she speaks aloud.)**

**O fair-faced Rādhā! The tears from your lotus eyes are turning your black mascara into mud;your sighsare making your bodice dance;and a network of erect hairs make your body prickly. I think the sweetness of Mādhava must have approached your ears.**

***rādhikā*** *(anākarṇitakenaiva sotkampam): lalide, puṇo eso jjebba kobi saddo vikkamadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Not hearing her words she begins trembling.) O Lalitā! Again this strange sound is attacking me.**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*eṣa sthairya-bhujaṅga-saṅgha-damanāsaṅge vihaṅgeśvaro*  
*vriḍā-vyādhi-dhurā-vidhūnana-vidhau tanvaṅgi dhanvantariḥ \|*  
*sādhvī-garva-bharāmburāśi-culukārambhe tu kumbhodbhavaḥ*  
*kālindī-taṭa-maṇḍalīṣu muralī-tuṇḍād dhvanir dhāvati \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā! A sound rushes from the mouth of the flute on the bank of the Yamunā. It is Garuḍa since it is devoted to destroying the composure of all the snakes. It is Danvantari, god of medicine, for it cures shyness with violent shaking. It is Agastya in attempting to drink the great ocean of chaste women.**

***rādhikā:*** *sahi, jādā maha hiae kābi guruī beaṇā \| tā gadua supissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend! A great, indescribable pain has arisen in my heart. Going over there, I will sleep.**

***viśākhā:*** *halā rāhi! tuha beaṇā-biddhaṁsaṇaṁ kimpi edaṁ osahaṁ maha hatthe baṭṭadi \| tā sevehi ṇaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Rādhā! I have a medicine in my hand to destroy that pain. Please use it.**

***rādhikā:*** *viśākhe, ehi \| aṅgaṇobakaṇṭhe phulla-kaṇṇiāra-maṇḍalī-cchāaṁ ajjhāsia pekkhahme\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Viśākhā! Come. Entering into the shade of the blossoming** ***karṇika*** **flowers near this open area, we will observe.**

**Перевод:**

**(They exit.)**

*dvitīyo’ṅkaḥ*  
*manmatha-lekhā*

## Act Two: Cupid’s Letter

*(tataḥ praviśati nāndīmukhī \|)*

***nāndīmukhī:*** *ādiṭṭhahmi tattahodie paurṇamāsie, jadhā—a{i} ṇandimuhi, sudaṁ mae nibbharā asuttha-sarīrā me bacchā rāhī \| tā gadua jāṇīhi se tattaṁ tti \| tado muharā-gharaṁ gamissaṁ \| (iti parikramya puṇaḥ paśyanti \|) kahaṁ idha jjebba kandantī muharā āaccha{i}\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Nāndīmukhī enters.)**

**I was ordered by Paurṇamāsī: “O Nāndīmukhī! I have heard that my child Rādhā is very ill. Go to here and find out the truth.” Therefore, I will go to Mukharā’s house. (She starts to go, but then sees. Mukharā.) Why is Mukharā coming here weeping?**

***mukharā:*** *haddhī haddhī, hadahmi manda-bhāiṇi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! I am most unfortunate! I am devastated.**

***nāndīmukhī:****ajje muhare, kīsa roasi?*

**Перевод:**

**O Mukharā, why are you crying?**

***mukharā****(vilokya): bacche, rāhī-sandābeṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing her) O child! It is because Rādhā is suffering.**

***nāndīmukhī:*** *kerisaṁ ceṭṭha{i} rāhī?*

**Перевод:**

**How does she act?**

***mukharā:*** *bacche, bāulā bhavia kiṁ bi palaba{i} \| (iti saṁskṛtena)*

**Перевод:**

**krurāṇām alināṁ kulair malinayā kṛtyaṁ na me mālayā**

*bālāhaṁ kim u narmaṇas tava padaṁ dūrībhava prāṅgaṇāt \|*  
*ity ādīni durakṣarāṇi paritaḥ svapne tathā jāgare*  
*jalpanti jalajekṣaṇā kṣapayati kleśena rātrindivam \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O child! She has gone mad and babbles nonsense. “My garland should not be blackened by the swarm of cruel bees. I am only a child. Why do you joke with me? Go away from this yard.” Waking or sleeping, uttering such words, lotus-eyed Rādhā passes her days and nights in pain.**

***nāndīmukhī*** *(sva-gatam):* *uvasagga-kidā na kkhu erisī palāba-muddā \| tā diṭṭhiā vikkamidaṁ ettha kahṇa-vilāseṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Her talk is not caused by madness. By great fortune she is overpowered by Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes.**

***mukharā:*** *bacche, ahaṁ gadua bhaavadiṁ biṇṇabissaṁ \| tumaṁ bedasī-kuñjaṁ ubasappia rāhiaṁ peccha \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child! I will go and inform Paurṇamāsī. You should go to the cane bower and oversee Rādhā.**

*(iti niṣkrānte \|)*  
*(tataḥ praviśati sakhibhyām upāsyamānā rādhā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(She exits. Rādhā enters, served by two friends.)**

***rādhikā*** *(sodvegaṁ sva-gatam): hada-hiaa! jassa paḍicchanda-daṁsaṇamettādo irisī durūha-saṅgamā ubatthidā de abasthā ttha bi puṇo rāaṁ bahasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself in an agitated state.) O my injured heart! Just be seeing his picture, such an incomprehensible connection has taken place. You have developed great attraction just by this.**

***ubhe:*** *halā rāhī! āmaehinto vilakkhaṇo de beaṇāṇubandhe lakkhiadi \| tā kīsa ahmesu tattaṁ ṇa kadhesi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā and Viśākhā speak: O Rādhā! We can see symptoms of suffering in your illness. Why not tell us the truth.**

*(rādhikā niḥśvasya vaktraṁ vyāvartayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā sighs and turns her face away.)**

***viśākhā*** *(puro’bhigamya, saṁskṛtena)*  
*cintā-santatir adya kṛntati sakhi svāntasya kiṁ te dhṛtiṁ*  
*kiṁ vā siñcati tāmram ambaram ati-svedāmbhasāṁ ḍambaraḥ \|*  
*kampaś campaka-gauri lumpati vapuḥ-sthairyaṁ kathaṁ vā balāt*  
*tathyaṁ brūhi na maṅgalā parijane saṅgopanāṅgīkṛtiḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(She comes in front of Rādhā.)**

**O friend! How has this host of anxieties broken the steadinessof your heart? Why has your red cloth become excessively wet with perspiration? How has trembling forcibly stolen away the stiffness of your body? O Rādhā, as golden as a** ***campaka*** **flower! Please tell the truth. It is not auspicious to conceal things from friends.**

***rādhikā*** *(sāsuyam) a{i} ṇiṭṭhure bisāhe, tumaṁ ebbaṁ pucchantī bi ṇa lajjasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With hatred) O cruel Viśākhā! Are you not ashamed to ask about this?**

***viśākhā*** *(saśaṅkam) halā, kahiṁ pi abaraddhahmi tti ṇa sumarāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Worried) O friend, I do not remember having offended you in any way.**

***rādhikā:*** *a{i} ṇikkibe, kīsa ebbaṁ bhaṇāsi? sumaria pekkha \|*

**Перевод:**

**You are without compassion! Why do you speak in this way? Just trying remembering!**

***viśākhā:*** *halā, garueṇa bi ppaṇihāṇeṇa ṇa me sumaraṇaṁ hodi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I am trying hard but cannot remember anything.**

***rādhikā:*** *ummatte, gahaṇe imassiṁ acchāhidāṇala-kuṇḍe tumaṁ jjebba maha pakkhebaṇī*

**Перевод:**

**O insane woman! You have thrown me into a deep pool of tortuous fire.**

***viśākhā:*** *kadhaṁ bia \|*

**Перевод:**

**How did I do that?**

***rādhikā*** *(serṣyam) a{i} micchā-sarale! ālekkha-gada-bhuaṅga-saṅgiṇi! ciṭṭha ciṭṭha! (iti sa-vaivaśyaṁ saṁskṛtena)*

*vitanvānas tanvā marakata-rucīnāṁ rucivatāṁ*  
*paṭān niṣkrānto’bhūd dhṛta-śikhaṇḍo nava-yuvā \|*  
*bhruvaṁ tena kṣiptvā kim api hasatonmādita-mateḥ*  
*śaśī vṛtto vahniḥ param ahaha vahnir mama śaśī \|\|3\|\|*  
*(ity ardhoktau vāk-stambhaṁ ṇaṭayati \| sakhyau sa-bhrū-bhāngam anyo’nyaṁ paśyataḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) False friend! You are a friend to the snake in this picture. Remain where you are. (Out of control). A new youth whose body emanated the attractiveness of a beautiful sapphire and was decorated with a peacock feather came out of the picture and laughed at me while moving his eyebrows at me. I have become insane. To me, the moon is now fire and fire is the moon. (In the midst of reciting this, she stops speaking. Her two friend look at each other while frowning.)**

***lalitā:*** *halā, kiṁ eso siviṇṇassa vilāso?*

**Перевод:**

**Did this happen in a dream, my friend?**

***rādhikā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*kiṁ svapnasya vilakṣaṇā gatir iyaṁ kiṁ jāgarasyāthavā*  
*kiṁ rātrer upasattir eva rabhasād ahnaḥ kim ahnāya vā \|*  
*itthaṁ śyāmala-candrikā-paricaya-spandena sandīpitair*  
*antaḥ-kṣobha-kulair ahaṁ parivṛtā prajñātum ajñābhavam \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Did these strange events happen in a dream, because of rashly worshipping at night or did it happen while awake because of hasty worship during the day. In this way, I have become ignorant to properly understand, since I have become inundated by many internal disturbances intensified by vibrations of the dark moon.**

***viśākhā*** *(sākutam) halā rāhī, ṇuṇaṁ eso de citta-vibbhamo jebba kkhaṇio \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Expressing a wish) O my friend Rādhā, this agitation of the heart is only temporary.**

***rādhikā*** *(sābhyasuyam) a{i} avisaddhe, viramehi, kīsa appaṇo dosaṁ jhampiduṁ pa{u}ttāsi? (iti saṁskṛtena)*  
*kṛtāṁ bhakti-cchedair ghusṛṇa-ghana-carcām adhivahan*  
*punar labdho lubdhaḥ priyaka-taru-mūle caṭula-dhīḥ \|*  
*lapantyāḥ sākṣepaṁ nahi nahi nahīti smita-mukho*  
*haṭhān me durlīlaḥ sa kila bhuja-vallī-dalam adhāt \|\|5\|\|*

*tataś ca—*  
*daronmīlan-nīlotpala-dala-rucas tasya niviḍād*  
*virūḍhānāṁ sadyaḥ kara-sarasija-sparśa-kutukāt \|*  
*vahantī kṣobhāṇāṁ nivaham iha nājñāsiṣam idaṁ*  
*kva vāhaṁ kā vāhaṁ cakara kim ahaṁ vā sakhi tadā \|\|6\|\|*

*iti vaivaśyaṁ nāṭayati sva-gatam \|) a{i} duṭṭha-hiaa makkaḍa, kahṇo vaiṇavio sāmala-kisoro tti tiṇesu purisesu rāaṁ vahanto bi tumaṁ ṇa lajjasi \| tā dāṇiṁ appāṇaṁ bābādia pāmaraṁ tumaṁ hadāsaṁ karissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Indignantly) O faithless one! Stop this! Why do you try to cover up your fault? Anointed with thick** ***kuṁkuma*** **with various colorful lines, filled with intense greed, sitting with fickle mind at the base of a** ***kadamba*** **tree, he smiled and, as a wicked sport, suddenly placed the petal of his creeper arm upon me while I protested, saying “No, no, no.”**

**Enflamed by desire from the touch of the lotus hand of Kṛṣṇa, with complexion of the petals of slightly blossoming blue lotuses, immediately I develop a host of contrary disturbances. O friend! I do not know where I am, who I am, or what I have done.**

**(Out of control, she speaks to herself.) O wicked heart! O ape! You have attraction for three persons—Kṛṣṇa, the flute player and the dark youth-- but you are not ashamed. Now, by destroying myself, I will also make you, a wicked person, miserable.**

***lalitā:*** *hanta hanta! hata-mammaha-sacivassa vasantassa vipphujideṇa dusidā ede parisarā dīsanti \| tā kiṁ ettha saraṇam?*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! With the appearance of spring, the minister of wretched Cupid, the whole area has become contaminated. How can one take shelter here?**

***rādhikā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*vikrīḍantu paṭīra-parvata-taṭī-saṁsargiṇo mārutāḥ*  
*khelantaḥ kalayantu komalataraṁ puṁs-kokilāḥ kākalīm \|*  
*saṁrambheṇa śilīmukhā dhvani-bhṛto vidhyantu man-mānasaṁ*  
*hāsyantyāḥ sakhi me vyathāṁ param amī kurvanti sāhāyakam \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Let the winds which have touched slopes of Malaya Mountain play. Let the frolicking cuckoos warble softly. Let the humming bees pierce my mind with intensity. They will assist me when I attempt to give up my pain.**

***ubhe*** *(sāsram): halā, edāhiṁ ghora-cintāhiṁ kīsa kilimmasi \| ahmehiṁ takkidaṁ adimetta-dullaho ṇa kkhu de hiaa-ṭṭhido attho \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Weeping) O friend! Why do you exhaust yourself with such terrible thoughts? We have guessed that the object in your heart is not so hard to attain.**

***rādhikā*** *(niḥśvasya saṁskṛtena):*  
*iyaṁ sakhi suduḥsādhyā rādhā hṛdaya-vedanā \|*  
*kṛtā yatra cikitsāpi kutsāyāṁ paryavasyati \|\|8\|\|*  
*tā viṇṇavemi imassiṁ osare jadhā sudiḍhaṁ ekkaṁ ladā-pāsaṁ lahemi tadhā siṇehassa ṇikkidiṁ karedha \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Sighing) O friend, the pain in Rādhā’s heart is most difficult to cure. Even if diagnosed by a doctor, it would end up in my condemnation. On this occasion I reveal that if you make a strong noose from a creeper you can produce a cure since that will result in removing all affection.**

***ubhe*** *(****sa-vyatham):*** *halā, ebbaṁ dāruṇaṁ bhaṇantī mā kkhu sahīṇaṁ jīvidaṁ lumpehi \| ṇaṁ paccāsaṇṇā de ahīṭṭha-siddhī \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In pain) O friend, do not take our lives by saying such terrible things. You will soon attain what you desire.**

***rādhikā:*** *sahio, ṇa jāṇīdha imāe hada-rāhīe hiaa-duṭṭhattaṇam, jaṁ ebbaṁ matedha \|*

**Перевод:**

**You give this advice because you do not know the wicked nature of wounded Rādhā’s heart.**

***ubhe:*** *kadhidaṁ jebba sabbaṁ pia-sahie \|*

**Перевод:**

**You, our dear friend, tell us everything.**

***rādhikā:*** *ṇahu ṇahu, guruī lajjā ṇibāredi \|*

**Перевод:**

**No, great shame prevents me.**

***sakhyau:*** *halā, appa-saāsado bi guruo ahmesu tuha siṇiho lakkhīadi \| tā bahiraṅgāe lajjāe ko ettha aṇuroho \|*

**Перевод:**

**You have more affection for us than for yourself. What obligation do you have to external shyness?**

***rādhikā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*ekasya śrutam eva lumpati matiṁ kṛṣṇeti nāmākṣaraṁ*  
*sāndronmāda-paramparām upanayaty anyasya vaṁśī-kalaḥ \|*  
*eṣa snigdha-ghana-dyutir manasi me lagnaḥ paṭe vīkṣaṇāt*  
*kaṣṭaṁ dhik puruṣa-traye ratir abhūn manye mṛtiḥ śreyasī \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On hearing only once the name Kṛṣṇa,I have lost my intelligence. On hearing another person playing a flute, I have developed intense madness. And seeing a person with dark complexion in a picture, my mindbecomes attached to him. How unfortunate! Ihave become simultaneously attached to three persons. It would be better forme to die.**

***ubhe*** *(saharṣam) halā, kadhaṁ tuhmādisīṇaṁ goula-sundarīṇaṁ goulinda-ṇandaṇaṁ ujjhia aparaṁssi aṇurāo saṁbhavadi \| tā suṇāhi \| ekko jjebba eso mahā-ṇāaro kahṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully) O friend, how can the women of Gokula like you give up Kṛṣṇa and have attraction for anyone else? Please listen. This is only one person-- clever Kṛṣṇa.**

***rādhikā*** *(socchvāsam ātma-gatam) hiaa, samāsassa samāsassa \| dāṇiṁ jādā tuha jia-loa-nivāsa-lālasā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Sighing, to herself) O my heart! You can be relieved: you have developed longing for he who is the shelter of all beings in the world.**

***ubhe*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*sā saurabhyormi-paridigdha-digantarāpi*  
*bandhyaṁ januḥ sutanu gandha-phalī bibharti \|*  
*rādhe na vibhrama-bharaḥ kriyate yad-aṅke*  
*kāmaṁ nipīta-madhunā madhusūdanena \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful, slender Rādhā! Even if it anoints the directions with waves of sweet fragrance, a c*****ampaka*** **lives in vain if a playful bumblebee does not drink its honey and perform pastimes there.**

***nāndimukhī****(parikramya): kahaṁ aggado jebba esā rāhī \| (ity upasṛtya) jaadu jaadu pia-sahi\|*

**Перевод:**

**(She enters.) Why is Rādhā here? (Approaching her) Victory to you, dear friend!**

***rādhikā*** *(sāvahittham) sahi, kusalaṁ bhavadīe \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I hope you are well.**

***nāndimukhī:*** *tuha ullāhattaṇe jāde (iti rādhāṁ nibhālya sva-gatam \|) appekkhia ccea mae paḍhamaṁ ṇiṭṭaṅkidam \| tahabi pucchissam \| (prakāśam, saṁskṛtena)*

*na mugdhe vaidagdhī-garima-paridigdhā tava matir*  
*virāmo nedānīm api vapuṣi bālyasya vayasaḥ \|*  
*kam apy antaḥ-kṣobhaṁ prathayasi tathāpi tvam athavā*  
*sakhi jñātaṁ vṛndāvana-madana-visphūrjitam idam \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You have overcome your sickness. (Looking at Rādhā she says to herself) Not looking at anything, first I should express myself in words. I will ask. (She then speaks aloud.) O foolish girl! Your mind does not have great intelligence. Childhood age does not leave your body even now. You are permeated with some inner turmoil. Or you have experienced the manifestation of the Cupid of Vṛndāvana.**

***lalitā:*** *a{i} aliāsaṅkiṇi! sīdala-dakkhiṇāṇila-hetuaṁ kampa-pulaaṁ pekkhia kīsa dūsahaṁ parivādaṁ desi \|*

**Перевод:**

**You are too suspicious! Seeing her hairs stand on end because of the cool south wind, why do you make such intolerable accusations?**

***nāndimukhī*** *(sa-smitam, saṁskṛtena):*  
*romāñcaḥ pariceṣyate katham ayaṁ nāsmābhir utkampavān*  
*duṣkīrtiṁ nahi dakṣiṇāya marute dākṣiṇya-śunye vada \|*  
*etan manmatha-koṭi-sambhrama-bharair bambhromyate subhruvaḥ*  
*svānte nāgara-cakravarti-nayana-prāntasya līlāyitam \|\|12\|\|*  
*tā saccaṁ kahehi \| kadā edāe paccakkhi-kido goulāṇando \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Why then do our hairs not stand on end? O unkind one! Do not defame the southern wind. The playful movement of the eyes of the most cunning prince with beautiful brows causes confusion because he has the intense charm of a million Cupids.**

**Please speak the truth. Did she see he who gives bliss to Gokula?**

***viśākhā:*** *evaṁ edam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

***nāndimukhī*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*dara-vicalita-bālyā vallabhā bāndhavānāṁ*  
*viharasi bhavane tvaṁ patyur āmoda-pātrī \|*  
*ahaha paśupa-rāmā-kāmino mohanatvaṁ*  
*tvam api yad amunāntar bāḍham unmāditāsi \|\|13\|\|*  
*tā ahaṁ bhaavadiṁ tuvareduṁ gamissam \| (iti niṣkrāntā):*

**Перевод:**

**You are slightly beyond childhood and dear to your friends. You are the delight of your husband. You play in your house. Ah! The cause of your bewilderment is he who desires the cowherd women. By his bewilderment you have become internally agitated. Therefore I will immediately go and see Paurṇamāsī. (Exits)**

***rādhikā*** *(vimṛśya, saṁskṛtena)*  
*sā kalyāṇī kula-yuvatibhiḥ śīlitā dharma-śailī*  
*drāg asmābhiḥ katham avinayotphullam ullaṅghanīyā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Reflecting) Why do we pious girls disobey without modesty the auspicious rules of religion practiced by well-bred young women?**

*(ity ardhokte \| punaḥ sotkaṇṭham \|)*  
*hā dṛg-bhaṅgī-parimala-kalā-karmaṭho’yaṁ kathaṁ vā*  
*hātuṁ śakyaḥ paśupa-nagarī-nāgarī-nāgarendraḥ \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(After uttering half the verse she continues with longing.) How is it possible to renounce the king of cunning, who is lusty for the cowherd women, who is skilful in the arts and laden with attractive glances?**

*(tataḥ praviśati nāndīmukhī-mukharābhyām anugamyamānā paurṇamāsī \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Paurṇamāsī followed by Nāndīmukhī and Mukharā enters.)**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *mukhare, kim aduḥsādha-bādhā tarkitā tvayā rādhā?*

**Перевод:**

**O Mukharā, do you think Rādhā has an ailment which can be cured?**

***mukharā:*** *bhaavadi, suṇāhi \|*  
*agre vīkṣya śikhaṇḍa-khaṇḍam acirād utkampam ālambate*  
*guñjānāṁ ca vilokanān muhur asau sāsraṁ parikrośati \|*  
*no jāne janayann apūrva-naṭana-krīḍā-camatkāritāṁ*  
*bālāyāḥ kila citta-bhūmim aviśat ko'yaṁ navīna-grahaḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī, listen! Upon seeing peacock feathers in front of her, this girl suddenly begins trembling. When she sees** ***gūnjā*** **berries, she constantly cries with tears in her eyes. I do not know if some young demon had possessed her heart, causing astonishment by unprecedented dancing.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sva-gatam):* *so’yam uddaṇḍasya navānurāga-rāśeḥ ko’pi caṇḍimā \| (prakāśam) mukhare, sādhu vijñātam \| yad atra dānava-kulāvataṁsāḥ kaṁsādayo rādhām anviṣyanti \| tena ko’py ayam aṅganāgraho bālām āviveśa \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) This is the indescribable passion of extraordinary, abundant new love. (Aloud) O Mukharā, I understand. Kaṁsa and others, the leaders of the demons, are searching for Rādhā. Because of them, some female ghost has entered the girl.**

***mukharā:*** *bhaavadi, ko ettha paḍiāro?*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī, what is the cure?**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *ayi dānavārer dṛṣṭir eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! She should see the enemy of the demons.**

***mukharā:*** *bhaavadi, kuḍilā kkhu jaḍilā edaṁ ṇāhiṇaṁdissadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Paurṇamāsī! This will not please crooked Jaṭilā.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *mukhare! sā khalu mad-girā sandiśyatām—jaṭile! mā śaṅkiṣṭhāḥ \| kṛṣṇam ātma-vidyayaiva saṅghaṭayiṣyāmīti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Mukharā! She should hear my words. “Jaṭilā! Do not worry. I will make Kṛṣṇa appear by my mystic powers.”**

*(mukharā namaskṛtya niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Offering respects to Mukharā she exits.)**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(upasṛtya): vatse, nijābhīṣṭa-lābhena kṛtārthībhuyāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Approaching) O child! You will be successful with the attainment of your lover.**

*(rādhā sāvahitthaṁ praṇamati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā offers respect while hiding her feelings.)**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sva-gatam):*  
*bhajantyāḥ sa-vrīḍaṁ katham api tadāḍambara-ghaṭām*  
*apahnotuṁ yatnād api navama-dāmoda-madhurā \|*  
*adhīrā kālindī-pulina-kalabhendrasya vijayaṁ*  
*sarojākṣyāḥ sākṣād vadati hṛdi kuñje tanuvanī \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) While Rādhā attempts to hide the trumpeting of the elephant which wanders on the bank of the Yamunā in the forest of her body, which is sweet with fragrance of new musk and fickle because of shyness, that body announces victory to the elephant in the bower of her heart.**

*(punar nirūpya janāntikam\|): hanta nāndīmukhi! nirbhara-gabhīra-premormi-nirmita-manaḥ-kṣobhā kim apy eṣā viceṣṭate \| tad iyam avadhāryatām anurāga-virasya kāpi durvibodha-gabhīra-vikrama-vaicitrī \| tathā hi—*  
*pratyāhṛtya muniḥ kṣaṇaṁ viṣayato yasmin mano dhitsate*  
*bālāsau viṣayeṣu dhitsati tataḥ pratyāharantī manaḥ \|*  
*yasya sphūrti-lavāya hanta hṛdaye yogī sumutkaṇṭhate*  
*mugdheyaṁ kila tasya paśya hṛdayān niṣkrāntim ākāṅkṣati \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Considering again she whispers) O Nāndīmukhī! Her mind is agitated by waves of intense, deep love. One should understand that this is the incomprehensible, deep, wondrous course of the hero of love. The yogī withdraws his consciousness from the objects of the senses and desires to fix it on Kṛṣṇa in the heart. This young girl, however, withdraws Kṛṣṇa from her heart and desires to fix her heart on the objects of the senses.The great yogī yearns to see him for abrief moment in the heart,and this charming girl wishes that he may leave her heart.**

***nāndīmukhī:*** *bhaavadi, irisassa bhāassa viṇṇāne muḍhahmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī, I am confused in understanding such emotions.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatse satyam āttha \| durgamo’yaṁ gāḍhānurāga-vivartaḥ \| śrūyatām \|*  
*pīḍābhir nava-kāla-kūṭa-kaṭutā-garvasya nirvāsano*  
*niḥsyandena mudāṁ sudhā-mādhurimāhaṅkāra-saṅkocanaḥ \|*  
*premā sundari nanda-nandana-paro jāgarti yasyāntare*  
*jñāyante sphuṭam asya vakra-madhurās tenaiva vikrāntayaḥ \|\|18\|\|*  
*tad ehi, bhāvam asyāḥ parīkṣevahi \| (ity upasṛtya) vatse, kim api praṣṭavyāsi \|*  
*patiḥ premodāttaḥ sucarita-kathā gokula-pure*  
*prasiddhā te śuddhe janir api ca lakṣmīvati kule \|*  
*apūrvā kurvāṇā matim iha mahā-sāhasamayiṁ*  
*suhṛdbhyas tvaṁ lajjām api kim iva rādhe na bhajasi \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O child, what you said is true. It is hard to understand the changes that deep** ***anurāga*** **produces. Please listen.**

**O beautiful friend! If the devotee of Kṛṣṇa develops** ***prema*** **in his heart, then poisonous pride will be clearly destroyed by suffering and sweet nectar will appear by an inundation of various joys which restrict pride. In this way, sweetness, taking a crooked path, will be pervasive.**

**Come, let us test her love. (Approaching) O child, we want to ask something.**

**Your loving husband, your good conduct and your birth in a wealthy, pure family are famous in Gokula. O Rādhā! Are you not ashamed for acting so inconsiderate to friends as never before?**

*(rādhikā kātaryam abhinīya sa-lajjaṁ lalitā-karṇa-mūle lagati \|)*  
*(Feeling pain, Rādhā whispers in Lalitā’s ear in shame.)*  
***lalitā:*** *ajje, viṇṇavedi rāhī \| (iti saṁskṛtena \|)*  
*doṣodgāraṁ tvam api kuruṣe hā mayi vyākulāyāṁ*  
*pādebhyas te bhagavati śape nāparādhyāmi sādhvi \|*  
*parṇaiḥ karṇotpala-valayibhis tāḍyamāno’pi dhūrto*  
*na śyāmātmā mama tanu-pariṣvaṅga-raṅgaṁ jahāti \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī, Rādhā īs explaining. “You blame me, though I am tormented. I have committed no mistake. I swear this at your feet, O chaste lady! Though I struck the rascal with leaves, bracelets and ear decorations, that dark person did not stop embracing my body.”**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(serṣyam ivālokya): mugdhe! kim anyāṁ prauḍha-mudrāṁ noddaṇḍayasi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at her as if with anger) Foolish girl! Why do you not use other bold gestures?**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-roṣam saṁskṛtena):*  
*krośantyāṁ kara-pallavena balavān sadyaḥ pidhatte mukhaṁ*  
*dhāvantyāṁ bhaya-bhāji vistṛta-bhujo rundhe puraḥ paddhatim \|*  
*pādānte viluṭhaty asau mayi muhur daṣṭādharāyāṁ ruṣā*  
*mātaś caṇḍi mayā śikhaṇḍa-mukuṭād ātmābhirakṣyaḥ katham \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Angrily) O angry mother! When I shout to call someone, strong Kṛṣṇa immediately covers my mouth with his soft hand. When I try to flee in fear, he stretches his arms and blocks the path. When I bite my lower lip in anger, he falls down at my feet repeatedly. Please tell me how I can protect myself from Kṛṣṇa.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sva-gatam):* *niṣkampatayā baddha-mūlo’yaṁ prema-palāśī \| (prakāśam)*  
*tvayā nīto vāmaḥ phalaka-milad-aṅgo madhu-ripuḥ*  
*sukhāśābhiḥ krīḍā-kutukini kuto netra-padavīm \|*  
*kukulāgni-jvālā-paṭala-kaṭu-kelir yad adhunā*  
*daśeyaṁ hanta tvāṁ jvalayati himāniva nalinim \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Her tree of prema is firmly rooted, being unshakable. (Aloud) O Rādhā fond of playing! Why do you look at uncooperative Kṛṣṇa, whose limbs are drawn in a picture? Your present condition, now a blazing conflagration, scorches you just as frost withers a lotus.**

***rādhā*** *(kṛṣṇam uddiśya sopālambham ātma-gatam)*  
*śiśiraya dṛśau dṛṣṭvā divyaṁ kiśoram itīkṣitaḥ*  
*parijana-girāṁ viśrambhāt tvaṁ vilāsa-phalāṅkitaḥ \|*  
*śiva śiva kathaṁ jānīmas tvām avakra-dhiyo vayaṁ*  
*niviḍa-baḍavā-vahni-jvālā-kalāpa-vikāsinam \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself, addressing Kṛṣṇa with censure) Having faith in the words of friends, who told me to look upon a youth to soothe my eyes, I looked at your picture drawn in jest. Ah! How could we simple-minded women know you would manifest intense flames like the fire in the ocean?**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sa-sneham ālokya \|) vatse, kṣaṇam ekānte niviśya puṣpeṣu lekho nirmīyatām \| yathāyaṁ kṛṣṇāya sva-sakhibhyāṁ samarpyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at her with affection) O child! Enter among these flowers alone for a moment and write a letter which your two friends can deliver it to Kṛṣṇa.**

*(rādhā sakhībhyāṁ saha niṣkraṇtā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā exists with Lalitā and Viśākhā)**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(parikramya): nāndīmukhi! kṛṣṇo’pi nātidūre bhaviṣyati, yad atra dakṣiṇato naicikī-nikurambasya hambā-ravāḍambaro’yam ambaram ākrāmati \| tad ahaṁ snāhārthaṁ vrajāmi \|(iti niṣkrānte \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking) O Nāndīmukhī! Kṛṣṇa is not far from here since the loud mooing of herds of cows, coming from the south, fills the sky. I will go to take a bath now.**

*(tataḥ praviśati)* ***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sodvegam):*  
*yad-avadhi tad akasmād eva vismāyitākṣaṁ*  
*nava-taḍid-abhirāmaṁ dhāma sākṣād babhūva \|*  
*tad-avadhi cira-cintā-cakra-saktā viraktiṁ*  
*mama matir upabhoge yoginīva prayāti \|\|24\|\|*  
*(puro’nusṛtya): hanta, raṅgaṇamālyām upanetuṁ prasthito vayasyaḥ kathaṁ vilambate?*

**Перевод:**

**(He enters and speaks with agitation.) From the time that the form pleasant as fresh lightning and astonishing to the eyes suddenly appeared, my mind continually thinks of her and become detached from material enjoyment as if I had attained brahman realization. (Going ahead) Oh! I sent my friend to fetch a garland of** ***raṅgaṇa*** **flowers. Why does he delay?**

*(praviśya mālya-hastaḥ)* ***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *kadhaṁ ajja dummaṇāedi pia-baasso \| hodu \| pasaṅgado jāṇissaṁ \| (iti parikramya kṛṣṇaṁ paśyan sva-gatam, saṁskṛtena)*  
*phulla-prasūna-paṭalais tapanīya-varṇām*
