*ālokya campaka-latāṁ kila kampate’sau \|*  
*śaṅke niraṅka-nava-kuṅkuma-paṅka-gaurī*  
*rādhāsya citta-phalake tilakī-babhūva \|\|25\|\|*  
*(ity upasṛtya) bho geṇha(iti mālyaṁ nivedayati)*

**Перевод:**

**(Madhumaṅgala enters with a garland in his hand.) Why is my dear friend depressed today? Let that be. We will know in time. (Walking, he looks at Kṛṣṇa and says to himself) Seeing the golden colored** ***campaka*** **creeper with masses of blossoms, he trembles. I think that golden Rādhā decorated with pure, fresh** ***kuṁkuma*** **has become most excellent on the slate of his heart. (Approaching Kṛṣṇa) Please take this. (He offers the garland.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(anākarṇitakenaiva)*  
*kanakādri-niketa-ketakī*  
*kālikā-kalpa-kalevara-dyutiḥ \|*  
*hṛdi sā mudirāli-medure*  
*capalā māṁ kim alaṅkariṣyati \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(As if not hearing) When will she, whose bodily effulgence is like a cluster of** ***ketakī*** **buds growing on a golden mountain, fickle as lightning, decorate my heart, thick as a mass of clouds?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *phalidaṁ me takkeṇa \| (prakāśam uccaiḥ) bho pia-baassa \| saṁmuhe vikkosandaṁ bi kīsa maṁ ṇa pecchasi?*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) My suspicion is correct. (Aloud) O my friend, why do you not see me shouting in front of you?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sāvahittham) sakhe, campaka-latāyā lāvaṇyākṛṣṭena mayā nopadṛṣṭo’si \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Concealing emotions) O friend, I did not see you since a** ***campaka*** **creeper caught my attention.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *saccaṁ ccea bhaṇāsi, kiṁ tu saṁcāriṇie campaa-ladāe \|*

**Перевод:**

**You speak the truth. But why were you absorbed in a** ***campaka*** **creeper which moves about?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, kāmām asambhāvyaś campaka-latāyāḥ sañcāraḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, it is impossible that the** ***campaka*** **can move.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *baassa, kkhaṇaṁ viramedu baṅkattaṇam \| ujjuaṁ kahehi kahaṁ suṇṇa-hiao si tti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, give up your crooked talk for a moment. Speak the truth. Why is your heart vacant?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-smitam) sakhe, mālāṁ vinā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O friend, I do not have a garland.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bālaṁ tti bhaṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**You should say “I am without a girl.”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *mudheyaṁ te viśaṅkā \|*

**Перевод:**

**That is a foolish doubt.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(saṁskṛtena āśritya)*  
*na jānīṣe murdhnaś cyutam api śikhaṇḍam yad akhilaṁ*  
*na kaṇṭhe yan mālyaṁ kalayasi purastāt kṛtam api \|*  
*tad unnītaṁ vṛndāvana-kuhara-līlā-kalabha he*  
*sphuṭaṁ rādhā-netra-bhramara-vara-vīryonnatir iyam \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O elephant who sports in the houses of Vṛndāvana! The peacock feather has fallen from your head to the ground, but you are unaware of that. There is a prepared garland lying in front of you, but you do not put it on. From that I can infer that the power of the bees, in the form of Rādhā’s eyes, has agitated you.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *kathaṁ nikhilam eva tarkitaṁ dhūrtena? tad alaṁ pratārya \| (prakāśam) sakhe, yathārtham āttha \| tad ākarṇyatām \|*  
*mama rādhā nisarga-sthaṁ, pratīpam anayan manaḥ \|*  
*mahā-jyaiṣṭhīva sahasā, pravāhaṁ saura-saindhavam \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) How does the rascal guess everything? I should not cheat him. (Aloud) O friend, I will speak the truth. Please listen. My mind situated in its natural state has been transformed just as the full moon of Jyeṣṭha month transforms the waves of the Gaṅgā.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ṇūṇaṁ acchīṇaṁ de paccakkhī-bhūdā esā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Have you seen her personally with your eyes?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *atha kim \| subalataḥ sā paricikye ca \| (ity autsukyam abhinīya)*  
*bhramad-bhrū-vallīkaiḥ pratidiśam apāṅgasya valanaiḥ*  
*kuraṅgībhyo bhaṅgī-bharam upadiśantīm iva dṛśoḥ \|*  
*tatas tāṁ bimbauṣṭhīṁ kalayati mayi krodha-vikaṭo*  
*mano-janmā pauṣpaṁ dhanur anupamaṁ sajjam akarot \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Certainly. I have known about her from Subala. (Showing longing) With great terrifying anger, Cupid fires his incomparable flower bow at me when I gaze at her with her** ***bimba*** **lips as she teaches the deer how to make their eyes glance in every direction with moving eyebrows .**

***madhumaṅgala:*** *abi ṇāma saṁbuttaṁ aṇṇoṇṇa-daṁsaṇaṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**Have you seen each other?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *nahi nahi—*  
*tasyāḥ sakhe mukha-tuṣāra-mayūkha-bimbe*  
*dūrān mamākṣi-padavīm adhirūḍha-mātre \|*  
*nirbandhataḥ śapatha-koṭibhir ambayāhaṁ*  
*nītaḥ kṣaṇād ahaha sadmani bhojanāya \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**No, no. O friend! As soon as I see her cooling, effulgent face from afar, my mother quickly brings me home to eat with a thousand curses and great persistence.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *baassa, ciṭṭhanti bahulāo ballava-sundario \| tad abi kīsa ekāe rāhie nibbharaṁ aṇurajjasi?*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, there are many cowherd girls. Why are you so attracted to one person, Rādhā?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, rādhāyām asādhāraṇi kāpi mādhurī \| tathā hi—*  
*tasyāḥ kānti-dyutini vadane mañjule cākṣi-yugme*  
*tatrāsmākaṁ yad-avadhi sakhe dṛṣṭir eṣā niviṣṭā \|*  
*satyaṁ brūmas tad-avadhi bhaved indum indīvaraṁ ca*  
*smāraṁ smāraṁ mukha-kuṭilatā-kāriṇīyaṁ hṛṇīyā \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, Rādhā has extraordinary sweetness. O friend! As long as my eyes are absorbed in seeing the splendid beautiful of Rādhā’s face and her charming eyes, on remembering the moon and the blue lotus, my mouth curls in disgust.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *daṁsaṇado paḍhamaṁ jjebba tattha tujjha rāo mae takkido tthi \| tā kiṁ tti lāvaṇṇobāhio tti bhaṇāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I have suspected your great attraction for her from the beginning, from seeing you. Why do you say she has such great beauty?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe satyam āttha \| sva-cittābhiniveśād eva tasyāṁ ko’pi mahimonnāhaḥ pratīyate \| tathā hi—*  
*yatra prakṛtyā ratir uttamāhāṁ*  
*tatrānumeyaḥ paramo’nubhāvaḥ \|*  
*naisargikī kṛṣṇa-mṛgānuvṛttir*  
*deśasya hi jñāpayati praśastim \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I will speak the truth. By absorbing the mind in her, one will realize her supreme greatness. Where elevated persons develop attraction because of genuine qualities, one can infer the highest dignity of the object of love, just as the natural presence of a black deer indicates the glory of a place.**

***nepathye:*** *sahi sārie, diṭṭho tue ettha ballavinda-ṁandaṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(offstage) O friend, do you see Kṛṣṇa over there?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, nedīyān ayaṁ sukumāri-kaṇṭha-dhvanir udañcati \| tad atra tuṣṇim āsvahe*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, that is the voice of a young girl very near. Let us be quiet.**

*(tataḥ praviśato lalitā-viśākhā)*

**Перевод:**

**(Lalitā and Viśākhā enter)**

***lalitā:*** *pekkha eso diṭṭhiā purado kahṇo \| tā upasappahme \| (ity ubhe tathā kṛtvā \|) jaadu jaadu goulāṇando \|*

**Перевод:**

**By good luck that is Kṛṣṇa just in front of us. Let us approach him. Glory to the joy of Gokula!**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi lalite! śaṅke manohāri-kusuma-patram ādātum adya vṛndāṭavī-madhye avatīrṇāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Lalitā! I think you have come to the Vṛndāvana forest to pick attractive flower petals.**

***lalitā:*** *viṇṇādaṁ bi ṇuṇaṁ āāreṇa saṁgobesi jaṁ dāduṁ ti ṇa bhaṇāsi \| tā geṇha ṇaṁ kaṇṇiāra-koraa-pattam \| (ity anaṅga-lekhaṁ kṛṣṇa-kare’rpayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**Since you do not say “to give flowers,” by your gestures, you are hiding what you understand. Therefore take this letter made of a** ***karṇikara*** **bud. (She gives the love letter to him.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *cetaḥ! samāśvasihi samāśvasihi \| tvad-abhīṣṭa-bījasyāṅkuro’yam iti śaṅke\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) O my heart! Be calm, be calm. I think this is the sprout from the seed of your desire.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho lalide! kiṁ imiṇā akkharāṇaṁ patteṇa sakkarāṇāṁ pattaṁ samappehi\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā! What is the use of this leaf with letters on it? Give him a leaf with sugar candy on it.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, vācaya patram \| kadācid etan naḥ karṇa-rasāyanasya pātrībhavati \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, read the letter. Perhaps this will be the cup for drinking nectar in our ears.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho baassa, diṭṭhā tuhma goāla-jādie vadāṇṇadā \| ṇaṁ ahma bahmaṇa-jādiṁ jebba gauraeṇa vandāmi, jaṁ tahiṁ diahe jaṇṇia-bahmaṇihiṁ ca{u}viheṇa aṇṇena bhoidahma \| (iti lekhaṁ vācayati \|)*  
*dharia paḍicchanda-guṇaṁ*  
*sundara maha mandire tumaṁ vasasi \|*  
*taha taha rundhasi baliaṁ*  
*jaha jaha ca{i}dā palāemi \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I have seen the generosity of your cowherd birth. But I offer respects with reverence to those of us who have** ***brāhmaṇa*** **birth, for on that day we feasted because of the wives of the sacrificial priests, eating four types of food. (He reads the letter.)**

**You, having the qualities of the picture, dwell in my temple, O beautiful one! Wherever I flee in fear, there you block me, surrounding me.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, duradhigamārthā tāvad iyaṁ gāthā \| tena punar bhaṇyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, this message is hard to understand. Read it again.**

*(madhumaṅgalas tathā karoti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(He reads it again.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānandaṁ sva-gatam): kula-striyo hi dharma-bhīravo bhavanti \| tad-upekṣayā bhāva-niṣṭhāṁ niṣṭaṅkayāmi \| (iti saṁrambham abhinīya prakāśam \|) haṁho! paśyata paśyata!*  
*snigdhair ebhiḥ sakhibhir akhilair dhenu-vṛndānusārī*  
*nārī-vārtā-vimukha-hṛdayaḥ kānanānte carāmi \|*  
*māṁ svairiṇyas tad api yad imā dūṣayanti prakāmaṁ*  
*tad-vijñaptiṁ drutam iha jarad-gopa-goṣṭhyāṁ kariṣye \|\|34\|\|*  
*(iti kṛtrimāmarṣeṇa drutaṁ parikrāmati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully to himself) Well-bred girls are afraid of transgressing dharma. Because of that, I can infer her determination in love. (Showing excitement he speaks aloud.) Ah! Look, look! Following the herds of cows, I wander in the forests with affectionate friends, with my heart disinterested in topics of women. Since these unrestrained women really contaminate me, I will notify the elder cowherds about this immediately.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(smitam āvṛtya): bho bahmaāri-sihāmaṇe! kkhaṇaṁ ṇivaṭṭia imāo dummuha-goiāo paccuttareṇa ṇijjittia biḍḍābehi \| ahaṁ kkhu edaṁ sabbaṁ dhiṭṭhāṇaṁ buttantaṁ goulesarie viṇṇavissam \| (iti pāṇau dhṛtvā vyāvartayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Covering a smile) O best of** ***brahmacārīs*****! Desisting for a moment, defeat these ugly gopīs with an answer and make them flee. I will inform Yaśodā all about the impudence of this event. ( He takes Kṛṣṇa’s hands and then turns away.)**

**(Lalitā and Viśākḥā look at each other with dismay.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi viśākhe, cāturakṣikaṁ prekṣaṇam api nāsti \| kutas tāvat parito rodhanam? tad anuyāmi \| kenāpy apareṇa nāgareṇa tasyāḥ svāntam uccālitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Viśākhā! We have never seen each other face to face. Why has she been blocked? I will pursue that person. Is her heart agitated by some other man?**

***viśākhā*** *(saṁskṛtena āśritya):*  
*kas tādṛg vraja-maṇḍale’tha valate śakyo gariyān asau*  
*yenoccālayituṁ balāt kulavatī-ceto-giri-grāmaṇīḥ \|*  
*ity asmābhir avakra-vikrama-lavād utkṣipta-govardhano*  
*hetus tvaṁ kila paṅkajākṣa paṭubhis tatrāsi niṣṭaṅkitaḥ \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What great person appears in Vraja, who can agitate chaste women’s hearts, which are steadfast like the best of mountains, by force? You lifted Govardhana with a particle of incomprehensible prowess. O lotus-eyed Kṛṣṇa! We have cleverly indicated you in that letter.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *a{i} bāālie! ciṭṭha ciṭṭha! diṭṭho mae ukkhitta-daṇḍa-maṇḍalehiṁ gobehiṁ govaḍḍhaṇo dharido \| tumaṁ kīsa ekkaṁ jjebba pia-baassaṁ saṁbhāvesi?*

**Перевод:**

**O garrulous woman! Stop, stop! I saw that Govardhana was lifted by a group of cowherds using raised sticks. Why do you honor my dear friend in this way?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *lalite, alam ati-prasaṅgena \| tan nivartasva \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā! Enough of this incidental talk. Please stop.**

***lalitā:*** *suṁdara, sabba-goula-suha-kāriṇo bi tuatto kadhaṁ sā ekā jjebba dukkhaṁ arihadi barīasī?*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful Kṛṣṇa! You are the cause of happiness in all Gokula. Why does she, most excellent, deserve to suffer because of you?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*saṅgī me madhumaṅgalo na sahate dharmādhvano vicyutiṁ*  
*śridāmā parimārgayan mama nahi cchidrāṇi nidrāyati \|*  
*kaṁsaḥ śāsti khalaḥ kṣitiṁ katham ato mugdhe vidheyaṁ mayā*  
*niḥśaṅkaṁ kula-sundarī-paribhavaj-jvālā-mahā-sāhasam \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**My associate Madhumaṅgala does not tolerate deviation from the path of dharma.Śridāmā, searching for my faults, does not sleep. Evil Kaṁsa controls the earth. O foolish girl! How then could I fearlessly punish the most beautiful woman in the fire of disgrace?**

***lalitā*** *(samārṣaṁ saṁskṛtena):*  
*antaḥ-kleśa-kalaṅkitaḥ kila vayaṁ yāmo’dya yāmyaṁ purīṁ*  
*nāyaṁ vāñcana-sañcaya-praṇayinaṁ hāsaṁ tathāpy ujjhati \|*  
*asmin sampuṭite gabhīra-kapaṭair ābhira-palli-viṭe*  
*hā medhāvini rādhike tava kathaṁ premā gariyān abhūt \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Angrily) With hearts marked by suffering, today we will go the kingdom of death. He does not give up smiling affectionately while deceiving us. O intelligent Rādhā! How could you have developed such intense love for this lover of cowherd women filled with deep deception? (She weeps.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *a{i} muddhe, saala-sattha-visārao jassa ahmāriso amacco hoi sobi kiṁ edaṁ dhammaṁ adikkamissa{i}? tā alaṁ araṇṇa-rudideṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**Fool! How can he who is expert in all scriptures and for whom I am an advisor trespass dharma? Enough of crying in the desert!**

***viśākhā*** *(sva-gatam):* *ṇaṁ rāhīe guñjāaliaṁ kahṇassa deṁtī iṅgidaṁ lakkhemi \| (prakāśam, saṁskṛtena)*  
*udīrṇa-rāgeṇa karambitāntarā*  
*parisphurat-kṛṣṇa-mukhī guṇāñcitā \|*  
*guñjāvalī mañjutarāvalambatāṁ*  
*sā rādhikeyaṁ tava kaṇṭha-saṅgamam \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Giving Kṛṣṇa Rādhā’s** ***guñja*** **necklace, I will observe his response. (Aloud) This attractive** ***guñja*** **necklace, most excellent, which is strung on a thread, whose edges are black, and whose exterior is marked with red, should rest on your throat.**

*(iti kaṇṭhe svayam arpayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(She puts in on his neck.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā sa-kapaṭerṣyam):*  
*rāgiṇam api sukaṭhoraṁ*  
*suvṛttam api muhur udīrṇa-mālinyam \|*  
*yuvatīnām iva bhāvaṁ*  
*nahi guñjā-hāram icchāmi \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling, he speaks with false anger.)**

**I do not want the** ***guñja*** **necklace, since, though the beads are red, they are very hard, and though they are round, they are very impure, like the emotions of young women.**

*(ity ajānan iva kaṇṭhād avatārya raṅgaṇamālikām arpayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(He takes off the** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland and gives it to her, as if not realizing he has given the wrong thing.)**

***viśākhā*** *(sva-gatam):* *imassa bhamo bi ahmāṇaṁ maṅgalo saṁbutto \| (iti vastreṇa saṁvṛṇoti\|)*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) His mistake produces auspiciousness for us. (She covers the garland with a cloth.)**

***lalitā:*** *halā, gobiā-koḍi-bhuaṅgassa imassa akkhalidaṁ accariaṁ bahmacariaṁ diṭṭhiā paaḍi-bhūdam \| tā ahmebi gadua taṁ taṁ atthāṇānurāiṇiṁ rāhīaṁ nivaṭṭāvehma \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend! The amazing, unswerving** ***brāhmacārya*** **of this snake dedicated to a million gopīs is by chance now revealed. We must go and stop Rādhā, who has misplaced her affections.**

***viśākhā:*** *sahi, juttaṁ mantesi \| (ity ubhe parikrāmataḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**You have given good advice. (They both begin to leave.)**

***lalitā:*** *bisāhe, tumaṁ gadua imāe raṅgaṇa-māliāe pia-sahiṁ āsāsehi \| ahaṁ kkhu edaṁ buttantaṁ bhaavadīe biṇivedissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Viśākhā! Go and appease our dear friend with this** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland. I will inform Paurṇamāsī of what happened.**

*(iti niṣkrānte \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(They exit.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho baassa! ādarijjaṁtaṁ bi appāṇaṁ kīsa ādarābesi? idaṁ kkhu paccāddāba-pabbatāhirohaṇassa ahirohiṇī-ṇimmāṇaṁ dāva \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend! Why do you force Lalitā and Viśākhā to respect you when they already do? You are building a ladder which will cause you regret when you ascend it.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, satyaṁ braviṣi \| sāhasikyaṁ hasitenaivānuṣṭhitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, you speak the truth. I acted rashly for laughs.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *pekkha goi-jualaṁ netta-paham adikkamidam \|*  
*śrutvā niṣṭhuratāṁ mamendu-vadanā premāṅkuraṁ bhindatī*  
*svānte śānti-dhurāṁ vidhāya vidhure prāyaḥ parāñciṣyati \|*  
*kiṁ vā pāmara-kāma-kārmuka-paritrastā vimokṣyaty asūn*  
*hā maugdhyāt phalinī manoratha-latā mṛdvī mayonmūlitā \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look, the two have already gone beyond sight. Hearing of your cruelty, moon-faced Rādhā will crush her sprout of love. Suffering internally, making peace her objective, she will now become unfavorable. Or fearful of wicked Cupid she may give up her life. Ah! I have foolishly uprooted the tender creeper of her desire, which should have borne fruit.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *dāṇīṁ kiṁ ettha saraṇam?*

**Перевод:**

**Now what should we do?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, pratyanaṅga-lekhaṁ vinā nānyat paśyāmi śaraṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, I see no alternative but to answer the love letter.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *kiṁ ettha leha-sāhaṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**What will writing a letter accomplish?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *vaśikāra-kriyā-praśasto rāgavān javā-niryāsaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**A letter scented with hibiscus perfume, filled with affection, should bring her under control.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ehi, uḍḍra-mahāḍa{i}ṁ-maṇḍidaṁ ṇādidūre pakkaṁdaṇa-tītthaṁ gacchahma \| (iti niṣkrāntau \|)*

**Перевод:**

**Come, let us go to nearby Praskanda-tīrtha, with a huge forest of hibiscus. (They exit).**

*(tataḥ praviśati viśākhayā prabodhyamānā rādhā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā enters with Viśākhā, who explaining to her.)**

***rādhā*** *(sa-khedaṁ, saṁskṛtena):*  
*yasyotsaṅga-sukhāśrayā śithilatā gurvī gurubhyas trapā*  
*prāṇebhyo’pi suhṛttamāḥ sakhi tathā yūyaṁ parikleśitāḥ \|*  
*dharmaḥ so’pi mahān mayā na gaṇitaḥ sādhvībhir adhyāsito*  
*dhig dhairyaṁ tad-upekṣitāpi yad ahaṁ jīvāmi pāpīyasī \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(In lamentation) O friend! Desiring the happiness of his embraces, Irelax my great shyness in front of elders. Although you are my best friend, dearer than my own life, I have given you so much trouble. I even put aside my great vow, kept by the most elevated women. What useless self-control I have! Although he has now neglected me, I, most sinful, am still living. ( iti mūrcchati) (She faints.)**

***viśākhā*** *(sa-sambhramam) sahi, samāssasa samāssasa \| (iti raṅgaṇa-mālāṁ ghrāṇe’rpayati\|)*

**Перевод:**

**(In excitement) O friend, be calm, be calm. (She takes the** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland and gives it to her.)**

***rādhikā*** *(saṁjñāṁ labdhvā): halā, kiṁ edam accariaṁ jaṁ saṁmohaṇaṁ bi pabohedi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Regaining consciousness) O friend, what is this astonishing thing that awakens me from fainting?**

***viśākhā*** *(mālyaṁ nivedya, saṁskṛtena):*  
*aṅgottīrṇa-vilepanaṁ sakhi samākṛṣṭi-kriyāyāṁ maṇi-*  
*rmantro hanta muhur vaśīkṛti-vidhau nāmāsya vaṁśī-pateḥ \|*  
*nirmālya-srag iyaṁ mahauṣadhir iha svāntasya sammohane*  
*nāsāṁ kas tisṛṇāṁ gṛṇāti paramācintyāṁ prabhāvāvalīm \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Giving her the garland) O friend! The sandal paste from Kṛṣṇa's forehead is a jewel that draws people to it. The name of Kṛṣṇa, the husband of the flute, is a mantra that brings people under its control. Kṛṣṇa's flower-garland is a great herb that charms people's hearts. Who does not speak of the inconceivable power of these three things?**

***rādhikā*** *(sva-gatam):* *evaṁ-guṇeṇa imiṇā ubekkhidaṁ bi ṇaṁ hada-sariraṁ kadhaṁ ajjabi ṇillajjāhaṁ dhāremi \| tā kālia-hada-pavesobāam aṇusarissam \| (prakāśam) bisāhe, biṇṇabehi guru-aṇaṁ jaṁ bārahāicca-tītthaṁ gadua suraṁ accidukāmahmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Why do I hold on to this shameless, wounded body, since it is ignored by Kṛṣṇa who possesses great qualities? I shall end it all by entering Kāliya Lake.**

**Перевод:**

**(Aloud) O Viśākhā! Please inform the elders that I went to Dvādaśādiya-tīrtha with a desire to worship the sun.**

***viśākhā:*** *sāhu sumarāidaṁ pia-sahie jaṁ ajjāe jaḍilāe ahaṁ bi edaṁ jjebba dāṇiṁ ādiṭṭhahmi \| tā ehi \|(ity ubhe parikrāmataḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**My dear friend remember, that, since I was ordered by elderly Jaṭilā, let us both go. (They both walk.)**

***rādhikā*** *(savyāmoham):*  
*maṁ parihara{i} muuṁdo taha bi durāsā virohiṇī ḍaha{i} \|*  
*maha sahi gahīra-ṇīrā saraṇaṁ bahiṇī kidaṁtassa \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Confused) Though Kṛṣṇa has given me up, still my wicked desire, opposing everything, burns me. The deep water, the sister of death, is my friend, my shelter.**

***viśākhā:*** *halā, pekkha patthāṇe maṅgala-suāṇāiṁ sa{u}ṇāiṁ \| tā ebbaṁ mā bhaṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, look! There are auspicious signs at our departure. Therefore do not speak.**

***rādhikā*** *(puro dṛṣṭvā):* *halā, katham esā pūbba-disā-muhe ā āliī sañjhā dīsa{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking in front) O friend, why in the east we can see the evening at the wrong time?**

***viśākhā:*** *ṇa kkhu saṁjhā \| pekkha pakkandaṇe surassa ballahā pariphullidā oḍra-rāī rāadi \| tā imassa agghaṁ kāduṁ ṇaṁ avaciṇahma \| (ity ubhe kurutaḥ):*

**Перевод:**

**It is not evening. Look! At Praskanda the hibiscus flowers, dear to the sun, are glowing red. Let us pick some of the flowers to make an offering of** ***arghya*** **to the sun god.**

*(tataḥ praviśati baṭunā saha kṛṣṇaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Madhumaṅgala enters with Kṛṣṇa.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, seyaṁ rādhādhara-kānti-taskarī javā-rājiḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, the hibiscus has stolen the beauty of Rādhā’s lips.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ado ṇaṁ ṇippiḍia nimmāhi paccaṇaṅga-leham \|*

**Перевод:**

**Squeeze the juice from the red flowers and write a reply to her letter.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(parikampya sa-vismayam):*  
*eṣā nāntika-vartini sura-girer ailāvṛti hanta bhur*  
*agre kiṁ kalayāmi kāñcana-rucām udgāra-gaurīr diśaḥ \|*  
*āṁ jñātaṁ maṇi-nūpura-dhvani-bharād āli-janālaṅkṛtā*  
*kāntināṁ kula-devatā vilasituṁ vṛndāṭavīṁ vindati \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Quivering in astonishment) This is certainly not the earth near Mount Meru in Ilāvṛta-varṣa. Why do I see in front of me the directions emanating the brilliance of golden women? Yes, I understand. The goddess of beautiful women along with her friends has come to the Vṛndāvana forest to play, since I heard the loud sounds of jeweled ankle bells.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *hanta bhoḥ! maggijjaṁtahmi bāurā-sāhaṇe kuraṅgī saaṁ hatthaṁ gadā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Oh! The doe has been caught while we are attempting to make a trap.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānandam): sakhe, sādhu vijñātam \| tad atra vṛkṣāntaritau śṛṇuvaḥ kim asau prastauti \|*  
*(iti tathā sthitau \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(in bliss) O friend, you have understood well. Hiding behind these trees we will hear what she says. (They hide)**

***rādhikā*** *(viśākhām āliṅgya sāsram): halā, eso jaṇo kadhāpasaṅge saaṁ sumaridabbo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Embracing Viśākhā, she speaks with tears.)**

**You should remember this person whom we discussed.**

***viśākhā*** *(sa-baṣpam): sahi, akkhīṇa-dhīrattanādi-guṇā bhaṇijjasi \| tā kiṁ tti ebbaṁ ubbiggāsi?*

**Перевод:**

**They say you have qualities like strong sobriety. Why then are you so disturbed?**

***rādhikā:*** *sahi, ṇigguṇī-kidahmi tiṇā dhutteṇa \| (iti saṁskṛtena)*  
*yasyoras-taṭa-maṇḍalaṁ dhṛti-nadi-rodha-kriyā-paṇḍitaṁ*  
*vaktreṇduḥ kula-dharma-paṅkaja-vanī-saṅkoca-dīkṣā-vrati \|*  
*dor-yupau nitarām udañcita-cira-vriḍābhidārādhvarau*  
*hā kaṣṭaṁ nikhilaṅ-gilā sakhi dṛśor bhaṅgī-bhujaṅgi tu sā \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I have lost all qualities because of that rascal. His broad chest is expert at blocking the river of my resolution. His moon-like face has taken a vow to diminish the lotus forest of my chastity. The pillars of his arms are used in sacrifices for casting spells against my long-enduring shyness. O friend, what disaster! The crooked snake of his eyes devours everything.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, tvan-mādhuryeṇa mādhavaś ca jaḍī-kṛtya nirguṇām avasthāṁ nīto’yam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, Kṛṣṇa, who has been stunned by your sweetness, has become devoid of all qualities.**

***rādhikā*** *(ākāśe añjaliṁ baddhvā, saṁskṛtena): hanta bho bakī-hantaḥ!*  
*gṛhāntaḥ-khelantyo nija-sahaja-bālyasya balanād*  
*abhadraṁ bhadraṁ vā kim api hi na jānīmahi manāk \|*  
*vayaṁ netuṁ yuktāḥ katham aśaraṇāṁ kā api daśāṁ*  
*kathaṁ vā nyāyyā te prathayitum udāsīna-padavī \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Folding her hands to the sky) O killer of Pūtanā! Playing in the house we did not know at all good from bad, on the strength of our natural childhood innocence. Having been led to this most helpless position, is it right that you then simply ignore us?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, kaḥ khalu jijīviṣur jīvātu-bhūtāyāṁ siddhauṣadhī-latāyām udāste \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved! Who, desiring to live, would ignore the creeper having the power to revive a person?**

***rādhikā*** *(niḥśvasya) halā, esā piā me ekā-ali tue appaṇo kaṇṭhe dhāraṇijjā \| (iti kaṇṭhād ekāvalim uttārayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Sighing) O friend, please put my favorite necklace around your neck. (She takes off her necklace.)**

***viśākhā*** *(haṭhān nivārya):* *halā, ebbaṁ aṇuciṭṭhatī kiṁ tti maṁ ḍahasi? jaṁ lalidaṁ paḍikkhia nirujjamahmi \|(iti roditi)*

**Перевод:**

**(Preventing her) Acting in this way, why do you torture me, since I have lost all power to act while waiting for Lalitā? (She weeps.)**

***rādhikā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*akāruṇyaḥ kṛṣṇo yadi mayi tavāgaḥ katham idaṁ*  
*mudhā mā rodīr me kuru param imām uttara-kṛtim \|*  
*tamālasya skandhe vinihita-bhuja-vallarir iyaṁ*  
*yathā vṛndāraṇye ciram avicalā tiṣṭhati tanuḥ \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though Kṛṣṇa isunkind,what offense have you done to me? Do not weep uselessly. Please perform the following funeral rites: please make arrangements so that my body will remain forever in Vṛndāvana undisturbed, with its creeper-like armsembracing a** ***tamāla*** **tree.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sāsram): sakhe, dṛṣṭānurāgasya sādhiṣṭhatā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I have now seen the fitness of her love.**

***rādhikā*** *(sva-gatam):* *tuvarāvedi maṁ kāvi ghaṇukkaṇṭhā \| (prakāśam) halā \| suram accia kiṁ pi abbhatthidu-kāmāhmi \| tā jāva siṇāṇaṁ kadua ṇibuttā bhave, tumaṁ ettha pupphaṁ avaciṇehi \| (iti tirthābhimukhaṁ dvitrāṇi padāni gatvā punar ātma-gatam \|): hanta, so tilloka-mohaṇo muha-cando puṇo mae na diṭṭho \| (iti sotkaṇṭhaṁ nivṛtya prakāśam \|) halā, pasīda pasīda \| daṁsehi taṁ paḍicchandaam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Intense longing makes me hurry. (Aloud) O friend, after worshipping the sun I have a desire. After I have completed my bath and disappeared, place a flower here. (Facing the** ***tīrtha*** **she takes a few steps and again speaks to herself.) Ah! I will no longer see the moon-face which bewilders the three worlds. (Aloud, with longing) O friend, be merciful to me. Be merciful. Show me that picture of him.**

***viśākhā:*** *sahi, ṇatthi attha citta-phalaam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, the picture is not here.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-vyatham):* *tado paṇināṇeṇa ṇaṁ paccakhīkarissam \| (iti dhyānaṁ naṭayati)*

**Перевод:**

**(In pain) I will make him appear by meditation. (She begins to meditate.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, pītam apīta-pūrvam unmādakaṁ śrotra-mādhvikam \| tad agrato gacchāvaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I have drunk honey by my ears, the most intoxicating subtance never before tasted. Let us go ahead.**

*(iti ubhau tathā kurutaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(They go forward.)**

***viśākhā*** *(vilokya sānandaṁ sa-sambhramam): diṭṭhiā tujjha suha-jjhāṇeṇa phalidam \| tā jhatti ugghāḍehi loaṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing Kṛṣṇa, she speaks in joy and confusion.) By good fortune, your auspicious mediation has given good results. Open your eyes at once.**

*(rādhikā dṛśaṁ daronmīlya camatkāraṁ nāṭayati\|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā slightly opens her eyes and becomes astonished.)**

***viśākhā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*yad-arthaṁ saṅkīrṇe patasi hata-kandarpa-kadane*  
*mṛduṁ vā durvāre jvalayasi tanuṁ prema-dahane \|*  
*akhaṇḍenāpiḍaṁ sakhi nava-śikhaṇḍena kalayan*  
*vilāsī so’yaṁ te sphurati purato jīvita-patiḥ \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The lord of your life, your lover, wearing a head piece with unbroken peacock feathers, for whom you have been killed by thwarted love and burn your tender body in the unrestrained fire of love, is standing before you.**

***rādhikā:*** *ammahe siviṇassa māhuri \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! What a sweet dream!**

***viśākhā:*** *avisaddhe, eso de apubbo siviṇo jo ṇiddāe viṇā bi ṇippaṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**Don’t be so faithless! This is an unprecedented dream, produced without sleep!**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*asau dṛg-bhaṅgībhiḥ kusuma-śaram āngi-kṛta-śaraṁ*  
*sṛjantī dantīndra-kramaṇa-kamaniyālasa-gatiḥ*  
*adūre rambhorur iha vadana-bimbasya suṣamā*  
*samārambhād ambhoruha-mādhurīmāṇaṁ damayati \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Creating the most excellent flower arrows by her glances, with slow gait more attractive that the walking of a elephant, with thighs like banana stems, with lips more beautiful than the** ***bimba*** **fruit, close up, this woman completely defeats the sweetness of the lotus.**

***rādhikā*** *(kṛṣṇe dṛg-antaṁ nartayanti sva-gatam): sāhu re hiaa, sādhu diṭṭhi-ā muhuttaṁ vilambidam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Kṛṣṇa she says to herself) O my heart! It is fine. I have seen nicely. The time is late.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): dhūrte viśākhe! samantān mṛgyamāṇā diṣṭyā tvam atra dṛṣṭāsi \| yad adya bhavatyā rūpa-sādṛśyād apākima-guñjā-hāreṇa māṁ pratārya durlabhā me raṅgaṇa-mālikāpanītā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O treacherous Viśākhā! By good fortune I see you after searching everywhere. Some girl like you, cheating me with the** ***guñja*** **necklace, took my** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho ṇaṁ rāhīe kaṇṭhādo dīsatīṁ appaṇo raṅgaṇamāliaṁ saaṁ jebba āaḍḍia geṇha \|*

**Перевод:**

**Grab your** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland that I see around Rādhā’s neck.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, jānatāpi bhavatā kim idam anyāyyam upanyastam \| na khalu svapne’pi mayā kāminī-sparśaḥ smaryate \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, did you know that I mistakenly gave her the garland? I cannot remember even in a dream touching a woman.**

***rādhikā*** *(sva-gatam):* *imassa parihāso bi eso saṁkidāe mama sacco paḍibhādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Although he is joking, I suspect his words have some truth.**

***viśākhā*** *(vihasya): ayi varāṅgaṇā-taraṅgiṇināṁ mahā-sāara, ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \| dāṇiṁ bi imāiṁ dīsaṁtti tujjha aṅgesu tāṇaṁ ciṇhāiṁ \| (iti saṁskṛtena)*

*ākṛṣṭāni kaṭākṣa-bhaṅgībhir alaṁ gopāṅganānāṁ tvayā*  
*raktāny atra manāṁsi yāni nimiṣonmuktāni netrāṇy api \|*  
*tāny etāni bhavān navāñjana-tano guñjāvalināṁ chalāt*  
*piñchānāṁ ca sadā prasādhana-dhiyā sandhārayan nandasi \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) Stop, great ocean with many waves of women! I can see their marks on your limbs. O newly anointed youth! Attracting the impassioned minds and unblinking eyes of the gopīs with your side glances, by intentionally wearing** ***guñja*** **necklaces and peacock feathers, you take great delight.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-harṣam ātma-gatam):*  
*pramada-rasa-taraṅga-smera-gaṇḍa-sthalāyāḥ*  
*smara-dhanur anubandhi-bhrū-latā-lāsya-bhājaḥ \|*  
*mada-kala-cala-bhṛṅgī-bhrānti-bhaṅgīṁ dadhāno*  
*hṛdayam idam adāṅkṣīt pakṣmalākṣyāḥ kaṭākṣaḥ \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully to himself) With cheeks smiling with waves of liquid joy, with dancing brow like Cupid’s bow, Rādhā’s glance endowed with eyelashes, darting about because of intoxicated bees hovering around me, has bitten my heart.**

*(****nepathye****): ṇattiṇi bisāhe \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) O grand daughter Viśākhā!**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *katham akhāṇḍe jarā-pāṇḍureyaṁ jaṭilā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Why has Jaṭilā pale with old age come unexpectedly?**

*(praviśya)* ***jaṭilā*** *(puro dṛṣṭvā sva-gatam): kahaṁ ettha kahṇo? (prakāśam) bisāhe, kiṁti imāni dhua-gandha-ratta-candanāiṁ tue visumaridāiṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, she looks ahead and speaks to herself.) Why is Kṛṣṇa here? (Aloud) Viśākhā, have you forgotten the incense, perfumes and red sandalwood?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):*  
*candrikāṁ candra-lekhāyāś cakore pātum udyate \|*  
*pidhānaṁ vidadhe hanta śarad-ambhodarāvali \|\|52\|\|*  
*(prakāśam) mātur mātulāṇi, praṇamāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) When the** ***cakora*** **bird is about to drink the moonlight of the crescent moon, autumn clouds have caused obstruction. (Aloud) O maternal aunt, I offer you respects.**

***jaṭilā:*** *mohaṇa, ballaa-kiśori-ule avaṅka-diṭṭhi hohi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O enchanter, gaze at the gopīs with upright attitude.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(vihasya): bho dadhici-haḍḍa-kakkase, eso sabbado udāra-diṭṭhi ccea majjha pia-vaasso \| tumaṁ kkhu ke-aracchi \| tāvappāṇam āsaṁsehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(laughing) O lady, hard as Dadhīci’s bones, hard as thunderbolts. My dear friend has a generous glance whereas you have crooked eyes. You should give benedictions to yourself.**

***jaṭilā:*** *bho kiśori-bhuaṅga, kīsa tumaṁ āadosi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O snake for young girls! Why have you come?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *ārye, lokottarānurāga-camatkāriṇiyaṁ sujavā-lakṣmiḥ kaṁ vā nākarṣati?*

**Перевод:**

**O respectable woman! Who would not be attracted to the wealth of beautiful hibiscus flowers with most astonishing color?**

***jaṭilā*** *(sva-gatam):* *ṇuṇaṁ bhaavadīe vijjā-pahāva-saṁbhāvidā imassa ettha ubasatti \| (prakāśam) mohaṇa, jhatti ido gacchehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) All-knowing Paurṇamāsī has probably told him to come here. Boy, leave here immediately.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *ayi jalpāki vṛddhe! kim ity ākulāsi? svacchandato gaccheyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O garrulous old woman! Why do you complain? I am going at my leisure.**

***jaṭilā*** *(kuṭilaṁ vilokya, saṁskṛtena)*  
*nirdhautānāṁ nikhila-dharaṇī-mādhurīṇāṁ dhurīṇā*  
*kalyāṇī me nivasati vadhūḥ paśya pārśve navoḍhā \|*  
*antar goṣṭhe caṭula naṭayann atra netra-tri-bhāgaṁ*  
*niḥśaṅkas tvaṁ bhramasi bhavitā nākulatvaṁ kuto me \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looks askance) Look! This newly married woman, chief of pure sweetness for the whole world, resides by my side. You wander about fearlessly in the fields, making side glances. Why should I not be in anxiety?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *mṛṣāśaṅkini vṛddhe! mā pralāpaṁ kṛthāḥ \| yāvad etāṁ te vadhūm ākarṇayaṁ tāvan mānyāṁ bhāvayāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O useless, worrying old woman! Do not talk nonsense. The more I hear about this woman the more I respect her.**

***jaṭilā:*** *bisāhe, kiṁti ettia vilambidāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākḥā, why are you so late?**

***viśākhā*** *(smitvā): ajje, ṇaṁ dullalidaṁ kuraṅgaṁ pekkhantī vihmidahmi \|(iti sa-dṛṣṭi-kṣepam)*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Seeing a wayward deer in front, I have become astonished. (She glances at Kṛṣṇa.)**

*akaruṇa mukkia caṅgaṁ kuraṅga pemmeṇa saṅgadaṁ hariṇim \|*  
*vihalaṁ kuddaṇa-caḍulo tumaṁ baṇādo baṇaṁ bhamasi \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O merciless one! Giving up a beautiful doe with whom you associated in love, you wander from forest to forest uselessly, playing unsteadily.**

***jaṭilā:*** *a{i} atthāṇa-duraggahe, muñca kuraṅga-koduhalam \|*

**Перевод:**

**You are obstinate at the wrong time! Give up fascination with a deer.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *pia-baassa, pekkha \| eso satiṇṇo bi kīra-juāṇo ṇaṁ mahurāṁ dāḍimīṁ ṇa paḍipajja{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**Look dear friends! The young parrot, though thirsty, does not accept the sweet pomegranate.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā):*  
*hṛdi tāḍito’pi dāḍimi sumanorāgeṇa te ruciṁ vahatā \|*  
*paktrima-rasāsi kiṁ vā neti śukaḥ śaṅkayodāste \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O pomegranate! Though his heart is struck by the redness of the flower, endowed with beauty, the parrot remains unattached, wondering whether it is ripe or not.**

*(viśākhā sa-dṛg-bhaṅgaṁ rādhikām avalokate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Viśākhā glances at Rādhā.)**

***rādhikā*** *(sva-gatam): hiaa, samassassa samassassa \| (iti sa-khedam apavārya, saṁskṛtena)*

**Перевод:**

**O heart, remain calm, remain calm. (Concealing her pain, she speaks.)**

*pītaṁ navāg amṛtam adya harer aśaṅkaṁ*  
*nyastaṁ mayādya vadane na dṛg-añcalaṁ ca \|*  
*ramye cirād avasare sakhi labdha-mātre*  
*hā durvidhir virurudhe jaratī cchalena \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I did not get the chance to drink fearlessly the nectar of Kṛṣṇa's words, or to gaze at hisface from the corner of my eyes. After a long time I finallyobtained this delightful opportunity, and now unkind fate, in the form of old Jaṭilā has obstructed this.**

***jaṭilā*** *(sva-gatam):* *ahmahe kahṇa-diṭṭhiṇo māhambhaṁ \| jaṁ vahue so uvasaggo taha ṇatthi\| (prakāśam) bisāhe, pekkha—adikkamadi majjhaṇho \| tā turidaṁ sura-maṇḍavaṁ pavissahma\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) See the power of Kṛṣṇa’s glance by which a woman feels no misfortune. Viśākhā, look! Noon has passed. We should quickly enter the sun temple. (The three exit.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, kaumudīyaṁ paurṇamāsīm anuvartate \| tad ehi tām eva pratipadyevahi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, this girl, like moonlight, is obedient to Paurṇamāsī (full moon). Come here. Let us approach her.**

**Перевод:**

**(The two exit.)**

*(iti niṣkrāntau \|)*  
*tṛtīyo’ṅkaḥ*  
*rādhā-saṅgaḥ*

## Act Three: Meeting Rādhā

*(tataḥ praviśati lalitayānugamyamānā paurṇamāsi \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Paurṇamāsī enters, followed by Lalitā.)**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatse, nūnaṁ mattas trapamāṇo nābhinandati nanda-kumāras te sakhi-saṅgamam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child! Kṛṣṇa, ashamed in front of me, does not rejoice in meeting your friend.**

***lalitā:*** *bhaavadi, dubbohaṁ kkhu loottarāṇāṁ cittaṁ ṇa jhatti biasadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Great soul, he does not readily reveail his extraordinary, unfathomable heart.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(puro’valokya): vatse, paśya kadamba-vāṭikāyāṁ madhumaṅgalena sārdhaṁ sa-maṅgalaṁ vardhate madhu-mardanaḥ \| (punar nirūpya)*  
*parāṁṛṣṭāṅguṣṭha-trayam asita-ratnair ubhayato*  
*vahantī saṅkīrṇau maṇibhir arunais tat-parisarau \|*  
*tayor madhye hīrojjvala-vimala-jambūnada-mayī*  
*kare kalyāṇīyaṁ viharati hareḥ keli-muralī \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking ahead) Child, look! Kṛṣṇa along with Madhumaṅgala increases auspiciousness in the** ***kadamba*** **grove. (Looking again) Kṛṣṇa’s pastime flute, studded with sapphires for five centimeters on both ends and crusted with rubies at its tips, with gold and glitterering diamonds in the middle sections, frolicks in his hand.**

*(tataḥ praviśati yathā-nirdiṣṭaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ):*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa, as just described, enters.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānutāpam):*  
*trapayā nitarāṁ parāṅ-mukhī*  
*sahasā smera-mukhī dhṛtāñcalā \|*  
*gamitādya haṭhena rādhikā*  
*na kathaṁ hanta mayā bhujāntaram \|\|2\|\|*

*(niḥśvasya) sakhe madhumaṅgala, khañjarīṭa-dṛśaḥ sā vilāsa-mañjarī corayati me citta-cañcarīkam\| (ity autsukyaṁ nāṭayan)*  
*chinnaḥ priyo maṇi-saraḥ sakhi mauktikāni*  
*vṛttāny ahaṁ vicinuyām iti kaitavena \|*  
*mugdhaṁ vivṛtya mayi hanta dṛg-anta-bhaṅgīṁ*  
*rādhā guror api puraḥ praṇayād vyatānīt \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When suddenly she turned away out of shyness, grasping the edge of her smiling friend’s garment, would I not have Rādhā forcibly in my arms today? (Sighing) O my friend Madhumaṅgala! The bud of pleasure, Rādhā, whose eyes are like** ***khañjana*** **birds, steals my bee-like heart. (He expresses regret.) She said, “O friend, my necklace has broken. I must look for the round pearls.” Deceiving others in this way, Rādhā in front of her elders glanced from the edge of her eye at me out of affection, expressing her joy.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(dūrata eva kṛṣṇaṁ nirvarṇya, sāśankam):*  
*akṣṇor dvandvaṁ prasarati darodghūrṇa-tāraṁ murāreḥ*  
*śvāsāḥ kḷptāṁ kila vicakilair mālikāṁ mlāpayanti \|*  
*keyaṁ dhanyā vasati ramaṇī gokule kṣipram etāṁ*  
*nītas tivrām ayam api yayā kām api dhyāna-niṣṭhām \|\|4\|\|*  
*athavā kṛtaṁ sandehena \| vatsā rādhikaiva khalv atra kāraṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing Kṛṣṇa from a distance, she speaks with anxiety.) The pupilsof Kṛṣṇa’s eyes move restlessly, and his repeated sighs wilted his jasmine garland. What fortunate, beautiful girl in Gokula has put him into such difficult, indescribable meditation? Let us have no doubts! The cause is my child Rādhikā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(paurṇamāsīṁ paśyann upasṛtya): bhagavati, praṇamāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing her, he approaches.) O great woman, I offer respects to you.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *nāgara, gopi-stana-taṭiṣv alampaṭī-bhava \|*

**Перевод:**

**O clever boy, please do not have lust for the breasts of the gopīs.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(kiñcid vihasya): kṛtaṁ piṣṭa-peṣiṇibhir āśirbhir yad aham eva gopīti prasiddhāṁ śyāmāṁ vallīm api na pāṇi-pallavena spṛśāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling slightly) Stop giving blessings already accomplished, since I have never touched the beautiful dark creeper known as “gopī” with my tender hand.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(vihasya): bhoḥ, kiṁ ahmāṇaṁ sāmāe, guari jjebba maggijja{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) Ah, what use to we have of a black vine? We are looking for a golden creeper.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sa-narma-smitam):*  
*gopeśvarasya tanayo’si nayopapannaḥ*  
*khyātas tathā vraja-kule bhujayor balena \|*  
*līlā-śatais tad api kiṁ kula-yoṣitas tvam*  
*unmādam udvahasi mādhava rādhikāyāḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(With a joking smile) You are the righteous son of the lord of the cowherds and are famous in Vraja for the strength of your arms and for hundreds of pastimes. Why are you mad after Rādhā, a high-born woman?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *a{i} bibarīda-vādiṇi buḍḍhie, ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \|*  
*tujjha rāhīāe jjebba eso ahma pia-baasso ummādio \|*  
*jaṁ sehara-siṅga-vettā{i} dāṇiṁ kahiṁ vibhaṭṭāiṁ ti ṇa jāṇādi \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Old woman you speak wrongly. Stop, stop! My dear friend has been so bewildered by your Rādhikā that he does not know where he dropped his crown, horn, and stick.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-lajjam): ārye, vācāṭo’yaṁ baṭur mṛṣā jalpati \| kintu niścitaṁ te vyāharāmi \| na tāsu mac-citta-rāgas tvad-gopīṣu \| tad atra tattvataḥ pṛcchyatāṁ ayam\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Ashamed) Respected lady, this talkative** ***brāhmaṇa*** **boy speaks falsely. I will speak the truth. My heart is not attached to your gopīs. You should ask him for the truth.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ajje! saccaṁ saccaṁ \| ahma-pia-baassa-hiaassa ajjavi rāo tuhma-goiāṇaṁ aṅgesu ṇa mae diṭṭhotthi \| patthuda tāṇaṁ aṅgarāo jjebba imassa hiaye disa{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected lady, it is true. It is true. Till now I have not seen attachment for the gopīs in the heart of my dear friend. Rather I have seen their cosmetics on his heart.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-praṇaya-roṣam): dhiṇ mūrkha! viśrambhād ādṛto’pi jihmatāṁ na jahāsi\|*

**Перевод:**

**(With affectionate anger) What a fool! Though I faithfully respect you, you do not stop lying.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *satyam āha baṭuḥ \| tathā hi—*  
*kāmaṁ sad-guṇa-maṇḍalāśrayatayā tanvan mahiṣṭhāṁ ruciṁ*  
*vaicitrī-bhara-bhāk sadā śubha-daśā-śreṇi-śriyām āspadam \|*  
*vaṁśī-huṅkṛti-līlayā śithilatām eṇi-dṛśāṁ nīyate*  
*vāsaḥ kaṁsa-nisūdanādya bhavatā deheṣu geheṣv api \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***brāhmaṇa*** **boy speaks the truth. O killer of Kaṁsa! By the playful sound of your flute you loosen the clothes which spread the greatest beauty by taking shelter of the beautiful gopīs fully, and loosen their household situations which broadcast attractiveness with abundant wealth and grains. Those clothes are filled with variety of enjoyment by various colors like white, blue and red, as well as garlands and sandalwood and the household situations have variety created by the various types of architecture and rooms. The clothes are the shelter of beauty because of having materials of different types. The conditions are the shelter of wealth created in various auspicious planetary periods.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ajje kiṁ bi jāṇāsi jaṁ vaṁśī-huṁkidi-līlāe tti bhaṇāsi \| diṭṭhaṁ tahiṁ diahe kaṇṇaānaṁ tīra-ṭṭidāiṁ ambarāiṁ appaṇo hattheṇa ukkhivia imiṇā kkhandhe ṇikkhittāiṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respectable lady! What do you really know, when you speak about the playful sound of his flute? Today I saw him grab the girls’ clothes on the river bank, and throw them over his shoulder.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-bhrū-bhaṅgaṁ baṭum āvārya): ārye, huṅkārād api tathā-bhāvād bhavad-gopikānām abhivyaktaḥ sādhvī-bhāva-prabhāvaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Frowning, he restrains Madhumaṅgala.) O respected lady, your gopīs clearly remain chaste on hearing the sound of the flute.**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*kenāpi dhūrta-patinā khalu śikṣito’si*  
*mantraṁ vaśī-karaṇa-kāraṇam auṣadhaṁ vā \|*  
*puṇyojjvalāny akhila-gopa-vilāsinīnāṁ*  
*yena tvayā gṛha-sukhāni viluṇṭhitāni \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some king among rascals has taught you a mantra or some magic potion to control others. By this, the most pious household happiness of all the cowherd women has been plundered.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *saccaṁ kahedi lalidā \| aṇṇadhā mantādim antareṇa pavvaduttuṅgā mahā-dāṇaā ṇavindīvarādo bi somma-sīdala-pa{i}diṇa kadhaṁ imiṇā saṁharijjanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā speaks the truth. Without using mantras, how could he destroy great demons, towering like mountains, since he is softer and cooler than a blue lotus?**

***lalitā:*** *ajja, jassa sumaranaṁ bi tathā santāvanaṁ taṁ edaṁ appaṇo baassaṁ mā kkhu sīalaṁ bhaṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected boy, when we think of him we suffer. Do not say that your friend has a cooling effect.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho baassa, paidi-sīdalo bi tumāṁ goiāhiṁ uṇṇo tti bhaṇijjasi \| tā ppaṁsia jānissam \| (iti kṛṣṇa-vakṣasi hastaṁ nyasya sasaṁbhramam \|) aho, saccaṁ jjebba kahedi lalidā\| (ksaṇaṁ vimṛśya) \| lalide, viṇṇādaṁ viṇṇādam \| tuha rāhiā ccea ṇuṇaṁ uṇṇā jāe hiaa-vaṭṭhiṇīe canda-koḍi-sīdalo bi esa uṇṇī-kido \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Kṛṣṇa! Though you are cool the gopīs say you are hot. I can verify the truth by touching you. (He places his hand on Kṛṣṇa’s chest with respect.) Oh! Lalitā has spoken correctly. (He reflects for a moment) O Lalitā, I understand. Though he is cooler than a million moons, he has been made hot by Rādhā’s presence in his heart since she is hot by nature.**

***lalitā:*** *ajja, ettha rāa-paṭṭa-patthara-hiae tāe duranta-pemma-saukumajja-hūdāe maha sahie kudo paveso sambhāviadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected boy! How is it possible my friend Rādhā with the most tender prema to enter into a heart hard as a thunderbolt?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-roṣam):* *cabale, ahma-baasso tado bi tuhma-sahīdo birbbharaṁ siṇeha-komalo jaṁ eso bañcida-ṇindo joindo bia ekkagga-citto ṇaṁ jjebba sabbadā cintei \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) Fickle girl! My friend Kṛṣṇa is more delicate than your friend since he, giving up sleep like yogī, thinks of her at all times with concentrated mind.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sāpatrapam): dhig bāliśa, kṛtam alīkena narma-puñjena \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Embarrassed) O what a fool! Enough of these unpleasant jokes!**

***lalitā*** *(svagatam):* *diṭṭhiā vaḍḍhadi pia-sahī \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) By good fortune, my dear friend has become joyful.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *sundara, viśrāmyatu narma-mudrā \| ākarṇaya mad-vivakṣitam \|*  
*hitvā dūre pathi dhava-taror antikaṁ dharma-setor*  
*bhaṅgodagrā guru-śikhariṇam raṁhasā laṅghayantī \|*  
*lebhe kṛṣṇārṇava nava-rasā rādhikā-vāhini tvāṁ*  
*vāg-vīcibhiḥ kim iva vimukhī-bhāvam asyās tanoṣi \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beautiful boy, stop the joking. Please hear what I wish to say. O black ocean!**

**The river of Rādhā, full of new rasa, after rejecting the tree of her husband on the path, smashing through the dam of dharma, leaping over mountains of elders, now attains you. Why do you seem to reject her with deceitful waves of words?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *a{i} śuddha-buddhie ajjabi edaṁ ccea pucchasi \| pekkha kuantāṇaṁ hada-kokilāṇaṁ vitta-saṇatthaṁ mae edaṁ puppha-koaṇḍam ṇimmidam\|*

**Перевод:**

**O woman of pure intelligence! Even now you ask this. Look! I have made a powerful flower bow to frighten away the evil, wretched cuckoos.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *candrānana, sāpi vatsā \|*  
*ālīnāṁ pratihāra-rodhana-vidhau vīkṣya prayatnāvaliṁ*  
*bālā tarkita-mādhavī-parimala-sphūrtir bhayād vepate \|*  
*kiṁ cālokya sudhāṁśu-kānta-salila-syandān alinde kṣaṇād*  
*eṇāṅkodaya-śaṅkinī vikalatām ātanvatī murcchati \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O moon-faced Kṛṣṇa, she is a child. Seeing the great efforts of friends to block the door, the child, smelling the fragrance of what could be** ***mādhavī*** **flowers, trembles in fear. On seeing the moon stone suddenly melt on the terrace and suspecting the rising of the moon, overcome with agitation, she faints.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(svagatam):* *hanta, kaṭhoro’yaṁ daśā-vivartaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Ah! Her transformed condition is serious.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *sundara!*  
*praṇayiṣu militeṣu prema-bhājām upekṣā*  
*ghaṭayati kaṭu-pākāny uccakair dūṣaṇāni \|*  
*dina-maṇir anurāgī projjhya sandhyāṁ raktāṁ*  
*tamasi nikhilam ugre majjayaty eṣa lokam \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beautiful Kṛṣṇa! Neglect of those possessing love produces sin with bitter results to the extreme. The impassioned sun, giving up the red evening, plunges the whole world into darkness. (Kṛṣṇa shamefully lowers his head.)**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(punar nibhālya, sānandaṁ svagatam): diṣṭyāyaṁ smitāliṅgitam aṅgīkurvan dakṣiṇaṁ nyamilayad īkṣaṇam \| (prakāśam) gokulānanda! purastād iyaṁ mākanda-vedī svayam alaṅkartavyā nimīlati heli-bimbe sakhyor ekatarā tvāṁ abhīṣṭa-deśaṁ prāpayati \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at him again, she speaks to herself joyfully.) By good fortune, he is smiling and his right eye is closed. (Aloud) O bliss of Gokula! Stay at this raised area in the mango grove in front of you. When the sun sets one of my two friends will bring you to the appointed place.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sāpatrapam): yathāha bhagavatī \| (iti sa-vayasyo niṣkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Embarrassed) I will do as you say. (He exits with Madhumaṅgala.)**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *putri lalite, kāmaṁ nirvṛtāsmi \| tad ehi \| rādhām anusarāvaḥ \| (ity ubhe parikrāmataḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O my daughter Lalitā! I am really happy. Come, we will find Rādhā. (Both begin walking.)**

*(tataḥ praviśati viśākhayā saha saṅkathayanti rādhā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā enters, while talking to Viśākhā.)**

***rādhikā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*sakhi jalpita-nārikela-nīraṁ*  
*smita-karpūra-vṛtaṁ harer nipīya \|*  
*tanu-saṅga-sudhāṁ vinā na tasya*  
*glapitāhaṁ garalena jīvitāsmi \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, drinking the coconut water of his words mixed with the camphor of his smile, I have become hot and exhausted because of the poison. Without the nectar of the touch of his body, I cannot live.**

***viśākhā:*** *a{i} aviṇṇāda-ṇia-māhādmye, tādiso tuha rā-assa garimā jena so kkhu sāmasundaro bi vādhaṁ rattikido tadhā bi appaṇo māliṇṇaṁ saṅkasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! You do not know your own glories. Your love is so great that Kṛṣṇa has become strongly attached to you, but still you consider yourself vile.**

***rādhikā*** *(punaḥ saṁskṛtena)*  
*nālikinīṁ niśi ghanotkalikām aśaṅkaṁ*  
*kṣiptvāvṛtīr atanu-vanya-gajaḥ kṣuṇatti \|*  
*atrānurāgiṇī cirād udite’pi bhānau*  
*hā hanta kiṁ sakhi sukhaṁ bhavitā varākyāḥ \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Tonight the wild elephant of desire, breaking fences, fearlessly uproots and tramples the fully budding lotus. Alas! Alas! O friend, will this wretched, passionate girl become happy when the sun finally rises?**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(puro rādhām drṣṭvā): putri lalite, sakhyās tava premokti-mudrām udghāṭayitum utkaṇṭhitāsmi \| tad bhavatyā tuṣnim eva bhavitavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing Rādhā in front of her) O daughter Lalitā! I am eager to hear your friend express utterances of love. Please be quite.**

***lalitā:*** *jaṁ ānāvedi tattha hodu \|*

**Перевод:**

**I will do as you command.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(rādhām upetya sa-kaitava-viṣādam):*  
*bhavad-aṅga-sānga-viṣaye priyoktibhir*  
*muhur arthito’pi madirākṣi mādhavaḥ \|*  
*manute manāg api na hīti hṛd-vyathā*  
*pratikāra-yuktir aparā vidhīyatām \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Approaching Rādhā, she fakes lamentation.)**

**O Rādhā with wagtail eyes! Kṛṣṇa pleads with sweet words for association with your limbs. He understands that there is no other remedy for the pain in his heart.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-vyāmoham): alaṁ ettha lajjideṇa \| (ity añjaliṁ baddhvā)*  
*abbhaṁlihahmi ḍahane laḍahaṁ raṅgaṇa-ladaṁ lihantahmi \|*  
*kā paḍiāre juttī mukkia sāmalaghaṇullāsaṁ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Bewildered) I will give up my shyness. (She folds her hands.) When a towering fire burns the beautiful** ***raṅgaṇa*** **creeper, what is the remedy other than the appearance of a dark cloud?**

***paurṇamāsī:***  
*jaratyās tvaṁ naptrī sa tu kamalayā lālita-padaḥ*  
*kathaṅkāraṁ tasmai muhur asulabhāya sprḥayasi \|*  
*prasīda vyāhāre mama racaya ceto divi-caraṁ*  
*gṛhītuṁ pāṇibhyāṁ vidhum ahaha mā bhūt kutukinī \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You are the granddaughter of an old woman. His feet are desired by Lakṣmī. Why do you desire this person so difficult to attain. Be content and concentrate your mind on my words. Do not be desirous of catching the moving moon with your hands.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-gadgadaṁ saṁskṛtena):*  
*mayā te nirbandhān mura-jayini rāgaḥ parihṛto*  
*mayi snigdhe kintu prathaya paramāśis-tatim imām \|*  
*mukhāmododgāra-grahila-matir adyaiva hi yataḥ*  
*pradoṣārambhe syāṁ vimala-vana-mālā-madhukarī \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(With choked voice) You advise me to give up attraction for Kṛṣṇa. O my affectionate friend! Bestow a great benediction on me. Please bless me so that this evening, I may become a bee restingin Kṛṣṇa's spotless garland,with my mind absorbed in the perfume emanating from his mouth.**

***viśākhā:*** *bhaavadi, parittāhi parittāhi \| iam uttānida-ṇettā kiṁpi dāruṇaṁ dasā-visesaṁ lahedi rāhī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī! Save her, save her. Rādhā has arrived at a deplorable state with upturned eyes.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sa-vegam): hā dhik! keyaṁ balād ākṛṣṭā mahā-vipat-kāla-sarpī? (iti sa-dayaṁ rādhām āliṅgya) \| vatse! samāśvasihi samāśvasihi \| bhāvābhivyaktaye protthāpitāsi \| tad idaṁ yathārtham ākarṇyatām \|*  
*amita-vibhavā yasya prekṣā-lavāya bhavādayo*  
*bhuvana-guravo’py utkaṇṭhābhis tapāṁsi vitanvate \|*  
*ahaha gahanā-diṣṭānāṁ te phalaṁ kim abhiṣṭuve*  
*sutanu sa tanur jajñe kṛṣṇas tavekṣaṇa-tṛṣṇayā \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Disturbed) What misfortune! The black snake of danger has been attracted here with force. (She compassionately embraces Rādhā.) O child! Be calm, be calm. You have been agitated by a manifestation of love. Please listen to the truth. Śiva and others, gurus of the world, endowed with unlimited powers, perform austerities with longing to see you even slightly. Ah! How can I praise the results of your previous acts? O Rādhā with beautiful body! Kṛṣṇa has become thin because of thirst to see you!**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*tvad-vārtottara-gīta-gumphita-mukho veṇuḥ samantād abhūt*  
*tvad-veṣocita-śilpa-kalpana-mayī sarvā babhūva kriyā \|*  
*tvan-nāmāni babhūvur asya surabhī-vṛndāni vṛndāṭavī*  
*rādhe tvan-maya-valli-maṇḍala-ghanā jātādya kaṁsa-dviṣaḥ \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His flute now continually plays excellent songs for you. All his activities are completely dedicated to making ornaments for you to wear. Your names have become his perfumes. For him, Vṛndāvana has become crowded with creepers filled with your presence.**

***rādhikā*** *(samāśvasya svagatam): cañcala he citta, ajjabi ṇa pattiāesi*

**Перевод:**

**(Recovering, she speaks to herself) O fickle heart! Even now you have not departed!**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *putri lalite, bāḍhaṁ pragalbhāsi \| tad viśākhā yāvan mākanda-mūlān mukundena saha pratyāvartate tāvad atra mayā tu sva-kṛtyāya gantavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter Lalitā! You have been very bold. I will go to do my duties until Viśākhā returns with Kṛṣṇa from the base of the mango tree.**

*(iti tisro niṣkrāntāḥ)*

**Перевод:**

**(The three leave.)**

***viśākhā*** *(dūraṁ parikramya): so mākando eso puro disa{i} jattha kahṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking from far off) I see the mango tree in front. There is Kṛṣṇa.**

*(tataḥ praviśati kṛṣṇaḥ) \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa enters.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sotkaṇṭhaṁ pratīcīm avalokya):*  
*sadyas tapta-hiraṇya-piṇḍa-madhuraṁ caṇḍa-tviṣo maṇḍalaṁ*  
*saṅgaṁ hanta taraṅgiṇī-rati-guror aṅgīcakārāmbhasi \|*  
*drāg etāny api ghūka-netra-paṭalī siddhāñjana-kṣodatāṁ*  
*bibhranti dvipa-vibhramāṇi rurudhur dhvāntāni vṛndāvanam \|\|20\|\|*  
*(sautsukyaṁ panthānam udvīkṣya) \| katham adyāpi sakhi kācin netrādhvani me nāvatatāra? (iti parāvṛtya prācīṁ paśyan)*

**Перевод:**

**(He looks back with longing.) The fiery globe of the sun, a charming ball of molten gold, has accepted association with the ocean in the water. Darkness, producing ointment for the eyes of owls, bewilders elephants and covers Vṛndāvana. (He looks at the path with longing.) Why do I not see some sakhī at this time?**

*sāndrāḥ supta-kumudvatī-kula-vadhū-nidrā-bhidā-kovidāḥ*  
*kurvāṇāḥ kaluṣa-śriyaṁ paribhavātaṅkena paṅkejinīm \|*  
*saṁrambhād abhisārikābhir asakṛd vyākruṣyamāṇodgamā-*  
*bhāsāḥ śītakarasya hanta haritaṁ pūrvāṁ pariṣkurvate \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The light of the moon, expert at breaking the sleep of lotus-like women, which pollutes the beauty of the lotus by giving them anxiety of their defeat, whose presence is lamented by lovers going to meet out of desire, now appears in the east.**

*(iti vaiyagryaṁ nāṭayati)*  
*dhyātvā dharmaṁ dhṛtim udayinīṁ kiṁ babandhādya rādhā*  
*tīvrākṣepaiḥ kim uta gurubhir lambhitā vā nivṛttim \|*  
*kiṁ vā kaṣṭām abhajata daśāṁ tām avispanda-mandām*  
*indau vindaty udayam api yan nājagāmādya dūtī \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(He becomes completely disturbed.) Thinking of firm dharma, did Rādhā today stop her emerging love? Was she forced to give up coming by elders using sharp scolding? Did she encounter some difficult conditions causing her to become inactive? Though the moon has risen, a messenger has not come.**

***viśākhā*** *(latāntare sodgrāvikam): eso ṇuṇaṁ ukkaṇṭhāe maha jjebba paavīṁ viloedi kaṁho \| tā kkhaṇaṁ parihasissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In the bushes, trying to see) He is looking for me in the creepers. I will joke with him for a moment.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānandam) iyaṁ viśākhāpi cañcala-pañcasākhā sakhi militā \| (ity upasṛtya) sakhi tavopalambhāt tām eva rambhoruṁ labdhām avaimi yad viśākhā-rādhayor advaitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully) I have met her friend Viśākhā with fickle hands. (Approaching her) O sakhī, in meeting you, I experience beautiful Rādhā, since you two are not different.**

*(viśākhā mukham ānamayya maunam ālambate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Viśākhā, bowing her head, becomes silent.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi, kim atra tūṣṇīm asi?*

**Перевод:**

**O sakhī, why are you silent?**

***viśākhā:*** *canda-muha, manda-bhāiṇī hmi \| tā kiṁ viṇṇavissam?*

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, I am most unfortunate. How shall I explain it?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(saśaṅkam):* *kim artham idam?*

**Перевод:**

**(Worried) What is the matter?**

***viśākhā:*** *sundara, ṇa me sarassa{i} nissaradi \| hodu \| tadhā bi saṁvaridum ṇa juttam idam \| (iti mukha-vaikṛtyam abhinīya) \| bho bhaṭṭi-dāraa, sā pia-sahi ahimaṇṇuṇā hadāseṇa mahurā-pattaṇammi \| (ity ardhokte śuṣkaṁ roditi) \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, my words do not come out. Let that be. But it is not proper to conceal things. (She makes a face.) O prince! My dear friend has been taken to Mathurā City by despicable Abhimanyu.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-vyatham):* *kadā nāma nītā?*

**Перевод:**

**(In pain) When did he take her?**

***viśākhā:*** *jadā bhaavadi tuhma sa-āsaṁ laddhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**When Paurṇamāsī was with you.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-khedam):* *viśākhe, kathaṅkāraṁ nitā?*

**Перевод:**

**(Lamenting) O Viśākhā! Why was she taken?**

***viśākhā:*** *tuhammi bhāaṁ takkia \|*

**Перевод:**

**He suspected she fell in love with you.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kathaṁ sa tarkitaḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**Why did he suspect that?**

***viśākhā:*** *loottarī-honto attho ṇa kassa takkaṇijo hoi \|*

**Перевод:**

**No one can comprehend something beyond human experience.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*glapayati vapur duḥśīlo me balān malayānilo*  
*vikirati karair induḥ kṣodaṁ tuṣāgni-bhavaṁ ruṣā \|*  
*madana-hatakas tarjaty eṣa sphuṭair ali-huṅkṛtais*  
*truṭir api vinā rādhāṁ netuṁ mayā na hi śakyate \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The wicked Malaya wind withers my body with force. The moon angrily shines with dust from burning chaff. Cowardly Cupid scolds me with the loud humming of bees. Without Rādhā I cannot bear another moment. (He faints.)**

***viśākhā*** *(sa-khedaṁ sa-saṁbhramam): goulānanda, samāssasa samāssasa, mae kkhu parihasidam \| sa tabassiṇi tāe raṅgaṇa-māliāe rakkhida-parāṇatthi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Lamenting and confused) O bliss of Gokula! Be calm. I was only joking. Being most austere, she has been brought to life by your** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(samāśvasya):* *dhūrte, bhadreṇa kadarthito’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Relieved) O cunning woman, you have tortured me.**

***viśākhā:*** *appaṇo guṇaṁ ṇa sumarasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**You do not remember your own qualities.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi varṇyatāṁ premṇām aṅkaḥ priyāyāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O sakhī, please describe the signs of love in my beloved.**

***viśākhā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*dūrād apy anuśaṅgataḥ śruti-mite tvan-nāma-dheyākṣare*  
*sonmādaṁ madirekṣaṇā viruvatī dhatte muhur vepathum \|*  
*āḥ kiṁ vā kathanīyam anyad api te daivād varāmbhodhare*  
*dṛṣṭe taṁ parirabdhum utsuka-matiḥ pakṣa-dvayīm icchati \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When, even from far away, lovely-eyed Rādhā hears one syllable of your name,she trembles, becomes insane and shouts. When she happens to see a beautiful, dark cloud, eager to embrace it, she desires two wings.**

***Kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *tad ehi \| satvaram eva preyasīṁ prekṣāvahi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Come then. Let us see my beloved immediately.**

*(iti parikrāmataḥ) \|*

**Перевод:**

**(The two walk.)**

*(tataḥ praviśati lalitayārādhyamānā rādhā) \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā enters while being worshipped by Lalitā.)**

***Rādhā*** *(sa-khedam, saṁskṛtena):*  
*pratyūhena parāhatā nu kim abhūd gantuṁ sakhi kṣamā*  
*tasyāḥ kintu niveditena hi harir viśrambham ābhyāyayau \|*  
*hā hanta pratikulatāṁ mayi gataḥ kiṁ vā vidhir dāruṇo*  
*yad dūrād vana-mālikā-parimalo py adyāpi nāsādyate \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Lamenting) Was my sakhī unable to go because of obstacles? Did Kṛṣṇa believe what she said? Ah! Cruel fate has acted against me since from afar I cannot smell the fragrance of his forest garland.**

***viśākhā*** *(puro’nusṛtya, saṁskṛtena):*  
*namrī-kṛtya śiro muhus taru-vṛtām ālokate vartanīm*  
*utthāya kṣaṇam āsanāt punar aho niṣīdaty asau \|*  
*dvitrāṇy etya padāni vīkṣya lalitāṁ bhūyaḥ parāvartate*  
*paśyāgre tava saṅgamotsukatayā rādhā pariklāmyati \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Approaching in front of her) Lowering her head, she constantly looks at the path surrounded by trees. She rises from her seat for a moment, and then again sits. Seeing Lalitā, she proceeds for several steps and then turns back. Looking in front with longing to meet you, Rādhā becomes exhausted.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*vadana-dīpti-vidhūta-vidhūdayā*  
*kumuda-dhāma-dhurā-madhura-smitā \|*  
*nakha-jitoddur iyaṁ hariṇekṣaṇā*  
*tṛṇayati kṣaṇadā-mukha-mādhurim \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The effulgence of her face destroys the rising moon. Her smile is sweeter than a multitude of lotuses. Her nails defeat the stars. With her doe-like eyes she defeats the sweetness of the first part of the evening.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-kātaryam saṁskṛtena):*  
*dṛg-bhaṅgīnāṁ kim u parimalaiḥ preyasībhir niruddhaḥ*  
*kiṁ vā svairī mayi vihitavān uddhatāyām upekṣām \|*  
*hā cāndrībhir dyutibhir abhito grasyamāne’pi loke*  
*prāpto nāyaṁ yad iha latikā-mandire nanda-sūnuḥ \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Dejected) Was he stopped by other gopīs who used the fragrance of their glances? Or being independent, is he indifferent to me, since I am arrogant? For, though the world is bathed in moonlight, the son of Nanda has not come to the creeper bower.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(puro’nusṛtya): aho, sādhīyān prasādaḥ paurṇamāsyāḥ, yad iyam āmodayati kaumudī\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Approaching in front) Ah! It is the mercy of the full moon that the moonlight gives pleasure.**

***rādhikā*** *(camatkṛtim abhinīya sva-gatam): huṁ, ettia bhāa-dheāṇaṁ bhāaṇaṁ saṁvutto esa jaṇo \| (iti vaivaśyam ālambate) \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Showing astonishment, she speaks to herself.) This person is endowed with auspicious symptoms. (She loses control.)**

***viśākhā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*aho dhanyā gopyaḥ kalita-nava-narmoktibhir alaṁ*  
*vilāsair ānandaṁ dadhati madhurair yā madhubhidaḥ \|*  
*dhig astu svaṁ bhāgyaṁ mama yad iha rādhā priya-sakhī*  
*puras tasmin prāpte niviḍa-jaḍimāṅgī viluṭhati \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The fortunate gopīs give bliss to Kṛṣṇa by their fresh joking words and sweet gestures. What misfortune for me! My dear friend Rādhā, with limbs completely stunned, rolls on the ground on attaining his presence.**

***lalitā:*** *a{i} lajjālue rāhie, aggado eso de māṇasa-haṁsa-haro ṇāaro \| tā mā kkhu sajjhaseṇa bihmalā hohi \| jaṁ pagabbhadā jebba ajja kajja-sāhiṇī \|*  
*(iti rādhikāṁ balād ivākṛṣya kṛṣṇāntikam āsādya ca saṁskṛtena) \|*  
*vidūrād ālokya prabalatara-tṛṣṇā-taralitaḥ*  
*sakhī-ceto-haṁsas tava vadana-padme nipatitaḥ \|*  
*bhramad-bhrū-pāśābhyāṁ kitava tam abadhnād iha bhavān*  
*kim asmāsu nyāyyā vyavasitir iyaṁ te visadṛśī \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O shy Rādhā! In front of you is the boy who steals your mind. Do not be overcome with fear, since boldness will produce good effects now. (Pulling Rādhā by force, she brings Rādhā near Kṛṣṇa.) Seeing you from afar, the swan of Rādhā’s heart, trembling with strong thirst, has fallen into the lotus of your face. You have tied up her heart with the ropes of your moving brows. Is it proper to use this unique artifice against us?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): lalite, mad-vidhāḥ khalv abalārtha-hāriṇo na bhavanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O Lalitā, people like me do not steal goods from weak women.**

***viśākhā:*** *dhammia, saccaṁ saccaṁ \| bhadda-kāli-tīttha-kalambo jjebba ettha pamāṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O follower of dharma, you speak the truth. The** ***kadamba*** **tree at the temple of Bhadrakālī is the witness.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi lalite, mad-viśuddhau kathaṁ vaḥ pratītiḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**O sakhī Lalitā, how do you know of my purity?**

***lalitā:*** *chuilla! parikkhā-vihāṇeṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**Cunny boy! I will know by testing.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *vāme, kāmaṁ kathyatāṁ parīkṣā \| mama bhrājiṣṇur ayaṁ kīrti-śubhrāṁśur na mṛṣā kalaṅkī-kartuṁ śakyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**O crooked girl, please explain the test in detail. You cannot falsely spoil the radiant white rays of my fame.**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*tvam unnaddhe rādhā-stana-kanaka-kumbhāntara-milat-*  
*tanūjālī-kāloraga-yuvati-mūrdha-praṇayiṇi \|*  
*yadi kṣobhonmuktaḥ kalayasi karaṁ nāyaka-maṇau*  
*tatas te dhvastāṅkaḥ pracarati yaśo-maṇḍala-śaśi \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**If without disturbance you can place your hand on crest jewel situated on the head of the young black snake-- the line of hair rising between her pot-like breasts-- then the moon of your fame will appear without blemish.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(kṛtrimaṁ trāsam abhinīya): hanta niṣṭhure, nāmnaiva lalitāsi \| yad alpīyasī tāvad-arthe garīyasīṁ sarpa-ghaṭākhyaṁ parīkṣām upakṣipasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Showing fake terror) O cruel woman, you are called Lalitā (gentle). For an insignificant fault, you propose a severe test of touching a snake in a pot.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-praṇayerṣyam): lalide, ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \| (iti sa-bhrū-bhaṅgam avalokyate)\|*

**Перевод:**

**(With affectionate anger) O Lalitā, stop, stop! (She frowns.)**

***lalitā:*** *bisāhe, ṇaṭṭha-ghaṇuddesa-kāriṇiṁ maṁ kīsa tajjati rāhiā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Viśākhā, why does Rādhā scold me though I defeat the motives of the cloud?**

***viśākhā:*** *lalide, imāe hiaa-ṭṭhidaṁ āudaṁ mae jāṇīadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā! I know the aspirations within her heart.**

***lalitā:*** *taṁ kadhei \| suṇissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Please tell me. I am listening.**

***viśākhā*** *(saṁskṛtena āśritya):*  
*spṛśantaṁ yo meghān agham anagha-karmā tam avadhīd*  
*viṣa-jvālā-jālonmada-mada-mayat-kālīyam ahim \|*  
*akārṣīd gopendra-druham ajagaram divya-puruṣaṁ*  
*bhujaṅgācārye’smin kim iva ghaṭate pannaga-ghaṭaḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What is a snake in a pot to the guru of snakes, a person of pure actions, who killed the sinful Agha towering to the clouds, who killed Kāliya intoxicated with his store of burning poison, who pulled away the python, actually a** ***devatā,*** **who had attacked Nanda?**

***lalitā*** *(vihasya): halā rāhi, appaṇo pariara-rūbāe ṇa jāṇāsi māhāppaṁ imāe, pekkha \| tathā hi—*  
*abi garuḍassa sihā-maṇim uraga-vahu-gavva-hāri-virudassa \|*  
*pahaba{i} sahi moheduṁ tuha ṇaa-romāali-bhuagi \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) My friend Rādhā! You do not know the glory of that line of hair, which is your servant. Look! The fresh line of hair resembling a snake has the power to bewilder the crest jewel of Garuḍa, whose cries steal the pride of the wives of the snakes.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-praṇaya-roṣam): a{i} dhiṭṭhe lalide, ettha āṇabia maṁ viḍambesi \| tā gadua buḍḍhiāṇaṁ goīṇaṁ viṇṇavissam \| (iti gantum icchati)*

**Перевод:**

**O bold Lalitā! Bringing me here, you ridicule me! I will go to the village and inform the elders about you. (She desires to leave.)**

***lalitā:*** *a{i} muddhe \| ṇaṁ sāhuṁ coraṁ vā jāṇia jāhi \| (iti paṭāñcalam ādadāti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O foolish Rādhā! Go after understanding whether he is honest or a thief.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *caṇḍi lalite! yad yato durāgrahān na viśrāntāsi, tataḥ karavāṇi parīkṣām \| (iti rādhām anusarpati\|)*

**Перевод:**

**O cruel Lalitā! Since you are so insistent, I will take the test. (He approaches Rādhā.)**

***lalitā*** *(vilokya): cha{i}lla, ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \| viṇṇadaṁ viṇṇadam \| (iti saṁskṛtena)*  
*prārabdhe purataḥ parīkṣaṇa-vidhau trāsānuviddhasya te*  
*khinno’yaṁ kara-pallavas taralatāṁ kampodgamaiḥ puṣpayati \|*  
*romāñcaṁ śikhi-piccha-cuḍa-nibiḍaṁ mūrtiś ca dhatte tato*  
*jñātas tvaṁ nanu paśyato hara-puri-sāmrājya-dhaureyakaḥ \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at him) Crafty boy! Stop, stop! I understand. At the beginning of this test, the bud of your hand began to flower with unsteadiness, with quivering hairs standing on end, since you became afflicted with fear. Your cluster of peacock feathers on your head began to stand up. Seeing this, I understand that you must be the ruler in the kingdom of thieves.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(saṅkucan namri-bhuya): hanta, dhi-gauravaṁ gauriṇaṁ yad aham eva cauri-kṛto’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Humiliated) Ah! Her great intelligence says that I am a thief.**

***lalitā:*** *cha{i}lla, diṭṭhiā appaṇo muheṇa aṅgīkidam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Clever boy, by luck you yourself have admitted this.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi, sauhṛdenopadiśyatāṁ me śreyasaḥ panthāḥ yena aparadhī-bhavan na vrajāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O sakhī, out of friendship, please teach me the correct path so that I will not be an offender.**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtam āśritya):*  
*gatānāṁ rādhāyāḥ stana-giri-taṭe yogam abhito*  
*vivikte muktānāṁ tvam iha tarali-bhuya tarasā \|*  
*viśuddhānāṁ madhye praviśa saraṇārthi sahṛdayā*  
*bhajante sād-guṇyād api pṛthula-doṣaṁ hi puruṣam \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Trembling, desiring to surrender, you must quickly approach the pure, liberated souls who have perfected yoga on the slopes of great mountains of success. Being merciful because of their great qualities, they respond to even the most sinful person.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi sādhūpadiṣṭaṁ tvayā \| (iti sānandam upasṛtya pānau rādhāṁ dadhāti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O sakhī, you have explained this correctly. (In bliss he approaches and touches Rādhā.)**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-gadgadam): sundara, ajuttaṁ tujjha edam \| (iti pāṇim ācchidya śākhināṁ tirodadhāti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(With choked voice) O beautiful Kṛṣṇa, this is not proper! (She blocks his hand and disappears in the trees.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(rādhām aprekṣya sa-śaṅkam): hanta sakhyau, kva vāṁ priya-sakhī?*

**Перевод:**

**(Not seeing her, he becomes worried.) O my two friends, where is your dear friend?**

***ubhe:*** *mohaṇa, ṇiruvia bhaṇissahma \| (iti śākhi-prṣṭham āsādya) halā rāhī \| ṇamma-silaṁ kanḥaṁ parihasiduṁ laddho osaro \| tā kkhaṇaṁ sāvahitthā hoi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O enchanting Kṛṣṇa, if we find her we will tell you. (They approach her from behind.) O my friend Rādhā, now is the time to play a joke on Kṛṣṇa, who likes joking. Hide your emotions for a few moments.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-vyājaṁ bhruvau vibhujya): lalide, parihasiduṁ ti kiṁ bhaṇāsi \| jaṁ īrisaṁ sāhasaṁ ṇa kkhu mārisīe juttam \| tā patthidahmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(She pretends to frown.) O Lalitā, why do you tell me to joke with him? Such boldness is not suitable to me. I am leaving.**

***lalitā*** *(kṛṣṇam abhyupetya): candāṇaṇa! ahma-pia-sahī kimpi viṇṇavidu-kāmā bibhāedi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Approaching Kṛṣṇa) O moon-faced Kṛṣṇa! Though wanting to say something, our dear friend is afraid.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi, vaśa-vartini jane na khalu bhītir avakāśaṁ labhate \| tan nikāmam ājñāpayatu \|*

**Перевод:**

**O sakhī! One should not fear a person under one’s control. Let her order me as much as she likes.**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtam āśritya):*  
*cetas tāmyati me bhayormibhir alaṁ pāṇi-dvayaṁ kampate*  
*kaṇṭhaḥ sajjati hanta ghūrṇati śiraḥ svidyanti gātrāṇy api \|*  
*goṣṭhākhaṇḍala caṇḍa-sāhasa-vidhau tenāsmi nāhaṁ kṣamā*  
*yad dūrād abhisārito niśi bhavān etan mama kṣāmyatu \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**My heart chokes in the waves of fear. My two hands tremble to the extreme. My throat becomes blocked. My head revolves. My limbs perspire. O Indra of the cows! I cannot tolerate such fierce punishment. Please forgive me that you have come for this appointment at night from so far away.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *na jāne narmato dharmato vāyaṁ girāṁ garimā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) I do not know whether her weighty words are in jest or from a sense of dharma.**

***rādhikā*** *(kiñcid āvirbhuya): sahi! tuṇṇaṁ patthābehi ṇam \| jāva kobi ṇa pekkhadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Appearing partially) O sakhī! Quickly send him away so that no one sees.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-khedam ātma-gatam): capala-premāṇo hi bālā-ramaṇyaḥ \| tat kim ivāsambhāvyaṁ nāma? (prakāśam):*  
*tvayāhutaḥ pārśve praṇaya-nikurambeṇa rabhasād*  
*asiddhārtho rādhe bhavitum iha yuktaḥ katham aham \|*  
*śriyākṛṣṭaḥ kṛṣṇāyasa-maṇir ayas-kānta-śilayā*  
*sphuṭaṁ tām aspṛṣṭvā bhajati kim adūre sthagitatām \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Lamenting to himself) Young women have unsteady love. What is not impossible for them? (Aloud) O Rādhā! In haste you have called me to your side because of great love. Is it proper that I cannot attain my purpose at all at this time? Why is iron stopped without touching the magnet, though very close, and though drawn by the magnet of your beauty?**

***lalitā:*** *goulāṇaṁda, rāhiaṁ kīsa ubālahesi \| ṇaṁ dhamma-hadaaṁ ccea ubālaha, jo kkhu hadāso doṇaṁ ṇibbharāṇurattāṇaṁ antare paḍibandhī-hodi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O bliss of Gokula, why do you scold Rādhā? Rather you should criticize the wretched nature of dharma which, creating hopelessness, acts as an obstacle to two people deeply in love?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *paśya paśya \|*  
*sakhi nirbharam anuraktāḥ*  
*praṇayinam anuyānti dharmam api hitvā \|*  
*iyam atirāgiṇī prācī*  
*cumbati vidhum indra-nāthāpi \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look, look! O sakhī! Those who are in love pursue their lover, giving up dharma. The eastern direction, though the wife of Indra, kisses the moon.**

***lalitā:*** *tuhmāṇaṁ pa{u}ttare kā ṇāma pahabadi? tā ido vijaantu sāmi-pādāe \|*

**Перевод:**

**What woman can be respected by your answer? May the husbands be victorious!**

***rādhikā*** *(sākutam anusṛtya): lalide, appaṇo muheṇa kiṁvi viṇṇavia ṇaṁ nivaṭṭhāva{i}ssaṁ\| (iti lalitām avekṣya saṁskṛtena):*  
*samantān me kīrtir mukharita-satī-maṇḍala-mukhā*  
*kalaṅkenonmuktaṁ kulam avikala-śrīr api patiḥ \|*  
*calac-cillī-līlā-jita-madana-dhanvoddhatir ayaṁ*  
*tad asminn ārambhe hṛdayam aphalaṁ viklavayati \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(With purpose, Rādhā approaches Lalitā.) After personally saying something, I will make him go. My fame resounds among all people. My family is free of blemish. My husband is endowed with unimpaired wealth. With audacity of moving brows which defeat Cupid’s bow, he has agitated my unfulfilled heart from the beginning.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(rādhāṁ nirūpya socchvāsam ātma-gatam):*  
*dhāvantyāḥ śruti-śaṣkulī-parisaraṁ saṅgād apāṅgād apāṅga-śriyo*  
*dhatte hīraka-kuṇḍalaṁ marakatottaṁsa-dyutiṁ subhruvaḥ \|*  
*vāg-antaḥ smita-bhāg vibhāti tad idaṁ sāṅke sakhī-śikṣayā*  
*vaimukhyaṁ kila kṛtrimaṁ vilasati klāntiṁ mano mā sma gāḥ \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing Rādhā, he speaks to himself while sighing.)**

**From association with her glance which spreads to her ear holes her diamond earrings take on the luster of blackish ear ornaments. The words this woman with beautiful brows and her attractive glance show a smile. I suspect that this repulsion to me is fake, instructed by her sakhī. O mind, do not become weary.**

***lalitā*** *(kṛṣṇa-mukham ālokya janāntikam): bisāhe, iṅgideṇa lakkhemi \| uṇṇidaṁ imiṇā ahmāṇaṁ rahassam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing Kṛṣṇa’s face, she speaks privately.) O Viśākhā! I notice by a sign that our secret has been found out.**

***viśākhā:*** *adha iṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes, it is certain.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-smitam): lalite, kṛtam atra vañcana-cāturī-prapañcena \| na hi latayā prasāritās tantavo gandha-sindhurasya bandhanāya prabhavanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O Lalitā! You have tricked me with deceptive words. Webs of creepers cannot catch an elephant.**

***viśākhā:*** *sahi rāhi, ṇipphalaṁ bilambesi \| jhatti kidatthī-kuṇa appaṇo pia-jaṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā! You uselessly delay. Quickly satisfy your dear beloved.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānurāgam)*  
*karṇa-dvandvam idaṁ rutair iha kuhū-kaṇṭhasya kuṇṭhī-kṛtaṁ*  
*sadyaḥ komala-bhāratī-parimalenollāghaya ślāghayā \|*  
*niḥśaṅkaṁ kila śītalī-kuru parirambheṇa rambhoru me*  
*gambhīra-smara-vahni-tāpa-laharī-pātrāṇi gātrāṇy api \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the fragrance of your pleasing soft words, immediately cure my two ears dulled by the warbling from the cuckoo’s throat, O Rādhā with beautiful thighs! By your embrace, fearlessly cool my limbs afflicted by waves of Cupid’s intense fire.**
