***viśākhā:*** *sundara, esā bhaavadī lajjā rāhiā-rūbeṇa uttiṇṇa \| tā jāva ṇaṁ cāḍu-baṁdheṇa saṁmuhī-kadua samappehma tāva bhavanteṇa somma-sīala-buttiṇā hodabbam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful Kṛṣṇa! The goddess of shyness has appeared in the form of Rādhā. If we bring her with flattering words and offer her to you, you must be gentle and cool.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sādaram):*  
*ayam atra nisarga-śītalaḥ*  
*sakhi rādhā-kucayor avasthitim \|*  
*nava-kāñcana-kumbhayor ahaṁ*  
*sphurad-indīvara-dāmavad bhaje \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Respectfully) O sakhī! I am by nature cool. As a garland of blossoming blue lotuses I will respect a place on Rādhā’s breasts, which are like new gold pots.**

*(iti mandaṁ mandaṁ rādhām upasarpati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Slowly he approaches Rādhā.)**

***rādhikā*** *(kiñcid upasṛtya): sahi bisāhe, suṭṭhu bhīdahmi \| tā kiṁtti mam ubekkhasi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Withdrawing slightly) O my friend Viśākhā! I am very frightened. Why do you neglect me?**

***lalitā:*** *rāhe, esā bisāheti vikkhādā kadhaṁ tumaṁ pacchādia rakkhiduṁ pahavadu, tā rakkhaṇa-kkhamaṁ ṇaṁ baṇa-māliaṁ jjebba bhajehi \| jaṁ esā āaṭṭida-śilīmuhā dīsa{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā! How can she who is known as Viśākhā (without branches) hide and protect you? Use the garland, which is capable of giving protection, since it can be seen to attract the bees.**

***rādhika*** *(sa-praṇaya-roṣam): a{i} dummuhi lalide, siddhā ccea tujjha maṇoradhā, tahabi ṇa nivuttāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In affectionate anger) O foul-mouthed Lalitā! Your desire has been achieve but you do not desist.**

***viśākhā:*** *halā rāhī, sabbāṇaṁ goula-jaṇāṇaṁ abhaa-dāṇa-sattre dīkkhido kahṇo \| tā ido kiṁtti bhāesi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā! Kṛṣṇa is engaged in a sacrifice to give fearlessness to all the people of Gokula. Why do you fear?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sundari rādhe, tvam eva suṣṭhu baliṣṭhāsi \| tataḥ kathaṁ mattas tava bhītiḥ? tathā hi—*  
*ahīno bhrū-gucchaḥ kuṭila-valanair veṣṭayati māṁ*  
*kharas te netrānto mayi vitanute tāḍana-vidhim \|*  
*pralambaḥ keśānto harati haṭha-vṛttyā mama balaṁ*  
*bhajadbhis tvām etair aham iha jitair asmi vijitaḥ \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful Rādhā! You are stronger. Why do you fear me? Your broad brows surround me with their crooked coils. The rough edges of your eyes beat me. The extended edges of your hair steal my strength with great force.**

***lalitā:*** *kahṇa, kudo imāe baliṭṭhattaṇaṁ jaṁ appaṇo dhaṇaṁ tuatto moābiduṁ ṇa samatthā?*

**Перевод:**

**How has she become so strong that she cannot free her treasure from you?**

***viśākhā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*vidhatte kaṁsāriḥ sakhi paramahaṁsāliṣu ratiṁ*  
*manohaṁsendraṁ te katham api na nirmokṣyati tataḥ \|*  
*badhānāmuṁ sadyas tam api bhuja-vallī-vilasitaiḥ*  
*śaṭhe kaḥ kṣemārthi sumukhi nahi śāṭhyaṁ ghaṭayati \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, Kṛṣṇa has love for the friends of the greatest swans. Why should he not deliver the king of swans, your mind? Bind him with the gestures of your creeper arms. O cunning Rādhā with beautiful face, no person desiring peace should show deception.**

***rādhikā*** *(sābhisūyam): pābe visāhie! tumaṁ bi lalidāe bisa-ladāe mārudeṇa dūsidāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With hatred) O sinful Viśākhā! You are contaminated by the wind from the poisonous vine of Lalitā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi lalite, sva-prasādāmṛte kāmam adattāvagāhanayā katham adyāpi taṭasthī-kṛto’smi rādhayā?*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Lalitā! Why does Rādhā act so indifferently to me today, not freely giving the nectar of her mercy, immersing me in it?**

***lalitā:*** *kahṇa, muñca cādūrī-bitthāraṁ \| ṇa kkhu candāalī bia jhatti vāa-mettaeṇa sulaha-ppasādā ahma-pia-sahī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, give up your cunning tricks. Our dear friend never gives mercy quickly just by words, like Candrāvalī does.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kathaṁ sulabhas te sakhī-prasādaḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**How can I get her mercy?**

***lalitā:*** *seā-saṇtāṇena \|*

**Перевод:**

**By constant service.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānandam, rādhāṁ paśyan):*  
*kiṁ candanena kucayo racayāmi citram*  
*uttaṁsayāmi kavarīṁ tava kiṁ prasūnaiḥ \|*  
*aṅgāni laṅgima-taraṅgi kareṇa kiṁ vā*  
*saṁvāhayāmy atanu-kheda-karambitāni \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Blissfully, while looking at Rādhā)**

**Shall I draw pictures on your breasts with sandalwood? Shall I put flowers in your braids? Shall I massage your limbs afflicted by the pain of Cupid with my undulating hand?**

*(ity agre parikrāmati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(He approaches)**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-līlam apakramya sāṅguli-tarjanam): pāmari, sumarissasi osare \| tā esā gharaṁ gacchanti jihmāṇaṁ tuhmāṇaṁ hatthādo appāṇaṁ moāva{i}ssam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O vile woman! You will remember this occasion. Going home, I will free myself from you deceitful people.**

***lalitā*** *(paṭāñcalam ākṛṣya):*  
*sahi rāhi jāhi na gharaṁ para-hatthe patthidahmi ṇia-haṁse \|*  
*a{i} bahire hiraṇṇaṁ desi kudo añcale gaṁṭhiṁ \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Pulling her cloth) O my friend Rādhā, do not go home since the swan of my other hand is advancing. Will you give the gold tied in your cloth?**

***rādhikā:*** *muñcehi muñcehi añcalam \| ido gadua ajjiaṁ viṇṇavissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Let go of my cloth. I will go home and inform my mother-in-law.**

*(****nepathye****): hanta ṇattiṇi lalide, kahiṁ de pia-sahi rāhiā?*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) Ah! O granddaughter Lalitā! Where is your dear friend Rādhā?**

***lalitā:*** *hanta, esā ajjiā muharā idha jjebba āacchadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! Respected Mukharā is coming here.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-śaṅkam): tato davīyān bhavitāsmi \| (iti tathā sthitaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Worried) I should flee from here. (He does this.)**

***mukharā*** *(praviśya puro dṛṣṭiṁ nikṣipya sāśaṅkam ātma-gatam): jo kkhu dūrado kobi ṇīlima-puñjo maraada-tthahmaṁ biḍambaṁto diṭṭhiṁ me āa\[u\]di \| ṇūṇaṁ so eso kahno bhave jaṁ aūbbaṁ kimpi sorabhaṁ pasappa\[i\] \| (iti kṛṣṇāntikam anusarpati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, she glances in front and speaks to herself with worry.) That rascal who, from far off, appearing like a blue mass, conquering a pillar of sapphire, attracts my vision, is certainly Kṛṣṇa, since he emanates a most wonderful fragrance. (She approaches him.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *ārye! (ity ardhokte)—*

**Перевод:**

**O respected woman! (Interrupted)**

***mukharā*** *(sa-kapaṭākroṣam): ko kkhu ajje ajje tti khulakhulāedi?*

**Перевод:**

**(With false anger) What rascal utters “respected woman”?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *ārye mukhare! sukhaṁ vardhase \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected Mukharā, may your happiness increase!**

***mukharā:*** *mohaṇa! jāva tuha vaṁśiāe muattaṇaṁ ṇa saṁvuttaṁ tāva kudo ahmāṇaṁ suham?*

**Перевод:**

**O enchanting Kṛṣṇa! How can we be happy as long has your flute is not silent?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *ārye, kiṁ te’parādhyati vaṁśī?*

**Перевод:**

**O respected woman! Has my flute offended you?**

***mukharā:*** *puccha imāo savva-goula bāliāo jāo kaṇṇa-sīhaṁ pavisaṁtammi baṁśiā-phukkārārambhe bāraṁ bāraṁ ṇivārijjantīo bi baṇe dhāanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ask all the girls of Gokula who run to the forest, though blocked repeatedly, when the flute begins to sound and enters their ears.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(vihasya): mukhare, satyam yathārtha-nāmāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) O Mukharā! You are true to your name.**

***mukharā:*** *mohaṇa, padose tujjha ettha paveso maṁ saṅkāulaṁ karedi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O enchanting Kṛṣṇa, your coming here in the evening worries me.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *mukhare, kṛtam atra śaṅkayā \| yad adya paurṇamāsyā me varṇitaṁ tavātra catvarāṅke caṅkramīti kāpy adbhūtā hariṇīti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Mukharā! Do not worry! Paurṇamāsī has told me that a wonderful doe roams in your yard here.**

***mukharā:*** *ṇāara, pahāde pecchisassi \| ṇaṁ dāṇiṁ sāhehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O deceitful boy! You will see that in the morning. Now go away.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *hanta vṛddhe! gaḍḍa-viṣāṇa-kaṭhore! viśrabdham āsyatām \| eṣo’haṁ vrajāmi\| (iti śākhinām antardadhāti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**Old woman! You are hard as a ram’s horn. But I trust you. I am going. (He disappears in the bushes.)**

***mukharā:*** *lalide, saccaṁ gado kahṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā! Kṛṣṇa has actually gone.**

***lalitā:*** *adha iṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *ghūrṇākuleyaṁ jaratī \| tad atra tuṣṇīm etya rādhā-paṭāñcalam akarṣayāmi \| (iti tathā karoti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) This old woman is very upset. Therefore, silently approaching, I will pull Rādhā’s cloth. (He does this.)**

***mukharā*** *(cakṣuṣī vikāśya sākroṣam): dhiṭṭi lalide, aggado eso pīdambaro kahṇo rāhī-sāḍi-añcalaṁ āaḍḍhaṁto bia dīsa{i} \| tā kīsa tumaṁ maṁ padāresi maṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**(Opening her eyes, in anger) Impudent Lalitā! I see that Kṛṣṇa wearing yellow cloth is pulling Rādhā’s cloth. Why do you deceive me?**

*(kṛṣṇaḥ sa-śaṅkaṁ kiñcid apasarpati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa flees with a little consternation.)**

***lalitā*** *(sva-gatam):* *ratti-andhiaṁ buḍḍhiaṁ bañcemi \| (prakāśaṁ saṁrambham abhinīya saṁskṛtena \|)*  
*mudhā śaṅkām andhe jarati kuruṣe yāmuna-taṭe*  
*tamālo’yaṁ cāmīkara-kalita-mūle nivasati \|*  
*samīra-preṅkholād aticaṭula-śākhā-bhujatayā*  
*vayasyāyā yena stana-vasanam āsphālitam abhūt \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) I will fool the old lady who is blind at night. (Aloud, in anger.)**

**Blind old woman! You worry foolishly. A** ***tamāla*** **tree with a golden root grows on the bank of the Yamunā. Because of the** ***tamāla*** **tree, with its moving branches swaying in the wind, the cloth on the breast your friend flutters.**

***mukharā*** *(svagatam):* *asaccaṁ ṇa kahei \| (prakāśam) vatse, ghummāulahmi \| tā gharaṁ gadua suvissam \| (iti niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Lalitā does not lie. (Aloud) I am completely confused. I will go home and sleep.**

***viśākhā:*** *halā rāhi! kahṇassa muha-maṇḍalumīlidaṁ ghamma-jala-biṁdu-jālaṁ ṇia-añcaleṇa abaṇehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Rādhā! Wipe away the drops of perspiration on Kṛṣṇa’s face with the edge of your cloth.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-bhrū-bhaṅgam): bisāhe, tumaṁ jebba abaṇehi \| jā kkhu ākomāraṁ imassiṁ bbade ggahida-dikkhāsi*

**Перевод:**

**(Frowning) Viśākhā! You wipe away his perspiration. From childhood you have vowed to do such things.**

***viśākhā:*** *rādhe, kaṇṭha-ṭṭhidā de raṅgaṇa-māliā bhaṇādi \| mā kuppa \| tumaṁ bi taha dikkhā-vihāṇeṇa kārijjanta-saṅkalpāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā! The** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland on your neck announces this also. Do not be angry. You will also take this vow.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(raṅgaṇa-mālāṁ dṛṣṭvā sa-ślāgham):*  
*śaṅke cirāt kim api raṅgaṇa-puṣpa-saṅghaḥ*  
*puṇyaṁ purā parama-tīrtha-vare vyadhatta \|*  
*yasmān mamāpy asulabhe madirākṣi sākṣād*  
*aṅgī-cakāra tava vakṣasi saṅga-saukhyam \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing the garland he praises it.) O girl with beautiful eyes! I think that the** ***raṅgaṇa*** **flowers have previously performed pious acts at the most exalted holy place. Because of this they have accepted the happy association of your chest directly, though I cannot attain it.**

***rādhikā:*** *halā bisāhe, jā kkhu maha kaṇṭhādo baleṇa āaḍḍhia nīdā tue aṇagghā guñjāalī sā dāṇiṁ samappīadu \| esā sukkhā appaṇo raṅgaṇamāliā geṇhiadu \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Viśākhā! Give me the priceless** ***guñja*** **necklace that you forcibly took from my neck. Take your dried up** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland.**

***viśākhā:*** *goulāṇaṁda! guñjā-hāra-kide maha kuppa{i} pia-sahī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O bliss of Gokula! My dear friend is angry with me, who made the** ***guñja*** **necklace.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *rādhe, sannidhehi \| tava kaṇṭhe guñjāvalim ādadhāmi \| (ity upasarpati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, come here. I will put the** ***guñja*** **necklace on your throat. (He approaches.)**

***lalitā*** *(sa-smitam ātma-gatam): guñjā-hāra-samappaṇa-miseṇa rāhī-kañcu-añcalaṁ pphaṁsadi kahṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling to herself) On the pretext of offering the** ***guñja*** **necklace, Kṛṣṇa will touch her breast.**

*(rādhikā sa-bhrū-vikṣepaṁ parāvartate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā stops frowning.)**

***viśākhā:*** *halā rāhe, jaṁ laddhuṁ ukkaṇṭhāsi taṁ kiṁ kkhu laddhāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā! You have attained that which you longed for.**

***rādhikā*** *(bimbādharaṁ saṇdaśya): dhiṭṭhe, ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \| (iti līlāravindena tāḍayati) \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Biting her lips) Impudent Viśākhā! Stop, stop! (She strikes her with a toy lotus.)**

***viśākhā*** *(vihasya): saaṁ āsaṅkiṇi, mā kuppa \| guñjā-hāraṁ pucchemi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) Fearful Rādhā, do not be angry. I will ask for the** ***guñja*** **necklace.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*kva tapas tathā mamāste*  
*līlāmbuja-hatim avāpnuyāṁ \|*  
*yena māṁ cañcalena tāḍaya*  
*locana-kamalāñcalenāpi \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What austerity should I perform in order that you will strike me with the play lotus? Strike me with the edge of your fickle lotus eye.**

***lalitā:***  
*hariṇo samappia taṇuṁ kibiṇāsi kadhaṁ darāvaloammi \|*  
*diṇṇe cintā-raaṇe ṇa saṁpuḍammi ggaho juttī \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Offering your body to him, why are you a miser in looking at him only slightly? One who has given a** ***cintāmaṇi*** **stone should not be attached to the case.**

***rādhikā:*** *lalide, ebbaṁ jappaṁti guru-loesu mā kkhu imaṁ jaṇaṁ abaraddhaṁ karehi\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā! Talking in this way, do not make him offend my elders.**

***viśākhā:*** *sahi, kīsa saṅkesi \| ṇaṁ bhaavadī jebba ettha samāhāṇe dakkhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend why do you worry? Paurṇamāsī is expert in solving such problems.**

***lalitā*** *(sa-harṣam ātma-gatam) diṭṭiā pia-sahī hasidāabāṅga-taraṅgeṇa kahṇaṁ āliṅgadi\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully to herself) By good fortune, my friend embraces Kṛṣṇa with her smiling glance.**

***viśākhā*** *(saṁskṛtena): lalite, paśya paśya \|*  
*śaśī vyomotsaṅgaṁ śaśinam abhitaḥ kānti-laharī*  
*puro vṛṇdāraṇyaṁ sumukhi sahasā kānti-laharīm \|*  
*harir vṛṇdāraṇyaṁ harim api kileyaṁ tava sakhī*  
*sakhīṁ premṇaḥ pūro nija-suṣamayāmaṇḍayad ayam \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā, look! The moon decorates the horizon with its great beauty. The horizon with waves of beauty decorates the moon. Kṛṣṇa, full of love, decorates Vṛndāvana. Vṛndāvana decorates him. Rādhā decorates Kṛṣṇa with her beauty. He decorates her with his beauty.**

***lalitā:*** *haddhī haddhī \| bisāhe pekkha sasi-kāṁta-maṇi-pasudehiṁ jala-purehiṁ sura-puaṇa-vei-purado kidāiṁ viluppianti alevaṇa-maṇḍalāiṁ tā ehi \| ṇaṁ puppha-keāriaṁ ṇehma \|*

**Перевод:**

**What misfortune! Look Viśākhā! The water from the moonstones has washed away the sandalwood patterns on the altar for worshipping the sun. Come. Let us fetch some** ***kedara*** **flowers.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, nedānim api vāmyād virāmas te \| (ity añcalaṁ gṛhṇāti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved! Even now you do not give up your stubborn attitude. (He grabs her cloth.)**

***rādhikā:*** *muñcehi muñcehi \| sahīo maṁ āābeṁti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Let go, let go. My friends are calling.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kaṭhore! mayy atra māṅgīkuru bhaṅguratām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hard hearted Rādhā! Do not be dishonest with me now.**

***rādhikā*** *(sasmitam): dei sarassa{i}, vandijjasi jaṁ saccā jjebba paaḍaasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O goddess Sarasvatī! I offer respects to you so that you will reveal the truth.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(kiñcid vihasya):*  
*padminyās te sumukhi parama-prema-saurabhya-pūro*  
*dūrotsarpī yad-avadhi mudā kṛṣṇa-bhṛṅgena bheje \|*  
*ākrānto’yaṁ tava nava-mukhāmbhoja-mādhvīka-pāna-*  
*pratyāśābhis tad-avadhi ruvan saṁbhramī bambhramīti \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling slightly) O Rādhā with a beautiful face! Until you, coming from afar, full of the highest fragrance of the lotus, are enjoyed blissfully by the bee of Kṛṣṇa, that agitated bee will wander about buzzing in agitation, hoping to drink the nectar of you fresh lotus face.**

*kiṁ ca—*  
*muktānām upalābhyām eva kucayoḥ sālokyam ālokya te*  
*hitvā saṅgam ahaṁ samasta-suhṛdāṁ kaivalyam āsedivān \|*  
*vaiṣamyaṁ tilam apy anāśritavatoḥ sāndrāmṛta-syandibhir*  
*māṁ purṇaṁ kuru tanvi turṇam anayoḥ sāyujya-dānotsavaiḥ \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing that the pearls on your breast have attained** ***sālokya,*** **giving up association of all my friends, I now undertake oneness. Quickly satisfy me with the joy of oneness in the form of thick nectar flowing from your breasts, which do not give shelter to any unevenness.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, paśya paśya \|*  
*apāṁ patyuḥ puṣṭi-karaṇa-rasa-pākaḥ kumudini-*  
*kadambānām aṅga-jvara-haraṇa-śitauṣadhi-ghaṭaḥ \|*  
*mṛgāṅko’yaṁ koki-pariṣad-abhicārādhvarā-dhurā*  
*purodhāḥ kālindi-parisara-pariṣkāram akarot \|\|52\|\|*  
*tad etāṁ vāsantikā-kānti-maṇḍita-maṇḍalasya candramasaś candrikā-cakra-cumbitaṁ vicarāvo nikuñja-candraśālikām \| (iti niṣkrāntau) \|*

**Перевод:**

**My beloved, look! The moon, elixir to nourish the ocean, acting as a cool medicine to destroy the fever of all the lotuses, the chief priest in a sacrifice for killing the** ***cakravāka*** **birds, comes near the Yamunā. Let us go to the pavilion in the bower, kissed by the light of the moon decorated with the beauty of spring. (They exit.)**

*(iti niskrāntāḥ sarve\|)*

**Перевод:**

**All exit.**

## Act Four: Stealing the Flute

*(tataḥ praviśati nāndīmukhī):*

**Перевод:**

**(Nāndīmukhī enters.)**

***nāndīmukhī:*** *bhaṇidahmi lalidāe—halā ṇāṁdīmuhi, go-maṇḍale goṭṭhaṁ pa{i}ṭṭhe ehniṁ kahṇo tubaraṁta go-aḍḍhaṇāhi-muhaṁ patthido \| tā tumaṁ tattha gadua sualaṁ biṇṇabehi, jadhā eso osare ṇia-baassassa rāhiaṁ sumarābedi tti \| (parikramya) kadhaṁ ettha pa{u}mā āaccha{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā has said, “O my friend Nānḍīmukhī, with the cows entering the pastures, Kṛṣṇa has now arrived in front of Govardhana. Go there and inform Subala, so that Kṛṣṇa should remember Rādhā at this time.” (Walking) Why is Padmā coming here?**

*(praviśya)* ***padmā:*** *halā ṇāndimuhi, kāmaṁ kusalāsi \| tā kaṁpi uvāaṁ kadhehi jeṇa ubbiggaṁ candāalīm āsāsemi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O my friend Nāndīmukhī, are you well? Please tell me how I can pacify Candrāvalī.**

***nāndīmukhī:*** *kiṁ se ubbea-kāraṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Why is she disturbed?**

***padmā:*** *halā, jāṇāsi jjebba tumaṁ jadhā padose sabbaṁ kkhu goulaṁ vibbhameṇa kahṇo paccahaṁ rañjedi \|*

**Перевод:**

**You know that, every evening of every day, Kṛṣṇa colors all of Gokula with his beauty.**

***nāndīmukhī:*** *adha iṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

***padmā:*** *saṁpadaṁ dāva ettha dakkhiṇe goṭṭhaddhe imassa gandho bi dullaho \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nowadays, it is hard to find even the scent of Kṛṣṇa in the southern half of the pastures.**

***nāndīmukhī:*** *halā, mā duṇehi \| (saṁskṛtena)*  
*dṛṣṭaṁ bimbita-dhātu-citra-racanaṁ śaibyā lalāṭaṁ mayā*  
*śyāmā-kuntala-cāmaraṁ ca viluṭhad-vanya-srajoḍḍāmaram \|*  
*guñjā-hāra-latārdha-mañjur adhunā bhadrā-bhujāntas tathā*  
*tathyaṁ viddhi sa nāgarī-gurur abhūd govardhanasyātithiḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Do not be distressed. I saw Śaibya, and her forehead was marked with mineral patterns. I saw Śyāmalā’s hair excellently decorated with quivering forest garlands. Bhadrā’s shoulders are attractive with** ***guñja*** **necklaces. Know truly that the guru of cunning women was the guest of Govardhana.**

*(****nepathye****):*  
*kṛtvā vaṁśīm akhila-jagatī-gīta-saṅgīta-bhaṅgī-*  
*sāṅgī-bhāva-prathama-vasatiṁ saṅginīṁ vāma-pāṇau \|*  
*eṣa premṇā vrajati nayanānandano nanda-sūnur*  
*mandaṁ govardhana-śikhariṇaḥ kandarā-mandirāya \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage, Vṛndā speaks.)**

**Playing the flute, the chief abode of all types of music, manifesting as song in all the worlds, while holding it in his left hand, the son of Nanda, with blissful eyes and filled with prema, walks slowly to a room in a cave on top of Govardhana.**

***nāndīmukhī:*** *pa{u}me, tumaṁ imiṇā buttanteṇa candāaliaṁ suhābehi \| ahaṁ subalaṁ aṇusarissam \| (iti niśkrāntā):*

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā! You should make Candrāvalī happy by giving this news. I am going to find Subala.**

***padmā*** *(punaḥ paśyanti): esā karālāe ejjiāe cittaṁ aṇubaṭṭantī baṇa-deadā bundā candāaliaṁ sacchalaṁ ṇivāredi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking again) This Vṛndā, imitating the mood of old Karālā, obstructs Candrāvalī using tricks.**

*(****nepathye****):*  
*kiṁ rādheva durantam icchasi balād unmādam ālambituṁ*  
*mugdhe mānaya mānanīya-jaratī-vākyaṁ bahir mā vraja \|*  
*eṣa smera-vilocanañcala-rucā cāpalyam ullāsayann*  
*āyāti vraja-sundarī-gaṇa-mano-māṇikya-hārī hariḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) Like Rādhā, why do you passionately desire to embrace that insane, wretched person? O fool! Respect the words of the elder women, and do not go outside. Producing unsteadiness with the beauty of his smiling side glance, he steals the rubies of the gopīs’ minds.**

*(praviśya)* ***candrāvalī*** *(sautsukyaṁ samantād avalokya): kadhaṁ bundāe aliaṁ bia vyāharīadi\| kudo ettha kahṇo? (iti khedaṁ nāṭayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, looking everywhere with great zeal) Why does Vṛndā tell lies? Where is Kṛṣṇa? (She laments.)**

***padmā*** *(upasṛtya, saṁskṛtena):*  
*na santāpaṁ svāntād davayasi kathaṁ dāva-viśamaṁ*  
*ghana-svāśaiḥ kiṁ vā malinayasi bimbādharam api \|*  
*vanāntān kekābhiḥ sakhi śikhari-kakṣe mukharayan*  
*sakhī-sthalyāḥ kalyāṇy abhajad upaśalyaṁ yadu-patiḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Padmā approaches her.) Why do you not drive the formidable forest fire of pain from your heart? Why do you make your red lips wither with heavy sighing? O friend, O auspicious girl, Kṛṣṇa, who makes the forests resound with peacock cries, has arrived near Sakhīsthala on the slope of Govardhana.**

***candrāvalī*** *(vilokya) kadhaṁ pia-sahī pa{u}mā? (iti gādham āliṅgya) abi ṇāma akkhalidaṁ bhaṇidāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing her) How is my dear friend Padmā? (Embracing her tightly) Have you spoken truthfully?**

***padmā:*** *adha iṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

*(tataḥ praviśati subalenānugamyamānaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa enters, followed by Subala.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *paśya paśya—*  
*akalita-tāpas taraṇer*  
*asta-śiro-vīthibhis tirodhānāt \|*  
*asphuṭa-timira-vijṛmbhaḥ*  
*prathayati toṣaṁ niśārambhaḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look! Because the sun sets by the paths of the western mountain peak, the heat of the sun has dissipated. The beginning of night, with the appearance of slight darkness, produces satisfaction.**

***subalaḥ:*** *baassa, ajja go-dohaṇaṁ bi aṇavekkhia salālaso bia kiṁtti ettha laddhosi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, today, ignoring milking the cows but showing eagerness, what will you gain here?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, mayūraṁ varṇayatā kenacit priyāṁ candrāvalīṁ smārito’smi tatas tad-vilokanāya lālaseyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, someone has described a peacock. That made me remember dear Candrāvalī. Thus I am eager to see her.**

***subalaḥ:*** *kerisaṁ mora-vaṇṇaṇam?*

**Перевод:**

**How was the peacock described?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*unmadena purataḥ śikhaṇḍinā*  
*tāṇḍave pṛthuni maṇḍalī-kṛtām \|*  
*paśya nindita-mahendra-kārmukāṁ*  
*kṛṣṇa-candra cala-candrakāvalīm \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Someone said, “O Kṛṣṇa, like the moon! Look in front at the spread tail of the peacock, deriding the rainbow, a mass of moving feathers (*****candrakāvalīm*****) as it dances everywhere madly.”**

***subalaḥ:*** *tado āaḍḍhaṇaṁ baṁsī-kalaṁ ullāsehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then attractively play your flute.**

*(kṛṣṇo vaktre veṇuṁ vinyasyati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(He puts the flute to his mouth.)**

***candrāvalī*** *(niśamya sa-ghūrṇam): sabbadā suṇṇaṁtī bi assudaarī bia bihmābedi dummuhī muralī \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Hearing with confusion) On being heard, the evil-mouthed flute always produces astonishment--as if never heard before.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe subala, adya candrāvalī-prasāde tvayā mamānukūlena bhavitavyam\|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Subala, today, supporting me, you should make Candrāvalī show me mercy.**

***subalaḥ:*** *adha iṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

***padmā:*** *halā, pekkha \| eso veṇu-saṇṇāe tumaṁ tuvarāvedi goulenda-ṇandaṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, look! With a message from his flute, the prince of Gokula makes you hasten to him.**

***candrāvalī*** *(vilokya saṁskṛtena):*  
*sakhi murali viśāla-cchidra-purṇā*  
*laghur atikaṭhinā tvaṁ granthilā nīrasāsi \|*  
*tad api bhajasi śaśvac cumbanānanda-sāndraṁ*  
*hari-kara-parirambhaṁ kena puṇyodayena \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend the flute! You are full of holes, small, very hard, full of knots and without sap. By what pious acts are you always deeply, blissfully kissed and embraced by his hands?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(puro dṛṣṭvā, sānandam) sakhe, seyaṁ mama locanendīvara-candrikā candrāvalī \| (iti sādaram upetya) priye,*  
*candras tava mukha-bimbaṁ*  
*candrā nakharāṇi kuṇḍale candrau \|*  
*nava-candras tu lalāṭaṁ*  
*satyaṁ candrāvali tvam asi \|\|8\|\|*  
*(candrāvalī lajjate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking front with bliss) O friend, there is Candrāvalī, the moon for my lotus eyes. (He approaches respectfully.) O beloved, your face is a moon, your finger nails are moons, your earrings are moons, and your forehead is a new moon. Truly, you are a host of moons. (Candrāvalī becomes embarrassed.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, duṣṭa-dānava-damanābhiniveśāt tvan-mukha-candram aprekṣamāṇasya yātayāmā bhavantyo’pi na yāta-yāmā bhavanti mamāmūr yāminyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, though I have let time pass without seeing your moon-like face--since I have been absorbed in killing wicked demons-- still my nights are not wasted.**

***candrāvalī:*** *sundara, bhamarassa bia ṇava-ṇavāṇusāriṇī de pa{i}dī kadhaṁ cirāsaṅga-ṇīrasāsu pa{u}miṇīsu ahiramadu \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful Kṛṣṇa! Your nature is to seek newer and newer flowers like the bee. How can you enjoy lotuses devoid of nectar for a long time?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye candrāvali! pratipad-āloke tvaṁ sarveṣāṁ nava-navāsi \| tad adya nirvāpaya virahottāpaṁ pariśvaṅga-rasena \|*

**Перевод:**

**O dear Candrāvalī! Each time I see you, you are newer than anything else. Now extinguish the pain of separation by the rasa of an embrace.**

***padmā:*** *pia-sahī-biraheṇa kudo tuhma tābuppatti?*

**Перевод:**

**Why do you experience pain by separation from my dear friend?**

***subalaḥ:*** *a{i}, mā kkhu ebbaṁ bhaṇa \| eso candāali-biraheṇa saṁtatto sīdalāe jala-dhārāe kacche dehaṁ ṇikkhibia satiṇho caoro bia ṇaṁ jjebba candāaliaṁ sabbado paccha{i} baasso\|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! Do not speak in this way. Pained by separation from Candrāvalī, he throws his body in a cooling cloud. Like a** ***cakora*** **bird which throws itself to a cool cloud, my friend looks at Candrāvalī and nothing else.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, śrūyatām—*  
*vipināntare milantī*  
*madhura-rasā śitala-sparśā \|*  
*amṛta-mayī tvad-virahe*  
*samajani mama tāpa-nuttaye rādhā \|\|9\|\|*  
*(iti sa-sambhramam) dhārā dhārā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, please listen. In the forest, during separation from you, I experienced what was cooling to the touch, full of sweet rasa, and full of nectar. It combined to destroy my suffering. It was Rādhā (in fright)-- I mean it was a stream of water (*****dhārā*****), that relieved me of pain.**

***candrāvalī*** *(sābhyasūyam): gacchehi, rāhiaṁ jjebba sebehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With anger) Go away and serve Rādhā!**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, dhārety avadam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, I said** ***dhārā,*** **a stream, not Rādhā.**

***candrāvalī:*** *jādaṁ kadhaṁ doṇṇaṁ baṇṇāṇaṁ bibarīdattaṇaṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**Why did we hear you say the syllables reversed into the name Rādhā?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, dvayor varṇayoḥ karṇayor vā viparītatvam ity asminn asti vicāraḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, I think that you heard the syllables reversed, or your ears were reversed.**

***candrāvalī*** *(roṣāruṇaṁ mukham ānamayya): a{i} dāna-sauṇḍa \| alam edāe abahitthāe \| ajja appaṇo maṇa-hāriṇo subaṇṇa-jualassa biṇṇāsādo sāhu-māhurī-purida-kaṇṇahmi kidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Bowing her head, with red face) O generous Kṛṣṇa! Stop hiding your feelings. Now my ears are filled with great sweetness because I am wearing golden earrings which attract the mind.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*yathārtheyaṁ vāṇī tava cakita-sāraṅga-nayane*  
*suvarṇālaṅkāro madhurayati yat te śruti-yugam \|*  
*mukhendor antas te bahir api suvarṇa-cyutir iyaṁ*  
*mama śrotra-dvandvaṁ nayana-yugalaṁ cākulayati \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Candrāvalī, with eyes of a frightened doe! A golden ornament makes your two ears beautiful. The excellent syllables emanating externally from the middle of your moon-like mouth agitate my two ears and the gold extending to your cheeks agitates my eyes.**

***padmā:*** *halā appaṇo adiṭṭhaṁ sumaranti mā khijjehi \| jutto rāhāṇurattassa imassa rāhā-ṇāma-mayi saṅkadhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, remembering your fate, do not lament. It is quite proper that this person who is attracted to Rādhā utters the name of Rādhā.**

***candrāvalī*** *(niśvasya): sahi pa{u}me, evam ṇṇedam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Sighing) My friend Padmā, so it is.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, bāḍham anāśaṅkanīyam evedam \| yataḥ—*  
*tasya ṣoḍaśa-kalasya ṣoḍaśī*  
*vallabhā sphurati yā nabhas-tale \|*  
*rādhayā suvadane kathaṁ tayā*  
*saṅgatir bhuvi mamādya saṁbhavet \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, you should not worry about this. O Candrāvalī with beautiful face! On this earth, how can I meet Rādhā, the dear sixteenth constellation Viśākhā, belonging to the moon which appears in the sky?**

***padmā:*** *ca{u}ssaṭṭhi-kalā-sāliṇo de ṇa kkhu sā bi solaha-kala-ballahā dullahā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā, dear to the moon with sixteen phases, is not hard to attain for you, since you are endowed with sixty-four arts.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-praśrayaṁ avalokya):*  
*candrāvalī-vadana-puṣkara-saṅgi-gaṇḍa-*  
*candrāv alīkatara-tarka-kalaṅkitāṅgau \|*  
*śaṅkākulo’tra kalayan kamalāyatākṣi*  
*saṁ kāku-lola-hṛdayaḥ praviśāmi nāham \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at her with respect) Seeing the moon-like cheeks on the lotus face of Candrāvalī, which are blemished by false arguments, I do not attain happiness, since I am overcome with worry and my heart is agitated with misery.**

***candrāvalī*** *(sa-vyāja-prasādam): deo, ṇaṁ kkhu goula-jaṇa-ji-aṇa-bhudassa de sabba-suha-kāridā-guṇaṁ kā kkhu hada-buddhiā ṇa sahadi? tā ṇipphaleṇa saṅkoeṇa sādaṅko hohi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Pretending to be pleased) What girl whose intelligence has been destroyed does not resist your qualities which give bliss, since you are the life of all Gokula? Do not be afflicted with useless reserve.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *gariṣṭhām api manyu-mudrāṁ dhīreyaṁ mukha-mādhuryeṇa nihnute \| (prakāśam) priye kṛtam anena gaurava-viśodgāreṇa \| roṣokti-mādhvīkam eva varaṁ variṣṭham \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) This serious girl hides her intense anger with sweet words. (Aloud) O beloved, enough of spouting the poison of serious words! It is much better to use sweet words in anger.**

***candrāvalī:*** *goulāṇanda, tuhma purado muhaṁ daṁseduṁ ṇa pahavāmi \| jaṁ pragabbhaṁ bāharaṁti abaraddhammi \| tā gharaṁ gamissam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O bliss of Gokula, I cannot show my face in front of you since, showing boldness, I have offended you. I will go home.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānunayam): priye, prasīda prasīda \| baddho’yam añjaliḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Gently) O beloved, please be happy. I pray to you.**

***candrāvalī:*** *suhaa, ujjuaṁ bbāharaṁtīṁ kīsa māṁ aliaṁ saṅkasi? tā aṇujāṇehi maṁ bhadda-ālī-daṁsaṇassa \| (iti padmayā saha niśkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O fortunate Kṛṣṇa, why do you fear that I am deceptive, when I am acting truthfully? Please permit me to see Bhadra-kālī. (She exits with Padmā.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, mahānubhāvām etāṁ mac-citta-mahākāśa-candrāvalīm api baliyas tamaḥ-kandalībhir avaskanditām ālokya nirāloko’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend, seeing great Candrāvalī situated in the sky of my heart now covered by great darkness of Rāhu, I have become blind.**

***subalaḥ:*** *pia-baassa, kiṁti ebbaṁ bhaṇāsi? sā kkhu adakkhiṇā ṇa diṭṭha \|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear friend, why do you speak like this? I have never seen her in an uncooperative mood.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, bāḍhaṁ durūhā mahīyasāṁ prakṛtiḥ \| tathedānīm—*  
*nyaviśata nayanānte kāpi sāralya-niṣṭhā*  
*vacasi ca vinayena stotra-bhaṅgī nyavātsīt \|*  
*ajani ca mayi bhūyān sambhramas tena tasyā*  
*vyavṛṇuta hṛdi manyuṁ susṭhu dākṣiṇyam eva \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, the nature of great souls is difficult to understand. At this time, great sincerity resides in the edge of her eye. Praises with humility reside in her words. Her great respect for me completely expertly covers the anger in her heart.**

*tad ehi manohāriṇi, tasmin keśara-kuñje niviśya candrāvalī-saṅgamopāyam aṅgīkaromi \| (iti parikramya) sakhe, seyaṁ bakulāvali-mañjulā nikuñja-vīthī \| paśya paśya \|*  
*sphurati saro dakṣiṇataḥ*  
*savye vāpī samantataḥ kulyā \|*  
*iti keśarāṭavīyaṁ*  
*pramadaṁ nīrādhikā kurute \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Come. Entering this attractive** ***keśara (bakula)*** **grove, I will think of a method of meeting Candrāvalī. (Walking) O friend, the path to the bower is attractive with** ***bakula*** **groves. Look, look! On the right there is a lake. On the left there are artificial ponds. This** ***bakula*** **grove produces bliss with plentiful water ((*****nīrādhikā*****)).**

***subalaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *laddho mae osaro \| (prakāśam) baassa, sa-rāhiā jjebba tuha pamadaṁ kuṇa{i} kiṁtti ṇirāhiā tti bhaṇāsi?*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) I now have the opportunity to give a different meaning. I will make him remember Rādhā. (Aloud) O friend, Rādhikā gives you bliss. Why do you say that without Rādhā (*****nīrādhikā*****) you get bliss?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(subalam āliṅgya): sakhe, satyaṁ bravīṣi \| tad adya rādhikā yathemāṁ keśara-nikuñja-lakṣmīm alaṅkaroti tathā mad-girā saṇdiśyatāṁ lalitā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Embracing Subala) O friend, you speak the truth. Lalitā should be instructed by these words so that today Rādhikā will decorate the beauty of this** ***bakula*** **bower.**

***subalaḥ:*** *jaṁ āṇabedi pia-baassa \| (iti niśkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**I will do as my dear friend says. (Exits)**

*(tataḥ praviśati padmā madhumaṅgalaś ca \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Padmā and Madhumaṅgala enter.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *pa{u}me, mae ajja vaasseṇa cāḍu-āriṇā aṇunidā bi candāvali ṇa pasaṇṇa*

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā, Candrāvalī was not pleased by my friend though he spoke many pacifying words.**

***padmā:*** *adha iṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ṇaṁ baasso bi bisaṇṇa baṭṭa{i} \| tā juttā doṇaṁ saṅgame ahmāṇaṁ saha-āridā \|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend is very sad. It is proper that we help the two to meet.**

***padmā:*** *ajja, ado jjebba mae aṇusaridosi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected friend, I agree with you.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(puro dṛṣṭvā): pa{u}me, pekkha eso pia-baasso chappada-metta-sahāo kesara-kuḍuṅge kiṁpi mantedi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking in front) O Padmā, look! Is that my dear friend taking counsel of only the bees in the** ***bakula*** **bower?**

***padmā:*** *ajja, ladā-jālehiṁ antaridā bhavia suṇahma kiṁ eso bhaṇādi tti \| (iti tathā sthitau \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O respected friend, let us hide behind these creepers and hear what he says. (They go behind the creepers.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(rādhāṁ smaran sotkaṇṭham):*  
*prasarati yad bhrū-cāpe*  
*ślathajyam akarot smaro dhanuḥ pauṣpam \|*  
*mādhurīma-maṇi-mañjuṣā*  
*bhuṣāyai me priyā sāstu \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Remembering Rādhā with longing) May she, a jewel box of sweentess, be my ornament. When she raises the bow of her brow, Cupid slackens his flower bow.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *pa{u}me, eso ukkaṇthāe tujjha pia-sahīṁ ccea baṇṇedi \| tā ehi \| turidaṁ gadua ṇaṁ samāṇehma \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā, he describes your dear friend with great longing. Come, let us quickly go and bring her.**

***padmā:*** *ajja, suṭṭhu ṇiṭṭhaṅkidaṁ suṇahma jaṁ bahu-vallaho eso \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected friend, let us hear fully what he says, since he has many lovers.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(punaḥ sautsukyam)*  
*sā mukha-suṣamā nirjita-*  
*rākā-candrāvalī lasan-madhyā \|(ity ardhokte)*

**Перевод:**

**(Again with longing) The beauty of her face defeats a host of full moons. Her waist is beautiful with folds.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *pa{u}me, alaṁ idaṁ imādo pareṇa sudeṇa tuṇṇaṁ gacchahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā, enough of hearing more. Let us quickly go.**

***padmā:*** *juttaṁ kadhesi\| (ity ubhau javena dūraṁ parikrāmataḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**You speak correctly. (The two begin to walk quickly far off.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*muhur ādhāsyati rādhā*  
*mad-urasi rasikā ātmānam \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Will Rādhā, full of rasa, place herself on my chest?**

***padmā:*** *ajja, ebbaṁ bhaṇāmi \| māṇiṇie pia-sahie saaṁ samāameṇa lāhavaṁ hodi \| tā parāvaṭṭia kahṇaṁ biṇṇabehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected friend, I say this. On meeting, my dear friend will treat him insignificantly in anger. Go and inform Kṛṣṇa of this.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *sohaṇaṁ mantesi \| (iti kṛṣṇāntikam āsādya) pia-baassa, pacchaṇṇeṇa bhavia sabbaṁ de āaṇṇidaṁ mae ukkhaṇṭhābaaṇaṁ \| tā āṇabehi \| taṁ jjebba tujjha ballahaṁ turiaṁ samāṇemi \|*

**Перевод:**

**You give good advice. (Approaching Kṛṣṇa) Dear friend, hiding I heard all your words of longing. Order me. I will quickly bring your beloved.**

**Перевод:**

**(Praising him, Kṛṣṇa embraces him) O friend, with my mercy, please bring her quickly.**

*(madhumaṅgalaḥ parikramya padmayā saha niśkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Madhumaṅgla goes to Padmā and they exit.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *aho paramotkaṇṭhānāṁ premṇām utkaṇṭhā-kāritvam \|*  
*bhramare’pi guñjati nikuñja-koṭare*  
*manute manas tu maṇi-nūpura-dhvanim \|*  
*anilena cañcati tṛṇāñcale’pi tām*  
*purataḥ priyām upagatāṁ viśaṅkate \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Love causes longing in those who are in love. When a bee hums within a bower, my mind thinks it is the sound of her jeweled anklets. When the tips of grass move in the breeze, my mind thinks that my beloved has arrived.**

*(tataḥ praviśati padmā-madhumaṅgalābhyāṁ saṅgatā candrāvalī \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Candrālaī enters with Padmā and Madhumaṅgala.)**

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā, do I see the** ***bakula*** **bower?**

***padmā:*** *adha iṁ \| tā tuṇṇaṁ ehi \| (iti parikrāmati)*

**Yes. Go there quickly. (Walks)**

**Перевод:**

**(Hearing ankle bells) Ah! I am very confused by the humming of the bees. Enough of thinking she has come. (Becomes agitated)**

*puraḥ phalāyām āśāyāṁ*  
*janaḥ kāmaṁ viḍambyate \|*  
*āsanne hi ghanārambhe*  
*dvi-guṇaṁ ranti cātakaḥ \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When desire’s fruit is close at hand, man surpasses desire. The** ***cātaka*** **bird cries twice as loud when clouds approach.**

*(punar utkarṇo bhavan) katham abhyarṇe bhūṣaṇa-śiñjitaṁ śrūyate? (ity udgrīvikām dattvā sa-sambhramam) satyam asau militā me preyasī \| (iti tarasā candrāvalī-pārśvam āgatya)*

**Перевод:**

**(Again listening) Do I hear ornaments jingling nearby? (He stretches his neck, in excitement) Ye it is true. My beloved has come. (He goes quickly to Candrāvalī.)**

*hṛd-bhṛṅga-jaṅgama-latā*  
*maṅgala-bhā rādhikā mayonmuditā \| (ity ardhokte)*

**Перевод:**

**The moving creeper for the bee of the heart, Rādhā, with auspicious beauty is exceedingly joyful by attaining me.**

*(candrāvalī serṣyaṁ madhumaṅgalam ālokate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Candrāvalī looks angrily at Madhumaṅgala.)**

**Перевод:**

**My friend Candrāvalī, my dear friend describes you, saying you are filled with exceeding auspiciousness.**

*suhṛd-anurāga-vitandrā*  
*candrāvalir añjasālambhi \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself with astonishment) Why did he bring Candrāvalī? Anyway, I will continue with the** ***brāhmaṇa’s*** **interpretation. (Aloud) With active love for her friend, Candrāvalī has come quickly.**

*(candrāvalī sa-lajjaṁ kṛṣṇa-kaṇṭhe vaijayantīṁ vinyasyati \|)*

**(Candrāvalī in shame places a** ***vaijayanti*** **garland on Kṛṣṇa’s neck.)**

*ekaṁ prayāti paricarya cakora-rājī*  
*candraṁ priye nija-manoratha-pūra-pūrtim \|*  
*candrāvalī kim u mamākṣi-cakorayos tvaṁ*  
*prītiṁ dvayor api na dhāsyasi sevyamānā \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Flocks of** ***cakora*** **birds are fully satisfied by serving one moon. Candrāvalī, a host of moons, being properly served, will you not give love to the two** ***cakoras*** **of my eyes.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-garvam): bho baassa, diṭṭā tue majjha-vilakkhaṇā biakkhaṇadā, jo kkhu ananta-guṇa-sāliṇābi tue moāiduṁ ṇa pārido so pia-sahie māṇa-gaṇṭhi ṇaa-guṇa-dhāriṇā mae moābido \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Proudly) O friend, you have seen my extraordinary intelligence. The knot of her pride which you could not undo with your unlimited qualities I have undone with my nine stranded** ***brāhmaṇa*** **thread.**

**Перевод:**

**O friend, you are the great minister of war and peace, among the six qualities for Cupid’s pastimes.**

***padmā:*** *ajja, puro paphullāiṁ malli-pupphāiṁ papphuranti \| tā ehi, imāni geṇhahma \|(ity ubhau niśkrāntau \|)*

**Перевод:**

**Respected friend, in front there are some blossoming jasmines. Come, let us pick them.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *kuñje’sminn āgata-mātrāṁ rādhāṁ tarkayāmi \| tad anyataḥ prasthāsye \| (prakāśam) priye, purastān nātidūre nāgara-raṅgocitā nāga-kesarāṭavī \| tad atraivānusarāvaḥ \| (iti niśkrāntau \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) I suspect that Rādhā had just arrived in this bower. I will go elsewhere. (Aloud) O beloved Candrāvalī, not far away there is a grove of** ***nāga-keśaras*** **suitable for pastimes of lovers. Let us go there.**

*(tataḥ praviśati lalitayā saha saṅkathayantī rādhā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā enters, discussing with Lalitā.)**

***rādhā:*** *halā, pekkha pekkha \| anda-ārehiṁ gholidaṁ sabbaṁ disā-muham \|*

**Перевод:**

**O sakhī look, look! All directions have become frightening because of darkness.**

***lalitā:*** *pia-sahi, timirāhisārocidehiṁ sāmala-ppasāhaṇehiṁ maṇḍido kiṁ kkhu tue appā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear friend, are you decorated with dark clothing suitable for meeting in the dark?**

***rādhikā:*** *adha iṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

***lalitā*** *(vilokya sasmitam saṁskṛtena):*  
*dhammillopari nīla-ratna-racito hāras tvayā ropito*  
*vinyastaḥ kuca-kumbhayoḥ kuvalaya-śreṇi-kṛto garbhagaḥ \|*  
*aṅge kalpitam añjanaṁ vinihitā kastūrikā netrayoḥ*  
*kaṁsārer abhisāra-sambhrama-bharān manye jagad-vismṛtam \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looks at Rādhā with a smile) You have placed the sapphire necklace in your braided hair,the lotus flower hair decoration on your full breasts,mascara for your eyes on your arms and legs,and musk perfume for the body around your eyes. In great haste to meet Kṛṣṇa, you have forgotten everything.**

***rādhikā:*** *halā, muñcehi parihāsaṁ \| turiaṁ uddesehi kesara-kuṇḍaga-maggam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O sakhī, stop joking. Quickly show the path to the** ***bakula*** **bower.**

***lalitā:*** *ido ido pia-sahi \| (iti parikrāmantī sa-śaṅkam saṁskṛtena)*  
*timira-masibhiḥ saṁvītāṅgyaḥ kadamba-vanāntare*  
*sakhi mura-ripuṁ puṇyātmānaḥ saranty abhisārikāḥ \|*  
*tava tu parito vidyud-varṇās tanu-dyuti-sūcayo*  
*hari hari ghana-dhvāntāny etāḥ sva-vairiṇī bhindate \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Here, dear friend. (She proceeds with worry.) Friend, covering their limbs with dark ointment, many girls with pious credits go to meet Kṛṣṇa in the** ***kadamba*** **forest. Alas, the lightning splendor of your limbs, appearing through the cloth and dispelling the deep darkness, has become your enemy.**

***rādhikā:*** *alaṁ imiṇā ubālambheṇa \| pekkha paccāsaṇṇo baula-kuṇḍago \| (iti saṁbhramād upasṛtya sa-parāmarśam, saṁskṛtena)*  
*vidūrān na ghrāṇaṁ madayati murāreḥ parimalo*  
*na kuñjo’yaṁ tasya sphurati nakhara-dyota-nikaraiḥ \|*  
*tataḥ śaṅke kasminn api rahasi vallī-valayite*  
*parihāsākāṅkṣī priya-sakhi nilīnas tava sakhā \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Stop scolding me. Look, the** ***bakula*** **bower is in front. She approaches in fear, and considers.) His fragrance does not cause madness from afar. The bower does not shine with light from his nails. Dear friend, I fear that your friend, wanting to joke, is hiding secretly in some enclosed creepers.**

***lalitā:*** *halā, ehi \| bāmado kadamba-kuṇḍagaṁ biiṇuhma \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, come. We will search the** ***kadamba*** **grove on the left.**

***rādhikā*** *(tathā kurvatī): a{i} cha{i}lla, diṭṭhosi diṭṭhosi, kīsa aṅgehiṁ aṅgāiṁ saṅgobesi \| (iti samantān mṛgayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(She goes left.) Clever boy, I see you, I see you. Why do you hide your body behind the tree branches? (She searches everywhere.)**

***lalitā:*** *sahi, muñca maggaṇa-ggaham \| ehi keli-kuṇḍaga-kappaṇaṁ kuṇahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Give up this eagerness to search. Come, let us make a pastime bower.**

***rādhikā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*racaya bakula-puṣpais toraṇaṁ keli-kuñje*  
*kuru varam aravindais talpam indīvarākṣi \|*  
*upanaya śayanāntaṁ sādhu mādhvīka-pātraṁ*  
*sahacari harir adya ślāghatāṁ kauśalaṁ te \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Make an arch of** ***bakula*** **flowers in the pastime bower. Make an excellent bed from lotuses, O lotus-eyed friend! Place a cup of honey liquor artfully at the edge of the bed. May Kṛṣṇa praise your skill today, O assistant!**

***lalitā*** *(tathā kṛtvā): halā, pekkha \| kahṇo vilambedi \| tā kuñjaṁ pavisia ṇaṁ paḍibālehma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Doing as Rādhā instructed) O my friend, look! Kṛṣṇa is late. Entering that bower, let us wait for him.**

***rādhikā*** *(parikramya udvegaṁ nāṭayantī, saṁskṛtena):*  
*ruddhaḥ kvāpi sakhī-hitārtha-parayā śaṅke hariḥ padmayā*  
*prāptaḥ kuñja-gṛham yad eṣa na tamī-yāme’py atikrāmati \|*  
*paulomi-rati-bandhu-diṇ-mukham āsan hā hanta saṇtarpayann*  
*unmilaty abhisāra-lubdha-ramaṇī-gotrasya śatruḥ śaśī \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Going, she speaks with agitation) I fear Kṛṣṇa, having come to the bower, has been stopped by Padmā, dedicated to helping Candrāvalī, since he does not neglect me even in the dark period on the fourth lunar day. The moon, the enemy of women going for meetings, dwelling in the east and satisfying it, now appears.**

*(ity ubhe niśkrānte \| tataḥ praviśati kṛṣṇaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Both exit and Kṛṣna enters.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(samantād avalokya)—*  
*āsaṅgaḥ kumudākareṣu śithilo bhṛṅgāvalīnām abhūd*  
*vīkṣante nija-koṭarāṅkitam amī kṣauṇīruhaṁ kauśikāḥ \|*  
*saṅkoconmukhatāṁ prayāti śanakair auttānapāder dyutiḥ*  
*kiṁ bhānur nanu pūrva-parvata-taṭīm āroḍhum utkaṇṭhate \|\|26\|\|*

*(iti parikramya) na jāne navīna-vipralambhena sambhṛta-nirbhara-saṁrambhā kiṁ nāma pratipatsyate’dya rādhā \| (vimṛśya) bhavatu \| keśareṇa nāga-keśaraṁ pratipādayiṣye \| tad amūni nāga-keśarāṇi vicinomi \| (iti tathā kṛtvā puro’nusarpan)*

*kapaṭī sa latā-kuṭīm imāṁ*  
*sakhi nāgād adhunāpi mādhavaḥ \|*  
*iti jalpa-parītayā tayā*  
*klama-dīrghā gamitā kathaṁ tamī \|\|27\|\|*  
*(parikramya bakula-kuñjaṁ paśyan sa-viśādam)*  
*tambūlaṁ ghanasāra-saṁskṛtam adaḥ kṣiptaṁ puro rādhayā*  
*hārī hanta hariṇmaṇi-stavakito hāro’yam utsāritaḥ \|*  
*pauṣpī ceyam udāre saurabha-mayī cūḍā nakhaiḥ khaṇḍitā*  
*tasyāḥ śaṁsati vipralambha-janitaṁ kuñjo’yam antaḥ-klamam \|\|28\|\|*  
*(ity agrato gatvā) iyam eva rādhāyaḥ sūryārādhana-vedikā \| tad asyāḥ pārśvam āsādayāmi \| (iti parikrāmati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking all around) The bees have lost their attachment to the multitude of lotuses. The owls look for the trees with their nest holes. The light of the pole star is gradually fading. Is the sun eager to rise on the eastern mountain? (Walking) I do not know if Rādhā will be extremely angry today because of fresh separation. (Reflecting) Anyway, I will come to the** ***nāga-keśara*** **described by Subala as** ***keśara.*** **I will pick the flowers. (Picking flowers, he comes in front.)**

**She will say, “The liar has not come even now to this creeper blower.” How can she pass the night with such continuous fatigue? (Walking, he sees the** ***bakula*** **grove and speaks sadly.) Rādhā has thrown down the** ***tambūla*** **mixed with camphor over here. Ah! She has taken off her necklace strung with emeralds. She has frantically torn apart her fragrant crown of flowers with her nails. The bower proclaims her internal exhaustion arising from separation. (Going forward) This is the altar Rādhā uses for worshipping the sun. I shall approach its side. (He approaches.)**

*(tataḥ praviśati sakhībhyām anugamyamānā rādhā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā enters along with her two sakhīs.)**

***rādhā*** *(puro vilokya): halā lalide, pekkha beiā-ṇediṭṭho so tujjha cha\[i\]llo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking in front) O Lalitā, look! Your clever boy is there beside the altar.**

***lalitā:*** *sahi, kañcaṇa-paḍimebba kaṭhorā hohi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, become stiff like a golden statue.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *purastād eṣā saha-parivārā priyā \| tad idam uṭṭaṅkayāmi \| (ity upasṛtya) lalite, sādhu sādhu \| dṛṣṭaṁ tava gariṣṭham atra durmantra-tantra-caryāyām ācāryatvam \| yad adya bhavatyā keśara-nikuñja-vedyām aham ujjāgara-vrata-dīkṣāṁ parigrāhito’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Before me is my beloved with her friend. I will find out about her. (Approaching) O Lalitā, very good, very good! I have seen here that you are a teacher of worship involving black magic with malicious mantras since today you have made me take a vow of wakefulness at the altar in the** ***bakula*** **grove.**

***lalitā*** *(sa-sāmbhramam saṁskṛtena): aho vaiparityam, aho vaiparityam \|*  
*keśara-nikuñja-kuhare*  
*kuhaka vasantī sakhī tvayā rahite \|*  
*śrita-nava-pallava-talpā*  
*truṭim api kalpādhikāṁ mene \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) Just the opposite, just the opposite! O cheater! Without you, staying within the** ***bakula*** **bower on a bed of new shoots, my friend considers a moment to be a** ***kalpa.***

***Kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(kapaṭenāṭopaṁnāṭayan): aho, dambha-bharārambheṣu gāmbhiryam asyāḥ \| (iti nāga-kesarāṇy uddhāṭya darśayan)*  
*aratiṁmama niśi paśyan*  
*na klāmyan nāga-keśaro’py asakṛt \|*  
*vigalan-madhubhiḥ kusumair*  
*ebhir netrair ivodasraiḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Showing false pride) Ah! She shows deep deceit. (Pointing to the** ***nāga-keśara*** **trees) Seeing my pain in the night, even the** ***nāga-keśara*** **trees have become completely exhausted with tears from their eyes in the form of honey dripping from the flowers.**

***lalitā:*** *ammahe, dhuttataṇaṁ, jaṁ ba\[u\]la-bāiṇā kesareṇa dāṇiṁ nāa-kesaro vikkhāvīadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**What lies! He uses the word** ***nāga-keśara*** **to indicate the** ***bakula.***

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-vyāja-nirvedam): lalite, viśrāmyatu taveyaṁ śabdārthasyānyathā-kalpanena vañcana-cañcutā \| athavā kas te dośaḥ? dṛṣṭa-doṣābhir api gaurāṅgībhiḥ sauhārdyam abhilaṣyatā mayaivāparaddham \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Pretending indifference) O Lalitā, stop accusing me of cheating by playing with the meanings of words. What is your fault? My only fault is desiring friendship with golden gopīs who find fault in me.**

***viśākhā:*** *ko kkhu gauraṅgīṇaṁ diṭṭho tue doso?*

**Перевод:**

**What fault do you see in the gopīs?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *paśya paśya—*  
*nava-rasa-dhāriṇi madhure*  
*dharaṇī-saṇtāpa-hāri-visphuraṇe \|*  
*vidadhati na kṛṣṇa-mudire*  
*gauryaḥ kṣaṇa-rociṣaḥ sthairyam \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look, look! The temporary golden lightning does not remain steady in the black cloud sweet with showers of fresh water, eager to destroy the miseries of the earth.**

***viśākhā:*** *tasmiṁ kulisa-kuḍa-kaṭhora-ceṭṭhide tāṇaṁ komalāṇaṁ juttā jjebba tadhā pa{u}tti\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is proper that soft gopīs act in this way towards someone hard as a thunderbolt.**

***lalitā:*** *bisāhe, suṇāhi kaṁpi gāham \| (iti bhṛṅgaṁ darśayanti \|)*  
*campaa-ladaṁ siṇiddham ṇaa-kañcaṇa-kāṁti-kusuma-gaurāṅgīṁ \|*  
*mukkia dhāba{i} bhamaro cabalā bia sāmalā hoṁti \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Viśākhā, listen to a remarkable song. (She points to a bee.) The bee flees, giving up the** ***campaka*** **creeper with fresh golden flowers. It is like black lightning.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): satyaṁ vāgminām asi rājñī \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) You are the queen of truthful speech.**

***lalitā*** *(apavārya) halā, suṭṭhu ṇīsaṅkena, vaaṇāḍobeṇa aṇabaraddhaṁ jjebba ṇaṁ takkemi*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I do not think he is free of fault since he speaks without fear and with strong pride.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*vāmyād bhaven na viratir nava-yauvanānāṁ*  
*vāma-bhruvām iti jana-śrutir avyalīkā \|*  
*cāṭūni kartum ucitāni vimucya khinnaṁ*  
*māṁ pratyutādya yad amūr aparañjayanti \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There is a truthful saying that young beautiful women never give up being uncooperative. Thus today they have made me suffer greatly, giving up use of sweet words.**

***lalitā*** *(apavārya): halā, saccaṁ ujjāara-kkhinno kahṇo \| tā pasīda \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside to Rādhā) O friend, Kṛṣṇa is really distressed from staying awake all night. Be merciful to him.**

***rādhikā*** *(kṛṣṇam apaṅgenāvalokya): muddhāṇaṁ bañcaṇa-kalā-biaddho si \|*

**Перевод:**

**You are skilful in cheating the foolish.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānandam): phulla-keśara-kalāpenāmunā dhammilla-śrīs tavālaṅkriyatām \| baddhyatāṁ mā vindatu mama prayāsaḥ \| (iti puṭikām udghāṭya) priye, paśyāmūni sugandhinām agresarāṇi keśarāṇi yair ahaṁ sadyaḥ suvāsito’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Blissfully) You should decorate the beauty of your hair with these blossoming** ***bakula*** **clusters. Tie them up. Do not let my endeavors go to waste. (Opening a case) O beloved, see these most fragrant** ***bakula*** **flowers which immediately perfume me.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-narma-smitam) ṇuṇaṁ candāali-parimaleṇa vāsidosi tumam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling to find a false fault) Indeed you are perfumed with the fragrance of Candrāvalī.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, pārihasitāny api te vacāṁsi na kadācid api vyabhicaranti \| yad adya mad-aṅgatas candrāvalī-saurabhyam udañcati \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, these joking words never disturb me since today the smell of Candrāvalī actually emanates from my limbs.**

*rādhikā (sa-smitam): samappehi pupphāiṁ \| (iti paṭāñcalaṁ praśarayati)*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Give me the flowers. (She spreads her cloth.)**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (rādhā-mukhaṁ prekṣya sva-gatam): hanta, vibhrama-maṇḍitasya cillī-kodaṇḍasya tāṇḍāva-kalā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Rādhā’s face, he says to himself) Ah, the art of dancing in the bow of her brow decorated with agitation!**

***viśākhā*** *(janāntikam): lalide, pekkha pekkha \| sammohaṇeṇa rāhīe kaḍakkha-bāṇeṇa lakkhi-kido puppha-puḍi-āe saddhaṁ añcale diṇṇaṁpi veṇuṁ na jāṇādi kahṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To Lalitā) O Lalitā, look, look! Kṛṣṇa, distracted by the arrows of Rādhā’s bewildering side glances, does not know that he has put the flute along the flower casket into her cloth.**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*nidrāgamo’pi sakhi nanda-sutasya hartuṁ*  
*yāṁ śaknuvanti na parāḥ paśu-pāla-bālāḥ \|*  
*dhanyā kaṭākṣa-kalayā kila mohayantī*  
*tāṁ rādhikādya purato muralīṁ jahāra \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Today fortunate Rādhikā, enchanting him with the movement of her glances, in front of his eyes, has stolen Kṛṣṇa’s flute, which the best cowherd boys cannot steal from him even while he sleeps.**

***rādhikā*** *(apavārya, saṁskṛtena):*  
*yā nirmāti niketa-karma-racanārambhe kara-stambhanaṁ*  
*rātrau hanta karoti karṣaṇa-vidhiṁ yā patyur aṅkād api \|*  
*gaurīṇāṁ kurute guroṛ api puro yā nīvī-vidhvaṁsanaṁ*  
*dhūrtā gokula-maṅgalasya muralī seyaṁ mamābhud vaśā \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside) Today I have got control of Kṛṣṇa’s rascal flute which paralyzes our hands when doing household chores, which attracts us out of our husbands’ laps, and which loosens the belts of the women in front of their elders.**

*(****nepathye****) are kuraṅgao, diṭṭho tuhmehiṁ pia-baasso?*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) O herd of deer, have you seen my dear friend?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kathaṁ milaty eṣa madhumaṅgalaḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**Why is Madhumaṅgla here?**

*(praviśya mālya-hastaḥ)* ***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *sudaṁ subala-muhādo jaṁ ajja ṇiuñja-majjhe rāhiā jāgaridā āsi \| tā gadua ṇaṁ pocchāha{i}ssam \| (ity upasṛtya, saṁskṛtena)*  
*avirala-vana-mālālaṅkṛta-snigdha-mūrtiḥ*  
*sphurita-kaṭaka-kāntir dhātubhir maṇḍitāṅgaḥ \|*  
*akhila-bhuvana-tuṅgo netra-bhaṅgyā vikṛṣṭaḥ*  
*katham iva sakhi rādhe kṛṣṇa-śailas tvayābhūt \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering with a garland in his hand) I have heard from Subala that today Rādhā stayed awake in the bower. I will go there and give her courage. (He approaches.) O friend Rādhā, how have you turned him into a black stone simply by attracting him with the movement of your eye though his pleasing form is decorated with many forest garlands, though he shines with golden bracelets, though his limbs are decorated with mineral colors, and though he is the chief in all the world?**

*(rādhikā smayate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā smiles.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, vetti me tamas tamī-saṁbhavaṁ vayasyo’yam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, my friend knows the grief which has arisen in the night.**

***rādhikā:*** *ajja, daṁsidaṁ ajja siṇeha-dakkhiṇaṁ jaṁ kāntāra-sindhu-santāra-kosalāiṁ sikkhābidahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected one! You have shown today skill in showing affection. By this I have learned the art of crossing the ocean of difficult paths in the forest.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *sahi, sāhu ahme ubālahijjahma, jehiṁ calaṁtīṁ bi ballīṁ tumaṁ takkia baṇe basantehiṁ sādaṅkaṁ jāaridaṁ \| tuhme kkhu salāha{i}jja{i}, jāhiṁ pia-baassa-saṇāhaṁ bi kuñjaṁ aṇibandheṇa suṇṇaa ghare pabisantīhiṁ ṇirādaṅkaṁ suttaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, it is right that you scold us. Because of us, you have stayed awake in the forest, thinking moving creepers were wild animals. You are to be praised. Understanding that the bower, though protected by my friend, is free of obstructions, you can go home and sleep peacefully.**

***rādhikā:*** *ajja, kim ebbaṁ bhaṇāsi? (iti saṁskṛtena)*  
*nikuñjaṁ kaṁsārer bata nakhara-candrāvali-ruci-*  
*cchatāgras taṁ nāgre muhur api yadā prekṣitam abhūt \|*  
*tadā sadyaḥ prodyah-vidhu-hataka-vikrānti-hatayā*  
*mayā labdhāraṇye klama-nivaha-pūrṇā pariṇatiḥ \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected one, why do you say this? When I cannot see before me the bower continually filled with a mass of light from the moons of Kṛṣṇa’s nails, I become flooded with fatigue, having come to the forest only to be struck by the cowardly attacks of the rising moon.**

***madhumaṅgalah*** *(sva-gatam):* *aho kahaṁ kuḍaṅga-saṅgadā candāali bi rāhiāe diṭṭhātthitā bañcanaṁ mukkia ṇaṁ ukkarisa{i}ssaṁ \|(prakāśaṁ saṁskṛtena)*  
*klāntena te vadana-candram anakalayya*  
*kalyāṇi gokula-purandara-nandanena \|*  
*candrāvalī...*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Ah, how did Rādhā see Candrāvalī in the bower? Giving up deception I will show that she is superior to Candrāvalī. (Aloud) O auspicious girl, not seeing the moon of your face, exhausted Kṛṣṇa was suffering even though Candrāvalī ...**

*(candrāvalīty ardhokte kṛṣṇo bhrū-saṁjñayā nivārayati \| sarvāḥ parasparaṁ sākūtam avalokayanti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(On his saying the name of Candrāvalī, Kṛṣṇa stops him with a sign from his brow. Everyone looked at each other with meaning.)**

***madhumaṅgalah*** *(sva-gatam):* *hanta hanta kidaṁ mae bahmana-batu-ocidam cabalam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Ah, ah, I have spoken recklessly as befits a** ***brāhmaṇa*** **boy.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(vibhāvya): vibhāvarī-bhavaṁ me varīyaḥ kaṣṭaṁ bāṣpa-ruddha-kaṇṭho’yaṁ saṁvṛttaḥ \| tad aham eva vākyaṁ samāpayāmi \| (iti smitvā)*  
*candrāvalīna-nayanāntatayā kilāsya*  
*sādṛśyataḥ katham api kṣapitā kṣapeyam \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Thinking) Better for me that he be stopped, his throat choked with tears, than acting like a loquacious woman. I will complete his sentence. ..By the moon light entering his eye, which somehow resembled her effulgence, he was somehow able to pass this night.**

***madhumaṅgalah:*** *pia-bassa sabbaṇṇosi \| kiṁ tti maha hiaaṭṭhidaṁ pajjarddhaṁ ṇaṁ jāṇissasi\|*

**Перевод:**

**O dear friend, you have all knowledge. How did you know the last half of the verse that was in my mind?**

***lalitā:*** *rāhe ajja bi sandiddhāsi \| pekkha pekkha rati-bilāsa-pisunāiṁ ṇāarassa caṅgāiṁ aṅgāiṁ \|(iti serṣyaṁ saṁskṛtena)*  
*bāle gokula-yauvata-stana-taṭī-dattārdha-netrād itaḥ*  
*kāmaṁ śyāma-śilā-vilāsi-hṛdayāc cetaḥ parāvartaya \|*  
*vidmaḥ kiṁ na hi yad vikṛṣya kulajāḥ kelibhir eṣa striyo*  
*dhūrtaḥ saṅkulayan kalaṅka-tatibhir niḥśaṅkam unmuñcati \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, even today you are in doubt. Look at the attractive limbs of the playboy, indicating love pastimes.**

**My child! Withdraw your heart from Kṛṣṇa who is addicted to gazing at the breasts of young** ***gopīs*****, and whose heart is a glistening blackstone. Do we not know that he attracts us chaste girls by his play, contaminates us,and then, without worry, simply throws us away?**

***rādhikā:*** *haddhī haddhī sutthu viḍambidahmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**What misfortune! I have been thoroughly cheated.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, mudhaiva mam dūṣayasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, you uselessly blame me.**

***rādhikā*** *(sopalambham, saṁskṛtena):*  
*muktāntar-nimiṣaṁ madīya-padavīm ālokyamānasya te*  
*jāne keśara-reṇubhir nipatitaiḥ śoṇī-kṛte locane \|*  
*śītaiḥ kānana-vāyubhir viracito bimbādhare ca vraṇaḥ*  
*saṅkocaṁ tyaja deva daiva-hatayā na tvaṁ mayā dūṣyase \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Scolding) You gave up blinking your eyes while gazing constantly at the path I would tread to arrive here. I know your eyes are red because of the pollen, and you have been injured on your red lips by the cold forest wind without being aware, because of** ***prema*** **for me. Give up fear. O Lord! Since I am struck by fate, I will not criticize you.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye tavādhīnasya me saṅkoco’py alaṅkarayaiva \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, I am dependent on you. Though you are reluctant, please embrace me.**

***rādhikā:*** *sāhīṇo sabba-loa-vikkhādosi \| kadhaṁ mamāhīṇo hubissasi?*

**Перевод:**

**You are famous in all the worlds for being independent. How can you become dependent on me?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *tavādhīno nāham eva kevalo’smi, kintu te mama daśāvatārāś ca \| tathā hi,*  
*cañcan-mīna-vilocanāsi kamaṭhotkṛṣṭa-stanī saṅgatā*  
*kroḍena sphuratā tavāyam adharaḥ prahlāda-saṁvardhanaḥ \|*  
*madhyo’sau bali-bandhano mukha-rucā rāmās tvayā nirjitā*  
*lebhe śrī-ghana-tāḍya mānini manasy aṅgīkṛtā kalkitā \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Not only am I dependent on you, but all my ten** ***avatāras*** **are also dependent on you. Your eyes are as playful asrestless fish,your breasts are like two handsome turtles on your splendid chest,your lips increase bliss, your waist is marked with three lines, and the loveliness of your face defeatsa host of goddesses of fortune. Though you possess intense beauty,you mind is polluted with anger.**

***rādhikā:*** *halā lalide, āaṇṇidaṁ tue?*

**Перевод:**

**Dear Lalitā, did you hear that?**

***lalitā:*** *kahṇa tuha odārā tuammi jjebba santi \| jaṁ edāṇam cinhāiṁ dīsanti \| (iti saṁskṛtena)*  
*vanyāntar-guru-cāpalaṁ kaṭhinatā go-saṅgatiḥ pāṇija-*  
*krauryaṁ dambha-ruciḥ sucaṇḍi-madhurā laṅkeśa-vidhvaṁsanam \|*  
*aśrāntonmada-laulyam iṣṭa-kadanaṁ nistriṁśa-līlonnatir*  
*mīnendrādy-avatārataḥ sphuṭam amī bhrājanti bhāgās tvayi \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, all your** ***avatāras*** **are within you only, since we can see their qualities there--great restlessness among all animals like the fish, hardness like the turtle, association with the earth like Varāha, cruelty of your nails to women like Narasiṁha, a taste for deception like Vāmana, intense with ferocity like Paraśurāma, killing Rāvaṇa like Rāma, unsteadiness with continual drunkenness like Balarāma, destroying sacrifices like Buddha, and raising your sword sharply like Kalki.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-smitam): sakhe, paśya paśya—*  
*lalitājani durlalitā*  
*babhūva rādhā durārādhā \|*  
*tapte mayi na cchāyāṁ*  
*śaśāka kartuṁ viśākheyam\|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, look! Lalitā has become ill-mannered. Rādhā has become difficult to be won. Viśākhā, without branches, cannot give shade to me, burning in the heat of the sun.**

*(iti batoḥ karān mallī-dāma gṛhītvā sa-cāṭu-praṇāmam \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Taking the jasmines from Madhumaṅgala, he offers polite respect.)**

*srag iyam uru-guṇā te citta-vīthīva rādhe*  
*śucir ati-sukumārī kāmam āmodinī ca \|*  
*nakha-pada-śaśi-rekhā-dhāmni puṣṇātu kāntiṁ*  
*tava kuca-śiva-mūrdhni svardhunī-vibhrameṇa \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This garland with the greatest qualities is like the path to your heart, O Rādhā! It is pure, most delicate, and most fragrant. May it increase the beauty on your breasts, the abode of the moon-like nail marks, just as the Gaṅgā beautifies Śiva’s head.**

*(iti bhrū-saṁjñayā viśākhām anukūlayan mālyaṁ samarpayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(He offers the garland to Viśākhā after gaining her favor with a sign of his brow.)**

***viśākhā*** *(mālyaṁ nivedayantī saṁskṛtena):*  
*yasmin netra-saroruhaṅgana-bhuvaḥ prapte vidūraṁ manak*  
*sadyas te nimiso’pi yati tulanaṁ tanv-aṅgi manvantaraiḥ \|*  
*vṛndāraṇya-kadamba-maṇḍapa-taṭa-krīḍā-bharākhaṇḍale*  
*tasmin kaku-parāyaṇe tava kathaṁ kāmyāni vāmyāny api \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(She offers the garland to Rādhā.) O slender Rādhā! How can your disregard be of any value when Kṛṣṇa, who plays like Indra on the edge of a** ***kadamba*** **grow inVṛndāvana, surrenders with pleading voice, while you experience a moment to be many** ***manvantaras*** **when you cannot see him even for a moment?**

***rādhikā*** *(sābhyasūyam): abehi nibuddhie, abehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With hatred) Foolish woman, go away, go away.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*dhūli-dhūsarita-candrakāñcalaś*  
*candrakānta-mukhi vallabho janaḥ \|*  
*arpayan muhur ayaṁ namaskriyāṁ*  
*bhikṣate tava kaṭākṣa-mādhurīm \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, whose face is more beautiful than the moon! The lover, his peacock feather covered with dust, offering respects to you, begs for your sweet glance.**

***lalitā:*** *rāhe, jhatti kandaraṁ parāvaṭṭehi \| puṭṭhado āāredi ajjiā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, quickly turn your head. Old Mukharā is calling from behind.**

*(rādhikā tathā karoti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā turns.)**

*(praviśya)* ***mukharā*** *(kṛṣṇaṁ vilokya saṁskṛtena):*  
*vanāsaktaṁ cetaḥ praṇayati gṛhād yo viramayan*  
*vareṇyaṁ bandhūnāṁ praṇayam api vismārayati yaḥ \|*  
*mahā-dhūrta-śreṇī-guṇa-garima-vistāraṇa-guroḥ*  
*karotsaṅge tasya tvam api sarale putri patitā \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Mukharā enters and looks at Kṛṣṇa.) O innocent daughter, you have fallen into the hands of this person who is expert at revealing excessive qualities of rascals and who makes the gopīs give up their houses, become attached to the forest , and forget the praiseworthy affection of their relatives.**

***madhumaṅgalah*** *(janāntikaṁ):* *bho baassa māruda-bāālī-kida-muhī tujjhaṁ baṁśībba buḍḍhiā pattā \| tā ettha kiṁ bilambase?*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside) O friend, a garrulous woman full of wind like your flute has arrived. Why do you tarry here?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, kva me vaṁśī?*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, where is my flute?**

***madhumaṅgalah:*** *saaṁ jjebba jāṇāsi kahiṁ tti?*

**Перевод:**

**Do you not know where it is?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sphuṭaṁ rādhikayaiva hṛteyam \| tad enāṁ vinā kathaṁ prasthānam ucitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Clearly Rādhā has stolen it. Without my flute I cannot leave.**

***madhumaṅgalah*** *(sa-parihāsam): bho idaṁ kkhu ahmāṇaṁ guruaṁ bhāa-dheaṁ jam imāhiṁ mohinīhiṁ tumaṁ coriā ṇa saṅgobidosi \| tā ciṭṭhadu barāgī muraliā \| attāṇaṁ ghettūṇa palāahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joking) Ah, this is our great fortune that they did not steal and hide you. Let the vile flute remain here. Taking yourself, we should flee.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-smitam): re vācāla! tiṣṭha tiṣṭha \| (iti parikramya)*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O talkative fool! Stop, stop. (Moving)**

*sundari bindu-cyutake*  
*tava naipuṇyaṁ babhūva puṇyena \|*  
*śaśimukhi vaśī-kṛtābhūd*  
*vaṁśī mama yat tvayā tvarayā \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beautiful Rādhā! By pious acts you have become skilful at ornamental language such as bindu*****-cyuta*****. O moon faced Rādhā! Thus you have deftly stolen my flute.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-bhrū-bhaṅgam): muñcehi ṇaṁ bhaṅgīe kalaṅkārobaṇam \| kā jaṇādi tuhma vaṁsiāṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Frowning) Stop finding fault using word play. Who knows about your flute?**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*na kācid gopīnāṁ bhavati para-vitta-praṇayinī*  
*satīnām asmākaṁ na vada parivādaṁ nanu mudhā \| (ity ardhokte)*

**Перевод:**

**None of the gopīs are inclined to theft. Do not uselessly accuse us honest women. (half a verse)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhi lalite, prasīda prasīda \| darśaya sakhyau dākṣiṇyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Lalitā, be merciful , be merciful. Show kindness to me, your friend.**

***lalitā:***  
*alaṁ jalpair ebhir vraja nija-niketam drutam ito*  
*vayaṁ kiṁ saṁvṛttās tava kitava veṇoḥ pratibhuvaḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Enough of this talk. Quickly go home, O cheater! Why should we be guarantors for your flute?**

***rādhikā*** *(vṛddhām āsadya): ajje diṭṭhaṁ tue appaṇa-ṇattaṇo carittaṁ \| jam eso ahmāṇaṁ coriā-paribādaṁ dedi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Approaching Mukharā) Respected lady, you have seen the character of your grandson. He accuses us of being thieves.**

***mukharā*** *(sa-saṁrambham): re kahṇaḍa saccaṁ mae viṇṇādaṁ \| jaṁ ṇattiaṁ, maha tumaṁ bidambeduṁ laddhosi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Confused) O Kṛṣṇa, I have truly understood that you have come here to insult my granddaughter Rādhikā.**

***madhumaṅgalah:*** *a{i} niṭṭhura-saṁsiṇī ṇibbaṁśie baṁśiam haria tujjha ṇattiṇī tumam duggaṁ laddhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O liar! O classless woman! Your granddaughter, having taken the flute, has taken shelter of you for protection.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *ārye mukhare, satyam āha vayasyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Mukharā! My friend speaks the truth.**

***mukharā:*** *a{i} rāhie abi kiṁ saccaṁ edaṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā, is this true?**

***rādhikā:*** *ajje bundābaṇe indhaṇāṇaṁ kiṁ mahagghadā jādā jam hattha-mettā vaṁśa-kaṭṭhiā ahmehiṁ hāradabbā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected Mukharā! Has kindling in Vṛndāvana become so valuable that we would have to steal the short piece of wood known as the flute?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): hanta pīte pracaṇḍa-devi, yadi veṇuṁ na jaharthās tataḥ kathaṁ tad-vartāyāṁ smita-kuṭmalollāsād utphulla-kapolā dolāyita-dṛgantāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Ah fierce, golden deity! If you did not take the flute, then why are the corners of your eyes moving and why is your forehead blossoming with the joyful bud of a smile at this news?**

***mukharā*** *(sa-krośam): cañcala, ahimaṇṇuṇo sadhammiṇī tujjha bandanijjā, tahabi parihasijja{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) Impudent boy! Though you should worship the chaste wife of Abhimanyu, you joke with her.**

***madhumaṅgalah:*** *muhare, eso jaṇṇopabīdassa sabāmi \| diṭṭhaṁ mae puhabī-bilagga-sehareṇa ajja rāhiā bandidā pia-baassena \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Mukharā! I swear it is true on my thread that I saw my friend offer respect to Rādhikā with his head on the ground.**

***mukharā*** *(sānandam): tado imassa dhammo baḍḍhissadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then his dharma will increase.**

*(sarve smitaṁ kurvanti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Everyone smiles.)**

***mukharā:*** *kahṇaḍa imiṇā tujjha cābaleṇa khijjissadi ballabindo nando, tā gadua go-maṇḍalaṁ sambhālehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, Nanda is pained by your fickle behavior. Go and herd the cows.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *ārye, vinā veṇuṁ viprakṛṣṭāyā dhavalāvaler ākṛṣṭir durghaṭā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected lady, without my flute it is impossible to attract the excellent herds of cows.**

***lalitā:*** *kahṇa, abalāvaliṇo ti kīsa ujjuaṁ na kadhesi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Why do you not say instead “without my flute it is impossible to attract the gopīs” since that is the real truth.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *lalite, vṛddhayādya sabalā yūyam \| tataḥ katham idaṁ kathayiṣyāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā, you have become strong in the presence of elderly Mukharā. How can I say this?**

***mukharā*** *(sa-roṣaṁ saṁskṛtena):*  
*navīnāgre naptrī caṭula na hi dharmāt tava bhayaṁ*  
*na me dṛṣṭir madhye-dinam api jaratyāḥ paṭur iyam \|*  
*alindāt tvaṁ nandātmaja na yadi re yāsi tarasā*  
*tato’haṁ nirdoṣā pathi kiyati haṁho madhupurī \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) RestlessKṛṣṇa! Right in front of you is my young grand-daughter. You have no fear of violatingreligious principles.I am old and even at noontime cannot see verywell, but I am clever. O son of Nanda! If you do not leave my doorstep at once,thenI am not to be blamed. Mathurā is not far away.**

***madhumaṅgalah*** *(sa-roṣam): dummuhi buḍḍhie! tujjha kaṁsado kim ahme bhāehma jaṁ mahu-purāmāsannaṁ kahesi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) Foul-mouthed old woman! Why should we fear your Kaṁsa when you speak of going to Mathurā?**

***mukharā*** *(sa-vyajam): are ciṭṭha ciṭṭha! esāhaṁ ṇattiṇiaṁ ghettūṇa rāa-sahaṁ patthidohmi\| (iti rādhādibhir anugamyamānā niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Bluffing) Oh, stop, stop! Taking my granddaughter, I will go to the royal assembly.**

**(She exists with Rādhā and the others.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe samāgaccha \| kālindī-kaccham upetya gavām uddeśaṁ karavāvaḥ \| (iti parikramya \| valita-grīvaṁ pasyan socchvāsam)*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, come. Going near the Yamunā, we will find the cows. (They go. Turning back he speaks with a sigh)**

*mudrām dhairya-mayīṁ kṣaṇaṁ vitanute tāruṇya-lakṣmīṁ kṣaṇam*  
*sopekṣāḥ kṣaṇam ātanoti bhaṇitīr autsukya-bhājaḥ kṣaṇam \|*  
*śuddhām dṛṣṭim itaḥ kṣaṇaṁ praṇayate preṅkhat-kaṭākṣāṁ kṣaṇam*  
*roṣeṇa praṇayena cākulita-dhī rādhā dvidhā bhidyate \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One moment her behavior is sober and the next moment it is complexly childish. One moment she is indifferent and the next moment she speaks eagerly. At one moment she gives an innocent glance and at another moment gives a trembling side glance. Rādhā is divided in two, her intelligence agitated by both anger and affection.**

*(iti niṣkrāntāḥ sarve \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(All exit.)**

*pañcamo’ṅkaḥ*  
*rādhā-prasādanaḥ*

## Act Five: Pacifying Rādhā

*(tataḥ praviśati paurṇamāsī \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Paurṇamāsī enters)**

***paurṇamāsī:***  
*snehaḥ śoka-kṛśānor*  
*vinoda-sadanaṁ sadeti nātathyam \|*  
*snigdhādya rādhikāyāṁ*  
*yad ahaṁ tenāsu dagdhāsmi \|\|1\|\|*

*(puro’valokya) keyaṁ madhumaṅgala-saṅginī mām abhivartate? (punar nibhālya)*  
*ajanita-śāsana-bhaṅgā*  
*sthira-jaṅgama-maṇḍalaiḥ sva-vane \|*  
*nikhila-prāṇi-ruta-jñā*  
*vindati purataḥ kathaṁ vṛndā \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is not untrue that affection is the pleasurable abode for the fire of grief, since today, being close to Rādhā, I have been quickly burned by that fire. (Looking in front) Who is that girl with Madhumaṅgala approaching me? Why is Vṛndā, whose order is never broken by all moving and non-moving entities in her forest and who knows the languages of all living entities, coming towards me?**

*(praviśya)* ***vṛndā madhumaṅgalaś ca:*** *amba vande \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O mother, I offer respects.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *svasti yuvābhyām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Good health to you two.**

***vṛndā:*** *bhagavati, kathaṁ socanty asi?*

**Перевод:**

**O noble woman, why are you lamenting?**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatse! vidagdha-puṅgavasyāṅga-saṅgama-lakṣmāni rādhikāyām abhilakṣya manyumān abhimanyuḥ samprati madhu-puryāṁ sa-kuṭumbo vastum utkaṇṭhate \| tatrāpi tad-ambā tad-īrṣyā-jambālāvalī-jṛmbhāyāṁ kādambinī-bhāvam ālambya rādhā-marālīm udvejayati \| tenādya śocāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child! Seeing the marks of association with Kṛṣṇa on Rādhā’s limbs, Abhimanyu became angry and now desires to live with his family in Mathurā. His mother, like a host of clouds, caused by the appearance of the profuse mud of his anger, torments tender Rādhā who is like a swan.**

***vṛndā:*** *paurṇamāsī-śubhāśīś-candrikaiva vighnāndhakāra-saṁhāriṇī \|*

**Перевод:**

**The moonlight of Paurṇamāsī’s blessings will remove the darkness caused by obstacles.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ajje! kahaṁ rāhopari tujjha variṭṭhaṁ pemmam?*

**Перевод:**

**Why do you have such strong love for Rādhā?**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatsa, satyam api bhūriṇi premodaya-kāraṇe tasyām ananyāpekṣi mamedaṁ prema\|*

**Перевод:**

**Child, I have unalloyed love for her for many reasons.**

***vṛndā:*** *yuktam idam, yataḥ—*  
*jagati kila vicitre kutracin niscalātmā*  
*bhavati nirabhisandhiḥ kasyacit prema-bandhaḥ \|*  
*vilasati samudīrṇe kumbhaje khañjanālī*  
*kalitavatī tathāstāṁ hanta nāśaṁ prayāti \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That is proper: in this variegated world sometimes there exists a determined person who has unconditional bonds of love with another person. When the star Agastya appears, the murmuring** ***khañjana*** **birds rejoice. When Agastya star disappears, they also disappear.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *kerisaṁ nirāhisandhino pemmassa cinham?*

**Перевод:**

**What are the characteristics of this unconditional love?**

***paurṇamāsī:***  
*stotraṁ yatra taṭasthatāṁ prakaṭayac cittasya dhatte vyathāṁ*  
*nindāpi pramadaṁ prayacchati parīhāsa-śriyaṁ bibhratī \|*  
*doṣeṇa kṣayitāṁ guṇena gurutāṁ kenāpy anātanvatī*  
*premṇaḥ svārasikasya kasyacid iyaṁ vikrīḍati prakriyā \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When the woman hears praise, she remains neutral while feeling pain in the heart. When criticized she feels joy, taking it as a great joke. By seeing faults in her partner, her love does not decrease and by seeing good qualities it does not increase. In this way the actions of natural prema play.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ebbaṁ rūbaṁ kkhu doṇaṁ rāhā-māhavāṇaṁ pemma \|*

**Перевод:**

**The love of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa is like that.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatsa kiṁ ucyate? mādhurya-saṁsargino naisārgikasya paraspara-vallabhānāṁ vidagdha-mithunānāṁ prema-śṛṅkhala-bandhasya paramotkarṣa-rekhāyāṁ dṛṣṭāntaḥ kila rādhā-mādhavayor bhāvāmṛta-bhūmā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child, what can be said? The greatness of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa’s sweet love is the highest example of the natural bondage of conjugal love caused by prema’s chains, among affectionate couples who have mutual love.**

***vṛndā:*** *bhagavati śrūyatām —*  
*yaṣṭiṁ vasti na pāṇinā kalayituṁ śṛṅge na saṅgārthitaṁ*  
*dhatte dhātubhir aṅga-maṇḍana-mayīṁ nāṅgīkaroti kriyām \|*  
*parṇaṁ vādayate na ghūrṇita-manas tīre kṛtānta-svasuḥ*  
*kintūtklāmyati mukta-vibhrama-guṇa-grāmo’dya dāmodaraḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī, please listen. He does not want to hold his staff in his hand or keep his buffalo horn. He does not decorate his body with minerals. He does not make musical sounds using leaves. With confused mind, devoid of all his charming qualities, breathing his last, today Dāmodara lies exhausted.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sa-khedam): kim idam?*

**Перевод:**

**(Lamenting) Why is that?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *lalidā-kauḍilleṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**Because of Lalitā’s devious actions.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *nūnaṁ lalitāyā haṭhānuvartita-manā vartate rādhā \|*
