**Перевод:**

**Rādhā adopts the obstinate mind of Lalitā.**

***vṛndā:*** *atha kim \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *na jāne kva khalv adya lalitādayaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**I do not know where Lalitā and the others are today.**

***vṛndā:*** *tāsām uddeśāya mayā subalaḥ preṣito’sti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I sent Subala to find them.**

*(praviśya)* ***subalaḥ:*** *ajje baṁdemi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O Paurṇamāsī, I offer respects.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *subala! kva dṛṣṭā rādhādayaḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**Subala, where did you see Rādhā and her friends?**

***subalaḥ:*** *muharā-gharobānta-vaṭṭhino rasālassa mūle \|*

**Перевод:**

**Under the mango tree near Mukharā’s house.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatsa madhumaṅgala! tūrṇam anusṛtya rādhikām abhisārayanty asmi \| tad etayā sūkti-candrikayā tvam ānandaya mukundam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear Madhumaṅgala, I will quickly go and meet Rādhā. Make Kṛṣṇa happy with the moon of this good news.**

*(madhumaṅgalaḥ sa-harṣaṁ niṣkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Madhumaṅgla joyfully exits.)**

***vṛndā*** *(janāntikam): subala maya samarpitaṁ padyaṁ tvayā kiṁ nāma viśākhāyāṁ sañcāritam?*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside) Subala, did you deliver the message I wrote to Viśākhā?**

***subalaḥ:*** *adha iṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Yes.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vṛnde, yāvat prasādya prasādhyaca rādhāṁ sañcarayāmi tāvad adhunā yuvābhyāṁ puraḥ kadamba-nikuñje viśrāmyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Vṛndā, you two should rest in the** ***kadamba*** **grove in front while I go to dress and decorated Rādhā.**

*(vṛndā subalena saha niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Vṛndā exists with Subala.)**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(parikramya): kathaṁ laliteyam āyāti?*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking) Why has Lalitā come?**

***lalitā:*** *bhaavadi! tuhma saāsaṁ gacchaṁtī hmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī, I am coming to you.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *kim-artham?*

**Перевод:**

**Why?**

***lalitā:*** *ajje! tiṇṇa dhūttena puṇo puṇo abarañjidābi pia-sahī lāhavaṁ amaṇṇia suṭṭhu ukkaṇṭhedi \| tā kiṁ karissaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected lady, though being repeatedly insulted by that rascal, still Rādhā takes it lightly and longs for him intensely. What shall I do?**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatse, muñca mudhā kāluṣyam \| nāparādhyati mādhavaḥ \| kintu madhumaṅgala-pramāditaiva vaḥ khedāya babhūva \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child, give up this useless disturbance. Kṛṣṇa does not offend her. Rather Madhumaṅgala’s madness has made you suffer.**

***lalitā*** *(sva-gatam):* *mamābi ebbaṁ nandīmuhīe kathidam \| (prakāśam) ajje, pekkha esa rāhī rasālassa mūle kampaṁtī kimpi jappadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Even Nāndīmukhī has told me this. (Aloud) O Paurṇamāsī, look, under the mango tree, what is Rādhā uttering?**

*(tataḥ praviśati sānutāpaṁ)* ***rādhā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*karṇānte na kṛtā priyokti-racanā kṣipraṁ mayā dūrato*  
*mallī-dāma nikāma-pathya-vacase ruṣaḥ kalpitaḥ \|*  
*kṣoni-lagna-sikhaṇḍa-śekharām asau nābhyarthayann īkṣitaḥ*  
*svāntaṁ hanta mamādya tena khadirāṅgāreṇa dandahyate \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, speaking with remorse) No sweet words entered my ear. I immediately threw the jasmine garland far away. I was angry with the friend who spoke words for my benefit. I did not see him when he placed his peacock-feather crown on the ground and begged with many prayers. My heart continually burns because of** ***a khadira*** **cinder.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *putri, pracchannam upasṛtya śṛṇuvaḥ prema-vilāsam \| (ity ubhe tathā sthite )*

**Перевод:**

**O daughter, hiding ourselves, we will hear these pastimes of love. (The two hide.)**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-capalaṁ punaḥ saṁskṛtena):*  
*dhanyās tā hariṇī-dṛśaḥ sa ramate yābhir navīno yuvā*  
*(punaḥ sa-śaṅkaṁ)*  
*svairaṁ cāpalam ākalayya lalitā māṁ hanta nindiṣyati \|*  
*(punaḥ sautsukyaṁ)*  
*govindaṁ parirabdhum indu-vadanaṁ hā cittam utkaṇṭhate*  
*(punaḥ sāmarṣaṁ)*  
*dhig vāmaṁ vidhim astu yena garalaṁ mānābhidhaṁ nirmame \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Agitated) Fortunate are those women with whom the youthful boy enjoys.**

**(Worried) Seeing my obstinate, fickle nature, Lalitā will scold me.**

**(With eagerness) My heart longs to embrace moon-faced Govinda.**

**(With anger) How unfavorable is the creator for having created anger and pride!**

***lalitā*** *(sva-gataṁ):* *adakkhiṇe, ciṭṭha ciṭṭha! saaṁ jebba kahṇaṁ nirākadua bhaṅgīe maṁ dūsesi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) O inexperienced girl, stop this! You criticize me through a bee, after you yourself rejected Kṛṣṇa.**

***rādhikā*** *(bhrṅgīm avekṣya saṁskṛtena):*  
*kṛmir api namitātmā hanta vṛndāvane’smin*  
*kalayati nija-maulau barha-mauler nideśam \|*  
*anunayati muhur māṁ netu-kāmā nilīyaṁ*  
*yad amala-madhuroktis tasya dṛṣṭiṁ śaṭhasya \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing a bee she speaks.) Though it is an insect, it bows down in Vṛndāvana. It carries Kṛṣṇa’s orders on its head. Desiring to take me, concealed, to see that rascal, it constantly pacifies me with its pure sweet words.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sa-narma-smitam): nikhilam eva vṛndātavī-prāṇi-vṛndaṁ dūtī-bhūtam iyaṁ manyate mahā-māninī \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With a joyful smile) In great anger she things that all beings of Vṛndāvana are messengers.**

***rādhikā*** *(premāveśaṁ nāṭayantī sa-camatkāram): kadhaṁ eso maṁ moṭṭiaṁ pariraddhuṁ ubasaṇṇo kahṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Overcome with prema, she speaks in astonishment.) Why has Kṛṣṇa come to embrace me more strongly?**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *gambhīrānurāga-vivarto’yam \| yad asyāṁ mādhavasya visphuraṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**This is a transformation of deep anurāga. She has a vision of Kṛṣṇa.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-huṅkāraṁ parāvṛtya): haṁta bho baṅka-kalā-śāli caṁdāalī-koḍa-cirāsaṅga-bhaṅgura-kuḍaṅga avehi avehi \| eso tumaṁ parihabissasi mae \| (iti karṇotpalaṁ kṣipantī saṁskṛtena)*

*yamunā-tīra-kadambāḥ*  
*samprati mama hanta sākṣiṇo yūyam \|*  
*eṣa balān mām abalāṁ*  
*gokula-dhūrtaḥ kadarthayati \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Turning with contempt) Ah! Dishonest deer with crooked character, you associate constantly with Candrāvalī! Go away! Go away! I reject you. (She throws an ear ornament at the deer.) O** ***kadamba*** **trees on the bank of the Yamunā! You are my witness now. This rascal of Gokula torments this innocent girl with force.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *lalite, parāṁ koṭim adhirūḍhā rādhikotkaṇṭhā \| tad iyaṁ tvaritam abhisāryatām\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā, Rādhā’s longing has reached the highest stage of** ***adhirūḍha.*** **She should be taken to meet him.**

***lalitā*** *(parikramya): halā rāhi! ekkā jjebba kiṁ maṁtesi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking) O my friend Rādhā, are you taking advice all alone?**

***rādhikā*** *(lalidām ālokya sva-gatam): kadhaṁ saccaṁ jebba ekkahmi \| jaṁ kahṇo na disa{i} \| (iti sautsukyam) halā lalide!*  
*para-taṇu-pavesa-vijjā kaham iha sāmeṇa kāmiṇā paḍhidā \|*  
*mama hiae māṇaggī pabisia nibbārido jeṇa \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing Lalitā, she says to herself) Actually I am alone, since she cannot see Kṛṣṇa. (With eagerness) O Lalitā! Has lusty Kṛṣṇa learned the art of entering into others’ bodies? He has entered my heart and extinguished the fire of anger.**

*(praviśya)* ***viśākhā:*** *halā subala-hatthādo laddhā iaṁ pattiā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O friend I received this letter from Subala.**

***lalitā*** *(gṛhītvā vacayati):*  
*medhyo’pi mādhavikkayā madhupo yad eṣa*  
*kṣiptaḥ svayaṁ pracalatā nava-pallavena \|*  
*tasyāḥ khalu kṣitir iyaṁ suṣamā-kṣayeṇa*  
*nandaty ayaṁ tu viruvann aravindinīṣu \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Taking it, she speaks) Though the bee is without fault, it is cast aside by a movement of the fresh shoot of the** ***mādhavi*** **creeper. Her domain is thus devoid of beauty, but the bee will still enjoy, buzzing among the lotuses.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-viṣādam saṁskṛtena):*  
*ajani vimukhaḥ saṅke paṅkeruhākṣi vicakṣaṇo*  
*mayi madhu-ripur doṣa-śreṇī-vihāra-vana-śriyām \|*  
*akalita-rasaḥ sūcī-viddho rajaḥ-prasarāndha-dhīr*  
*na madhupa-yuvā kiṁ ketakyāṁ viraktim upaiṣyati \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Lamenting) O Lotus eyed Lalitā! I fear that the enemy of Madhu, being perceptive, is now averse to me, since I am a lush forest of many faults. Devoid of rasa, pierced by its thorns, blinded by its pollen, will he not reject the** ***ketakī*** **flower?**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *na hi candreṇa candrikāyā mokṣaḥ kadāpi sambhavati?*

**Перевод:**

**Can the moon ever be separated from the moonlight?**

***viśākhā:*** *halā samassassa samassassa \| tuha ukkaṇṭhidaṁ takkia mae kahṇa-pa{u}ttiṁ viṇṇāduṁ ṇāṁdīmuhī pesidatthi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, be calm! Suspecting your attraction, I have sent Nāndīmukhī to learn about Kṛṣṇa’s inclination.**

*(praviśya)* ***nāndimukhī*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*mṛdur api nisargatas tvaṁ*  
*katham ārdre mādhave kaṭhorāsi \|*  
*athavā nava-navanīta-puṭī*  
*hima-drave kakkhaṭā praikṣi \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) By nature you are soft. Why are you hard to tender Kṛṣṇa? You are like fresh butter hardened in cold water.**

***rādhikā:*** *halā, abi ṇāma suhaṁ vaṭṭadi māhavo \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, is Kṛṣṇa happy?**

***nāndimukhī*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*kṣaṇam api na suhṛdbhir narma-goṣṭhīṁ vidhatte*  
*racayati na ca cūḍāṁ campakānāṁ cayena \|*  
*param iha mura-vairī yogivan mukta-bhogas*  
*tava sakhi mukha-candraṁ cintayan nirvṛṇoti \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He does not talk with his friends even for a moment. He does not make crowns of** ***campaka*** **flowers by picking flowers. He is like a yogī, detached from enjoyment. Thinking of your face, he derives great bliss.**

***rādhikā*** *(viśākhāṁ pariṣvajya saṁskṛtena):*  
*bhūyo bhūyaḥ kali-vilasitaiḥ sāparādhāpi rādhā*  
*ślāghyenāhaṁ yad agharipuṇā bāḍham aṅgīkṛtāsmi \|*  
*tatra kṣāmodari kim aparaṁ kāraṇaṁ vaḥ sakhīnāṁ*  
*dattāmodāṁ praguṇa-karuṇā-mañjarīm antareṇa \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Embracing Viśākhā) Though I have repeated offended him by quarrelling, being praiseworthy, he still accepts me. O slender-waisted girl, what other reason is there for this, other than my friends who give pleasure, like excellent flowers of mercy?**

***nepathye:***  
*garvodagrāḥ kalam avikalaṁ tanvatām anya-puṣṭāḥ*  
*nispratyūhaṁ mṛga-yuvatayaḥ śaṣpam āsvādayantu \|*  
*sīmantinyo gṛha-naya-mayiṁ śīlayantu praṇālīṁ*  
*dhūrto veṇur viharati kare nādya pītāmbarasya \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) May the cuckoos warble loudly with great pride, may the young deer relish the grass without interruption, and may women perform their household tasks! Today the rascal flute of Kṛṣṇa is not in his hand.**

***rādhikā*** *(vaṁśīm udghāṭya sopalambhaṁ saṁskṛtena):*  
*sad-vaṁśatas tava janiḥ puruṣottamasya*  
*pāṇau sthitir muralīke saralāsi jātyā \|*  
*kasmāt tvayā sakhi guror viṣamā gṛhītā*  
*gopāṅganā-gaṇa-vimohana-mantra-dīkṣā \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(She takes the flute and scolds it.) O little flute! You are born in a good family and reside in the hand of the supreme person. You are gentle by nature. From which guru did you take this dangerous initiation into a mantra which bewilders the gopīs?**

***viśākhā:*** *halā accariā iaṁ baṁśī jaṁ mārudāhimuhī-kidā saaṁ saddāedi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, it is astonishing that the flute makes sounds by itself when facing into the wind.**

***rādhikā:*** *sahi parikkhassaṁ \| (iti tathā karoti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**I will test it. (She holds up the flute in the wind.)**

***viśākhā:*** *suṇijja{u} mahurā kāalī?*

**Перевод:**

**Do you hear the sweet sounds?**

***lalitā:*** *sambarehi sambarehi, mā suṇādu kahṇassa paribāro \|*

**Перевод:**

**Stop, stop! Do not let the associates of Kṛṣṇa hear it.**

*(praviśya)* ***vṛndā*** *(pracchannam): bhagavati na kadapi vaṁśī deyeti śrutaṁ mayā lalitā-durmantritam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, in a concealed voice) O Paurṇamāsī, I have heard Lalitā’s bad advice that the flute should not be given back.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatse yuktim āyatyāṁ kariṣyāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Child, later I will make arrangements.**

*(praviśya)* ***jaṭilā:*** *ṇūṇaṁ idha kahṇeṇa milidaṁ jaṁ muralī vaditā \| (vilokya) ammo, kahaṁ bārisahāṇavi-hatthe kahṇassa vaṁśī \| tā nihṇudaṁ gadua ṇaṁ gehissaṁ \| (iti sahasopasṛtya sāmarsam) ayi dubbiṇīda-goāla-puttie! muñcehi muraliam \| (ity ākṛṣya gṛhṇāti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) Kṛṣṇa must be here since the flute sounded. (Looking around) Oh! Rādhā has Kṛṣṇa’s flute. Sneaking up, I will grab it. O unworthy cowherd girl! Let go of the flute! (She grabs it.)**

***lalitā:*** *haddhī pamādo haddhī pamādo! kadhaṁ buḍḍhiāe atakkidaṁ muralī āaḍḍhidā?*

**Перевод:**

**What a mistake! How can the old lady unexpectedly take the flute?**

***jaṭilā:*** *naṁ kkhu bhaavadīe pauṇṇamāsīe daṁsa{i}ssaṁ jā majjha bhaṇidaṁ ṇa patthiāedi*

**Перевод:**

**I will show it to Paurṇamāsī. She does not believe what I have said.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *putri vṛnde! gahanaṁ kaṣṭam āpatitam \| paśya jaṭilā mamoṭaja-diśaṁ prayāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my daughter Vṛṇdā! A great calamity has occurred. Look! Jaṭilā is going towards my hut.**

***vṛndā:*** *bhagavati! mā cintaya \| kṣipram ahaṁ muralīṁ luṇṭhayāmi \| (iti niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī! Do not worry. I will immediately steal the flute. (She exits.)**

***lalitā*** *(sa-bhayam anusṛtya): ajje, kīsa aliaṁ saṅkasi? jaṁ esā kāliṁdī-kulahmi ahmehi laddhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Following Jaṭilā in fear) O respected Jaṭilā, why do you suspect deceit? We found it on the bank of the Yamunā.**

***jaṭilā*** *(sa-roṣam): capale! dummaṁtiṇi ciṭṭha ciṭṭha!*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) Impudent girl! You speak false words. Stop it.**

*(praviśya)* ***subalaḥ:*** *ajje jaḍile, pekkha dahi-lampaḍā makkaḍī tujjha gharaṁ pabisa{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) Respected Jaṭilā, look! A yogurt thief monkey has entered your house.**

***jaṭilā*** *(sāci-grīvam ālokya): subala saccaṁ kahesi \| makkhaṇa-coriṇī kkhu eṣā makkaḍī \| (iti parāvṛtya dhāvantī niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Straining her neck) O Subala, you have spoken the truth. The monkey is stealing the butter.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *nūnaṁ vṛndayā preritāsti kakkhaṭī nāma jaran-markaṭīyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**This is Kakkhaṭī an old monkey sent by Vṛṇdā.**

***subalaḥ:*** *ṇāṁdīmuhi! pekkha pekkha! pakkhittena veṇuṇā mūḍha-jaḍilāe makkaḍī tāḍidā\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look Nāndīmukhī! Foolish Jaṭilā hit the monkey with the flute.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sa-harṣam): diṣṭyā muralīm ādāya kakkhaṭīyaṁ kadambam adhirūḍhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully) By good fortune, Kakkhaṭī has taken the flute and climbed a** ***kadamba*** **tree.**

*(sarvāḥ praharṣaṁ nāṭayanti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Everyone becomes happy.)**

*(praviśya)* ***jaṭilā:*** *haddhī haddhī baccha suala! hatthādo me muralī gadā \| tā tujjha nimañchaṇaṁ jāmi \| samappehi me baṁśiaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O what misfortune Subala! The flute has left my hand. I worship you. Get me the flute!**

***subalaḥ:*** *ajje, jahattha-ṇāmā esā kakkhaḍī kealaṁ tujjha bahiṇī-puttādo visālādo bhāedi \| tā goaḍḍhaṇa-siṅge khelaṁtaṁ naṁ gadua abhyatthehi \| (jaṭilā niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O Jaṭilā, this monkey suitably named Kakkhaṭī (hard) is afraid of only your sister’s son, Viśāla. Go to him. On the peak of Govardhana where he is playing, and request him.**

**Перевод:**

**(Jaṭilā exits.)**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *diṣṭyā vyājena jaratīṁ dūram apasārya dhūrto’yaṁ bhrū-vibhrameṇa lalitāṁ tvarayati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Fortunately, by trick, the rascal Subala has got rid of Jaṭilā. Now he hurries to Lalitā while moving his brow.**

***lalitā*** *(netrāntaṁ kūṇayantī): halā rāhi, ehi \| beṇuṁ maggahme \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Contracting the edge of her eye) O Rādhā, come. Let us pursue the flute.**

***rādhikā*** *(sva-gatam):* *diṭṭhiā ahisāredi maṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) by good fortune he will meet me.**

*(praviśyāpaṭī-kṣepeṇa)* ***mukharā:*** *bisāhe, ahimaṇṇu saṁdisa{i}—ajja joisiāṇaṁ ubadeseṇa mae gomaṅgalā ṇāma caṇḍī pūaṇiā \| tā pūaṇobahāraṁ ghettūṇa tumaṁ ceṭṭha-rukkhassa tale rāhiaṁ lambhehi tti \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) Viśākhā, Abhimanyu says that the astrologers have told him to worship Gomaṅgala today. Taking the articles of worship, he told me to take Rādhā to the base of the** ***caitya*** **tree.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-khedam apavārya): haṁta haṁta! duddebassa pāḍiullaṁ \| (iti lalitā-mukhaṁ īkṣate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(With pain, without others hearing) What unfavorable misfortune! (She looks at Lalitā’s face.)**

***lalitā:*** *halā sacca-nāmā eso ahimaṇṇu \| tā gadua pūaṇobahāraṁ saṁbādehma \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, Abhimanyu (angry) is true to his name. Going there, let us arrange the articles of worship.**

*(iti sarvā niṣkrāntāḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(All exit.)**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(subalam anusṛtya sa-vyatham): vatsa, duḥsamādhāneyaṁ gatir upasthitā \| tad adya vṛndayā saha gatvā samāśvāsyatāṁ tvayā pāṭavena puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ \| mayā tu pramāṇika-purandhrīṇāṁ goṣṭhīm āsādya jaṭilā-kauṭilyaṁ varṇayiṣyate \| (iti niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Following Subala, she speaks with pain.) O child, a great problem has just arisen. Today, go with Vṛndā and skillfully pacify Kṛṣṇa. I will go to the assembly of authoritative elder women and describe to them Jaṭilā’s crooked nature. (Exits)**

***subalaḥ*** *(parikramya): esā tamāla-tale ḍāhiṇa-hattha-gahida-baṁśiā buṁdā ciṭṭha{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking) Vṛndā is under the** ***tamāla*** **tree, holding the flute in her right hand.**

*(praviśya)* ***vṛndā:*** *bhoḥ subala, vilokita-sarvārthāsmi \| tad alaṁ tad-vārtayā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O Subala, I have seen everything. No need to tell the news.**

***subalaḥ:*** *buṁde, turiaṁ ehi \| veṇuṁ jebba ubaharahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā, come quickly. We will present the flute.**

*(ity ubhau parikramatah):*

**Перевод:**

**(Both walk)**

***subalaḥ:*** *buṁde mahumaṅgaleṇa baḍḍhidukkaṇṭho pia-baasso maggaṁ jjeba pekkhanto ciṭṭha{i} \| tā ṇa jāṇe akidatthāṇaṁ ahmānaṁ tattha gamaṇe tassa ka dasā bhave*

**Перевод:**

**O Vṛndā, our dear friend Kṛṣṇa is looking at the path, his longing increased by Madhumaṅgala. What will happen to him if we go there without being successful?**

***vṛndā:*** *subala! satyaṁ bravīṣi \| paśyāyaṁ punnāga-taror upakaṇṭhe samutkaṇṭhate kaṁsāriḥ\|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala, you speak the truth. Look, Kṛṣṇa is standing full of long near that** ***punnāga*** **tree.**

***subalaḥ:*** *buṁde! tado bhaṇāmi cintehi juttiṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā! I am telling you to think of some remedy.**

***vṛndā*** *(vimṛṣya): subala, govindasya kṣaṇaṁ vinodāya cintitopāyāsmi \| tad ehi, tan-niṣpattaye tvarāṁ bhajāvaḥ \| (iti niṣkrāntau \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Reflecting) Subala, I have thought of a method to give Kṛṣṇa pleasure temporarily. Come, let us hurry to accomplish it. (They exit.)**

*(tataḥ praviśati madhumaṅgalenopāsyamānaḥ)* ***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sautsukyam):*  
*rādhā puraḥ sphurati paścimataś ca rādhā*  
*rādhādhisavyam iha dakṣiṇataś ca rādhā \|*  
*rādhā khalu kṣiti-tale gagane ca rādhā*  
*rādhā-mayī mama babhūva kutas tri-lokī \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, attended by Madhumaṅgala, he speaks eagerly.) Rādhā appears in front. She appears behind. She appears on the left and on the right. She appears on the earth and in the sky. Why do I see the three worlds filled with Rādhā?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *pia-baassa bhaavadīe ahisāridaṁ dāṇiṁ jebba pekkhissasi rāhiaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear friend, you will now see Rādhā. She is being brought by Paurṇamāsī.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*kareṇāntas tuṣṭyā sulalitam avaṣṭabhya lalitā-*  
*karāṅguṣṭhaṁ rādhā bhṛśam abhisarantī sarabhasam \|*  
*kim adya smerākṣī smara-parimalollāsi-valaya-*  
*dhvanir māṁ nirmāsyaty anupama-camatkāra-caṭulam \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**With inner satisfaction, Rādhā comes quickly and gracefully for the meeting, holding the thumb of Lalitā in her hand. With smiling eyes, jingling her bracelets, joyfully perfumed with love, will she today speak incomparably sweet and astonishing words to me?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho bho mā uttamma \| kaṅkaṇa-jhaṇakkāro succa{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah, do not despair. I hear jingling bracelets.**

*(****nepathye****) halā lalide, pekkha! so eso puṇṇāa-rukkho dīsa{i} \| (punas tatraiva) sahi rāhe dhiṭṭha-bhamara-jampidaṁ pekkha ṇaṁ \| tā kkhaṇaṁ idha jjebba ciṭṭhahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage, Subala is dressed as Rādhā and Vṛndā is dressed as Lalitā. They converse.) O Lalitā, look! I see the** ***punnāga t*****ree. O Rādhā, look at the aggressive bees humming. Let us stay here for a moment.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-capalam): bho pia-baassa bāmado kiṁ ṇa pecchasi \| esā lalidāe saddhaṁ rāhiā samāadā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Trembling) Oh, dear friend, do you not see to the left? Rādhā has arrived with Lalitā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sotkaṇṭham): diṣṭyā sākṣād adya mad-īkṣaṇayoḥ saukhyaṁ vistāryate sakhyā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With longing) By good fortune, today my eyes are happy because of my friend.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-garvam): bho kīsa na vittharidabbaṁ jattha ahaṁ biaḍḍho dūdohmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Proudly) Oh, why should you not be happy? I have been an expert messenger.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe puraḥ-sthayor mat-priyayor nirvyalīkatā nādyāpy avadhāritā \| yad ābhyāṁ na sannidhīyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, my two dear gopīs do not seem affectionate, since they do not approach.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *pia-baassa suṭṭhu pasannāṁ rāhiṁ jāṇīhi \| jaṁ sāḍi-añcala-jhampidā muralī jhalakka{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I know that Rādhā is pleased since the flute is covered by her cloth.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-sneham):*  
*vidhur eti divā virūpatāṁ*  
*śatapatraṁ bata śarvarī-mukhe \|*  
*iti kena sadā śriyojjvalaṁ*  
*tulanām arhati mat-priyānanam \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(With affection) The moon is disfigured during the day and the lotus is disfigured at night. With what can the most beautiful face of my beloved be compared?**

*(iti sa-kautukam anusarpati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(He approaches with curiosity.)**

*(****nepathye):***  
*bārisahāṇa{i} lacchī iyaṁ puro rā{i}ṇī samuggama{i} \|*  
*candāalī-kuḍumba-caora māhaba suppasahaṁ \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) The beauty of Rādhā filled with anger makes its appearance. O** ***cakora*** **of the moonlight! Do not run here with such force.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *lalide bhamidāsi \| na kkhu caoro \| pekkha eso rahaṅgiramano jena varisahana{i} lacchī kamijja{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā, you are mistaken. This is not a** ***cakora.*** **Look, this is a** ***cakravāka*** **who loves the beauty of Rādhā.**

*(nepathye punar anyataḥ): bho kahṇa suṇāhi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage, a different voice speaks.) O Kṛṣṇa, listen.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(vilokya sa-saṅkam): esā ḍāhiṇe bisālassa bahiṇī sāraṅgī ṇāma bāliā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking with worry) On the right is the sister of Viśāla named Sāraṅgī.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, mā śaṅkiṣṭhāḥ \| suṣṭhu bālikeyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, do now worry. She is only a girl.**

*(praviśya)* ***sāraṅgī:*** *bho kahṇa suṇāhi \| buḍḍhiā muharā bhaṇādi—kīsa tue mamaṇattiṇī aliaṁ dūsijja{i} \| jaṁ tujjha baṁsia ahmehiṁ kakkhiḍā-hatthe diṭṭhā \| tā maggehi ṇaṁ tti \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O Kṛṣṇa, listen. Old Mukharā says “Why do you deceptively contaminate my granddaughter. We saw your flute in the hand of Kakkhaṭī. Do not search for it.”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sāraṅgike vijñāpaya mukharāṁ yad ahaṁ labdha-muralīko’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Sāraṅgī, tell Mukharā that I I have obtained my flute.**

*(n****epathye****) halā! pacchaṇṇā hohi pacchaṇṇā hohi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, hide, hide.**

***sāraṅgī*** *(nepathyābhimukham avalokya serṣyam): halā rāhie cecca-rukkhassa tale tumaṁ āāredi me bhāduo \| tā tattha kiṁ tti ṇa gadāsi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Facing away from the stage, she looks with anger.) O Rādhā, my brother called you to the base of the** ***caitya*** **tree. Why have you not gone there?**

*(****nepathye****) hadāse sāhā-sāraṅga-muhi sāraṅgie! tumaṁ bi dudia jaḍilā saṁvuttā \| tā buḍḍha-saddūlassa tuṇḍa-koḍare paḍehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Off stage) O Sāraṅgī, you have the face of a monkey! You are a second Jaṭilā. Fall into the gaping mouth of an old tiger.**

***sāraṅgī*** *(sāmarṣam): lalide ollaṭṭia maṁ jebba tumaṁ tajjasi \| tā ahaṁ gadua māusiāe jaḍilāe biṇṇavissam \| (iti niṣkrāntā):*

**Перевод:**

**(With anger) O Lalitā, making offense, you scold me. I will go and tell Jaṭilā, sister of my mother. (She exits.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sāvajñam): sāraṅgī jādu ṇāma \| bāliā-palābe kassa bīsambho?*

**Перевод:**

**(Insulting) O Sāraṅgī, who will believe the prattling of a little girl?**

*(****nepathye****) sakhi rāhe! muñca muñca \|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Rādhā, let go, let go.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *suṇāhi saṁkideṇa kiṁ bhaṇādi lalidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Listen. Is Lalitā speaking sanskṛt?**

*(punar* ***nepathye****):*  
*kiṁ taskarīṁ yuvati-mana-dhanasya vaṁśīm*  
*aṅke karoṣi vikiratvarayā vidūre \|*  
*eṣā prayātu vanitāmbara-taskarāya*  
*yogyena saṅgam iha gacchatu vastu yogyam \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage again) Why do you keep on your lap that flute, a thief who steals the wealth of the young gopīs’ minds? Throw it far away. Let it go to the stealer of the gopīs’ clothing. Similar things should associate with each other.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): sakhe paśyeyam añcalād vaṁśīṁ balād ivākṛṣya purastāc cikṣepa \| tad imāṁ gṛhāṇa \| (madhumaṅgalas tathā karoti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O friend, look! Taking the flute from her cloth, she has thrown it in front. Take it. (Madhumaṅgala takes it.)**

*(****nepathye*** *dūratah): ammo sāraṅgie asaccaṁ ṇa bhaṇidaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage from afar) Ah! Sāraṅgī! A lie has not been spoken.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-vyatham):* *sakhe paśya! puro niṣṭhureyam upasthitā jaratī \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In pain) O friend look! The cruel old woman has arrived.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *haṁta sāaṇa-kahṇa-bhuaṅgībba kūra-muhī esā rosāveseṇa jaṭṭhiṁ khipaṁtti parusaṁ gajja{i} jaḍilā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! The old, foul-mouthed woman, like a black snake with ears, swinging a stick in anger, is spouted cruel words.**

*(****nepathye****): bho dukkulāṅgāra-dhūma-lehe, paccahaṁ bañcesi dāṇīṁ kā pa{u}ttī?*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) O line of smoke from the coals of a bad family! Daily you cheat. What are you up to now?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *haddhī haddhī! kaalībba kampa{i} rāhiā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O what misfortune! Rādhā is trembling like a banana tree.**

*(****nepathye****) ajje pasīda pasīda \| ṇa kkhu ahme abarajjhahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) O respected lady, be merciful. We have not been offended by him.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho baassa! pekkha rāhiaṁ hatthe ghettūṇa lalidāe samaṁ patthidā buḍḍhiā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, look! Taking Rādhā by the hand, the old woman is going with Lalitā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-khedam): sakhe, na jāne kim adya pratipadyate kaṭhoreyaṁ jaṭilā \| tad upasṛtya tattvam avadhāryatām \| (madhumaṅgalo niṣkrāntaḥ):*

**Перевод:**

**(In pain) O friend, I do not know what the cruel old woman will do now. Following, you should learn the truth. (Madhumaṅgala exits.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(niḥśvāsya):*  
*vyaktiṁ gate mama rahasya-vinoda-vṛtte*  
*ruṣṭo laghiṣṭha-hṛdayas tarasābhimanyuḥ \|*  
*rādhāṁ nirudhya sadane vinigūhate vā*  
*hā hanta lambhayati vā yadu-rājadhānīm \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Sighing) With my secret pastimes revealed, foolish Abhimanyu will in anger suddenly hide Rādhā, confining her to the house, or even worse, take her to Mathurā.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho pia-baassa, accariaṁ \| ṇūṇaṁ rāhiā kāmpi vijjāṁ jāṇa{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) This is astonishing. Rādhikā has some extraordinary powers.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kathyatāṁ kīdṛśī vidyā?*

**Перевод:**

**What power do you speak of?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *kula-buḍḍhāhīrī-maṇḍale ṇiviṭṭāe bhaavadīe aggado vikkosaṁtī jaḍilā rāhiaṁ ṇīdā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Jaṭilā, yelling, has taken Rādhā into the assembly of elder cowherd women, where Paurṇamāsī is seated.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *tatas tataḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**Then what happened?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *tado diṭṭhaṁ mae siṇeheṇa vikkhohidāsu tāsu sabbāsu rāhiā-ogguṇṭhaṇaṁ ucchāria hasaṁto subalo saṁvutto*

**Перевод:**

**Then I saw in the midst of the affectionate assembly, when she drew away Rādhā’s veil, Subala was revealed, smiling.**

*kṛṣṇaḥ (smitvā): tatas tataḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Then what happened?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *tado hāsa-kolāhale ubarade ruṭṭhāhiṁ sabbāhiṁ nibbhacchidā lajjāe ṇada-muhī jaḍilā palāidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**When the great laughter diminished, scolded by all the angry people, Jaṭilā fled in shame with bowed head.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kathyatām—tayor dvitīyā katham abhūt?*

**Перевод:**

**Please explain what happened to the second girl.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *rāhiāe kaṇṇe paḍideṇa keṇa bi maṁteṇa paḍhamaṁ jebba sā buṁdā kidā\|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā suddenly turned her into Vṛndā by uttering some mantra in her ear.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe na rādhikāyāḥ khalv iyaṁ vidyā, kintu tām abhimanyunā samāhūtām avadhārya mad-vinodāya vṛndayā praṇītam idaṁ kautūhalam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, this is not some magic of Rādhikā. Vṛndā, understanding that Rādhā was called by Abhimanyu, played a trick for my pleasure.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sāṭṭa-hāsam): bho saccaṁ bia kahesi \| diṭṭhaṁ mae puṇo bi buṁdāe ṇimmido rāhā-beso sualo muharā-ghare ppabisadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing loudly) Ah, you speak the truth. I saw Subala enter Mukharā’s house wearing Rādhā’s dress, created by Vṛndā.**

*(****nepathye****):*  
*dadhānā madhyāhna-jvalad-aruṇa-kānta-pratimayā*  
*vapus tulyaṁ gaṇḍa-sthala-tulita-kāraṇḍava-ruciḥ \|*  
*kṛśāṅgīyaṁ nidrā-parimala-daridrākṣi-kavalā*  
*sakhī rādhā bādhāṁ hari-viraha-khinnā prathayati \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) With body like a statue of sun stones blazing at noon, with cheeks pale as white ducks, thin-limbed Rādhā, suffering in separation from Kṛṣṇa, causes pain to her friends.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-dṛṣṭi-kṣepam): sakhe, diṣṭyā kīreṇāmunā samāśvāsito’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Throwing a glance in that direction) O friend, fortunately I am pacified by that parrot.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *ṇūṇaṁ buṁdā-bhāsidaṁ anukaredi kīro \|*

**Перевод:**

**The parrot is repeating the words of Vṛndā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe draṣṭum icchāmi tādṛśau vṛndā-subalau \| tatas tvaryatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, I want to see Vṛndā and Subala dressed like that. Let us hurry there.**

*(madhumaṅgalo vaṁśīṁ kṛṣṇa-kare nikṣipya parikramati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Putting the flute in Kṛṣṇa’s hand, Madhumaṅgala begins to walk.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *suvicyutāṁ vaṁśīm upalabdho’smi \| tad enāṁ pūrayāmi \| (iti tathā karoti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**I have obtained the lost flute. I will satisfy it. (He plays the flute.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(kṣaṇam utkarṇo bhavan, saṁskṛtena):*  
*manohārī ko’pi pratimukha-visārī mṛdutayā*  
*virāvo’yaṁ varyāṁ śravaṇa-paricaryāṁ racayati \|*  
*tataḥ karṇottaṁsīkṛta-caṭula-vaṁśī-kala-rutir*  
*nirāṭaṅkā śaṅke milati kalaviṅkāvalir itaḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Pricking his ears up) This indescribable, attractive sound, spreading in all directions with softness, produces the greatest happiness for the ears. Because of the sound, I think that all the** ***caṭaka*** **birds have assembled here, making the flute’s soft, sweet sound the ornament of their ears.**

*(punar vilokya) hī hī \| sadda-sādhammeṇa padārido hmi, jaṁ kaṅkaṇa-siñjidaṁ kkhu edam\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking again) How astonishing! I have been fooled by a similar sound. This is jingling of bracelets that I hear.**

***rādhikā:***  
*amiaṁ piasi sumahuraṁ*  
*vamasi ruaṁ vissa-mohanaṁ visamaṁ \|*  
*tujjha na dūsaṇaṁ adhabā*  
*murali jado dāruṇāsi kidā \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You drink the sweetest nectar of his lips and vomit poison which bewilders the universe. This is not your fault since you have been created with a harsh nature.**

***lalitā:*** *halā, purado puṇṇāassa mūle kahṇo rāadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, in front is Kṛṣṇa at the base of the** ***punnāga*** **tree.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(vilokya sa-harṣam): dūre maggaṇijjo attho kahaṁ saaṁ jebba hatthe ubatthido \| (iti parāvṛtya) pia-baassa pekkha—buṁdāe saddhaṁ subalo tujjha saṇṇihiṁ laddho \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing them with joy) How is it those for whom we searched for has come into our hands spontaneously? Dear friend, look! Subala is coming to you with Vṛndā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-sneham ālokya): hanta priye sakhyau! praviṣṭā me dṛṣṭiḥ prakāmam āmodate \| (iti parikramya) bho sakhīnāṁ śikhāmaṇe tarasā sannidhīyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing them with affection) Ah, dear friends! Seeing you, I have become happy. (Approaching them) O crest jewel among friends, come here quickly.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-smitam apavārya): halā lalide, maṁ kkhu sualaṁ jebba jāṇādi de baasso \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Concealing a smile) My friend Lalitā, your friend thinks that I am Subala.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe madhumaṅgala! paśya saṁvidhānakasya kim api sauṣṭhavam, yad asau sākṣād agrato rādhikaiva sa-vayasyā pratibhāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Madhumaṅgala, look! See the skill in the disguise. It looks exactly like Rādhā and her friend.**

***lalitā:*** *halā rāhe, apariphullo eso sura-ballaho \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, the** ***punnāga*** **tree has not blossomed.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sersyam): ṭhaggini buṁde, ajja bi kiṁ tti ahmāṇaṁ purado rāhī tti bhaṇāsi\| suala tti ujjuaṁ kahehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O rascal Vṛndā! Why do you mention the name of Rādhā in front of us? Call him Subala. That is correct.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe mā tvam evaṁ bravīḥ \| prakāmaṁ rādhābhidhānaṁ dhinoti mām \| tad anenāham apy āmantrayiṣye \| (iti sannidhāya) sakhi rādhe, pariṣvajasva māṁ kṣaṇam ahaṁ tad eva priyābhimarṣa-saukhyam anubhavāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, do not speak like this. Please me sufficiently with the name of Rādhā. I will also address him by that name. (Approaching) My friend Rādhā, embrace me for a moment. I will experience the happiness of touching my beloved.**

***lalitā*** *(rādhāṁ pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā): ṇāara, tattha gadua subalaṁ jebba aliṅgehi \| alam imiṇā*

*dambha-muddā-paoeṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Putting Rādhā behind her) O clever boy! Go over there and embrace Subala. Enough of this false act!**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-roṣam): buṁde, tumaṁ pa{i}dīebi ṇūṇaṁ lalidā saṁbuttā, jaṁ pajjūssuaṁ pia-baassaṁ bāresi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Angrily) O Vṛndā, by nature you show bold behavior and stop my friend with zeal.**

*(praviśya)* ***vṛndā:*** *sakhi rādhe! tvad-bhuja-vallarī-sparśa-kāmo’yaṁ purastād punnāgaḥ \| tad enaṁ dohada-dānenotphullaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Enters) O Rādhā, the** ***punnāga*** **in front desires to touch your creeper arms. Make it flower by giving the medicine to make its flowers unfurl.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-vismayam): baassa, diṭṭaṁ buṁdāe iṁdajālaṁ \| (iti sa-kautukaṁ avekṣya) iṁdajālini buṁde, ghaṇāidī bī dhūma-lehā biaḍḍha-sāraṅgaṁ kaḍḍhiduṁ ṇārihadi\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Surprised) O friend, I have seen the magic spell of Vṛndā. (Looking at her with curiosity) O magician Vṛndā, a thick cloud of smoke cannot attract the clever** ***caṭaka*** **bird.**

***vṛndā:*** *ārya, taḍid-dāma-kaṇṭhīyaṁ kādambinī pratīyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected boy, this appears to be a mass of clouds with lightning on its throat.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(nibhālya sa-vismayam): kathaṁ satyaṁ evānaya raṅgaṇamalikayā dustyaja-kaṇṭhīyaṁ priyā me vārṣabhānavī \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking with astonishment) Is it true that this is my dear Rādhā, who cannot take off the** ***raṅgaṇa*** **garland?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *a{i} dei buṁde, pasīda pasīda \| mā kkhu buḍḍhiṁ mohehi \| jaṁ raha caicca-rukkha-tale patthida \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected Vṛndā, look! Do not confuse our intelligence. Rādhā has gone to the base of the** ***caitya*** **tree.**

***vṛndā:*** *ārya, raṅgaṇamālikā-sparśānabhijña-kaṇṭhī kṛtrimaiva rādhikā viśākhayā sārdhaṁ tatra gatā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected boy, the false Rādhā, whose neck does not know the** ***raṅgana*** **garland, has gone to Mukharā’s house with Viśākhā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(rādhām avalokya):*  
*tavānukārāt subalaṁ didṛkṣuṇā*  
*mayā tvam āptā purataḥ sudurlabhā \|*  
*sādṛśyataḥ kācam ivābhilaṣyatā*  
*premāgra-bhūmir vaṇijā hariṇmaṇiḥ \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Rādhā) I desired to see Subala imitating you but I have obtained you, difficult to attain, in front of me, just as a merchant who desires glass attains an emerald, the topmost abode of love.**

***rādhikā:*** *ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \| viṇṇādo si \|*

**Перевод:**

**Stop, stop. I understand.**

***lalitā****: jala{i} sahī maha rāhī maṁdā jam hoi ṇīliṇī-rāā kahṇa \| tumaṁ ṇaṁdasi jaṁ dhaṇṇo haliddā-rāo si \|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Rādhā suffers miserably because of her bluish attraction to you. O Kṛṣṇa! You rejoice because you are fortunate with yellowish attraction.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*rohiṇy-adhara-śobhayā viharase jyeṣṭhāsi vāma-bhruvaṁ*  
*vāṇyā rājasi citrayā parijaneṣv ārdrāṁ dhiyaṁ yacchasi \|*  
*rādhe tvaṁ śravaṇottareti paritas tārodayollāsinī*  
*nāśleṣārpaṇa-dīkṣite mayi kathaṁ dākṣiṇyam ātiṣṭhasi \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You wander about with the beauty of your red lips. You are supreme with your graceful brows. You are remarkable with your words. You give happiness to your friends. O Rādhā! You excel with your ears. You are splendorous with your pearl necklace. Why do you not show favor to me, though I am expert at embracing?**

***vṛndā:***  
*mudhā mānonnāhād glapayasi kim aṅgāni kaṭhine*  
*ruṣaṁ dhatse kiṁ vā priya-parijanābhyarthana-vidhau \|*  
*prakāmaṁ te kuñjālaya-gṛha-patis tāmyati puraḥ*  
*kṛpā-lakṣmīvantaṁ caṭulaya dṛgantaṁ kṣaṇam iha \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O cruel Rādhā! Why do you uselessly exhaust your limbs with increased anger? Why do you show anger at the prayers of your dear friend? The master of the bowers languishes in front of you. Glance at him for a moment with full compassion.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*kaṭhorā bhava mṛdvī vā prāṇās tvam asi rādhike \|*  
*asti nānyā cakorasya candralekhāṁ vinā gatiḥ \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, be hard or soft, but you are my life! The** ***cakora*** **bird has no shelter other than the moon light.**

***rādhā:*** *saccaṁ māiṇaṁ bi tumaṁ bimohaṇo si \| (iti sa-śabdaṁ krandati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**You are really the bewilderer of magicians! She cries loudly)**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*dhārā bāṣpa-mayī na yāti viratiṁ lokasya nirmitsataḥ*  
*premāsminn iti nanda-nandana-rataṁ lobhonmano mā kṛthāḥ \|*  
*itthaṁ bhūri nivāritāpi tarale mad-vāci sācīkṛta-*  
*bhrū-dvandvā na hi gauravaṁ tvam akaroḥ kiṁ nādya rodiṣyasi \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For those desiring prema for Kṛṣṇa, streams of tears do not cease. Therefore your greedy mind should not have attraction for Kṛṣṇa. O fickle Rādhā! Though I have prevented you many times, frowning at my words, you did not take them seriously. Will you not cry today?**

*(kṛṣṇaḥ karāravindena rādhikāśru-bindūn apasārayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛsṇa wipes away Rādhā’s tears with his lotus hand.)**

***rādhikā:*** *muddha-jaṇe bi baṅkaṁ bavaharaṇto kīsa ṇa lajjasi?*

**Перевод:**

**Are you not ashamed of acting deviously with innocent people?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*smara-krīḍā-lubdhaḥ paśupa-ramaṇīṣu sphuṭam ahaṁ*  
*tathāpy akṣṇor vartis tvam asi mama divyāñjana-mayī \|*  
*tapādyāḥ kiṁ bhṛṅgaḥ pṛthulaṁ ṛtu-lakṣmīr na bhajate*  
*rasollāsād enaṁ tad api hi madhu-śrīr madayati \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though I am greedy for amorous pastimes with many gopīs, still you, with your shining cosmetics, are the orbit for my eyes. Does not the bee enjoy the beauty of other seasons such as summer? But the beauty of spring intoxicates the bee out of joy for its rasa.**

***vṛndā:*** *sakhi, yathārthaṁ vakti vanamālī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, Kṛṣṇa speaks the truth.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, tvayā sahacaryā vana-vihāram aṅgīkartum icchāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, I want to play in the forest with you as my assistant.**

***vṛndā:*** *tenāhaṁ sakhī-vṛndām avadhāpayāmi \| (iti paritaḥ pasyantī)*  
*smitaṁ vitanu mādhavi prathaya malli hāsodgamaṁ*  
*mudā vikasa pāṭale puraṭa-yūthi nidrāṁ tyaja \|*  
*prasīda śata-patrike bhaja lavaṅga-vallī śriyaṁ*  
*dadhātu saha rādhayā harir ayaṁ vihāra-spṛhām \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I will put all the** ***sakhīs*** **there. (She looks around.) O** ***mādhavī*** **creeper, please smile. O jasmine, release some laughter. O trumpet flower, blossom in joy. Golden** ***yūthi,*** **give up sleeping. O rose, be compassionate. O clove vine, show your beauty. May Kṛṣṇa obtain his desire to play with Rādhā!**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *hī hī \| kahaṁ kāṁtāra-jakkhiṇīe bāā-māttaeṇa upphullī-kiaṁ vallī-maṇḍalam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Haha (Lughing). Why are all the vines blossoming by the words of this forest spirit?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, cittam āmodayanti puṣpa-modavatyo me vīrudhaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, these flowering creepers delight the heart.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *baassa tuhmāṇaṁ sabbāo cittaṁ āmodenti ladāo \| mama uṇa ekkā hema-jūhī jjebba jaṁ goulesarīe saṁkiaṁ gabba-ghiaṁ bia tthabaaṁ dhāredi \|*

**Перевод:**

**All the creepers delight your heart. But only the golden** ***yūthi*** **delights me since has flower clusters yellow like the ghee made by Yaśodā.**

***lalitā*** *(smitvā): ajja, tado kkhu paaḍā de rasaṇṇadā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Respected boy, your knowledge of taste has been revealed.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sersyam): baassa, pekkha pekkha—imāo rattāo bi baṅka-kiṁsua-kaliāo goiāo bia maṁ ṇa suhābeṁti \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Indignantly) O friend, look! These crooked red** ***kiṁśuka*** **flowers resembling gopīs do not please me.**

***lalitā:*** *buṁde, ede vallaā bia pekkhīaṁtu jabā-tthabaā je kkhu loaṇa-lohaṇijjā bi ṇāmodaṁ bitthāreṁti \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Vṛndā, look at these hibiscus clusters. They are like cowherd boys. Though they are pleasing to the eye, they do not give real joy.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-roṣam): jāṇāhma tuhmāṇaṁ goiāṇaṁ kamma jā rasa-kumbhaṁ bi diḍhaṁ ṇimmahia siṇehaṁ kaḍḍhaṁti \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) We know the action of you gopīs. You churn the strong pot of rasa and draw out all the ghee (affection).**

***vṛndā*** *(smitvā): sakhi lalite!*  
*ye daṇḍa-pāśa-bhājaḥ*  
*sphuṭaṁ vahanto manaḥ-śilākalpam \|*  
*kāntāram āśrayante*  
*tebhyo vaḥ kṣemam ullasatu \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) My friend Lalitā! You should be tolerant with those who, carrying sticks and ropes, and having minds like stone, take shelter of difficult paths.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): vṛnde, jñātaṁ jñātam \| buddhiṁ murcchayatā kūrcikā-lobhena gopikāñcala-grāhiṇī tvaṁ kṛtāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O Vṛndā, I understand. You side with the gopīs, because of desire for their milk products which bewilders your intelligence.**

*(****nepathye****):*  
*kastūrikeva dūravac chada-saṅgameyaṁ*  
*gopī-tatir mada-mayī kila picchilā ca \|*  
*dākṣiṇyatas tanu-bhṛtām anurañjano’yaṁ*  
*vasanta-vāyur iva hanta murāntakārī \|\|36\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) These gopīs are difficult to attain, intoxicating and slippery like musk. Kṛṣṇa is like the southern, favorable spring breeze, attractive to all beings.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(pṛṣṭhato dṛṣṭiṁ kṣiptvā): sādhu bho kīra-rāja! sādhu!*

**Перевод:**

**(Casting a glance behind) King of the parrots, you have spoken the truth.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *vihaṅga-puṅgaa! ca{u}ddaha-bijjā-biakkhaṇo dīhāu hohi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Best of birds, have a long life, with fourteen types of knowledge.**

***lalitā:*** *haṇḍe caṇḍāla kīra! paaṇḍa-sasāaṇa-tuṇḍa-rāhuṇo pāghuṇo hodu de piṇḍa-sasī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Aagh! O parrot outcaste! May the moon of your body be the guest of Rāhu’s huge hawk-like beak.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, tūrṇam asmai samarpaya pākimāni dāḍimī-bījāni \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, please quickly give the parrot some ripe pomegranate seeds.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho buṁdābaṇa-bihappae! dāḍimī-bīehiṁto bi suṭṭhu kaṁtaṁ lalidāe*  
*daṁta-paṁtiṁ de dāissaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Bṛhaspati of Vṛndāvana, I will give you the beautiful teeth of Lalitā instead of pomegranate seeds.**

*(****punar nepathye****)*  
*cañcala sañjhāghaṇo bia muhutta rāaṁ taṇodi de sāmī \|*  
*baha{i} siṇehaṁ rāhī kealaṁ ṇabba-ṇaaṇīa-puttībba \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Fickle parrot! Your master has love for a moment, like the sunset. Only Rādhā has true affection, like the daughter of fresh butter.**

***lalitā*** *(sānandam): sahi sārie sohaggabadi hohi \| jaṁ paccuttareṇa nijjido tue dummuho kīro\|*

**Перевод:**

**(In bliss) O my friend, the** ***sārika*** **bird! Good fortune to you. The foul-mouthed parrot has been defeated by your answer.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *dhruvaṁ vṛndayedam adhyāpitaṁ kauśalaṁ vihaṅgayor dvandvam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Both birds have certainly been skillfully taught by Vṛndā.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-krodham): hañje! bhañjemi de tīkka-jappiṇaṁ cañcu-pūḍaṁ \| (iti sa-vyājaṁ daṇḍaṁ kṣipati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) O slave woman! I will break your beak which speaks nonsense. (He throws a stick as a pretense.)**

***rādhikā:*** *hanta kadham uḍḍīṇaṁ bābadūaṁ bihaṅga-mihuṇaṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! Why have the two garrulous birds flown away?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(rādhām avekṣya):*  
*sevante taru-gehinaḥ sumanasāṁ vṛndair madhu-syandibhir*  
*yatrotphulla-latā-vadhūbhir abhitaḥ saṅgatya bhṛṅgātithīn \|*  
*saṁvītā paśubhis tathā khaga-kulaiḥ kheladbhir avyāhataṁ*  
*na syāt kasya sukaṇṭhi seyam adhikānandāya vṛndāṭavī \|\|38\|\|*

*athavā—*  
*hariṇīr viḍambayasi netra-khelayā*  
*lalitair latāḥ pika-kulaṁ kaloktibhiḥ \|*  
*śikhinaś ca kuntala-kalāpa-vibhramair*  
*iti te puraḥ kim iva me vana-śriyā \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Rādhā) In the forest, the trees which are like householders, surrounded by their wives, the flowering creepers, continuously offer their guests, the bees, flowers with streams of honey. O Rādhā with beautiful throat! Filled with animals, flocks of frolicing birds the Vṛndāvana forest will delight all people.**

**You mock the does by the movements of your eye. You mock the creepers with your graceful gestures. You mock the cuckoo by your soft words. You mock the peacocks by the quivering of your locks. What should I do with the beauty of forest when you are present?**

***vṛndā:*** *paśya paśya—*  
*viratormir iyaṁ sunīrajā*  
*dhṛta-śuddhojjvala-sattva-santatiḥ \|*  
*sphuṭa-kṛṣṇa-rucir yamādṛtā*  
*muni-goṣṭhīva cakāsti bhanujā \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look, look! The Yamunā, having pure lotuses, has stopped its waves. It holds many living entities and is clearly colored black. Respected by Yama, the river shines like a group of sages.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye! paśya paśya!*  
*smita-ruci-virājitaṁ te*  
*mukham iva nīrajayaty adhīrākṣi \|*  
*nīraja-bāndhava-duhitur*  
*nīraja-rājī marud-bhramitā \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beloved, please look! O Rādhā with restless eyes! The lotuses of the Yamunā, moving in the breeze, seem to be offering** ***aroti*** **to your smiling face.**

***vṛndā*** *(parikramya nīrajāny āhṛtya ca): puṇḍarīkākṣa stokotphullam idaṁ gṛhāṇa līlā-puṇḍarīkam \| tathāvataṁsocitaṁ ca kokanada-dvandvam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking, she picks some lotuses) Lotus-eyed Kṛṣṇa! Please accept this slightly blossming lotus as a toy and these red lotuses as ear decorations.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-harṣam ādāya): vṛnde raktotpale rādhā-karṇayor ādhānena śriyaṁ labhatām \| (iti tathā kṛtvā sa-kautukam) hanta puṇḍarīka-kose cañcarīko vartate \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Taking them in bliss) O Vṛndā! This two red lotus should become beautiful when offered to the ears of Rādhā. (He does this excitedly) Ah! There is a bee in the petals of the white lotus.**

***vṛndā:***  
*madhupaḥ kamalena sārdham udyan-*  
*makarandena mukundam āsasāda \|*  
*saraseṣu vinirmito hi saṅgaḥ*  
*paramānanda-bharonnatiṁ tanoti \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Along with the lotus and its flowing honey the bee has approached Kṛṣṇa. The association which is produced among things with rasa gives the greatest bliss.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye! paśya paśya!*  
*asmin madīya-kara-saṅgini puṇḍarīka-*  
*koṣe kṣaṇaṁ kila vilambya śilīmukho’yam \|*  
*karṇāvalambi tava kokanadaṁ prapede*  
*kaṁ vā balān na hi haraty anurāga-lakṣmīḥ \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beloved, look! The bee resting in the white lotus in my hand has now landed on your red lotus in your ear. Who would not be forcibly attracted by your wealth of** ***anurāga*****?**

*(rādhikā sambhramaṁ nāṭayantī bhuja-latāṁ kṣipati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā becomes alarmed and waves her arms.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sphuṭaṁ vihasya):*  
*karṇottaṁsita-rakta-paṅkaja-juṣo bhṛṅgīpater jhaṅkriyā*  
*bhrāntenādya dṛg-añcalena dadhatī bhṛṅgāvalī-vibhramam \|*  
*trāsāndolita-dor-latānta-vilasac-cūḍā-jhaṇat-kāriṇī*  
*rādhe vyākulatāṁ gatāpi bhavatī modaṁ mamādhāsyati \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Because of the buzzing bee attracted to your red lotus ornament, your wandering side glances resemble the beauty of a swarm of bees with, and your bracelets jingle when you swing your creeper-like arms in fear. O Rādhā! Your agitation gives me bliss.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-trāsam celāñcalam udañcayantī): ajjabi kadhaṁ na caladi dhiṭṭho \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In fear she waves the edge of her cloth.) Why does the shameless bee not go away now?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*madhurākṣi mudhādya sambhrameṇa*  
*kṣipa celāñcalam añjasā na bhūyaḥ \|*  
*pibatu śravaṇotpalodgataṁ te*  
*madhupo’yaṁ madhu maṅgalaṁ kṛśāṅgi \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sweet eyed Rādhā! Do not uselessly wave your cloth any longer out of agitation. O slim Rādhā! Let the bee drink the auspicious honey (*****madhu maṅgalam*****) in your ear ornament.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho baassa! kīsa bahmāṇaṁ maṁ mahubeṇa pibāesi? (iti daṇḍena bhramaraṁ tāḍayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, why do you make the bee bite me, a** ***brāhmāna*****? (He beats the bee with a stick.)**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-ślāgham): ajja piaṅkaro mamāsi saṁbuttaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Praising the bee) Respected boy, you are kind to me.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *kahaṁ mahusūano takkālaṁ jebba tirohido jaṁ kudo bi ṇa lakkhijja{i}*

**Перевод:**

**Why did the bee (or Kṛsṇa) disappear? It cannot be seen anywhere.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-vyamoham): haddhī haddhī \| kahiṁ gado mahumahano? (iti saṁskṛtena)*  
*samajani davād vitrastānāṁ kim ārta-ravo gavāṁ*  
*mayi kim abhavad vaiguṇyaṁ vā niraṅkuśam īkṣitam \|*  
*vyaraci nibhṛtaṁ kiṁ vāhūtiḥ kayācid abhīṣṭayā*  
*yad iha sahasā mām atyākṣīd vane vanajekṣaṇaḥ \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Bewildered) What misfortune! Where has Kṛṣṇa gone? I think there must have been painful cries of the cows, frightened of a forest fire. Or did he see unlimited faults in me, for he has suddenly abandoned me in the forest? Was he called by some** ***sakhī*****? Shall I stay here alone?**

*(kṛṣṇaḥ saṁjñayā sarvān nivārya smitaṁ karoti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(With gesture Kṛṣṇa wards off everyone and smiles.)**

***rādhikā*** *(saṁskṛtena): hanta—*  
*vāsantībhir ayaṁ na me kaca-bharaḥ kaṁsāriṇottaṁsitas*  
*tasyoraḥ-sthala-cumbi-campaka-cayair nāgumphi mālyaṁ mayā \|*  
*mallībhiś ca nirargalaṁ parihasan nāyaṁ balāt tāḍitaḥ*  
*prārambhe’dya vanotsavasya virahac-chadmā davaḥ prodagāt \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**He did not decorate my hair with** ***vāsanti*** **flowers. I did not string a garland using** ***campaka*** **flowers for decorating his chest. In jest I did not freely strike him with jasmine flowers. At the beginning of celebrations in the forest, a fire has arisen on the pretext of separation from him.**

***vṛndā*** *(apavārya): kāmam andhaṁ kāriṇīyaṁ prema-bandha-kandalī yā khalu vispaṣṭam api nānusandhāpayati \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside) The intense bondage of prema makes her completely blind so that she cannot clearly see.**

***rādhikā:*** *sahi buṁde, rakkhehi maṁ \|(iti trāsaṁ nāṭayantī \|)*  
*sappī sappa{i} bhiṅga-paṁti-misado kālī rasālāṅkure*  
*rattāsoa-sire bireha{i} tadhā puppha-cchalādo sihī \|*  
*siṅge kiṁsua-sāhiṇo a kaliā-dambheṇa sambhediṇī*  
*maṁ bheduṁ kusumāuhassa bala{i} kurāddha candāalī \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Vṛndā, please save me. (Terrified) A black snake disguised as a swarm of bees is crawling towards me. There is a flame on top of the red** ***aśoka*** **tree, disguised as flowers. A host of cruel, half moon arrows of Cupid, disguised as buds on the** ***kiṁśuka*** **branch, have been released and are attempting to pierce me. (She loses control of herself.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sambhramād abhyupetya pāṇiṁ gṛhṇann uccaih): sukumari, kim akāṇḍe kātarāsi? yataḥ—*  
*tvan-mukha-lakṣmī-glapitā*  
*candrāvalīr iha bibheti pūrṇāpi \|*  
*praṇayāndhe tava kartuṁ*  
*kim ardha-candrāvalī kṣamate \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Quickly approaches, takes her hand and speaks loudly) O young woman! Why are you distressed for no reason? A host of full moons is frightened, wilting because of the beauty of your face. O Rādhā, blinded by love! What can a host of half moons do to you?**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-dhairyaṁ lajjaṁ nāṭayantī sva-gatam): kadhaṁ acchi-laggaṁ jjebba hāridaṁ maṇṇaṁtī khiṇṇahmi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Controlling herself, embarrassed, she speaks to herself.) Why was I distressed, thinking that he was taken way--when he is present before my eyes?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye! paśya paśya!*  
*pariṇata-vara-bīja-spardhi-dantoru-bhāsaḥ*  
*kusumam upahasantyās tanvi danta-cchadena \|*  
*phala-vijayi-kucāyās tvad-bhayād dāḍimīyaṁ*  
*mṛdula-pavana-dolā-dambhataḥ kampate’dya \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beloved, look! The great luster of your teeth defeas the best ripe pomegranate seeds. Your lips mock its flowers and your breasts defeat its fruit. This pomegranate tree in fear of you now trembles, on the pretext of swaying in the gentle breeze.**

***vṛndā:*** *sakhi rādhe, nirvarṇaya tava karṇikocita-korakaṁ karṇikāram amum \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Rādhā, look! This** ***karṇikāra*** **bud is suitable to decroate your ear.**

***rādhikā:*** *ṇaa-kaṇṇiāra-kusume bhamaro rasa-loha-ṇiccalo bhādi \|*

**Перевод:**

**There is a bee, greedy for the nectar, stuck in the new** ***karṇikāra*** **flower.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kāñcana-mañca-niviṣṭo rasa-rājo’yaṁ śarīrīva \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is like the king of rasa, seated on a golden throne.**

***rādhikā:*** *pekkha pekkha—*  
*uddhūra-maraṁda-mattā*  
*ruddhe sāreṇa gaṁdha-visāreṇa \|*  
*iha mañjula-malligeṇa*  
*rolambā haṁta guñjaṁti \|\|52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look, look! Intoxicated by the plentiful honey, many bees hum in the auspicous jasmine flowers filled with strong, pervading perfume.**

*(kṛṣṇa uddhūra-makarandety ādi paṭhati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa repeats the verse.)**

***vṛndā:*** *pītāti-sūkṣma-śikhara campaka-kalikeyam āyatā bhāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**This yellow** ***campaka*** **with delicate crown is beautiful.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *mānavatī-hṛn-mathanī haimī kāmasya śaktir iva \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is like Cupid’s** ***śakti*****, golden in color, agitating the hearts of proud women.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho baassa esa kamassa satti na hoi \| pekkha jaḍilā-khitta sa hari-ala-gori la{u}dia \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, this is not Cupid’s śakti. Look! It is a yellow club thrown by Jaṭilā.**

*(praviśya)* ***jaṭilā:*** *are jihma bahmaṇa! ettha la{u}ḍī mae bisumaridā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O crooked** ***brāhmaṇa*****! I forgot my stick.**

***rādhikā*** *(apavārya sa-bhayam): sahi, parittāhi parittāhi! esā kāla-rattībba dāruṇī buḍḍhiyā maṁ diṭṭhavadī \| (iti lalitā-vṛndābhyaṁ niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside, with fear) O friend, save me! This old woman, cruel like the dark night, sees me.**

**(Rādhā exits with Lalitā and Viśākhā.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(apavārya):*  
*mama saṅgamāmṛta-rasaṁ*  
*na jighṛkṣati na ca jihāsati prakaṭam \|*  
*jaṭilā-vyāghrī-cakitā*  
*tṛṣitā rādhā-kuraṅgīyam \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside) She did not want to drink the nectar of my company, and clearly did not want to give it up. Thirsty Rādhā, like a doe, has been frightened away by the tiger Jaṭilā.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho saramā-laṅgula-kuḍile! gheppa appaṇo juṭṭhiṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O woman, crooked as a dog’s tail! Take your stick.**

***jaṭilā*** *(yaṣṭim ādāya): are subala, kīsa tumaṁ bahūdiā-veseṇa maṁ sadā biḍambesi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Taking the stick) O Subala, why do you always mock me by dressing up as a girl?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *diṣṭyā subalatayā jñānam abhūt \| (prakāśaṁ sa-narma-smitam) jaṭile, gurubhyaḥ śapamāno’smi \| rādhikaiva sādhayati \| na khalv asau subalaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) By luck, she thinks Rādhā is Subala. (Aloud with a mocking smile) O Jaṭilā! I sweat by the elders. Rādhā goes there, not Subala.**

***jaṭilā:*** *re dhutta-biakkhaṇāhaṁ sabbaṁ parikkhiduṁ khamahmi \| tā alaṁ ettha ṭhaggattaṇeṇa\| (iti niṣkrāntā)*

**Перевод:**

**O rascal! I am able to test everything, since I am observant. Enough of your rascal ways! (Exits)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe samāgaccha \| gokulam eva praviśāvaḥ \| (iti niṣkrāntau)*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, come. We will enter Gokula. (The two exit.)**

*(iti niṣkrāntāḥ sarve \|)*  
*ṣaṣṭho’ṅkaḥ*  
*śarad-vihāraḥ*

## Act Six: Autumn Pastimes

*(tataḥ praviśati jaṭilā \|)*  
*(Jaṭilā enters.)*  
*jaṭilā: sudaṁ mae ajja pīa-paḍeṇa kiduttarīā bahu ghare ciṭṭha{i} \| tā gadua jahatthaṁ ṇiddhāra{i}ssaṁ \| (parikramya paśyantī) kadhaṁ esā visāhā ghummia ghummia alinde pada{i} \| tā sadda{i}ssam \| (ity upasṛtya) bisāhe, jādo ekka-ppaharo tahabi ghummasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I have heard that Rādhā is wearing a yellow top piece of clothing in the house. I will go and see if this is true.Why is Viśākhā wandering around dizzily on the veranda and falling on the porch? I will make her speak. O Viśākhā, why are you heavy with sleep, only a few hours after waking?**

*(praviśya) viśākhā (sva-gatam): sampadaṁ rāsa-mahūsaba-gabbāsu sabbarīsu kudo niddā-gandhobi ahmānaṁ \| tā juttaṁ jebba ghummaṇam \| (iti haṭhād dṛśau vikāśya prakāśam) ajje ajja bhaavadīe nideseṇa deadā-sadaṇe ahme dīṇṇa-jāara hma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, to herself) How could we have a trace of sleep after spending the night in the rāsa dance? It is natural to stagger about. (She suddenly opens her eyes and speaks aloud) O respected Jaṭilā, on the order of Paurṇamāsī we have been awake all night in the temple.**

*jaṭilā (sva-gatam): aho jebba ppadose bahūe sejjā suṇṇā āsi \| (prakāśam) bisāhe āārehi bahūam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) From the evening Rādhā has not slept. (Aloud) O Viśākhā, call Rādhā.**

*viśākhā: halā rāhe ido ido \|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Rādhā, please come.**

*(praviśya) rādhā (caksusu vimrjya sa-jṛmbham) bisāhe baḍhaṁ ṇiddāulahmi \| (iti dṛṣṭiṁ darodghāṭya sa-śaṅkaṁ sva-gatam) kadhaṁ idha jjebba ajjā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, rubbing her eyes and yawning) O Viśākhā, I am so sleepy. Why has Jaṭilā come?**

*jaṭilā (rādhāṁ nirvarṇya sva-gatam): haddhī haddhī \| saccaṁ jjebba edaṁ pīambaram \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Rādhā, to herself) What misfortune! It is truly a yellow cloth.**

*rādhikā (janāntikam): halā sudaṁ mae sāraṅgī-muhādo jaṁ nisīdhe buḍḍhiāe tassiṁ bilāsa-puliṇe gadaṁ āsi \| tā ṇūṇaṁ ahme tattha diṭṭhahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside) Friend, I heard from Sāraṅgī that Jaṭilā came to the arena of pastimes in the middle of the night. She must have seen us there.**

*viśākhā: nahu nahu \| jaṁ kadhidaṁ buṁdāe tumaṁ ghettūṇa tirohide kahne tathā ahmesu dosu sahīsu sa-śaṅkaṁ tuha uddeśassa gadāsu esā buḍḍhī ubaṭṭhidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**No, no! Vṛndā said that she came when Kṛṣṇa disappeared with you, and we two had gone looking for you.**

*rādhikā: tado kīsa iaṁ koha-bhaaṅkarīe diṭṭhīe maṁ pekkhaṁtī ciṭṭhadi?*

**Перевод:**

**Then why does she look at me with frightful anger?**

*jaṭilā (serṣyam): micchā-jappiṇi bisāhe kiṁ ṇāma aṁdhāsi tumam?*

**Перевод:**

**(In anger) O Viśākhā you are a liar! Are you blind?**

*viśākhā (rādhāṁ vilokya sa-khedaṁ janāntikam): a{i} bilāsa-bihmale kiṁ kkhu idam?*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Rādhā, she speaks to her, lamenting.) Did you do this because of confusion during the pastimes?**

*rādhikā (svaṁ vakso nirīkṣya sa-sambhramam): halā tumaṁ jebba saraṇaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at her chest, in confusion) Friend, I take shelter of you. What should I do?**

*viśākhā (jaṭilām avekṣya saṁskṛtena):*  
*mudā kṣiptaiḥ parvottarala-hṛdayābhir yuvatibhiḥ*  
*payaḥ-pūyaiḥ pītīkṛtam atiharidrā-drava-mayaiḥ \|*  
*dukūlaṁ dor-mūlopari paridadhānāṁ priya-sakhīṁ*  
*kathaṁ rādhām ārye kuṭilita-dṛgantaṁ kalayasi \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Jaṭilā) O noble lady, why do you stare at my dear friend Rādhā from the corners of your crooked eyes? The cloth she wears on her shoulders has become yellow because the young girls in joy, whose minds were excited by the festival threw water mixed with turmeric.**

*jaṭilā (sa-viśrambham): bisāhe tue jjebba cañcalāe mama putta-gharaṁ viṇāsidaṁ jaṁ jobaṇāṁdhāṇaṁ goīṇaṁ majjhe bahūḍiā nijja{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Believing her) Viśākhā, being most fickle, you have destroyed my son’s home because you have introduced Rādhā to gopīs who are blinded by their youthfulness.**

*viśākhā: ajje kiṁ tti maṁ tumaṁ ubālahesi? naṁ ubasaṇṇaṁ dīa-māliā-pabba-lacchīṁ ubālahehi jāe sabbaṁ ābāla-buḍḍhaṁ goulaṁ jjebba ummādidam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Jaṭilā, why do you scold me? You should scold the upcoming lamp festival of Gokula which excites everyone from infants to elders.**

*jaṭilā: vatse saccaṁ kahesi \| ajja rattimi diththaṁ mae savvāo goula-kisori-o tattha puline ummatti-bhavia kiṁ pi ciṭṭhaṁdi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Child, you are right. Last night I saw all the young women of Gokula on the bank acting in a frenzied state.**

*(viśākhā sa-dṛg-bhaṅgaṁ rādhikām īkṣate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Viśākhā looks at Rādhā.)**

*jaṭilā (sa-dainyam): a{i} bisāhe! pasīda pasīda \| esa aṅguli-siharaṁ muhe ṇikkhibia abbhatthemi \| tā hma ekkaṁ aṇuggahaṁ karehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In misery) O Viśākhā, have mercy, have mercy! Placing the tip of my finger on my head, I pray to you, be merciful to me.**

*viśākhā (sa-praśrayam): ajje kiṁ tti ebbaṁ bhaṇāsi? ṇikāmaṁ āṇabehi*

**Перевод:**

**(Humbly) What are you saying? Please tell me.**

*jaṭilā: bacche tumaṁ bisuddhasi \| tā kahṇassa hatthādo rakkhehi bahūḍiam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child, you are very pure. Please protect Rādhā from the hand of Kṛṣṇa.**

*viśākhā: ajje ṇicciṁtā hohi, jaṁ lalidā kkhu ettha dakkha biakkhaṇā a \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected Jaṭilā, be assured of this. Lalitā is definitely shrewd and attentive to this.**

*jaṭilā: kahiṁ gadā lalidā?*

**Перевод:**

**Where has Lalitā gone?**

*viśākhā: pekkha, pa{u}māe samaṁ idha jebba esā āacchadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Look, she is coming with Padmā.**

*jaṭilā: ahaṁ uppaliā ṇippādaṇassa gamissaṁ \| (iti niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**I will go and make balls of cow manure now. (She exits.)**

*(praviśya padmayā saha) lalitā: sahi pa{u}me kudo āacchasi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering with Padmā) O my friend Padmā, from where did you come?**

*padmā: halā kahṇassa saāsādo \|*

**Перевод:**

**I came directly from Kṛṣṇa.**

*lalitā: kahiṁ kahṇo?*

**Перевод:**

**Where is Kṛṣṇa?**

*padmā: māladī-bāḍiā-peraṁte*

**Перевод:**

**In front of the jasmine garden.**

*lalitā: kiṁ kuṇadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**What is he doing?**

*padmā: mahumaṅgala-dudio biharadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**He is playing with Madhumaṅgala.**

*lalitā (sa-parihāsa-smitam): halā, kiṁ ṇāma saṁpūridāhiṭṭhāsi?*

**Перевод:**

**(With a laughing smile) O friend, was your desire fulfilled?**

*padmā (vihasya): mā aṇṇadhā sambhāvehi \| mae māladī-seharo ekko gaṇṭhia tassa ubahārī-kido \| (smṛtim abhinīya) halā! kadhidaṁ me kahṇeṇa—pa{u}me! tumaṁ jadhā saṁtadaṁ mālaṁ samappesi, ebbaṁ lalidā bi me vicitta-ḍāu-lacchiṁ \| tā esā lehā-pattiā tue tassa hatthe deā tti \| (iti patrikām arpayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) Do not think otherwise! Making a best crown, I gave it to him. O friend, Kṛṣṇa said to me, “Padmā, just as you always offer me garlands, Lalitā give me various mineral colors. Give her this letter.”**

*lalitā (gṛhītvā sva-gatam): kadābi kahṇassa mae cāurāo ṇa samappidotthi \| tā ettha abareṇa keṇābi rahasseṇa hodabbaṁ \| (iti prakāśaṁ patrikāṁ vācayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Taking it, saying to herself) I have never given Kṛṣṇa mineral colors. This must have some secret meaning. (She reads the letter aloud.)**

*tvayā mukta-giriḥ pāṇau*  
*mamātuccha-pada-sthitiḥ \|*  
*nidhīyatām adhīrākṣi*  
*rāgi-dhātu-paricchadaḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O girl with unsteady eyes! You should place a covering of minerals from the top of the mountain on my hand.**

*(iti kṣaṇaṁ vimṛśya sva-gatam) rādhā mama pāṇau nidhīyatām \| ebbaṁ saṅkedeṇa imiṇā āṇattam \| (prakāśam) sahi tathā karissaṁ \| tā aggado rāhiaṁ āpucchia sāhehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Considering a moment, to herself she speaks.) “Rādhā should be placed in my hand.” This is indicated by the secret message. (Aloud) I will do as it says. First go and greet Rādhā.**

*padmā (rādhikāṁ upetya sa-narma-smitam): halā rāhe diṭṭhiā nibbibādaṁ jādam \| jadhā gouliṁda-ṇaṁdaṇeṇa ahmāṇaṁ aṁsuāiṁ abahāridāiṁ tadhā ahmehiṁ pi tassa idaṁ pīdaṁsuaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Going to Rādhā she smiles.) O my friend Rādhā! By good fortune, there have been no quarrels. Just as Kṛṣṇa stole our clothing, we stole his yellow cloth.**

*lalitā (smitvā): a{i} ṇillajji! kuṅkuma-paṅka-piñjaridaṁ pia-sahīe uttarīaṁ pekkhia kiṁ tti anatthaṁ āsaṅkasi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O shameless one! Looking at Rādhā’s cloth made yellow** ***by kuṁkuma*** **paste, why do you suspect some misfortune?**

*padmā (sa-smitam) halā rāhe aṇujāṇīhi maṁ \| turīaṁ sahitthalīṁ gadua kahṇassa līlāṁ gāaṁtiṁ pia-sahīṁ candāalīaṁ suhāba{i}ssam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O Rādhā, permit me to go. I will quickly go to Sakhī-sthali and satisfy Candrāvalī who is singing pastimes about Kṛṣṇa.**

*viśākhā (vihasya): pa{u}me dhaṇṇāo tuhme jāhiṁ adaṁsaṇe bi kahṇassa bilāsa-gīdihiṁ pia-sahī candāalī suhābīadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) O Padmā, you are fortunate, for you will satisfy Candrāvalī by singing beautiful songs about Kṛṣṇa.**

*padmā: bisāhe tuhmehiṁ kīsa tathā na kijja{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Viśākhā, why do you not do this?**

*viśākhā: a{i} kudo ahmānaṁ idisaṁ bhāā-dheaṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**How can we be so fortunate?**

*padmā: halā kadhaṁ ṇatthi?*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, why do you not singing about him?**

*viśākhā: muddhe! kahṇassa ṇāma-mette patthude sahī rāhiā bikkhubbhadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O innocent girl! As soon as we pronounce Kṛṣṇa’s name, Rādhā becomes completely disturbed. How could she hear a whole song? It is possible for Candrāvalī because she has no prema.**

*padmā (sva-gatam): sa-pakkhe pemukkariso imāe bikkhābido \| hodu \| (prakāśam) bisāhe, tuhme jjebba suṭṭhu suhiṇīo \| ahmānaṁ kkhu kā bi dukkha-dasā anubaṭṭha{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) By this statement she is announcing to her friends their greater prema. Let that be. (Aloud) O Viśākhā, you are very happy. We, however, are in a condition of suffering.**

*lalitā: pa{u}me kkhu tuhmāṇaṁ kiṁ pi dukkhaṁ sambhaviadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā, why are you unhappy?**

*padmā: halā lalide, mā ebbaṁ bhaṇa \| jaṁ hāra-gaṇṭhaṇa-kesa-pasāhaṇa-bimbāhara-rañjaṇa-pahudīhiṁ candāalīe nepacchāiṁ sabbadā kuṇaṁtīṇaṁ ahmānaṁ dukkha-jālassa aṁto ṇatthi\|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Lalitā, do not speak like that. We are constantly engaged in dressing Candrāvalī by stringing garlands, arranging her hair, coloring her lips etc. There is no end to our great suffering in doing this all the time.**

*viśākhā (vihasya): halā pa{u}me! saccaṁ tuhmāṇaṁ bahūiṁ dukkhāiṁ \| ahmānaṁ uṇa ekkaṁ jjebba \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) O Padmā, it is true that you have many sufferings. We have only one suffering.**

*padmā: halā, kiṁ taṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**What is that?**

*viśākhā: pa{u}me jā kā bi macca-dullahā āgāsatārā ppaphuradi \| tattha jādāhilāsassa kassa bi kāliṁdī-kula-ṇaṁdiṇo samadassa gaṁdha-kala-hīṁdassa sabbadā abbhatthaṇā-kadatthaṇaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā, Rādhā shines like a star in the sky, impossible for mortals to attain. Kṛṣṇa, like an intoxicated, young male elephant enjoying on the bank of the Yamunā, always yearns for her with tormented mind.**

***lalitā*** *(smitvā): bisāhe, aṇṇaṁ bi ekkaṁ garuaṁ dukkhaṁ tue kadhaṁ bisumaridam?*

**Перевод:**

**O Viśākhā, you have answered nicely. But why did you forget one other greater suffering we have?**

***viśākhā:*** *lalide, kiṁ taṁ sumarābehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**What is that? Remind me.**

***lalitā:*** *a{i} ujjue rāhīe pāa-pallaahmi jāvaa-rāassa kkhaṇe kkhaṇe biraaṇaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O honest Viśākhā, moment by moment we must apply lac to Rādhā’s tender feet.**

***viśākhā*** *(sa-hasam): aliā-saṅkiṇi lalide, viramehi viramehi \| kahṇassa uttamaṁge ḍhāuṇaṁ rāo jjebba rehadi ṇa kkhu jāvaāṇaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing)You worry about misfortune. Stop this. The color of minerals from Kṛṣṇa’s limbs are on her feet, not lac.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-lajjam): halā pa{u}me! imāṇaṁ dummuhīṇaṁ ppalābaṁ anāaṇṇia tuṇṇaṁ pia-sahīṁ caṁdāaliaṁ jjebba jāhi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Embarrassed) O my friend Padmā, do not listen to the gossip of foul-mouthed women. Quickly go to your dear friend Candrāvalī.**

***padmā:*** *jadha ādisadi pia-sahī \| (iti niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**I will do as my dear friend says. (She exits.)**

***lalitā*** *(sva-gatam):* *ehniṁ kahṇassa āṇṇaṁ karissaṁ \| (prakāśam) halā rāhe ehi \| pupphaṁ abaciṇia bhaabaṁtaṁ sūraṁ pūahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Now I will execute Kṛṣṇa’s order. (Aloud) My dear friend Rādhā! Come, after picking some flowers, let us worship the sun.**

***rādhikā*** *(sva-gatam):* *diṭṭhiā hiaa-ṭṭhido jjebba me kāmo imāe ubaṇīdo jaṁ kahṇassa daṁsaṇaṁ ettha sambhave \| (prakāśam) jadhāhiroadi pia-sahīe \| (iti niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) By luck, she can fulfill the desire in my heart since it may be possible to see Kṛṣṇa there. (Aloud) If it pleases you. (They exit.)**

*(tataḥ praviśati madhumaṅgalenopāsyamānaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa enters with Madhumaṅgala.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*nava-stavaka-vallarī-caṭula-gandha-vandīkṛta-*  
*bhramad-bhramara-jhaṅkṛti-plutam udagra-guñjārbudam \|*  
*śarat-kṛśa-kalindajā-pulina-vṛnda-saṁvardhitaṁ*  
*parisphurati candraka-sthagitam adya vṛndāvanam \|\|3\|\|*

*punar nibhālya sānandam)*  
*śaradi mukharitāśās tāra-nādāvalībhir*  
*valad-avicala-netrāḥ paśya vṛndāvane’dya \|*  
*vidadhati raṇa-raṅgaṁ vāsitā-saṅga-hetoḥ*  
*sa-rabhasam uru-śṛṅgaiḥ saṅgave puṅgavendrāḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Today Vṛndāvana vibrates with the humming of wandering bees like panegyrists because of the transient scent of new flower clusters on the creepers. It gleams with millions of guñja berries, with increased banks because of the meager Yamunā, and with crowds of open peacock tails. (He looks around again in bliss.)**

**Look! In Vṛndāvana in the autumn, the directions echo with a multitude of loud sounds. With firmly moving eyes, the bulls passionately fight with each other using huge horns when brought together because of the cows in heat.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sarvato vilokya):*  
*tuha saṅgameṇa ṇūṇaṁ muunda buṁdāḍaī ghana-cchāā \|*  
*ua dambheṇa kuruṇḍaa-bharassa pīdambaraṁ dhara{i} \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking all around) O Kṛṣṇa, by associating with you, the forest has become very dark in color.The forest wears yellow cloth in the form of the many** ***kuraṇṭaka*** **(yellow amaranth) flowers.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *kim adya niṣṭaṅkita-saṅketa-lekhārthaya pūrṇa-manorathī-kariṣye’haṁ lalitayā \| hanta śārada-mādhurī-sandoha-sandālitapi vṛndāṭavī-kakṣā khañjanākṣi-viprakarṣād ānanda-bindum api na me sandadhāti \| tad veṇu-saṅketaṁ sañcarayāmi \|(iti tathā kurvan)*

*divyo rathāṅgi samayaḥ sakhi saṅgamasya*  
*jajñe varāṅgi tarasā kuru pakṣa-pātam \|*  
*adhvānam ardha-nayanena vilokamānaḥ*  
*śokād ayaṁ sahacaras tava rauravīti \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Will I be able to fulfill my desires today through Lalitā to whom I gave the letter with a secret message? Ah! When I am separated from Rādhā, the interior of the Vṛndāvana forest, though filled with the sweetness of autumn, does not give me a drop of bliss. I will give hints using my flute. (He plays his flute.)**

**O** ***cakravakī*** **bird! The beautiful time for meeting your friend has arrived. O beautiful Rādhā, quickly sympathize with me. Your friend, looking at the path with half-closed eyes, cries for you in grief.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bho baassa, kim edaṁ apūbbaṁ bāditaṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, what was this extraordinary music you played?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, kuraṅgī-lokanārthaṁ mamāyam udyamaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, that was my effort to see the doe.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *saccaṁ kkhu kadhidaṁ \| kiṁ tu ekkaṁ akkharaṁ aṇṇadhā kiam \|*

**Перевод:**

**You have spoken the truth. But one word you spoke was not correct.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, sādhu viditaṁ kuraṅgī-locanārtham eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, you have understood correctly. It was an effort for doe-eyed Rādhā.**

*(****nepathye****)*  
*pibantīnāṁ vaṁśī-ravam iha gavāṁ karṇa-culukaiḥ*  
*payah-purā dūrād diśi diśi tathā śuśruvur amī \|*  
*akāle puṣpyadbhis tarubhir abhitaḥ śobhitam idaṁ*  
*yathā vṛndāraṇyaṁ dadhi-maya-nadī-mātṛkam abhūt \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Streams of milk from cows, drinking the sound of the flute with ears like cupped hands, flow from afar in all directions. The beautiful forest of Vṛndāvana has become a river of yogurt because the trees are flowering out of season.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe dakṣiṇataḥ paśya paśya \|*  
*tuṅgas tāmroru-śṛṅgaḥ sphurad-aruṇa-khuro ramya-piṅgekṣaṇa-śrīḥ*  
*kaṇṭha-vyālambi-ghaṇṭo dharaṇi-viluṭhitoccaṇḍa-lāṅgula-daṇḍaḥ \|*  
*so’yaṁ kailāsa-pāṇḍu-dyutir atula-kakun-maṇḍalo naicikīnāṁ*  
*cakre bhāti priyo me parimala-tulitotphulla-padmaḥ kakudmī \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, look to the right. Shining among the cows is my dear bull Padmagandha towering with huge red horns, gleaming red hooves, attractive, beautiful pink eyes, a bell hanging from his neck, tail dragging on the ground, his huge, round hump gleaming white like Mount Kailāsa, .**

*(tataḥ praviśati sakhībhyām anugamyamānā rādhā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā accompanied by her sakhīs enters.)**

***rādhā*** *(sva-gatam):* *jado disādo veṇu-saddo āado sā disā mohidāe mae ṇa bhālidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) This cannot be the direction from which the flute sound came. I have been bewildered.**

***lalitā*** *(sotprāsa-smitam): halā rāhie, kīsa akāṁḍe hariṇa-kaṇṇībba tumaṁ jādāsi?*

**Перевод:**

**(With derisive smile) My friend Rādhā, why suddenly do you prick up your ears like a doe?**

***rādhikā:*** *lalide, kiṁ tti appaṇo dhammaṁ parassa appesi? saccaṁ tumaṁ jjebba hariṇī jaṁ kala-saddeṇa harijjantī dīsasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā, why do you attribute your qualities to others? Truly you are a doe, joyful because of the sound of the flute.**

***lalitā:*** *rāhe, tumaṁ kkhu hariṇī jaṁ raṅgiṇī nāma hariṇī tuhma sahī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, you are a doe, since the doe Raṅginī is your friend.**

***rādhikā*** *(sva-gatam):* *diṭṭhiā esā kābi sorabbha-dhārā puro bāḍiādo dūdībba maṁ āaḍḍhadi\| (iti sa-vyājaṁ puraḥ prayāti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**By luck, this perfumed garden before us attracts me like a messenger. (She makes a pretense of going forward.)**

***viśākhā*** *(smitvā): halā rāhe! kīsa tumaṁ bhiṅgībba kimpi gaṁdhaṁ sappasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) My friend Rādhā, why do you search for fragrance like a bee?**

***rādhikā:*** *bisāhe! aggado phullāiṁ kusumāiṁ dīsantī \| tā edāiṁ ghettūṇa taṁ mittaṁ pūa{i}ssaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā, there are blossoming flowers. We will take them and worship the sun.**

***lalitā:*** *saccaṁ mittasya aṇurāaṁ tumaṁ taraledi \| so dāba gahaṇa-carassa jjebba na kkhu gaaṇa-carassa \|*

**Перевод:**

**Attraction to a friend (the sun) makes you unsteady. It is attraction for someone moving in the forest, not the sky.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-praṇaya-roṣam): a{i} adakkhiṇe kamala-baṁdhuṁ kadhemi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In affectionate anger) O unkind friend! I speak of the friend of the lotus (sun).**

***lalitā:*** *sahi, kīsa āāraṁ saṅgobesi?*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, why do you hide the meaning?**

***viśākhā:*** *lalide, sapattī-bhāeṇa īsā ccea saṅgobedi, ṇa uṇa pia-sahī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā, she hides it out of anger at a co-wife. She is not a dear friend.**

***rādhā*** *(sa-bhrū-bhaṅgam): a{i} vāme! attaṇo hiaa-ṭṭhidaṁ atthaṁ para-muṇḍe kīsa pāḍesi? tā tubarehi \| jaṁ ṇādidūre jjebba so tuhmāṇaṁ bimbāhara-kaṇḍu-khaṇḍaṇo \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Frowning) Impudent girl! Fool! Why do you reveal your thoughts to a vile person? Go quickly. Not far away is he who relieves the itching in your lips.**

***lalitā:*** *rāhe, ākomāraṁ ahmāṇaṁ akkhaḍidaṁ kulaṅgaṇā-bbadaṁ buṁdābaṇa-ladāo jjebba jāṇenti \| tā āttaṇo muheṇa kiṁ kadha{i}ssahma?*

**Перевод:**

**The creepers of Vṛndāvana know our unbroken vows of chastity from childhood. What more can we say with our mouths?**

***rādhikā*** *(vihasya): a{i} pa{i}bbade! janenti janenti \| tado jjebba kalli tuha bhua-balliṇo aṅke saṅkamidaṁ diṭṭhaṁ mae maara-kuṇḍala-lañchaṇam \| tadhā jjebba visāhāe tappha-tuli-obari pphuḍidaṁ sihaṇḍa-kirīḍaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O chaste wife! They know, they know. In the morning I saw the mark of** **a** ***makara*** **earring on your creeper arm. On Viśākhā bed I clearly saw a peacock crown.**

***lalitā*** *(smitvā): para-paribādini! abehi abehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O criticizer of others! Go away, go away!**

***viśākhā:*** *rāhe! kittiaṁ jhampissasi? ṇa kkhu candāloe canda-kānta-silā appasiṇṇā hoduṁ pahabadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā! Are you hiding something artificially? The moonstone cannot stop melting in the moonlight.**

***rādhikā*** *(puro sa-camatkāram): lalide! tuṇṇaṁ aṇujāṇehi, palāissaṁ \| (ity utkampate)*

**Перевод:**

**(Astonished at what is in front) Lalitā, excuse me but I will flee. (Trembling)**

***lalitā*** *(sa-śaṅkam): rādhe, kīsa bhāesi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Worried) Rādhā, what frightens you?**

***rādhikā*** *(sābhyasūyam): a{i} vaṅke! alaṁ alieṇa imiṇā ujju-attaṇeṇa \| ṇūṇaṁ imassa lampaḍassa hatthe pakkheduṁ maṁ dūraṁ āṇīdāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With hatred) Crooked woman! Enough of your polite words! You brought me far away in order to throw me into the hands of the lecher.**

***lalitā*** *(nipuṇaṁ nibhālya sva-gatam): ṇūṇaṁ dūrado viloijjantaṁ tamālaṁ jjebba iaṁ kahṇaṁ mannedi \| (prakāśam) huṁ, dāṇīṁ kadhaṁ palāissasi? laddho mae osaro \| (iti rādhām ākarṣati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking carefully she says to herself) Seeing the** ***tamāla*** **tree from afar, she thinks it is Kṛṣṇa. (Aloud) Ah, why should you flee now? I will take this opportunity. (She pulls Rādhā.)**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-kātaryam) sahi bisāhe! parittāhi parittāhi \| saraṇāadahmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In pain) O my friend Viśākhā! Save me, save me! I surrender to you.**

***visakhā:*** *a{i} pemmubbhamide! kadhaṁ tillokaṁ jjebba de kahṇaedi \| pekkha eso palāsī, ṇa kkhu tujjha vilāsī!*

**Перевод:**
