**O Rādhā, you are confused by prema! You take everything in the three worlds to be Kṛṣṇa. Look, this is a tree, not your lover.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kathaṁ nedānīm api pratyāsannā tanv-aṅgī? tan muralīm īrayāmi \| (iti tathā kurvan):*  
*ayi sudhākara-maṇḍali maṇḍaya*  
*tvam aṭavīṁ mṛdu-pada-visarpaṇaiḥ \|*  
*udaya-śaila-taṭī-nihitekṣaṇo*  
*nanu cakora-yuvā paritapyate \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Why has Rādhā not come yet? I will play the flute. (He plays) Ah! O moon, decorate the forest by spreading your soft rays. The young** ***cakora,*** **looking in the east, suffers.**

***viśākhā*** *(svayaṁ dhairyam avaṣṭabhya): halā rāhe! kīsa tumaṁ bhamanti kalambaṁ olambesi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Becoming serious) My friend Rādhā, why are you taking support of the** ***kadamba*** **tree in confusion?**

***lalitā:*** *sahi baṁśie! bāraṁ bāraṁ tumaṁ baṁdemi, jaṁ ugghāḍida-rahassā tue rāhī kidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend the flute! I offer respects to you repeatedly, since that you have drawn the secret out of Rādhā.**

*(rādhikā sa-lajjam avahitthaṁ nāṭayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā shyly conceals her feelings.)**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*viśadbhiḥ karṇānte tava visṛmarair adya muralī-*  
*kalair ūru-sthambho gurur ajani rambhoru tarasā \|*  
*viluptābhūd dṛṣṭir nayana-jala-vṛṣṭi-vyatikaraiḥ*  
*praṇītābhir yatnāt tad alam avahittha-laharībhiḥ \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā with beautiful thighs! The pillars of your thighs have immediately become heavy because of the soft sound of the flute, flowing today into your ear with various words. You have lost your sight because of a calamitous shower of tears. What is the use of endeavouring to hide your waves of feelings that the flute arouses?**

***viśākhā:*** *lalide, ko dāṇīṁ avahitthāe osaro? (saṁskṛtena)*  
*trapābhicaraṇa-krame parama-siddhir ātharvaṇi*  
*smarānala-samindhane sapadi sāmidheni-dhvaniḥ \|*  
*tathātma-paramātmanor upaniṣan-mayī saṅgame*  
*vilāsa-muralī-bharā virutir adya vairāyate \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā, is it appropriate to hide feelings now? The sound emanating from the playful flute has today become your enemy, a most powerful mantra from the Atharva Veda for kindling the fire of love, for making women unchaste, for producing the union of self with the Lord as described in the Upaniṣads.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-kṣobham): sahi, saccaṁ kadhesi \| ahmāṇaṁ vairiṇī saṁbuttā dāruṇī baṁsiā, tā ubālahissaṁ \| (iti saṁskṛtena)*  
*sūtis te dhanuṣaś ca vaṁśa-varato vande tayor antimaṁ*  
*viddhe yena janas tanuṁ viharayan nāntaś ciraṁ tāmyati \|*  
*viddhānāṁ hṛdi māra-patri-viṣamair dhvāneṣubhir nas tvayā*  
*krūre vaṁśī na jīvanaṁ na ca mṛtir ghorāvirāsīd daśā \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Disturbed) You speak the truth. The terrible flute has become my enemy. I will scold it.**

**You and the bow are both born from excellent bamboo. But I offer respects to the bow because a person pierced by the bow gives up his body without suffering for a long time.**

**O cruel flute! When you pierce us in the heart with the arrows of your sound, more painful that Cupid’s arrows, we enter a terrible state which is neither life nor death.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(puro vilokya sānandam):*  
*bhavitā savidhe’tra rādhikā*  
*yad iyaṁ riṅgati raṅgiṇī puraḥ \|*  
*mṛga-lañchana-lekhayeva yā*  
*mṛga-mūrtir na tayā viyujyate \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking in front with bliss) Rādhikā will soon appear here since Raṅginī frolics in front of us. The deer is never separated from Rādhā, just as the mark of the deer is not separated from the moon.**

*(punar nirūpya) sakhe, jñataṁ jñatam \| nāsau rādhikā-nyaṅkuḥ, yad niraṅko nedīyān induḥ\| (iti vismayam abhinīya)*  
*aṅkāt parityajya puraḥ kuraṅgaṁ*  
*śaṅke sudhāṁśur bhuvam āsasāda \| (punar nibhālya)*  
*āṁ jñatam utphulla-vilāsa-vṛndair*  
*ānandi rādhā-vadanaṁ cakāsti \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking again) Friend, I understand. That is not Rādhā’s deer. It is the spotless moon very close. (Showing wonder) I think the moon has thrown the deer from its lap and has descended to earth. Yes, now I understand. The face of Rādhā shines blissfully with a host of blossoming gestures.**

*(ity agre sarati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(He moves forward.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-parihāsam): bho baassa mā dhava \| lahu lahu jāhi \| ahavā tumaṁ kiṁ tti duṣijjasi jaṁ dhūtta-kisorīhiṁ duṭṭha-maṁteṇa ummādido si \| tā imassiṁ jogge osare tumaṁ nibāria siṇehassa ṇikkidaṁ karissaṁ \| (iti pāṇim ādadāti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) O friend, do not run. Go slowly. Have you been poisoned? The wicked young girls have made you insane, using evil mantras. Taking this opportunity, restraining you, I will have your affection answered.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, sādhu cestase yad adya rādhikopasarpaṇe kampena kṛta-vighnasya me datta-hastāvalambo’si \| (iti parikramya)*

*iyam ati-tṛṣitaṁ varānurago-*  
*jvala-sumanāḥ kamanīya-patra-lekhāḥ \|*  
*mama vara-tanur ācakarṣa cittaṁ*  
*madhupam aśoka-lateva puṣpitāgrā \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, you are acting properly since today, in pursuing Rādhā, you supported me with your hand when I have difficulty because of trembling. (Walking) This woman with excellent body whom I most desire, her mind decorated with the best love, decorated with attractive designs, attracts my heart just as an flowering** ***aśoka*** **creeper attracts the bee.**

***rādhikā*** *(kṛṣṇam apāṅgena vilokya sva-gatam saṁskṛtena):*  
*nava-manasija-līlā-bhrānta-netrānta-bhajaḥ*  
*sphuṭa-kiśalaya-bhaṅgī-saṅgi-karṇāñcalasya \|*  
*milita-mṛdula-mauler mālayā mālatīnāṁ*  
*madayati mama medhāṁ mādhurī mādhavasya \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Glances at Kṛṣṇa and speaks to herself) The sweetness of Kṛṣṇa, whose eyes wander about playfully like young Cupid, whose ear is decorated with a blossoming shoot, whose delicate head is decorated with jasmine garlands, agitates my memory.**

***viśākhā*** *(vihasya saṁskṛtena):*  
*vaśīcakre kṛṣṇas tava parimalair eva balibhir*  
*vilāsānāṁ vṛndaṁ katham iva mudhā kandalayasi \|*  
*jaye pāṇau datte raṇa-paṭubhir agresara-bhaṭaiḥ*  
*svayaṁ ko vikrāntiṁ punar iha jigīṣuḥ praṇayati \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) Kṛṣṇa is controlled by your strong perfume. Why do you uselessly manifest various indifferent gestures? When victory has been given to you by the best troops skilful in battle, what person, desiring victory over him, would r try show further prowess?**

***rādhikā:*** *a{i} dummuhi! ettiahmi saṅkaḍe maṁ ārobia ajjabi ṇa bīsantāsi \| tā ṇikkiba-hiaaṁ tumaṁ ujjhia ahaṁ siṇiddhaṁ pia-sahīṁ lalidaṁ caraṇaṁ pabisāmi \| (iti tathā kṛtvā saṁskṛtena)*

*atrāyāntaṁ calam api hariṁ lokayantī baliṣṭhāṁ*  
*tām ālambya priya-sakhi ghane nāsmi kuñje nilīnā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O foul-mouthed Viśākhā, even now you do not tire to give me difficulty. I will give you up since you are merciless and take shelter of my dear friend Lalitā. (She goes to Lalitā.) Dear friend , taking support of you, since you are strong, I will not hide in the bower even though I see Kṛṣṇa coming here.**

***lalitā*** *(sa-narma-smitaṁ saṁskṛtena)*  
*asmān mugdhe hṛdaya-nihitād adya pītāmbarāt te*  
*śakto nānyaḥ kuca-paricaye mat-puro mā vyathiṣṭhāḥ \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(With merry laughter) O beautiful Rādhā! It is not possible for anyone other than Kṛṣṇa or the yellow cloth on your heart to have intimacy with your breast.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānandam): kalyāṇi kāle labdhāsi \| (iti rādhām upasarpati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(In bliss) O beautiful Rādhā, now I have attained you. (Approaches Rādhā)**

***lalitā*** *(sāṭopaṁ parikramya kṛṣṇaṁ vārayantī): cha{i}lla! ṇahu ṇahu esā tuhma parihāsa-joggā ahmāṇaṁ pia-sahī \| tā abehi abehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Angrily approaches and blocks Kṛṣṇa.) O lecher! You should never joke with my dear friend. Go away!**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-smitam): lalite, nedaṁ goṣṭhāṅganam \| paśya—vṛndāṭavī-kukṣir asau \| tan neha vaḥ prabhaviṣṇutā \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā, this is not the cowherd village. Look, this is the middle of the Vṛndāvana forest. You do not have influence here.**

***lalitā:*** *kahṇa, aṇṇāo tāo kkhu muddhiāo jāo tuatto bi suṭṭhu bhāenti \| esāhmi pasiddhā lalidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Other foolish gopīs fear you, but I am the famous Lalitā.**

*(rādhikā calāpaṅgena kṛṣṇaṁ vilokya kampaṁ nāṭayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Rādhā glances at Kṛṣṇa and begins to tremble.)**

***lalitā:*** *rāhe, kīsa sajjhaseṇa kampasi jaṁ esā jīadi lalidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Why are you trembling in fear? Lalitā is living.**

***rādhikā:*** *lalide, gahidāiṁ bandhuga-pupphāiṁ \| tā ehi, kālindī-tīraṁ gacchahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā, we have got** ***bandhuka*** **flowers. Come, let us go to the bank of the Yamunā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kaṭhore! katham āhṛta-bandhujīvā dūraṁ gantum udyatāsi?(iti panthānaṁ āvṛnvan)*  
*parītaṁ śṛṅgeṇa sphuṭatara-śilā-śyāmala-rucaṁ*  
*calad-vetraṁ vaṁśa-vyatikara-lasan-mekhalam amum \|*  
*atikramyottuṅgaṁ dharaṇi-dharam agre katham itas*  
*tvayā gantuṁ śakyā taraṇi-duhitus tīra-saraṇīm \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O hard-hearted woman! Why, after stealing** ***bandhujīva*** **flowers, are you enthusiastic to go far away. (Blocking the path) Leaving the tall mountain Govardhana, spread with sharp points, shining with black rocks, covered with reeds and having a thicket of bamboos on its gleaming base, you can go by the path that winds by the bank of the Yamunā.**

***rādhikā*** *(vakraṁ vilokya huṁ kurvatī): ṇāara mama doso ṇatthi \| dāṇīṁ esā goulesarīṁ anusarissaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Frowning she protests) O lecher, I am not at fault. I will go to Yaśodā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *rādhe, kiṁ vibhīṣikayā \| kāmaṁ gamyatām \| tad-bhuja-mūla-sthaṁ pīta-dukūlam eva mamānukūlam \|(iti rādhāṁ didhīrṣati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O Rādhā, do not be frightened. Please go as you like. The yellow cloth on your arm is to my liking. (He tries to catch her.)**

***rādhikā*** *(bhrū-kuṭim ābadhya saṁskṛtena):*  
*sādhvīnāṁ dhūri dhāryā lalitā-saṅgena garvitā cāsmi \|*  
*hitam ālapāmi mādhava pathi mādya bhujaṅgatāṁ racaya \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I am the best of all chaste girls. Now that I am in the company of Lalitā, I feel very confident. I give you this good advice. O Mādhava, do not become a snake on my path.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *lalite, kiṁ aśrāvi vāg-bhaṅgir asyāḥ? tad ahaṁ nāparādhyāmi \| (iti bhuja-daṇḍāv uddaṇḍayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā, did I hear her playing on words? Then I am not at fault. (He raises his two arms.)**

***lalitā*** *(rādhāṁ pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā): kahṇa! sabba-loa-sālahanijja-guṇobi tumaṁ goulindassa ṇandaṇo si \| tā ṇedaṁ de dullīlataṇaṁ ahmesu joggam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Putting Rādhā behind her) You are Nanda’s son, praised by all people for your good qualities.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *a{i} gabbide! kiṁ ti buṁdāanaṁ biddhaṁsia tuhmehiṁ ahma pia-baassa pupphāiṁ harissanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Proud Lalitā! Destroying Vṛndāvana, you have stolen the flowers of my friend.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe tūrṇaṁ gaṇayāsāṁ puṣpāṇi yathā tat-saṅkhyayā kaṇṭhato hara-maṇīn āharāmi\|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, quickly count their flowers so that I can take the same number for jewels from their necks.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *pia-baassa! kidaṁ gaṇaṇaṁ \| tā rattāṇaṁ pupphāṇaṁ parivaṭṭena pa{u}ma-rāgāiṁ geṇha \| paṇḍurāṇaṁ uṇa hīra-mauttiāiṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear friend! I have finished counting. In exchange for the red flowers, take their rubies. In exchange for the white flowers take their diamonds and pearls.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe paryālocayam \| nāmūni puṣpa-mūlya-tulyāni \| tataḥ katham ebhir eva paryāptiḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, let me think. The jewels are not as valuable as the flowers. How can the jewels compensate?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-kāku-prapañcam): baassa! eso anugado bahmano abbhatthedi \| tā imehiṁ jjebba santuṭṭho hohi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In a plaintive voice) Friend! As a loyal** ***brāhmaṇa*** **I request that you should be satisfied with those jewels.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *yathā bravīṣi, vayasya \|*

**Перевод:**

**I will do as you say.**

***lalitā*** *(vihasya): ajja ssāmiṇo joggo jjebba amācco si \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) O respected master, I am a minister fit for such a master.**

***viśākhā*** *(sālīka-sambhramam) kahṇa, dūre ciṭṭhehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Showing false alarm) Kṛṣṇa, stay away.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kuṭile, kim iti?*

**Перевод:**

**Crooked Viśākhā, what is this?**

***viśākhā:*** *pekkha \| saṁrambheṇa saṅgaraṁ gamidā canda-hāsaṁ ullāsedi ahma-pia-sahī rāhā\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look, our friend Rādhā has come to fight because of anger and passion and shines with a moonlike smile.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): mugdhe paśya \| ahaṁ ca prapañcita-gāḍha-romāñca-kañcuko’smi \| tad ayatnaṁ rāmā-ratnaṁ hariṣyāmi \| (iti rādhām upasarpati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O beautiful girl, look. I have developed armor in the form of intense hairs standing on end. I will easily steal the jewel named Lakṣmī. (He approaches Rādhā.)**

***lalitā*** *(saṁrambham abhinīya): kahṇa, pekkhāmi de sāhasaṁ \| rāhiā-cchāaṁ pi tumaṁ phaṁsehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Feigning anger) Kṛṣṇa, I see your impudence. Please touch only the shadow of Rādhā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, nūnaṁ lalitā-rūpeṇa mahā-bhairavīyaṁ prādurbhūtā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, Mahā-bhairavī has appeared as Lalitā.**

***rādhikā:*** *halā kallāṇī hohi \| (iti lalitāṁ sākūṭam āliṅgati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, be peaceful. (She embraces Lalitā without duplicity.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(janāntikam): lalite, vimuñca kāṭhiṇyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside) O Lalitā, give up your harshness.**

***lalitā:*** *ukkoaṁ me dehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Give me a bribe.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): lalite satyaṁ te bravīmi rādhām api vipralabhya sāyam anaṅga-saṅgare tvām eva pratirīrayiṣye \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Lalitā, I will tell you the truth. Deceiving Rādhā, tonight I will be your opponent in the battle of love.**

***lalitā*** *(sa-rosaṁ parāvṛtya): abehi vidūsaa abehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Turning to him with anger) Go away, clown. Go away!**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *kathayotkocaṁ yatra te tuṣṭiḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tell me what bribe will satisfy you.**

***lalitā:*** *ṇāara! puppha-maggaṇa-raṅgeṇa buṁdābaṇaṁ bhammantī dūedi me pia-sahī \| tā dibba-pupphehiṁ ṇaṁ alaṁkadua suhābehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lecher! You afflict my friend who wanders in Vṛndāvana, searching for flowers and for pastimes of love. Make her happy by decorating her with the attractive flowers.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): yathābhirocate tubhyam \| (iti parikramya darpārabhaṭīṁ nāṭayan) lalite, bāḍhaṁ vikruśyatām \| na tvāṁ tṛṇāya manye \| (iti rādhikā-hāraṁ ākraṣṭuṁ karaṁ prasārayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Whatever pleases you. (He approaches, showing pride.) O Lalitā, you can cry out loudly! I do not consider you to be even a piece of grass. (He extends his hand to take Rādhā’s necklace.)**

***lalitā*** *(vāmaṁ vilokya sa-smitam): cha{i}lla! sūra-dea-pūjā kide kida-siṇāṇaṁ pia-sahīṁ akida-siṇāṇo kkhu tumaṁ mā phaṁsehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looks obliquely, smiling) Lecher, do not touch my friend who has taken a bath in order to worship the sun, since you have not taken a bath.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *ayi madāndhe samantād ullasini prakhedāmbu-pure mayi kathaṁ kṛta-mahābhisekaṁ na paśyasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O girl blinded by pride! Do you not see that I, while playing, have taken a complete, great ceremonial bath in my perspiration?**

***lalitā*** *(rādhām antarayanti sa-mantharyam) halā uddaṇḍa-kala-tamāla-gholena vana-khandena imassa pa-andada dusaha kida \| tā ahme haraṁ rakkhiduṁ kkhaṇaṁ somma hohma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To Rādhā, slowly) He has become intolerable because of the thick forest, filled with terrifying, tall** ***tamāla*** **trees emitting soft tunes. We should be gentle in order to protect our necklaces.**

***viśākhā:*** *kīdisaṁ sommā hohma?*

**Перевод:**

**How should we be gentle?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *hīhī ṇijjidāo gabbida-goiāo \| (iti nṛtyati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**Haha! The gopīs’ pride has been defeated. (Dances)**

***rādhikā:*** *a{i} muddhe lalide! bhaavantassa ubāsaṇaṁ tue ajja kiṁ visumaridaṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful Lalitā! Have you forgotten about the Lord’s worship?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *dei rāhie! kealaṁ tuhme jjebba ubāsaṇaṁ karedha tti mā gabbāedha jaṁ ahme bi ubāsaṇaṁ karehma \|*

**Перевод:**

**O worshippable Rādhā! Do not be proud, thinking that you alone engage in worship. We also perform worship.**

***viśākhā:*** *ajja kīdisaṁ tam?*

**Перевод:**

**Respected friend, what type of worship?**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *bhaadi bisāhe, suṇāhi \| gandha-puppha-purosaraṁ ṇiuñja-bediā-majjhe ujjāaraṇa-bhūiṭṭhaṁ tad-ekkagga-cittadāe kaṅkaṇa-ṇeurāṇaṁ saddobāsaṇaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected Viśākhā, listen. First we place sandalwood and flower on an altar in the bower. We stay awake all night and worship the sound of ankle bells with concentrated mind.**

*(sarvāḥ smayante\|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Everyone smiles)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-ślāghaṁ saṁskṛtena):*  
*āḍambarojjvala-gatir vara-kuñja-raktaḥ*  
*svairī parisphurita-puṣkara-cāru-hastaḥ \|*  
*dhanyāsi sundari yayā mṛdulaṁ hasantyā*  
*vandīkṛtas tarala-ballava-kuñjaro’yam \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Praising) O beautifulRādhā, you are fortunate. With his proud gait, attachment to the best bowers, with a blossoming lotus in his beautiful hand, the independent, most excellent amorous cowherd has been tied up by your soft smile.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye—*  
*rucira-sahacarīṇāṁ vīthibhiḥ sevyamānā*  
*mada-mṛdula-marālī-ramya-līlā-gati-śrīḥ \|*  
*śaśimukhi gata-nidraṁ kurvatī mām idānīṁ*  
*śarad iva bhavatīyaṁ loka-lakṣmīṁ tanoti \|\|22\|\|*

*tad arvācīnena hari-lāvaṇya-śṛṅgāreṇa bhavatīm alaṅkurvāṇaḥ śāradīṁ śriyam avadhyāyāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved! O Rādhā with moonlike face! Served by rows of attractive** ***jhinti*** **flowers, walking attractively like a gentle, intoxicated swan, making me sleepless, she spreads beauty throughout the world like the autumn season. By decorating you with new ornaments of golden beauty I disregard the beauty of autumn.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*bālānuja kalāpinām avakalayya kālajñatāṁ*  
*manaḥ kila balīyasīṁ mama bibharti vismeratam \|*  
*yad adya śarad-āgame tava vilokya līlotkaṭaṁ*  
*kiranti ruci-maṇḍalī-juṣam amī śikhaṇḍāvalīm \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa! Seeing that the peacocks understand the proper time, my mind is filled with great wonder. Today, with the coming of autumn, seeing your passion for pastimes, they sprinkle many feathers in an attractive circle.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe! sādhu lakṣitaṁ tan-mauli-kalpanāya candrakān āharāmi \| (iti baṭunā saha tathā karoti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, I will take the feathers for making a crown. (They begin picking up the feathers.)**

***rādhikā:*** *sahi lalide! jattha diṇṇa-bhārā ahaṁ ṇiccintahmi sā tumaṁ ja{i} sommāsi tado jāva kahṇo dūre gado tāva kaṅkelli-kuḍaṅgaṁ pabesissaṁ \| (iti tathā sthitā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Lalitā, I am free of worry when responsibility has been given. If you permit, while Kṛṣṇa is distant, I will enter the** ***kañkeli*** **bower. (She goes into the bower.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sakhe, nirmitaṁ pracalāka-śalākābhiḥ kirīṭaṁkhañjarīṭa-netrāyāḥ sīmanta-sīmani vinyāsa-saubhāgyam ālambatām \| (iti parikramya) lalite, kva sā te priya-sakhī?*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, the crown made of quivering sprouts should support the beautiful arrangement at the edge of Rādhā’s hair part. (Approaching) O Lalitā, where is your friend?**

***lalitā:*** *āttaṇo gharaṁ gadā \|*

**Перевод:**

**She has gone into her house.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *niṣṭhure, tiṣṭha tiṣṭha \| tūrṇam asau te dhūrtatā-garvam apaharāmi \| (iti samantāt paśyan sa-harṣam) vayasya paśya—sahaseyam avāptā gaurāṅgī priyā \| (ity upasarpati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**Cruel Lalitā! Stop, stop! Quickly I will remove your pride as a deceiver. (Looks everywhere) Look friend! Suddenly I have attained my golden beloved. (He approaches a flower.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(vihasya): bho baassa! cakkavādeṇa tiṇābaṭṭeṇa bhāmidassa de ajjabi ṇūṇaṁ bhamo ṇa gado \| pekkha, esā pīda-parāa-puñja-piñjaridā tthala-ṇaliṇī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, you were whirled around by Trṇāvarta, the whirlwind, but today you have not been whirled about. Look, this is just a land lotus made golden by yellow pollen.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(nirūpya): sakhe, satyaṁ bravīṣi \| (ity anyato gatvā) bho sakhe, paśya kuṅkumāṅgi niṣṭaṅkitam idānīm eva labdhā \| (iti didhīrṣuḥ pradhāvati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking) Friend, you speak the truth. (Going elsewhere) O friend, look! I have attained her. Her limbs marked with k*****uṁkum*****a. (He runs, wishing to embrace her.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *(sa-hasta-talam uccair vihasya): bho baassa ettha tujjha abarāho ṇatthi \| kiṁtu pemma-laharīe jjebba \| jāe sabbā bundāḍa{i} rāhiā ṇimmidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Loudly clapping his hands, laughing) O friend, you have not made any mistake here, since, because of waves of prema, all Vṛndāvana had become made of Rādhikā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-vailakṣyaṁ vilokya): katham utphulleyaṁ sahacarī \| (pārśvato vilokya) lalitāṅgi lalite, ito vāmya-parvatād avarohantī kāntāram itasya dadasva me hastāvalambam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking with astonishment) Why is this assistant woman so insolent? (Looking to the side) O beautiful Lalitā! Descending from the mountain of pride, give me your hand since I am pleased with my lover.**

***lalitā*** *(smitvā): sundara, visāhaṁ pucchehi \| esā kkhuṇaṁ jāṇādi \| (iti saṁjñāṁ nāṭayatī)*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O beautiful Kṛṣṇa, ask Viśākhā. She knows about her. (Making a sign to Viśākhā)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-harṣam apavārya): sakhe paśya—viśākhāyāḥ parokṣaṁ kiñcit tiro’valambantī lalitā bhrū-saṁjñayā kadamba-kuñjaṁ sūcayati \| tad atra nāsti manāg api sandigdhatā \| (iti parikramya sa-darpa-smitam) priye, vilokitāsi \| nirgamyatām \| (ity udgrīvikaṁ kṛtvā sa-hāsam) lalite, sādhu sādhu \| jataṁ tava dhūrtatā-latikāyāḥ sāphalyam idam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully, to Madhumaṅgala) O friend, look! Acting secretly with Viśākhā, Lalitā is indicating the** ***kadamba*** **grove with a signal from her brow. It cannot be doubted at all. (He goes with a proud smile) O beloved, I see you. Come out. (Bending his neck, laughing) O Lalitā, very good, very good! Your creeper of deception has borne fruit.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *baassa, esā mae jjebba laddhā tuha rāhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, I have got your Rādhā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-kautukam): vayasya, laliteva kaccid aviśrambhaṇīya-bhaṇitir nāsti \|*

**Перевод:**

**You do not speak lies like Lalitā.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *gāattīe sabāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I swear by the** ***gāyatrī*** **verse.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-viśrambham): sakhe, kva sā darśaya śīghram \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, where is she? Show her to me quickly.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *tuhma hattha-gadaṁ jjebba ṇaṁ karemi \| tā dehi me paritosiam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I will put her in your hands. Give me something pleasing.**

*(kṛṣṇaḥ sa-ślāghaṁ mālatī-mālayā maṇḍayati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa decorates him with a jasmine garland while praising him.)**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ:*** *gheppijja{u} esā \| (iti rādheti varṇa-dvayī-bhājāṁ patra-lekhām arpayati\|)*

**Перевод:**

**You should now accept Rādhā. (He gives Kṛṣṇa a leaf with the syllables Rādhā written on it.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): sakhe, satyam anenāpi bhavad-arpitena tarpito’smi \| yataḥ—*  
*kramāt kakṣām akṣṇoḥ parisara-bhuvaṁ vā śravaṇayor*  
*manāg adhyārūḍhaṁ praṇayi-janānām akṣara-padam \|*  
*kam apy antas-toṣaṁ vitarad-avilambād anupadaṁ*  
*nisargād viśveṣāṁ hṛdaya-padavīm utsukayati \|\|24\|\|*

*(iti parāvṛtya dakṣiṇato vikāśantam aśokam avalokya sa-vismayam)*  
*śaṅke saṅkulitāntarādya niviḍa-krīḍānubandhecchayā*  
*kuñje vañjula-śākhinaḥ śaśimukhī līnā varīvarti sā \|*  
*no ced eṣa tad-aṅghri-saṅgama-vinābhāvād akāle kathaṁ*  
*puṣpāmoda-nimantritāli-paṭalī-stotrasya pātrībhavet \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O friend, I am truly satisfied with your offering because the syllables of the lover’s name which come near the eyes or ears of the beloved even for a moment and immediately give an indescribable inner satisfaction, delight the hearts of all the world at every moment by their very nature.**

**(Turning to the right, he sees a blossoming** ***aśoka*** **tree and becomes astonished.)**

**I think moon-faced Rādhā has entered this thick grove of** ***aśoka*** **trees with a desire to enjoy intense pastimes here.If this were not so, then why do thebees proclaim theglories ofthe fragrant** ***aśoka*** **flower out of season? It could not happen unless Rādhā had not touched them with her feet.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-praṇayerṣyam) tuatto bhaeṇa jjebba palāido hmi \| ettha bi maṁ vidambeduṁ laddhosi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In affectionate anger) I fled in fear from you. Even here you have come to ridicule me.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sātma-ślāgham): dṛṣṭā me gabhīra-paṭavārabhaṭī yatas tirodhāna-vidyāpahāreṇa nirjitā yūyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Praising himself) You have seen my great cleverness and confidence, since you have been defeated. I have destroyed your art of disappearing.**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena): hanta bho vāṅ-mātra-jita-kāśin!*  
*asminn eka-saroja-sambhava-kṛta-stotro’si vṛndāvane*  
*rādhā bhūri-hiraṇyagarbha-racita-pratyaṅga-kānti-stavā \|*  
*hastodasta-mahidharas tvam asakṛn-netrānta-bhaṅgī-cchaṭā-*  
*kṛṣṭoccair dharaṇī-dhara mama sakhī tad vīra māhaṅkṛthaḥ \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O empty boaster! In Vṛndāvana only one Brahmā offered prayers to you. Rādhā has been praised by many Brahmā’s who praise the beauty of each of her limbs. You lifted Govardhana with your hand. My friend Rādhā has attracted the holder of the earth many times by the effulgent glances from the edge of her eyes! O valiant Kṛṣṇa, do not be proud.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *lalite, nilīne mayi vilokite nātathyaṁ tava vikatthanaṁ bhavatinaṁ vidaṅkaravani*

**Перевод:**

**O Lalitā, when I disappear, it will be a fact that I will destroy your boasting.**

***sarvāḥ:*** *ebbaṁ hodu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Let that be.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *iyam uttaras cañcarīka-sañcaya-rocir ullāsi-śyāmala-palāśa-gucchā dūratas tāpiñcha-viccholī \|tad eṣā savarṇatayā sakhī-bhāvam āpannā mām atra saṅgopayiṣyati \| (iti sa-vayasyo niṣkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**To the north, far away, is a grove of** ***tamāla*** **trees, with splendid, dark foliage. It is friendly since it is the same color as me. Thus it will hide me in its foliage. (He exits with his friend.)**

***lalitā:*** *halā rahe, kahṇassa adaṁsaṇeṇa mā uttamma \| ṇaṁ diṭṭhaṁ jebba jāṇehi \| tā vijutta ahme sabbado pasappahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**O my friend Rādhā, do not be disturbed on not seeing Kṛṣṇa. I know you will see him. Separated from him, we will search for him.**

***rādhikā:*** *jadhā bhaṇādi pia-sahī \|*

**Перевод:**

**As you say, dear friend.**

*(iti tisras tathā kurvanti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(The three begin searching.)**

***rādhikā*** *(uttarāṁ vana-lekhām āsādya sa-vimarśam): ṇūṇaṁ kahṇo ettha patto hubissadi jaṁ maṁ pekkhanto dakkhiṇaṁ pa{i}ṭṭho \| (iti parikramya saṁskṛtena)*  
*sa hariti bhavatībhiḥ svānta-hārī hariṇyo*  
*harir iha kim apāṅgātithya-saṅgī vyadhāyi \|*  
*yad anuraṇita-vaṁśī-kākalībhir mukhebhyaḥ*  
*sukha-tṛṇa-kavalā vaḥ sāmi-līḍhāḥ skhalanti \|\|27\|\|*

*(puro’bhyupetya samantāt paśyantī saṁskṛtena)*  
*yad agalita-marandaṁ vartate śākhi-vṛndaṁ*  
*milati ca yad alabdha-prema-ghūrṇā khagālī \|*  
*tad iha nahi sikhaṇḍottaṁsinī sā praviṣṭā*  
*nikhila-bhuvana-ceto-hāriṇī kāpi vidyā \|\|28\|\|*

*(iti savyataḥ parikramya saṁskṛtena)*  
*vighūrṇantaḥ pauṣpaṁ na madhu lihate’mī madhulihaḥ*  
*śuko’yaṁ nādatte kalita-jaḍimā dāḍima-phalam \|*  
*vighūrṇā parṇāgraṁ carati hariṇīyaṁ na haritaṁ*  
*pathānena svāmī tad-ibha-vara-gāmī dhruvam agāt \|\|29\|\|*

*(puro gatvā) esā bāmado kālī tamālālī dīsa{i} \| (iti sāci-kandaraṁ nibhālya saṁskṛtena)*  
*naisargikāny api nirargala-cāpalāni*  
*hitvādya saṅkula-tanuḥ pulakāṅkureṇa \|*  
*dṛṣṭiṁ cireṇa parirabdha-tamāla-śākhā*  
*śākhā-mṛgī-tatir iyaṁ kim adhas tanoti \|\|30\|\|*

*tā esā mañjulā tābiñcha-ṇiuñja-sāliā pekkhidabbā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Coming to the northern forest she reflects.) We will certain catch Kṛṣṇa here since,**

**on seeing me, he entered the southern part. (She walks) O does! Has he who steals hearts become the guest of your glances? I think it is so, because on hearing the echoing sound of his flute, the grass is falling, half-eaten, from your mouths.**

**(She comes in front and looks everywhere.) Since the branches do not flow with honey, since the flocks of birds are not dizzy with prema, that astounding talent which steals the minds of all the worlds, and which wears a peacock feather crown, is not here.**

**(She goes to the left.) These restless bumble-bees, disturbed in mind, do not drink the nectar of these flowers. His stunned parrot does not eatthe pomegranate fruit.This pale doe refuses to eat thetips of green grass.Certainly Kṛṣṇa,whowalks like the best of elephants,has traveled on this path.**

**(Going forward) I see the dark** ***tamāla*** **trees. (Looks with neck turned) Why have the groups of monkeys, embracing the** ***tamāla*** **branches, given up their natural restlessness? Why are their hairs standing on end? Why do they look at the ground? We should look at the attractive branches of the** ***tamāla*** **trees.**

*(praviśya)* ***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *satyam asyāś citta-catvara-saṅga-tvarī premāvalir eva mad-uddeśa-dūtī \| yad avilambitaṁ vijñāta-bhūyiṣṭho’smi saṁvṛttaḥ \| tataḥ sthāṇur iva niścalaṁ tiṣṭhāmi \| (iti tathā sthitaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering, he speaks to himself.) My intense prema eager for association with her is a messenger giving information about my whereabouts. I will become motionless like a pillar. (He stands motionless.)**

***rādhikā*** *(mūrdhānam ānamayya kṛṣṇaṁ paśyantī sa-vyājam) ettha kahṇo ṇatthi\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Lowering her head, she sees Kṛṣṇa but pretends not to see him.) Kṛṣṇa is not here.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *diṣṭyā na dṛṣṭo’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) By luck she did not see me.**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-smitam) eso ṇīlamaṇi-kīlo jjebba rehadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) This is a sapphire pole.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *nūnaṁ ghanāndhakārato nāhaṁ pratyabhijñātaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**I have not been detected because of the great darkness.**

***rādhikā:*** *ahmahe ujjaladā inda-ṇīla-kīlassa \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! The effulgence of this sapphire pole!**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-harṣam apavārya):*  
*re dhvānta-maṇḍala sakhe śaraṇāgato’smi*  
*vistārayasva tarasā nija-vaibhavāni \|*  
*abhyāsam abhyupāgatāpi muhur yathāsau*  
*navaiti māṁ nava-kuraṅga-taraṅgi-netrā \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(In joy, aside) O darkness, my friend! I surrender to you. Spread your powers quickly so that even though she approaches closely she will not recognize me.**

***rādhikā*** *(smitvā): accariaṁ accariam \| imassa nīlobalassa antarāle paḍibimbidā caṁdāalī lakkhīadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) This is most astonishing! I see many moons reflected inside a blue lotus.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitaṁ kṛtvā sva-gatam) kathaṁ saṁvidānā khalu narmātanoti \| (ity utthāya prakāśam) priye satyam āttha \| yad ayaṁ tvad-āsya-candro me hṛd-vṛtti-taraṅgeṣu bimbitaś candrāvalī babhūva \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling, he says to himself) Does she, knowing the truth, make a joke? (He stands up and reveals himself) O beloved, you have spoken the truth. The moon of your face, reflected in the waves of my heart, has become many moons.**

***rādhikā:*** *ammahe kahaṁ tumaṁ jebba \| tado nedaṁ accariam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah, it is you. Then it is not astonishing.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *vilāsini! kim anena viśleṣa-sampādyena keli-narmaṇā \| tad ehi \| dāna-gandhinā kusuma-vṛndeṣu pūrṇa-mūrdhani sapta-parṇa-kuñje kṣaṇaṁ viśramya saukhyam anubhavāvaḥ \|(iti tathā sthitau \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O playful Rādhā! What is the need of this playful joking, using puns? Come. Relax with me for a moment in this hut made of** ***sapta-parṇa*** **leaves filled with a profusion of flowers, while I emit the fragrance of an elephant’s** ***mada.***

***lalitā:*** *visāhe, pekkha \| kahṇeṇa saṅgadā pia-sahī \| jaṁ tassa padehiṁ sammilidāiṁ edāe padāiṁ disanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Look Viśākhā! Our dear friend is associating with Kṛṣṇa. This is shown by Rādhā’s footprints mixing with his.**

***viśākhā*** *(padāṅkān anusṛtya saṁskṛtena):*  
*priya-sakhi parirambhān ābhimukhyānubandhād*  
*asadṛśa-viniveśān narma-laulyorjitāni \|*  
*iyam aviṣama-manda-nyāsato jalpa-goṣṭhīṁ*  
*pada-tatir iha rādhā-kṛṣṇayor ātanoti \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Following the foot prints) The groups of footprints indicate the embraces of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa since Kṛṣṇa’s are facing towards Rādhā’s. Here the footprints indicate their intense joking since the prints are placed in all directions. Here they indicate a conversation, since the prints are faint and pointing in the same direction.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, nātidūre komalo’yaṁ kañci-dhvanir udañcati \| tatas tūṣṇīṁ śṛṇuvaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, not far off there is the soft sound of a belt jingling. Therefore, let us be silent.**

***viśākhā:*** *halā bitthiṇṇa-valli-maṇḍala-kuṇḍalide bi baṇa-khaṇḍe pia-sahīe kadhaṁ kahṇo turitaṁ laddho?*

**Перевод:**

**How did Rādhā find Kṛṣṇa so quickly in the deep forest entwined in profuse creepers?**

***lalitā:***  
*guruaṁ rama{i} jahiṁ jo ṇa tassa so hoi dullaho bhuaṇe \|*  
*ma{u}laohmi rasāle kalakaṇṭhī takkhaṇaṁ mila{i} \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When he who is guru enjoys, he is not hard to find in this world. A cuckoo lands on a mango blossoms at that moment.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye, pratyāsanne tava sakhyau \| tad ubhe parihasiṣyann antarito bhavāmi \| (iti tathā sthitaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O beloved, your two friends are coming this way. I will play a joke on them and disappear. (He disappears.)**

***lalitā*** *(parikramya puro rādhām ālokya ca sa-harṣam): halā, kudo so ṇāaro?*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking, sees Rādhā and becomes joyful) O friend, where is the lecher?**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-smitam): kā kkhu taṁ jāṇādi?*

**Перевод:**

**Who knows his whereabouts?**

***lalitā*** *(sa-narma-smitaṁ saṁskṛtam āśritya):*  
*kacā muktā muktāvalir api yayau nirguṇa-daśāṁ*  
*viśuddhaṁ te danta-cchada-yugam abhūd dānta-hṛdaye \|*  
*abandhāsīt kāñcī tad iva sakhi yuktāsi hariṇā*  
*satīnāṁ vaḥ kṛtyaṁ kim ucitam idaṁ gokula-bhuvam \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Your hair is undone. Your pearls have fallen from their string. Yours have lost the betel nut color. O women whose chest has been crushed by his embraces. This has been done by Kṛṣṇa. O friend, is this conduct proper for us chaste women in Gokula?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(puro’nusṛtya): lalite, nāham aparādhyāmi sakhyai \| vane saṅgopito’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Moving forward) O Lalitā, I did not do this to your friend. I was hiding in the forest.**

***lalitā:*** *kiṁ tti pia-sahīe saṅgobaṇijjo tumam?*

**Перевод:**

**Why did she make you hide?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *sundari, nija-kandarpa-kalā-prāgalbhyasyāpalāpāya \| (ity aṅgulyā darśayan) paśya paśya—*  
*kaṭhorāgrair bhūyo vraṇam ajanayad vakṣasi nakhair*  
*balād ākrāmantī vyakirad api māṁ piñcha-racanām \|*  
*vikṛṣya chinnāṅgīm akṛta-vana-mālāṁ ca rucirām*  
*idānīṁ jānīte na kim api puras te priya-sakhī \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O beautiful Lalitā, I did it to evade her bold advances. (Pointing his finger) Look, look! She made wounds on my chest with her hard nails. Attacking with vigor she completely tore apart my peacock crown, while pulling and destroying my attractive garland. Your dear friend standing before us does not know anything about this.**

***rādhā*** *(sāpatrapam): huṁ appaṇā kadua paraṁ dūseduṁ pattidosi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Embarrassed) Oh, you did that yourself and now blame others.**

*(****nepathye****) jaṭilā phuḍa-mañjarīhim (ity ardhokte)*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) Jaṭilā with buds … (half completed sentence)**

***rādhikā*** *(sa-trāsam): accahidaṁ accahidam \| bhaaṅkarī buḍḍhiā \| tā turiaṁ palāehma \| (iti sakhībhyāṁ saha niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(In fear) What calamity, what calamity! It is the frightful old woman. Let us quickly flee. (She exits with her two friends.)**

*(****punar nepathye****): bihūtimantā parāa-puñjeṇa hara-bhaṭṭā bia sabāe pphuranti satta-cchada-ppaarā\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) …the host of** ***sapta-chadda*** **trees sprout in the spring like devotees of the Lord having long locks of hair (jaṭilā).**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-vailakṣyam): hanta hanta sapta-parṇaṁ varṇayatā jaṭileti kaṭūdgāreṇa batunā kadarthito’smi \| tad agre suhṛn-maṇḍalam eva prayāmi \| (iti niṣkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Surprised) Ah, ah! I have been ruined by Madhumaṅgala with his sharp utterances, saying Jaṭilā when he means the** ***sapta-parṇa*** **tree. I will go to my group of friends in front. (He exits).**

*saptamo’ṅkaḥ*  
*gaurī-vihāraḥ*

## Act Seven: Pastimes of the Golden Girl

*(tataḥ praviśati vṛndā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Vṛndā enters.)**

***vṛndā*** *(samantād avalokya):*  
*kadambālī-jṛmbhā-parimala-bharodgāri-pavanā*  
*sphuṭad-yūthī- yūthī-kṛta-madhupa-gāna-praṇayinī \|*  
*naṭat-kekī-stomā mṛdula-yavasa-śyāmalima-bhūs*  
*tapānte’dya svāntaṁ mama rasayati dvādaśa-vanī \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking all around) After the hot season, with winds laden with the fragrance of blossoming** ***kadambas*****, the twelve forests, attracting swarms of bees to the blossoming jasmine creepers, filled with dancing peacocks and earth darkened by tender grass, delight my heart.**

*(nepathye dṛṣṭiṁ nikṣipya) katham asau paurṇamāsī nija-parna-kutiropānta-vaṭikāyām abhimanyunā saṅkathayantī vartate \| tad ahaṁ kṣaṇam atraiva tiṣṭheyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking offstage) Why is Paurṇamāsī talking with Abhimanyu in the garden near her thatched hut? Let me wait here for a moment.**

*(praviśya tathā-bhūtā)* ***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatsābhimanyo! kim-arthaṁ prātar evāham upasāditāsmi?*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering with Abhimanyu) O my child Abhimanyu! Why have you come to me this morning?**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *bhaabadi, tujjha āṇaṁ gehṇidum \|*

**Перевод:**

**I have come to take your permission.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *kasminn arthe?*

**Перевод:**

**For what purpose?**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *bārisahanavie mahurā-patthāne \|*

**Перевод:**

**To take Rādhā to Mathurā.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sa-vyatham): kas tatra hetuḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**(In pain) What is the reason for this?**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *doṇṇaṁ rāhā-māhavāṇaṁ cābalaṁ jebba \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa have been acting inappropriately.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vīra! kena tavedaṁ varṇitam?*

**Перевод:**

**O hero, who has told you this?**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *pia-baassena gobaḍḍhanena \|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Govardhana.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatsābhimanyo! caturaṁ-manyo’pi tvam anārya-buddhir asi \| yena bhojendra-vallabhasya kauṭilya-cakreṇa vibhramyase \|*

**Перевод:**

**Dear Abhimanyu! Though you think that you are clever, your intelligence is not proper. You are being turned on the wheel of crookedness of a cowherd loyal to Kaṁsa.**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *adi-pasiddhā esā pa{u}ttī keṇa vā ṇa kahijja{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**Their conduct is well known. Who does not speak of it?**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *putra! nūnaṁ karṇe-japānām upajāpena lupta-viveko’si \| tad ākarṇaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**O son, you have lost the power of discrimination by the divisive tactics of rascals. Listen to this.**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *āṇabehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Please tell me.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatsa! yena lāvaṇya-gandha-lava-lubdhena kaṁsa-śārdūlena svayam eva rādhā-mṛgī mṛgyate \| tasya dāruṇasya kaṁsa-śārdūlasya hastopari nyāyyaḥ katham asyāḥ prakṣepaḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**Child, Kaṁsa, greedy for a trace of her beauty like a tiger, hunts for Rādhā, the doe. Is it correct to throw her into the hands of the cruel tiger Kaṁsa?**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *bhaavadi! tattha kā cintā? so kkhu kusalī hodu suhittamo gobaḍḍhano jeṇa vijjā-māhurīhiṁ mahurindo basikīo \|*

**Перевод:**

**O respected Paurṇamāsī, why worry about this? My great friend Govardhana is skilful. He controls Kaṁsa by his sweet, wise words.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sa-khedaṁ kṣaṇam anudhyāya): haṁho dhanyānāṁ mūrdhanya! govinda-mātur mātuleyo’si \| katham alpāyuṣāṁ gokula-dveṣiṇāṁ maṇḍala-pātitām ālambase \| tad adya kayāpi maryādayā tvaṁ paryāpayitum icchāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(She ponders a moment in pain.) O chief of the fortunate! You are the cousin of Kṛṣṇa’s mother. Why do you depend on that unfortunate group which despises Gokula? Today I wish to put an end to the matter for you.**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *āṇabedu tatthahodi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Please instruct me.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatsa, sā kācin matsara-kalpitāpi kiṁvadantī yadi tvayā nātathyatayā pratīyate tataḥ svayam eva cakṣuṣor aparokṣī-kṛtya yatheṣṭam ceṣṭanīyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Child, if you don’t think this is a rumor manufactured out of envy, then see it with your own eyes. Then do as you like.**

***abhimanyuḥ*** *(sa-praśrayam): bhaavadi siro-gahidam de nidesa-kusumam \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With humility) I take the flower of your order on my head.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sānandam): somānana, gomān atra bhūyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In bliss) O handsome Abhimanyu! May you possess many cows!**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *bhaavadi, amba maṁ puṇo puṇo bhaṇādi—putta candāalī-caṇḍiāccaṇeṇa goaḍḍhano jahāttha-ṇāmā saṁbutto \| tā bahuḍiyā tattha dikkhā kijja{u}tti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected Paurṇamāsī! My mother tells me again and again, “Son, because Candrāvalī worships Durgā, Govardhana’s name is appropriate: he has many cows. Therefore Rādhā should also do this worship.”**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *maṅgala-mate! sarva-maṅgalārādhāne dīkṣitam avilambam eva vārṣabhānavīṁ viddhi \|*

**Перевод:**

**You are thinking of most auspiciousness person. Understand that Rādhā will soon begin worshipping the most auspicious person.**

***abhimanyuḥ:*** *bhaavadi, aṇukampidohmi \| (iti niṣkrāntaḥ):*

**Перевод:**

**You are merciful to me. (Exits)**

***vṛndā*** *(parikramya): vande bhagavatīm \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking) I offer respects to Paurṇamāsī.**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(vilokya śubhāśīrbhir abhivandya ca): vatse kāmaṁ kṛtārthāsi \| tad āvedaya rādhā-mādhavayor nikuñja-keli-mādhurīm \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing her, she offers blessings.) O child, you are most successful. Please inform me of the sweetness of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes in the bower.**

***vṛndā:***  
*sarvasvaṁ prathama-rasasya yaḥ prathīyān*  
*kaṁsārer udayati rādhāya vilāsaḥ \|*  
*vaktuṁ ko viramati taṁ janaḥ samantād*  
*ānandas tirayati ced girāṁ na vṛttim \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**What person could ever stop speaking of that extensive pastime which produces the ultimate stage of conjugal rasa of Kṛṣṇa for Rādhā, unless the bliss stops the production of words?**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(sānandam): putri vṛnde!*  
*harir eṣa na ced avātariṣyan*  
*mathurāyāṁ madhurākṣi rādhikā ca \|*  
*abhaviṣyad iyaṁ vṛthā visṛṣṭir*  
*makarāṇkas tu viśeṣatas tadātra \|\|3\|\|*  
*tad adya goṣṭha-madhye tavopasattir māṁ vismāpayati \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In bliss) O my daughter Vṛndā! If Kṛṣṇa had not appeared in Mathurā and if beautiful eyed Rādhā had not appeared, the world--and especially Cupid--would be useless!**

**I am surprised that you have come into the village today.**

***vṛndā:*** *bhagavati tvarate ko’pi māṁ garīyān arthaḥ \| tad atra lalitām apekṣamānāsmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī, some serious matter has made me hurry here. I am waiting here for Lalitā.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *kīdṛśo’yam?*

**Перевод:**

**What is the matter?**

***vṛndā:*** *pūrve-dyur ādiṣṭāsmi govindena, yathā—*  
*āhara gaurī-tīrthe, madhu-śriyaṁ tatra rantum icchāmi \|*  
*padmāvalambi-karayā, priyayā padmāvataṁsikayā \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Yesterday, Kṛṣṇa gave me an instruction: make Gaurī-tīrtha beautiful as the spring for I wish to enjoy there with Rādhā, who holds a lotus in her hand and wears a lotus crown.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *yuktam ādiṣṭam, yad adya saubhāgya-pūrṇimā \| tathā hi—*  
*prasūnair adbhutaiḥ kāntā*  
*kāntena śrāvaṇī-dine \|*  
*prasādhitā prasiddhena*  
*saubhāgyena vivardhate \|\|5\|\|*  
*tatas tataḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**That was a good instruction, for today is the auspicious full moon. The woman decorated by her lover with astonishing flowers on the full moon of Śvāvaṇa month will fully have an increase in her good fortune. Then what happened?**

***vṛndā:*** *tataś ca tad-vṛtte śārikā-mukhataḥ sakhī-saṁsadi sañcārite padyārthatas tarkita-rādhārtha-siddhir api padmā lalitāṁ katakṣayantī hathād avādīt—*  
*utphulla-mūrteḥ samam ullasantyās*  
*candrāvaleś candraka-maṇḍalena \|*  
*mlāsyanti saubhāgya-bhara-prabhābhir*  
*garvāndha-gopī-vadanāmbujāni \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When that message was broadcast in the assembly of sakhīs by a** ***śārikā*** **bird, Padmā glanced at Lalitā and spoke boldly, though she could suspect that the message referred to Rādhā. “The lotus faces of the gopīs blinded by pride will wither because of the effulgence of good fortune of Candrāvalī, the form of joy, experiencing bliss with Kṛṣṇa.”**

***paurṇamāsī*** *(vihasya):* *tatas tataḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) Then what happened?**

***vṛndā:*** *tataś ca smerayā dṛṣṭi-mudrayaiva tām adhīrām avadhīrayantī lalitā mayā saha rādhām upasādya kalye prasthānāya tām atisambhramaṁ lambhayāmāsa \| paśya vṛtte’dya yāme seyaṁ nājagāma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Disregarding impudent Padmā with a smiling glance, Lalitā went with me to Rādhā. We met her excitedly in order to arrange a meeting with Kṛṣṇa in the morning. A few hours have now passed and she has not come.**

*(praviśya)* ***lalitā:*** *sahi buṁde! juttaṁ gabbāidaṁ pa{u}māe \| dāṇiṁ jāṇidam \| tattha patthāṇe kudo ahmānaṁ joggadā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O my friend Vṛndā! Padmā has reason to show pride. Now I understand. We should go to that place.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *putri, katham evam?*

**Перевод:**

**Daughter, why do this?**

***lalitā:*** *bhaavadi tuhma purado ahmānaṁ tiṇā dohagga-salleṇa kiṁ ugghādideṇa \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Paurṇamāsī, why should we show our great misfortune in front of you?**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vatse suśrūṣur asmi \| varṇyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**O child, I am attentive. Please describe the events.**

***lalitā*** *(sāsram):* *ajje! gora-paṭṭa-sutteṇa ganthidā ekkā dibba-mālā pia-sahie kahṇassa diṇṇā \| sā ahmehiṁ pa{u}miā dhammille tak-kālaṁ jjebba diṭṭhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In tears) Respected Paurṇamāsī! Our dear friend Rādhā gave Kṛṣṇa an attractive garland strung on gold thread. We saw that garland in Padmā’s hair on that day.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *sthāne glānir iyam \| bāḍham asāmpratam etad govindasya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Your frustration is reasonable. This was not proper at all for Kṛṣṇa.**

***vṛndā:*** *śāntam amaṅgalam \|*

**Перевод:**

**This is not inauspicious.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *vṛnde kathyatāṁ kiṁ nāmedam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā, please explain what you said.**

***vṛndā:*** *varṇitaṁ me manuṣya-vākyayā tayā kakkhaṭikayā kadamba-śākhāyāṁ mālām ālambya kālindīm avagāḍhe vana-mālinī sampravṛtte ca ketakī-parāga-cakra-caṇḍe marun-maṇḍale padmā kilemāṁ jahāra \| marutas tu mudhā kalaṅkaṁ jagāmeti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I heard from Kakkaṭikā in human words that Kṛṣṇa put the garland on the branch of a** ***kadamba*** **tree and went into the Yamunā. Padmā stole the garland when the wind blew a cloud of ketakī pollen. Foolish Padmā blamed the wind.**

***lalitā:*** *dhutte muñca ṇaṁ vañcaṇaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**O rascal, stop fooling us.**

***vṛndā:*** *puṣpa-mañjarībhyaḥ śape \|*

**Перевод:**

**I swear by the flower buds!**

***lalitā*** *(viśrabhya):* *halā saccaṁ saccaṁ \| ahma purado appaṇo sohaggaṁ vikkheventi pa{u}miā mālaṁ bibaredi \| kahṇa-mittāṇaṁ agrato ūṇa ṇaṁ saṁbaredi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Trusting her) Friend, it is true. In front of us, Padmā, announcing her good fortune, showed the garland. But in front of Kṛṣṇa’s friends she hides it.**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *putri lalite! sphuṭam atra pūrṇimāyāṁ yuṣmākam anudyamāya padmayā tāṁ chadma-cāturīṁ prasārya gaurī-tīrthaṁ candrāvalī lambhitā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Daughter Lalitā, clearly, on this full moon day, without informing you, using her skilful deception, Padmā has taken Candrāvalī to Gaurī-tīrtha.**

***vṛndā:*** *yuktam āha bhagavatī \| tad adya gaurī-tīrthe rādhikopanītiḥ kalyāṇī me na pratibhāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Paurṇamāsī speaks the truth. It does not look auspicious to take Rādhā there today.**

*(praviśya)* ***viśākhā:*** *buṁde kallāṇī paḍibhādi tti bhaṇāhi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) O Vṛndā, say “It is auspicious.”**

***vṛndā:*** *katham evam?*

**Перевод:**

**Why?**

***viśākhā:*** *goulesari-muhādo ajja sohagga-puṇṇimāṁ ākaṇṇia karālāe candāalī appa-bhattuno mallassa pāse patthābīadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing from Yaśodā that it is the auspicious full moon, Karālā has arranged for Candrāvalī to stay with her husband Govardhana Malla.**

***lalitā*** *(sa-harṣam):* *bisāhe, iṭṭha-deo saroa-ṇāho de pasīdadu \| tā tubarīadu \|*

**Перевод:**

**(In joy) Viśākhā, may the sun god be merciful to you! Please hurry!**

***paurṇamāsī:*** *putri vṛnde! kām apy adyatanīm abhimanyor dāruṇāṁ durmantrita-mudrāṁ rādhāyām āvedya mayāpy asyāḥ śaṅkā-paṅkāvalī-saṅkalanāya gaurī-tīrthe bhavitavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Vṛndā, I will inform Rādhā of Abhimanyu’s ill-advised plan for today and go to Gaurī-tīrtha in order to relieve Rādhā of her great worries.**

***vṛndā:*** *bhagavati! pūrveṇa gaurī-tīrthaṁlavaṅga-kuḍuṅgasya prāṅgaṇe sa-viśākhayā rādhayā sārdhaṁ sādhayatu tatra-bhavatī \| tāvad āvāṁ mādhavam āsādayāvaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Paurṇamāsī, you should go along with Rādhā and Viśākhā to Kṛṣṇa’s bower of clove flowers near Gaurī-tīrtha. I will go with Lalitā to see Kṛṣṇa.**

*(paurṇamāsī viśākhayā saha niṣkrāntā):*

**Перевод:**

**(Paurnamāsī leaves with Viśākhā.)**

***lalitā*** *(vṛndayā saha parikramya):* *halā pekkhiadu ḍāhiṇe esā dūrado sebbāe samaṁ jappantī pa{u}mā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Walks with Vṛndā) O friend, look! To the south, at a distance, Padmā is speaking with Śaibyā.**

***vṛndā:*** *sakhi nāsaṅgataṁ vyāhared viśākhā \| (ity agrato gatvā sa-vimarṣam): sakhi paramautsukya-sambhūtena bhūriṇā sambhrameṇa sambhedite rādhikā-vinayam anirdhārya tūrṇam āvāṁ vidūram āgate \| tad atra gaṅgā-pāre paurṇamāsīṁ kṣaṇaṁ*  
*pratipālayāvaḥ \| (iti niṣkrānte \|)*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, Viśākhā would not act in an unbecoming manner. (Going forward, she reflects.) Because of great zeal arising from intense enthusiasm, without thinking of Rādhā’s modesty in meeting him, we have come a great distance. Let us wait a few moments for Paurṇamāsī on the opposite shore of the Yamunā. (They exit.)**

*(tataḥ praviśataḥ padmā-saibye \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Padmā and Śaibyā enter.)**

***padmā:*** *sahi sebbe! mā kkhu dummaṇā hohi \|*

**Перевод:**

**My friend Śaibyā, do not be depressed.**

***śaibyā:*** *pa{u}me paramāhiṭṭhassa alāhaṇeṇa salāhabam cittaṁ samādhāduṁ na kkhamahmi\|*

**Перевод:**

**Padmā, I cannot concentrate my fickle heart because our desires have not been fulfilled.**

*(****nepathye****) pa{u}me candāalī anijja{u} goaḍḍhaṇassa pāsahmi jhatti ṇibbaṭṭha{i} bacchā jaha kusumehiṁ suṇebatthā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) O Padmā, bring Candrāvalī to the side of Govardhana so that she may be decorated with flowers.**

***śaibyā:*** *pa{u}me sudaṁ jam ajjiā karālā taṁ jjebba jappa-garalaṁ puṇo uggiradi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā, I have heard that Karālā is again spouting poisonous gossip.**

***padmā:*** *halā amiaṁ kkhu ebaṁ jaṁ pibia ubaladdha-balahmi jādā \|*

**Перевод:**

**This is nectar, which on drinking gives me strength.**

***śaibyā*** *(sa-vailakṣyam): halā, kadhaṁ bia?*

**Перевод:**

**(Surprised) Friend, how is that?**

***padmā:*** *muddhie, goaḍḍhaṇassa girino pase jebba taṁ gauri-tīttham \|*

**Перевод:**

**Foolish girl, on the side of Govardhana Mountain is Gaurī-tīrtha.**

***śaibyā*** *(sa-harṣam): halā! saalattha-paṇḍidāsi \| tā utthehi \| candāalīaṁ tattha ṇehma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyful) Friend, you know everything. Get up. We will bring Candrāvalī.**

***padmā:*** *paḍhamam ccea candāalī mae cālidā \| tā tubarehi ṇaṁ anusarahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**I had Candrāvalī go there already. Hurry! We will follow her.**

*(ity ubhe parikrāmataḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Both walk.)**

***śaibyā:*** *pa{u}me, gaurī-kide jo kkhu sampādido so kahim ubahāro?*

**Перевод:**

**Where are the offerings for worshipping Gaurī?**

***padmā:*** *mahumaṅgala-hatthe samappidotthi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I gave them to Madhumaṅgala.**

***śaibyā:*** *pa{u}me, bibakkha{u}lassa ukkarisaṁ takkia uttamāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Just on thinking of the superiority of the rival party, I become faint.**

***padmā:*** *mā kkhu uttamma \| jam etāe mālāe daṁsidāe nirajjhabasāo kido mae bibakkha-pakkho \|*

**Перевод:**

**Do not despair. I deflated our rivals by showing the garland.**

*(śaibyā sa-harṣaṁ padmām aliṅgati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Śaibya embraces Padmā in joy.)**

***padmā:***  
*sauhāgga-puṇṇimāhe gaurī-tītthahmi phullide mahuṇā \|*  
*ajja ramantīṁ hariṇā suheṇa candāalīṁ pekkha \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Today see Candrāvalī enjoying happily with Kṛṣṇa at Gaurī-tīrtha, blossoming with honey on the auspicious full moon day!**

*(nepathye sauhāgga-puṇṇimāhe ity ādi paṭhyate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage, the verse is repeated.)**

***śaibyā*** *(sādbhutaṁ vilokya): halā! imāe muhaṁ baṅki-kadua bihaccha-ssareṇa paḍhantīe kakkhadiāe ahme ubahāsijjahma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looks in astonishment) The monkey Kakkaṭikā mocks us by reciting the verse in a disgusting voice while twisting her face.**

***padmā*** *(sa-smitam): duṭṭhe makkaḍi! tuṇḍaṁ de ḍahissaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) Wicked monkey! I will burn your snout.**

*(****nepathye:****) pa{u}mie ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \| suṇṇaṁ tujjha gharaṁ gadua ṇaaṇīāiṁ gilissaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) Padmā, stop, stop. I will go to your empty house and devour the butter.**

***śaibyā:*** *halā saccaṁ gilissadi, jam esā taṁ jjebba paḍhantī dhāidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**She will actually devour it, for she is now dashing there while reciting the verse.**

***padmā:*** *mā cintehi, ghare ajjia karālā ciṭṭhadi \| (iti parikramya saṁskṛtena) paśya paśya—*  
*sācīkṛtāṅgam iha savya-kareṇa yaṣṭiṁ*  
*viṣṭabhya vṛtta-saralām upakakṣa-kūpam \|*  
*tiṣṭhann adho viṭapinaḥ paśu-vṛnda-cārī*  
*rīrīti gītim adhunā subalas tanoti \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Don’t worry. Karālā is in the house. (Walking) Look, look! Fixing a round, straight stick near his armpit with his left hand, standing with bent body, Subala the cowherd is now singing a song beneath a tree.**

***śaibyā*** *(parikramya): halā pubbeṇa saṅkarisaṇa-kuṇḍaṁ candāalī dīsa{i} \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Walking) I see Candrāvalī near Saṅkarṣaṇa-kuṇḍa.**

***padmā*** *(sa-harṣaṁ saṁskṛtena):*  
*ayaṁ puraḥ smera-mukhāravindaḥ*  
*prayāṇa-līlā-kṛta-kumbhī-nindaḥ \|*  
*kalevara-dyoti-hṛtākṣi-tandraś*  
*candrāvalīṁ vindati kṛṣṇa-candraḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Joyfully) Beautiful Kṛṣṇa with smiling lotus face, who defeats an elephant when he walks, whose bodily effulgence relieves one of fatigue, enjoys with Candrāvalī in front of us.**

*(tataḥ praviśati kṛṣṇaś candrāvalī ca \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Kṛṣṇa and Candrāvalī enter.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(vartmāvarudhya): priye, diṣṭyādya saundarya-makaranda-bhrṅgārāyitāsi mamākṣi-bhrṅgayoḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Blocking the path) O beloved! By good fortune, today your beauty has become a golden vessel of honey for the bees of my eyes.**

***candrāvalī:*** *muñca muñca maggam \| jaṁ gaurī-tītthaṁ gadua kaccāaṇiam accissaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Leave the path since I am going to Gaurī-tīrtha to worship Kātyāyaṇī.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-smitam):*  
*labdhaṁ mām avalokya tanvi purato romālir abhyudgatā*  
*netre padya-vidhiṁ kṣaraj-jala-bhare prītyārpayāñ cakratuḥ \|*  
*vakṣaś ca skhalad-uttarīyam adiśad divyāsanaṁ sambhramād*  
*vāmāyās tava dakṣiṇaḥ parikaro diṣṭyādya vṛtto mayi \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) O beautiful Candrāvalī, seeing that I have come, your hairs have stood up in respect, and your eyes, full of flowing water, offer foot wash in affection. Your chest, with its cloth slipping away, has offered an attractive seat in respect. Though you are uncooperative, your friends by good fortune have behaved favorably to me today.**

***sakhyaḥ*** *(upasṛtya): sahi, santi bhūriṇo maggā, tā ekassiṁ ṇiruddhe ṇiruddhā na hohma \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Approaching her) There are many paths. Though he blocks one of the paths, we are not really blocked.**

***candrāvali*** *(sāci-grīvam ālokya): halā, diṭṭhiā tuhmehiṁ sahidahmi saṁbuttā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Bending her neck she sees them.) O friend, by luck I have come with you.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *katham adya rādhābhisisārayiṣor mamāntike candrāvalir upasthitā?*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Why has Candrāvalī come to me, though I desire to meet Rādhā?**

***padmā*** *(janāntikam): canda-muha! pa{u}mābalambi-karāe tti tujjha manoradhaṁ suṇia cchaleṇa mae candāalī lambhidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To Kṛṣṇa only) O moon-faced Kṛṣṇa! Hearing your desire to be with the gopī whose has the support of Padmā, I have brought Candrāvalī.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *āṁ jñātam \| padma-maṇḍanam abhilaṣyatā mayaiva dattāntarāsi \| kiṁ te dūṣaṇam? (prakāśam) sakhi, prasiddhaiva padmāyāḥ padmanābha-pakṣa-pātitā \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) I understand. Though I desired the gopī decorated with lotuses, you have given my sentence another meaning. But what is your fault? (Aloud) O friend, it is well known that Padmā is inclined to the Lord.**

***padmā:*** *aho turiaṁ gaurī-tītthaṁ lambhehi candāaliam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Oh, quickly take Candrāvalī to Gaurī-tīrtha.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *candrāvaler āgatir eva rādhikodyama-pratibandhinī vṛttā \| tad enām eva nirvyalīka-bhāvāṁ tāvat pramodayan svaṁ mano vinodayāmi \| (prakāśam)*  
*dhṛta-padmotsava-santatir*  
*alabdha-doṣodaya sadā sphurati \|*  
*sakhi kṛṣṇa-pakṣa-pūrṇā*  
*candrāvalir adbhutā tvam asi \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Candrāvalī’s coming has produced an obstacle to Rādhā’s coming. By pleasing her, since she is sincere, I will please my own mind. (Aloud) She who holds a continuous festival of lotuses has no faults at any time. O friend, fully favorable to Kṛṣṇa! You , Candrāvalī, are most astonishing.**

*(ity agre parikramya) kuraṅgākṣi! paśya kānanasya kamanīyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Coming forwards) Doe-eyed Candrāvalī! Look at the attractiveness of the forest.**

***padmā:*** *halā, eso purado suraṅga-nāma kahṇassa kuraṅgo \| jassa ghariṇi sa kidatthi raṅgiṇī nāma kuraṅgi \|*

**Перевод:**

**The black deer named Suraṅga is in front. The deer’s wife is named Raṅgiṇī.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sa-cakitaṁ nepathye karṇam dattvā sva-gatam): nūnam āgatā rādhā yad ayaṁ raṅgiṇī-kaṇṭha-dhvanir darodañcati \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Alarmed, he listens off stage and says to himself) Rādhā has certainly come, since the sound of Raṅgiṇī can be slightly heard.**

***padmā:*** *kadham eso suraṅgo dakkhiṇāhimuhaṁ dhāido?*

**Перевод:**

**Why did Suraṅga run off to the south?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(punar ātma-gatam): niṣṭaṅkitam eva raṅgiṇī-kaṇṭha-śabdenāyam ākṛṣṭaḥ kuraṅgo gaurī-tīrthaṁ jagāma \| saṅkarṣaṇa-tīrtha-tīra-vana-lekhāyāṁ vilambamānaḥ kṣaṇam udarkaṁtarkayāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Attracted by the sound of Raṅgiṇī’s voice, Suraṅga has gone without distress to Gaurī-tīrtha. I shall tarry for a while in the forest on the bank of Saṅkarṣaṇa-tīrtha. Later I will think what to do.**

***padmā****:*  
*ṇaa-pa{u}miṇī-sahassam aha-mahaṇa-rasuttaraṅga-vitthārī \|*  
*pekkha goulaṁ bia puro saroaraṁ reha{i} pphāraṁ \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Look at this wide pond in front, filled with a thousand lotuses and spreading high waves of rasa which destroy sin.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye! paśya paśya!*  
*mitre vicitram anurāga-bharaṁ vahanti*  
*saṁvardhitāli-nikarā sva-rasodayena \|*  
*sat-karṇikojjvala-rucir bhuvane samantāl*  
*lakṣmīṁ tanoti bhavatīva sarojinīyam \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beloved, please look! Carrying a remarkable burden of attraction for the sun, surrounded by many swarms of bees, its center shining with pure beauty, the lotus, like you, distributes beauty everywhere in the world by the appearance of its honey.**

***śaibyā:*** *naṁ maṇoharaṁ pa{u}miṇīṁ kīsa kalāṇihi-malāṇaṁ karedi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Why should the moon wither the attractive lotus?**

***padmā*** *(candrām upadiśya sākūṭam):*  
*surāṇuratta-hiaā iaṁ pa{u}miṇī pasāridāmo ā \|*  
*idhaṇa tumaṁ kkhaṇārāo tārā-hīsa kkhibehi karaṁ \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Pointing to the moon, she speaks with meaning.) Attracted to the sun, this lotus spreads its fragrance. O moon, since you are only red temporarily, do not cast your rays here.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *padme nātra tārāpatir aparādhyati \| yad iyaṁ padminī cañcalayā padmayā sāyaṁ mucyamānā mlāyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Padmā, the moon does not commit offense. The lotus withers because it has been given up by Lakṣmī.**

***candrāvali*** *(sa-smitaṁ puro vilokya saṁskṛtena):*  
*samada-madhupa-laulyotsekam ālokya śaṅke*  
*vihasati latikālī puṣpa-śobhā-bhareṇa \|*  
*visṛjati makaranda-cchadmanā bāṣpa-bindūn*  
*iyam ati-mṛdur ekā snehataḥ svarṇa-yūthī \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling, looking in front) I think that the groups of the creepers, seeing the increased greed of the intoxicated bee, are laughing through the beauty of their abundant flowers. And one delicate** ***svaṛṇa-yūthī*** **creeper sheds tears in the form of honey out of affection.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *priye! paśya paśya!*  
*ayam ucca-śiraḥ kadamba-rājaḥ*  
*sphurad-indindira-vṛndā-vandi-gītaḥ \|*  
*surabhī-kula-puccha-cāmarālī-*  
*marud-āvījita-vigrahaś cakāsti \|\|16\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Beloved, look, look! The king of the** ***kadamba*** **trees with raised head is praised by quivering swarms of bees and fanned by the wind of** ***cāmaras*** **in the form cow tails.**

***candrāvali:*** *ahmahe lalidā buṁdābana-lacchī \|*

**Перевод:**

**The wealth of Vṛndāvana is beautiful.**

*(tataḥ praviśati lalitā vṛndā ca \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Lalitā and Vṛndā enter.)**

***lalitā*** *(puro dṛṣṭva sa-vyatham): kakkhaḍaṁ purado saṅkaḍam edaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking ahead in pain) There is great obstacle ahead.**

***vṛndā:*** *hanta durlaṅghya-śāsanā kila karālā \| tat katham adya padmayātra candrāvalir upanītā?*

**Перевод:**

**It is most difficult to overcome Karālā’s vigilance. How then has Padmā brought Candrāvalī here today?**

***lalitā:*** *halā, saala-vijjā-biaḍḍhāsi, tā kaḍḍehi ido kahṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, you know all mystic arts. Pull Kṛṣṇa away from her.**

***vṛndā:***  
*svasya prema-maṇīnāṁ*  
*gaurava-bhājām iyaṁ varā pātrī \|*  
*hariṇā pariharaṇīyā*  
*kathaṁ nu candrāvalī bhavitā \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Candrāvalī is the excellent reservoir of the most respectable jewels of prema for Kṛṣṇa.**

**How can Kṛṣṇa be made to reject her?**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*yasyopalabhya gandhaṁ*  
*gaurava-kulam āśu cauravad bhramati \|*  
*udbhaṭam anurāga-bhaṭaṁ*  
*taṁ rañjita-nāgaraṁ naumi \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to that extraordinary warrior of** ***anurāga*** **which delights Kṛṣṇa. When he realizes a trace of that attraction, Candrāvalī’s respectful love will quickly run away like a thief.**

***vṛndā:*** *sakhi, yuktaṁ bravīṣi \| kintu dākṣiṇya-mudreyaṁ candrāvalyāṁ kṛṣṇasya tataḥ khalv amuṁ durākarṣaṁ kathayāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, you speak rightly. But Kṛṣṇa has become considerate to Candrāvalī. I say that it will be difficult to pull him away.**

***lalitā:*** *buṁde, saccaṁ bhaṇāsi \| tā imassim accahide kiṁ saraṇaṁ?*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā, you speak the truth. What method can we use at this time of calamity?**

***vṛndā:*** *prathamaṁ goṣṭhīm āviśya tattvam avadhārayāvaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**First, we should enter their group and understand the situation.**

*(ity ubhe parikrāmataḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(The two approach)**

***śaibyā*** *(vilokya janāntikam): halā pa{u}me! hanta ṇūṇaṁ gaurī-tītthe rāhī saṅgadā \| pekkha tad disādo lalidā miladi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing them, she speaks an aside.) O my friend Padmā, Rādhā must have come to Gaurī-tīrtha. Look, Lalitā has come from that direction.**

***padmā:*** *kā de hāṇī? jaṁ imiṇā duppariharā pia-sahī \|*

**Перевод:**

**What harm can they do to you? Kṛṣṇa cannot give up our dear friend Candrāvalī.**

***lalitā*** *(upasṛtya): halā candāali! vallahā-siṇehāṇahiṇṇassa kuraṅgī saṅgha-bhuaṅgassa kuraṅgassa ghare ṇa kkhu ahmehiṁ raṅgiṇī-bāsaṇijjā \| jaṁ imiṇā māsabbhantare bi sā kāla-sāra-kumārī ṇa sumarīadi tā ettha tumaṁ sakkhiṇīṁ kādum āadahmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Approaching them) O my friend Candrāvalī! We should not let the doe Raṅginī stay in the house of the deer which lusts after other does and is ignorant of the affection of his beloved. I have come to bear witness to you that the deer does not remember this other doe for the whole month.**

*(candrāvalī smayate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Candrāvalī smiles.)**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(****sva-gatam):*** *hanta mad-artham āgatā lalitā \| (candrāvalim ālokya chalam ālambya prakāśam) lalite, hṛdayeṅgitam avijñāya mudhā suraṅgam upālabhase \| tad eṣa sandeśas tvayā tasyām āvedyatām—*

*hariṇābhilaṣyamāṇā*  
*sāraṅga-ramaṇi sadā tvam atrāsi \|*  
*tad amuṁ tvad-vaśa-hṛdayaṁ*  
*hṛdayaṅgama-locane viddhi \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Oh, Lalitā has come for me. (Looking at Candrāvalī, he says aloud as a pretense) O Lalitā, you criticize Suraṅga uselessly, not understanding the desire in its heart. Please give this message to Raṅgiṇī: the deer always desires you, O wife of the deer. Know that you control his heart, O doe with touching eyes!**

***padmā*** *(janāntikam): kahṇa appaṇo piaṁ jaṇaṁ laddho si \| tā juttam ajoggāṇaṁ ahmāṇaṁ visajjaṇaṁ \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside) You have attained who is dear to you. It is right that you discard us, who are unsuitable for you.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:***  
*karavāṇi hanta divyaṁ*  
*divyāṅgi madonnatāsu gopīṣu \|*  
*anurāgitāṁ sakhi dadhe*  
*rādhā-gandhiṣu na vāmāsu \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O attractive friend! To please you, I swear I have no attraction for the unfavorable, intensely proud gopīs who are even slightly related to Rādhā.**

***padmā*** *(sa-darpa-smitam): sahi lalide! accariam accariam! tumaṁ kkhu aṇurāhā bhaṇijjasi \| tā kīsa ajja rāhiāe uadaaṁ binā udidāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(With a proud smile) O my friend Lalitā, how amazing! You are called Anurādhā. Why then do you appear here today without Rādhā?**

***lalitā*** *(saṁskṛtena):*  
*rolambī-nikurambaṁ*  
*cumbati gaṇḍaṁ pipāsayā tasya \|*  
*sarati tṛṣārtā sarasīṁ*  
*sa karīndras taṁ punar na hi sā \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All the bees out of thirst constantly kiss the cheek of the king of elephants. Pained by thirst, king of the elephant goes to the pond. The pond does not go to the elephant.**

***padmā:***  
*ekkam dhīmadi sebbe paheliaṁ me saheli jāṇīhi \|*  
*citta-phalaammi lihidā kā reha{i} māhabassa sadā \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Śaibya, my friend, hear one riddle: what is written on the slate of Mādhava’s heart?**

***śaibyā:*** *sahi candāalī \|*

**Перевод:**

**O friend, it is Candrāvalī.**

***vṛndā*** *(sa-smitam): sādhu vijñātam \| candra-maṇḍalāvali-maṇḍalena citraṁ khalu ramāpateḥ phalakaṁ śata-candram ācakṣyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) You have understood correctly. The shield of Viṣṇu is called “hundred moons’ since it is engraved with a series of moons.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sva-gatam):* *avadāta-śīleyam candrāvalī sa-lajjam apasavye kathaṁ prayāti?*

**Перевод:**

**(To himself) Why is Candrāvalī, very pure in character, walking to the left in embarrassment?**

***lalitā:***  
*maha bbāharehi buṁde paheliam dibba-paheli-viṇṇāṇe \|*  
*pia-sahi kim ahikkhāe likkhijja{i} māhavo bhuaṇe \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Please solve my riddle, O solver of attractive riddles! By what name is Mādhava known in the world, dear friend?**

***vṛndā:*** *sakhi! rādhābhikhyayā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, he is known by the name Rādhā.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *yuktam idam yad vaiśākha-paryāyau mādhava-rādhau \|*

**Перевод:**

**This is correct since the name Vaiśākha is synonymous with Mādhava and Rādhā.**

***padmā:*** *sebbe, alaṁ paheliā-pasaṅgena \| suhābehi kamalikkhaṇa-rasehim attāṇaam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Śaibyā, enough of riddles! Become happy with the rasas arising from seeing lotuses.**

***śaibyā*** *(kamalākāraṁ vilokya):*  
*bhamarassa tāba pamadaṁ padosa-mudidā kumuddadī kuṇa{i} \|*  
*jāba iaṁ pa{u}mālī binda{i} ṇahu diṭṭhiṁ edassa \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at the form of a lotus) The bee is joyfully absorbed in the water lily, which blossoms in the evening, as long as it does not see the group of lotuses.**

***padmā:*** *halā, saccaṁ bhaṇāsi \| tathā hi,*  
*vijjodantī rāhā pekkhijja{i} tāba tāraalīhiṁ \|*  
*gaaṇe tamāla-sāme ṇa jāba candāalī pphura{i} \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Friend, you speak the truth. Viśākhā constellation is seen shining along with the stars unless a host of moons shines in the sky which is as dark as a** ***tamāla*** **tree.**

***lalitā*** *(vihasya saṁskṛtena):*  
*sahacari vṛṣabhānujāyā prādurbhāve vara-tviṣopagate \|*  
*candrāvalī-śatāny api bhavanti nirdhūta-kāntīni \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Laughing) With appearance of the superior light of sun in Taurus sign, even hundreds of moons lose their beauty.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(smitvā): kiṁ vācāṭatayā? sannikṛṣṭasya surabheḥsaurābhyam anubhūyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) What is the use of all these words? We should enjoy the fragrance of the spring present at this time.**

***vṛndā*** *(sa-smitam):*  
*ullasati phulla-gātrī*  
*kā vallī nātra mādhave’bhyudite \|*  
*tan-nāmataḥ prasiddhāṁ*  
*tathāpi tāṁ mādhavīṁ naumi \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Smiling) What creeper does not bloom when spring comes? I offer respects to the** ***mādhav*****ī creeper famous for blossoming in spring.**

***padmā*** *(sa-vaimanasyaṁ parikramyoccaih): halā candāali, dhūtta-goṭṭhī-raṅge saṅgamia vigghesa-jaṇaṇī-pūaṇe kīsa siḍhilāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Depressed, she walks and speaks loudly.) My friend Candrāvalī, why have you become negligent in worshipping Durgā, by associating with this vulgar hoard?**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sopālambham):*  
*candrāvalīṁ mām anurudhyamānāṁ*  
*ruṇaddhi padme bhavatī balena \|*  
*mallīṁ tamālābhimukhaṁ milantīṁ*  
*hiṁsreva vallī purataḥ karālā \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Scolding) O Padmā, why do you forcibly obstruct Candrāvalī who is fond of me. This is similar to a frightful, violent creeper in front of us obstructing a jasmine which favors a** ***tamāla*** **tree?**

*(praviśya)* ***karālā****: ciṭṭhadha re ciṭṭhadha! diṭṭhiā magge ccea laddhāttha*

**Перевод:**

**(Entering) Stop, stop! By luck I have met you on the road.**

*(sarvaḥ paravṛtya sambhramaṁ nāṭayanti \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Everyone turns in fright)**

***śaibyā*** *(apavārya): haddhī haddhī \| kadhaṁ ettha ahme viṇṇādā buḍḍhiāe?*

**Перевод:**

**(Not letting her hear) O disaster! How did the old woman know we were here?**

***karālā****: ammo saccaṁ ccea jappidaṁ tāe ṇavaṇīa-lampaḍāe buḍḍha-makkadie \|*

**Перевод:**

**Ah! The old monkey greedy for butter spoke the truth.**

*(padmā sa-khedaṁ śaibyā-mukham īkṣate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Padmā looks at Śaibyā’s face in pain.)**

***lalitā*** *(svagataṁ): buḍḍha-makkaḍī kakkhaḍie sakkarārakkhidaṁ makkhaṇam de dāissaṁ\|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Old monkey Kakkhaṭikā, for this I will give you some butter mixed with sugar!**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(apavārya): priye, tirodhānāya sthānam api te na paśyāmi, yataḥ—*  
*savye giriḥ sphurati durgama-tuṅga-śṛṅgo*  
*gāḥ pālayaty ahaha dakṣiṇatas tathāryaḥ \|*  
*bhūḥ pṛṣṭhato virahitā vṛtibhiḥ purastāt*  
*krūrā viveśa jaratī katamātra yuktiḥ \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Aside to Candrāvalī) O beloved, I do not see a place where we can hide. Govardhana, with inaccessible, high peaks is on the left. To the right is your husband, herding the cows. The land behind is devoid of foliage. The cruel old woman has entered in front. What should we do?**

***candrāvali*** *(sva-gatam):* *hanta hanta! akaṇḍa-kakkasāe bhabbidabbadā caṇḍālīe caṇḍimā*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Oh, oh! What ferocity expressed by this woman who has become a demon with unlimited cruelty!**

***karālā*** *(saṁrambham abhinīya): pecchadha bho pecchadha imassa kusumbha-tella-kajjala-jāla-kālassa kāla-bhuaṅga-bhaaṅkara loanañcalassa sañcalassa bhuaṅgattaṇam jaṁ bāraha-maggaṁ gamido imiṇā saalāṇaṁ goula-kulaṅgaṇāṇaṁ maṅgalo kula-dhammo \| (iti sa-śiraḥ-kampam dṛśau visphārya) are sāmālayā kassa esā jāatti jāṇāsi \| suṇāhi re ṇisaṅka suṇāhi \| jo kkhu bhoiṁdassa dudio appā tassa mahā-mallassa \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Showing anger) Everyone look! Look at that depravity of this black snake with fearful eyes anointed with lamp black and oil. The conduct of all the women of Gokula has been destroyed. (She shakes her head and her eyes widen.) O black boy, do you know whose wife this is? Listen without fear. She is the wife of the great wrestler, who is a second Kaṁsa.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *karālike, tataḥ kim?*

**Перевод:**

**Karālikā, so what?**

***karālā*** *(sa-krodham): saccaṁ saccaṁ tumaṁ baṇa-majjhe appaṇaṁ dudiam rāaṇaṁ jāṇāsi\| so ccea rāa{u}la-gāmī goṭṭha-ṇāho appaṇo lalāḍaṁ tāḍissadi \|*

**Перевод:**

**It is true. It is true that you think yourself a second king in the forest. The leader of the cowherds, going to royal court, will strike his forehead in shame.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ:*** *karāle, tubhyaṁ śape \| candrāvalīṁ vilokya sādhvasaṁ gato’ham udvegam āsādayāmi\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Karālā! I swear to you. Seeing Candrāvalī, I tremble in fear.**

***karālā*** *(candrāvalīṁ vilokya sāmarṣam): ha ṇiuñjūjjāriṇi! ākomara-sikkhida-kahṇāhisāra-kosale! saṁrambhūnnaddha-gobiā-sahassa-juṭṭhāhara-bimbā-tiṇṇa-metta-biddhaṁsida-kula-bbade! ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \| kim dāṇīṁ bhāesi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Candrāvalī with anger) O girl, you spend your nights sleeplessly in the forest. Since childhood you have been expert in learning how to meet Kṛṣṇa. Your chastity has been destroyed by his red lips which have already kissed thousands of impassioned gopīs. Stop this! Are you now afraid?**

***lalitā:*** *ajje, ko kkhu doso jīaṇa-ṇāhāṇugadāe paccimā-disāe \| ko bā dosābahāriṇo surassa \| kintu edāṇam arūḍha-rāāṇam doṇaṁ rātam uppādia saṅgama-kāriṇīe sañjha-kuṭṭiṇīe ccea padosāṇubandhidā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Respected Karālā! What is the fault of the western direction which simply obeys the lord of its life? What is the fault of the sun, which removes faults? It is the twilight produces the evening by bringing together the sun and the western horizon, after turning them red.**

***karālā****: jāde saccaṁ kadhesi \| (iti prauḍham āṭopaṁ nāṭayantī) hañje pa{u}mie para-ghara-vighaṭṭiṇi! kuṭṭiṇī-kamma-lampaḍe dhiṭṭi-maṇḍala-cakkabaṭṭiṇi maha hatthādo kahaṁ mukkissasi \| (iti yaṣṭim udyacchate \|)*

**Перевод:**

**Daughter you speak the truth. (Showing deep pride) O slave Padmā! Destroyer of others’ homes, lusty to be a messenger! Leader of the impudent! Will you try to escape my hand? (Raises a stick)**

***padmā*** *(parāvṛtya): ajje, ṇa jāṇe kīsa kkhijjasi \| ahmehiṁ tujjha sāsaṇaṁ ccea kijjantam atthi\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Turning) Respected elder, I do not know why you lament since we are simply following your order.**

***vṛndā*** *(sva-gatam):* *nūnaṁ dhūrtayā śabda-cchalam ālambitaṁ padmayā \| (prakāśam) ārye, śaila-mallayor nāmādvaitena bhrānteyaṁ mugdhā bālā \| tad adya kṣamyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Deceptive Padmā now resorts to word play. (Aloud) Respected Karālā, she is a foolish girl confusing the mountain with the wrestler. Forgive her.**

*(karālā yaṣṭiṁ vimuñcati \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(She drops her stick.)**

***padmā*** *(sva-gatam):* *lalide, ciṭṭha ciṭṭha \| tuha nikkadaṁ kādum esā jaḍilaṁ gacchantī hmi \| (iti niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(To herself) Lalitā, stop, stop! I will go to Jaṭilā for requital. (Exits)**

***karālā*** *(candrāvalim ālokya): ehi bhoḥ kuḍuṅga-kuḍumbini, ehi \| (iti candrāvalīm ādāya śaibyayā saha niṣkrāntā \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking at Candrāvalī) Come here, forest housewife! Come. (Taking Candrāvalī she exits with Śaibyā).**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(socchvāsam): vṛnde, nūnaṁ sādhitārthāsi \|*

**Перевод:**

**(Sighing) O Vṛndā, you have achieved your goal.**

***vṛndā:*** *mādhava rūpiṇī mādhava-lakṣmīr gaurī-tīrthe khelati \| tayā copadhaukitaṁ sarvasvam idam daronmudritaṁ gandha-phalī-dvandvam \|*

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, the great beauty of spring plays at Gaurī-tīrtha. She has prepared two slightly opened** ***campaka*** **flowers.**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *(sānandam ādāya): vṛnde, yāvad gavāṁ cāraṇe vayasyān avasthāpya tatrānusarāmi tāvad bhavatībhyām agrataḥ prasthīyatām \| (iti niṣkrāntaḥ \|)*

**Перевод:**

**(Takes them in bliss) O Vṛndā, while I go and find the cows and have my friends care for them, you two should proceed ahead. (Exits)**

***vṛndā*** *(parikramya): lalite puraḥ sambhālaya kadamba-samrājam \| (ity upetya) hanta hanta—*  
*śaṅke paṅkaja-sambhavo’pi bhavataḥ saubhāgya-bhaṅgī-bhāraṁ*  
*vaktuṁ na kṣamate kadamba-nṛpate vṛndātavī-dyotinaḥ \|*  
*puṣpair yasya ramā-sahodaratayāpy udbhāsuraṁ kaustubhaṁ*  
*durlīlair avahelayadbhir abhitaḥ śaurer uraś chādyate \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā, look at the** ***kadamba*** **king in front. (Approaching) Ah! Ah! O king of the** ***kadamba*** **trees! I think that even Brahmā cannot describe your great fortune as you shine resplendent in Vṛndāvana. Your flowers, which playfully mock the Kaustubha which shines as the brother of Lakṣmī, completely cover the chest of Kṛṣṇa.**

***lalitā*** *(puro vilokya): buṁde, iaṁ visāhā-dudiā bhaabadī māanda-kuḍaṅge pracchannaṁ ciṭṭhadi\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Looking ahead) O Vṛndā, there is Paurṇamāsī with Viśākhā, hiding in a mango grove.**

***vṛndā*** *(lavaṅga-latāntike rādhāṁ vilokya): lalite, paśya paśya—*  
*kim itaḥ suṣamā vapuṣmati*  
*kim abhivyaktir alaṁ guna-śriyaḥ \|*  
*athavā praṇayābhisampadaḥ*  
*kim iyaṁ mūrtir udeti rādhikā \|\|31\|\|*

*(punar nirvarṇya)*  
*karṇālaṅkṛta-kamalā*  
*kuṇṭala-veṇi-śikharoccalat-kamalā \|*  
*kara-kamalāśrita-kamalā*  
*viḍambayaty alam asau kamalām \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Seeing Rādhā among clove creepers) Lalitā, look, look! Is this beauty incarnate? Is this the manifestation of a wealth of good qualities? Or is it form of perfection of love, Rādhikā?**

**(Looking again) Adorned on the ears with lotuses, with lotuses quivering at the tip of her braid, holding a lotus in her hand, she mocks Lakṣmī.**

*(nepathye)*  
*karṇāndolita-mugdha-gandha-phalikā-dvandvaḥ kadamba-srajā*  
*saṁvīto muralī-karambita-karaś cūḍāñcale candrikā \|*  
*dūrād eṣa manaḥśilā-tilakinā bhālena bibhrad-dyutiṁ*  
*mūrtaḥ khelati hanta nanda-gṛhiṇī-vātsalya-lakṣmī-rasaḥ \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**(Offstage) With two attractive** ***campakas*** **swinging in his ears, wearing a** ***kadamba*** **garland, with flute in his hand and peacock feather in his hair, shining with** ***manaḥśilā tilaka*** **on his forehead, the personified rasa of Yaśodā’s maternal affection, Kṛṣṇa, plays in the distance.**

***lalitā:*** *ṇūṇaṁ bhaavadīe dūre diṭṭho māhabo jaṁ vaṇṇīedi \|*

**Перевод:**
